## 1. Exact dependency cut

The repaired value dependency is:

```text
R2c 8[2] rank-free CandidateMatch
  -> R2d 9[0] receipt- and representative-free semantic projection
  -> R2d 9[1] complete-key comparison
  -> R2d 11[0] complete all-raw pass
  -> successful-leaf rank rho
  -> PairCandidateArena materialization
  -> ExactEvidenceRefV1 and CandidateEnvelopeRefV1
  -> final CandidateMatchOutputV1 and Level-II tag-3 payload
  -> root-0xe7 CheckedLeafOutcomeV1
  -> quotient classes and pair coverage
  -> R2d 11[1] pair disposition.
```

Slot `9[0]` consumes the frozen
`RankFreeCandidateMatchOutputV1` directly. It cannot inspect an arena rank,
`ExactEvidenceRefV1`, `CandidateEnvelopeRefV1`, final checked candidate, tag-3
payload, checked leaf outcome, class, coverage value, or disposition. This
makes quotient projection rank invariant and prevents a slot-9/slot-11 cycle.

Slot `11[0]` is the first operation allowed to see the complete terminal result
for every raw ordinal. It alone may assign `rho`, materialize both references,
and construct the final R2c pair. A success-only vector, claimed prefix count,
ambient insertion order, pointer identity, or digest-only lookup is forbidden.

## 2. Universal wire and authority boundary

All new values use the frozen R2a wire:

- enum and sum tags are `u8` in displayed zero-based order;
- root-local schema versions and binder depths are little-endian `u16`;
- type and record-field ordinals are little-endian `u16`;
- source, raw, path, class, member, and arena ordinals are little-endian `u32`;
- history/event/declaration ordinals and every count, length, or byte offset are
  little-endian `u64`;
- vectors carry a `u64` element count; and
- a rooted object is `root:u8 || schema_version:u16=1 || payload fields`.

A nested root-mapped value omits its root byte and root-local version unless a
field explicitly retains full rooted bytes. R2d assigns no final `TypeIdV1`;
R2e may assign only topologically earlier nonzero IDs after R2d and R3 close.

A quotient key, checked outcome, coverage record, comparison, or disposition is
canonical data, not authority. Authority remains in the privately constructed
`VerifiedEnvelopeCensus` and its owned arenas. A reference is usable only under
its exact owner and always retains its complete typed identity. A digest may
index a lookup but cannot decide equality, inequality, ownership, completeness,
or classification.

## 3. Fixed pair and private candidate arena

The private runtime binding is:

```text
PairEvaluationBindingV1 =
  (profile_binding:EnvelopeProfileRefV1,
   history_binding:EnvelopeHistoryRefV1,
   evaluation_binding:EvaluationBindingRefV1,
   anchor:OccurrenceId14V1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1)

PairCandidateArenaV1 =
  (binding:PairEvaluationBindingV1,
   exact_evidence:vector<ExactEvidenceTranscriptV1>,
   candidates:vector<CheckedCandidateEnvelopeV1>).
```

These records have no new root and are never caller input. The five binding
fields come from one privately owned evaluation instance and agree with the
raw enumeration, every invoked operation, and every final pair value by full
canonical bytes.

If a complete pair has `m` successful rank-free matches, both arena vectors
have `u64` length `m`, with `m <= 262,144`. For every checked `u32` position
`k<m`:

```text
candidates[k].exact_evidence = exact_evidence[k]
```

by full bytes. Both vectors are ordered by ascending successful raw ordinal.
They share one index domain; there is no separately ordered evidence arena.

## 4. Complete all-raw pass and shared rank

Let `R` be `RawEnumerationV1.codes` for the fixed pair and let `n=R.len`. R2b
already requires `R` to be the complete A3 structural order and
`n <= CompleteRawCodesPerPair = 262,144`. Slot `11[0]` must produce one terminal
leaf for every position of `R`, including a checked false from the first
rejecting pipeline operation. Decoder false never prunes a structural subtree.

The internal construction partition is reserved as:

```text
RankFreeCheckedLeafOutcomeV1 ::=                         // candidate, R3-open
  CandidateMatch=0(
    raw_ordinal:u32,
    raw_code:RawConstructorCodeV1,
    match:RankFreeCandidateMatchOutputV1,
    quotient_key:QuotientKeyV1)
| CheckedFalse=1(
    raw_ordinal:u32,
    raw_code:RawConstructorCodeV1,
    receipt:R3::CheckedFalseReceiptV1).
```

This is unrooted construction state, not a publishable `0xe7` value. It remains
schema-open because `QuotientKeyV1` and the qualified R3 receipt remain open.

For complete internal vector `P`:

```text
P.len = n

P[j].raw_ordinal = checked_u32(j)
P[j].raw_code    = R[j]

rho(j) = checked_u32(
  count { i:u64 | i < j and P[i] is CandidateMatch }).

m = count { j:u64 | j < n and P[j] is CandidateMatch }

candidate_arena.exact_evidence.len = m
candidate_arena.candidates.len     = m
```

If `P[j] = CandidateMatch(j_u32,r,C,K)`, then `j_u32=checked_u32(j)`,
`r=R[j]`, and `C` is the exact successful `SubjectConstruction[2]` output
invoked for `R[j]`. In particular:

```text
C.candidate.match_evidence.derive.raw_ordinal = j_u32
C.candidate.match_evidence.derive.raw_code    = r
C.candidate.envelope.raw_interface_and_implementation.raw_code = r
```

`K` is byte-identical to the key returned by the unique successful
`QuotientProjectionAndComparison[0]` invocation on that same `C`. These
equations forbid cross-leaf substitution of a match or quotient key even when
the substituted values are separately valid.

There is exactly one terminal value at every `j<n`, with no gap, duplicate,
deletion, reorder, or success-only substitute. An empty `R` lawfully produces
an empty `P` and empty arena if all census/cardinality checks complete. For a
successful leaf `j`, `rho(j)` is its dense zero-based arena rank. The bound on
`n` makes the displayed conversion exact; conversion failure is nevertheless
checked before allocation.

The first checked false excludes only that raw leaf and evaluation continues.
Any abort from raw preflight, an invoked per-raw operation, quotient projection
or encoding, integer conversion, allocation/resource accounting, comparison,
arena materialization, or final outcome encoding suppresses the entire pair.
On abort no complete census, class partition, pair coverage, or disposition
exists. A partially filled buffer has no canonical status.

## 5. Frozen R2c materialization equations

For `P[j]=CandidateMatch(j_u32,r,C,K)` with
`C.candidate=(E,X,M_0)`, R2d must use the frozen R2c conversion without
reinterpretation:

```text
M.derive.raw_ordinal          = M_0.derive.raw_ordinal
M.derive.raw_code             = M_0.derive.raw_code
M.derive.checked_built        = M_0.derive.checked_built
M.derive.core                 = M_0.derive.core
M.derive.exact_evidence       =
  ExactEvidenceRefV1(
    owner_local_index = rho(j),
    full_identity     = M_0.derive.exact_evidence_identity)

M.anchor                      = M_0.anchor
M.compatibility               = M_0.compatibility

Q.envelope                    = E
Q.exact_evidence              = X
Q.match_evidence              = M

L.profile_binding             = X.seed.profile
L.history_binding             = X.seed.history_and_evaluation.history
L.anchor                      = X.seed.anchor
L.evaluation_binding          =
  X.seed.history_and_evaluation.evaluation_binding
L.constructor                 = X.seed.constructor
L.raw_code                    = X.seed.raw_code
L.envelope                    =
  CandidateEnvelopeRefV1(
    owner_local_index = rho(j),
    full_identity     = E)
L.match_evidence              = M

candidate_arena.exact_evidence[rho(j)] = X
candidate_arena.candidates[rho(j)]     = Q

resolve_PairCandidateArena(M.derive.exact_evidence) = X
resolve_PairCandidateArena(L.envelope)               = Q

(Q_j,L_j):CandidateMatchOutputV1 = (Q,L).
```

Both references carry the same `rho(j)`. The exact evidence identity is
recomputed from full rooted `0xfc,u16=1` bytes; the candidate identity is the
full `RawCapabilityEnvelopeV1`. Neither digest nor full identity chooses the
rank.

Let `O` be the final ordered `CheckedLeafOutcomeV1` vector. Its exact projection
from the complete rank-free vector is:

```text
O.len = P.len = n

P[j] = CheckedFalse(j_u32,r,f)
  => O[j] = CheckedFalse(j_u32,r,f)

P[j] = CandidateMatch(j_u32,r,C,K)
  => O[j] = CandidateMatch(j_u32,r,(Q_j,L_j),K)

O[j].match.candidate.match_evidence.derive.raw_ordinal = j_u32
O[j].match.candidate.match_evidence.derive.raw_code    = r
O[j].match.level_two_payload.raw_code                  = r.
```

Here `(Q_j,L_j)` is the value constructed by the equations above from `C` at
the shared rank `rho(j)`. The two arena vectors contain exactly the successful
leaf values so constructed, and `O` contains exactly the displayed projection
of every `P[j]`; no extra arena value or final outcome exists.

## 6. Root `0xe7` outer reservation

The conservative final outcome sum is:

```text
CheckedLeafOutcomeV1 ::=                              // R2d/R3-open
  CandidateMatch=0(
    raw_ordinal:u32,
    raw_code:RawConstructorCodeV1,
    match:CandidateMatchOutputV1,
    quotient_key:QuotientKeyV1)
| CheckedFalse=1(
    raw_ordinal:u32,
    raw_code:RawConstructorCodeV1,
    receipt:R3::CheckedFalseReceiptV1).
```

The success branch retains the complete final pair reserved by R2c; it does not
silently discard the checked candidate and keep only the tag-3 payload. `Abort`
is absent. The future rooted bytes are exactly:

```text
0xe7 || little_endian_u16(1) || variant_tag || variant_payload.
```

This is an outer-position reservation, not a closed schema-DAG node. The false
branch contains R3 directly; the success branch contains R3 transitively
through `CandidateMatchOutputV1`; and `QuotientKeyV1` remains open. R3 may fill
its qualified receipt types but may not change these tags or field positions.
The displayed `CandidateMatch=0`, `CheckedFalse=1` order deliberately supersedes
the opposite order in rejected A4-O RC1. R4 must bind this repaired order
explicitly; it may not regenerate coverage fixtures by copying RC1.

## 7. The two quotient eliminations

Root `0xfd` performs two independent projections. Both are mandatory.

First, representative evidence is absent:

- all R3 receipts, cardinalities, resource accounts, meters, and limits;
- decode/build evidence and kernel replay vectors;
- normalization derivation steps and idempotence/presentation replay evidence;
- disposition typing/boundary/substitution replay vectors and construction
  transcripts;
- `EnvelopeCoreBytesV1`, `ExactEvidenceTranscriptV1`,
  `EvidenceCommitmentV1`, and evidence-only digests; and
- checked candidate, outcome, coverage, disposition, action-trace, and
  action-image ownership data.

Second, representative coordinates and raw presentation are absent:

- candidate `raw_ordinal` and `RawConstructorCodeV1`;
- nested `CtxCodeV1`, `TelCodeV1`, `SecCodeV1`, `SubCodeV1`,
  `RawContractCodeV1`, and `DischargeCodeV1`;
- raw contexts, raw telescope/section/substitution presentations,
  `source_term`, and `reindexed_term`;
- checked-built, exact-evidence, and candidate-envelope references;
- successful-leaf rank and every owner-local index;
- `SourceLeafV1.source_ordinal` and procedural support `slot_ordinal`,
  `raw_ordinal`, `work_ordinal`, and owner-map ordinals; and
- raw-membership/replay obligation rows whose truth depends on a particular
  presentation rather than the normalized semantic candidate.

A merely receipt-stripped R2c copy is therefore invalid. Retaining the second
list would tend to force different raw matches into singleton classes, contrary
to A3's explicit rule that several raw matches may inhabit one quotient class.

Exact source identities, occurrence identities, normalized carriers and terms,
binder keys, semantic field paths, lineage, dependency edges, normalized
support, and canonical graph order remain when they carry semantic meaning.
`RawFieldPathV1` is a schema/provenance path and is not the forbidden raw-code
ordinal. A procedural vector position that survives semantically is reconstructed
and named as a dependency ordinal rather than copied from an R2c raw ordinal.

## 8. Owner- and source-coordinate-free identity frontier

The quotient binding cannot reuse an owner-bound reference merely because its
full identity is present. Its semantic identities are:

```text
QuotientProfileIdentityV1    = ProfileManifestIdV1
QuotientHistoryIdentityV1    = CompleteHistoryIdentityV1
QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1 = BirthSourceCensusV1
QuotientSourceIdentityV1     = SourceRefV1

QuotientParentIdentitiesV1 =
  (jg1:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1,
   jg2a:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1,
   jg2b2a:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1,
   jg2b2b0:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1,
   jg2b2b1:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1,
   profile:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1)

QuotientBindingCandidateV1 =                               // candidate schema
  (versions:EnvelopeVersionBindingsV1,
   profile_identity:QuotientProfileIdentityV1,
   parent_identities:QuotientParentIdentitiesV1,
   history_identity:QuotientHistoryIdentityV1,
   evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   anchor:AnchorIdentityV1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1).
```

The six parent tags remain fixed by their field positions and each full
`EnvelopeParentIdentityV1` tag must agree with that position. The projection
drops only `owner_local_index`; it never replaces a full identity with a digest
unless that frozen identity type itself is a digest newtype.

For `C.candidate.envelope=E` and
`N=E.normalization.normalized_endpoint`, the group-1 projection is exactly:

```text
Q1.versions            = N.versions
Q1.profile_identity    =
  ProfilePayload(N.parents.profile.full_identity)
Q1.parent_identities   =
  (N.parents.jg1.full_identity,
   N.parents.jg2a.full_identity,
   N.parents.jg2b2a.full_identity,
   N.parents.jg2b2b0.full_identity,
   N.parents.jg2b2b1.full_identity,
   N.parents.profile.full_identity)
Q1.history_identity    = N.owned_history.history.full_identity
Q1.evaluation_identity =
  N.owned_history.evaluation_binding.full_identity
Q1.anchor              = N.anchor
Q1.constructor         = N.constructor.
```

`ProfilePayload(EnvelopeParentIdentityV1::Profile(p))=p`; any other tag in the
profile parent position is an invariant abort.

The profile identity must also equal the profile parent payload and the profile
identity inside the evaluation census; the owner-stripped
profile identity in the rank-free match's construction seed, when
cross-checked, must equal that same value.
The history commitment must equal the
census complete-head commitment; the constructor must agree with the version
binding and every selected group-2 tag. These are full-byte checks. No owner
index is copied into `Q1`.

Lineage likewise needs semantic source identities:

```text
QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1 =                  // candidate schema
  (origin_path:OriginPathV1,
   raw_field_path:RawFieldPathV1,
   principal_source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   pre_expansion_occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
   exact_anchor:OccurrenceId14V1)

QuotientFieldLineageEntryCandidateV1 =
  (raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1,
   source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   derivation_edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1)

QuotientFieldLineageCandidateV1 =
  (field_ordinal:u32,
   field_path:EnvelopeFieldPathV1,
   entries:vector<QuotientFieldLineageEntryCandidateV1>).
```

These remain `CandidateV1` members of the now-closed QG1 schema-candidate
subgraph. The audited projection proves full-identity equality after removing
only the owner/census position; their final names and TypeIds remain blocked by
the still-open complete root.

## 9. Initial normalized checked-value mirrors

The current safe semantic frontier omits raw codes, proof/replay fields,
`SourceLeafV1.source_ordinal`, and weakening images, whose exact source-tagged
reconstruction is frozen below:

```text
QuotientWeakeningViewCandidateV1 =
  (source_binder_keys:vector<BinderKeyV1>,
   target_prefix_binder_keys:vector<BinderKeyV1>,
   shift:u32)

QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1 =
  (path:RawFieldPathV1,
   source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   target_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   weakening:QuotientWeakeningViewCandidateV1,
   normalized_type:Term)

QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1 =
  (path:RawFieldPathV1,
   source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   target_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   weakening:QuotientWeakeningViewCandidateV1,
   expected_type:Term,
   normalized_term:Term)

QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1 =
  (binder_keys:vector<BinderKeyV1>,
   normalized_context:NormalizedContextV1)

QuotientCheckedTelescopeFieldCandidateV1 =
  (source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   path:RawFieldPathV1,
   introduced_binder:BinderKeyV1,
   checked_type:QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1)

QuotientCheckedTelescopeCandidateV1 =
  (base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   fields:vector<QuotientCheckedTelescopeFieldCandidateV1>,
   normalized:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
   end_context:NormalizedContextV1)

QuotientCheckedSectionFieldCandidateV1 =
  (source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   path:RawFieldPathV1,
   checked_term:QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1)

QuotientCheckedSectionCandidateV1 =
  (base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   target:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
   fields:vector<QuotientCheckedSectionFieldCandidateV1>,
   normalized:NormalizedSectionV1).
```

For frozen R2b values, the elementary projection functions are exactly:

```text
QSourceLeaf((source_ordinal,source)) = source

QWeakening(w) =
  (w.source_binder_keys,
   w.target_prefix_binder_keys,
   w.shift)

QType(v) =
  (v.path,
   QSourceLeaf(v.source),
   v.target_context,
   QWeakening(v.weakening),
   v.normalized_type)

QTerm(v) =
  (v.path,
   QSourceLeaf(v.source),
   v.target_context,
   QWeakening(v.weakening),
   v.expected_type,
   v.normalized_term)

QContext(c) =
  (c.binder_keys,c.normalized_context)

QTelescopeField(f) =
  (QSourceLeaf(f.source),
   f.path,
   f.introduced_binder,
   QType(f.checked_type))

QTelescope(t) =
  (t.base_context,
   map(QTelescopeField,t.fields),
   t.normalized,
   t.end_context)

QSectionField(f) =
  (QSourceLeaf(f.source),
   f.path,
   QTerm(f.checked_term))

QSection(s) =
  (s.base_context,
   s.target,
   map(QSectionField,s.fields),
   s.normalized).
```

These are structural functions, not caller callbacks. Before applying them,
the quotient compiler rechecks all frozen R2b shape equations. In particular:

```text
QContext source c:
  c.binder_keys.len = c.normalized_context.len

QTelescope source t:
  t.fields.len = t.normalized.len
  prefix(0) = t.base_context
  prefix(i+1) = append_type(prefix(i),t.normalized[i])
  t.fields[i].source = t.fields[i].checked_type.source
  t.fields[i].path = t.fields[i].checked_type.path
  t.fields[i].checked_type.target_context = prefix(i)
  t.fields[i].checked_type.normalized_type = t.normalized[i]
  t.end_context = prefix(t.fields.len)

QSection source s:
  s.fields.len = s.target.len = s.normalized.len
  s.fields[i].source = s.fields[i].checked_term.source
  s.fields[i].path = s.fields[i].checked_term.path
  s.fields[i].checked_term.target_context = s.base_context
  s.fields[i].checked_term.expected_type =
    PrefixSubstitute(
      s.target[i],s.normalized[0..i])
  s.fields[i].checked_term.normalized_term = s.normalized[i].
```

Every equality is full canonical-byte equality. `append_type` is the frozen
oldest-first dependent-context extension. The section checker has already
derived each retained `expected_type` by the frozen JG2b2b1 capture-safe
oldest-codomain-first `PrefixSubstitute` rule over exactly the preceding image
prefix; projection copies that derived value and rechecks the displayed
equation, never accepting a caller expected type. Any length, path, source,
prefix, expected type, normalized term, or end-context mismatch is an
invariant abort.

Every `QSourceLeaf` call in this record is relative to the one fixed group-1
census `Sigma`. Before erasing an ordinal it requires

```text
u64(source_ordinal) < Sigma.source_refs.len
Sigma.source_refs[u64(source_ordinal)] = source
```

by full bytes. Thus the shorthand function never accepts a detached tuple and
never turns an invalid census coordinate into a semantic identity.

Every retained term is therefore the exact canonical normalized term
reconstructed from the frozen checked value. `raw_code`, raw context,
`source_term`, `reindexed_term`, formation/typing replay, and source ordinal
have no image. These checked mirrors do not enter group 2 below: that group
retains only their normalized projections. They remain candidates for the
later support and subject-path projections, but their elementary projection
functions are now closed.

### 9.1 Checked-mirror closure conditions

The remaining checked-compound mirrors are outside group 2. They may be used
only where later support or subject-path structure needs their semantic
source-backed shape:

```text
QuotientCheckedSubstitutionCandidateV1 =
  (section:QuotientCheckedSectionCandidateV1)

QuotientCheckedGrammarOperationCandidateV1 =
  (parameters:QuotientCheckedTelescopeCandidateV1,
   results:QuotientCheckedTelescopeCandidateV1)

QuotientCheckedGrammarCandidateV1 =
  (exports:QuotientCheckedTelescopeCandidateV1,
   operations:vector<QuotientCheckedGrammarOperationCandidateV1>)

QuotientCheckedPortCandidateV1 =
  (operation_ordinal:u32,
   arguments:QuotientCheckedSectionCandidateV1,
   derived_output:NormalizedTelescopeV1)

QuotientCheckedContractCandidateV1 =
  (checked_type:QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1,
   checked_left:QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1,
   checked_right:QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1,
   carrier_order_value:NormalizedContractValueV1)

QuotientCheckedSchemeCandidateV1 =
  (trigger:QuotientCheckedTelescopeCandidateV1,
   ports:vector<QuotientCheckedPortCandidateV1>,
   contracts:vector<QuotientCheckedContractCandidateV1>,
   normalized:NormalizedSchemeValueV1)

QuotientCheckedDischargeCandidateV1 =
  (section:QuotientCheckedSectionCandidateV1,
   normalized:NormalizedDischargeValueV1).
```

Their exact structural projections are:

```text
QSubstitution(x) =
  (QSection(x.section))

QGrammarOperation(x) =
  (QTelescope(x.parameters),QTelescope(x.results))

QGrammar(x) =
  (QTelescope(x.exports),map(QGrammarOperation,x.operations))

QPort(x) =
  (x.operation_ordinal,QSection(x.arguments),x.derived_output)

QContract(x) =
  (QType(x.checked_type),
   QTerm(x.checked_left),
   QTerm(x.checked_right),
   x.carrier_order_value)

QScheme(x) =
  (QTelescope(x.trigger),
   map(QPort,x.ports),
   map(QContract,x.contracts),
   x.normalized)

QDischarge(x) =
  (QSection(x.section),x.normalized).
```

Projection erases each source `raw_code`, checked-particular proof, kernel
replay, and receipt. Every projected vector has exactly its source length and
source order. Grammar operations retain grammar order; ports retain dependency
order; checked contracts retain raw source order even though the independently
normalized contract set below is canonical. The semantic cross-checks are:

```text
NormalizedSubstitutionOf(q).delta  = q.section.base_context
NormalizedSubstitutionOf(q).gamma  = ContextWire(q.section.target)
NormalizedSubstitutionOf(q).images = q.section.normalized

NormalizedGrammarOf(q).exports = q.exports.normalized
NormalizedGrammarOf(q).operations.len = q.operations.len
NormalizedGrammarOf(q).operations[i] =
  (q.operations[i].parameters.normalized,
   q.operations[i].results.normalized)

q_contract.carrier_order_value =
  (q_contract.checked_left.normalized_term,
   q_contract.checked_right.normalized_term,
   q_contract.checked_type.normalized_type)

q_scheme.normalized.trigger = q_scheme.trigger.normalized
q_scheme.normalized.ports.len = q_scheme.ports.len
q_scheme.normalized.ports[i] =
  (q_scheme.ports[i].operation_ordinal,
   q_scheme.ports[i].arguments.normalized,
   q_scheme.ports[i].derived_output)
q_scheme.normalized.contracts =
  canonical_contract_set(
    map(.carrier_order_value,q_scheme.contracts))

q_discharge.normalized.section = q_discharge.section.normalized.
```

The checked-contract vector remains in source-backed path order. The normalized
contract vector is the independently canonical carrier set. The two conversions
used above are now fixed as follows:

```text
ContextWire(t:NormalizedTelescopeV1) =
  NormalizedContextV1(
    DependentContext(exact_oldest_first_term_vector(t)))

canonical_contract_set(v) =
  dedup_adjacent_full_bytes(
    lexicographic_sort_full_canonical_bytes(v)).
```

`ContextWire` is only the type-directed semantic-newtype rewrap of the exact
underlying term vector; it performs no normalization, insertion, permutation,
or binder shift. Its output must pass the frozen well-formed dependent-context
validation, which is already guaranteed by a valid checked substitution.
`canonical_contract_set` sorts complete `NormalizedContractValueV1` encodings,
then retains the first value of each byte-equal adjacent run. It has no digest,
term-only comparator, stable-source-order tie-break, or caller policy. These
definitions close the two names as deterministic conversions.

Weakening images may be erased only by reconstruction. For an enclosing
quotient type/term view, let `m:u64` be the source-binder-key count and
`r:u64=u64(weakening.shift)`. The source checked value must satisfy:

```text
target_context.len = checked_add_u64(m,r)

source = Current(_):
  target_prefix_binder_keys = source_binder_keys
  erased_images.len = m
  erased_images[i] =
    Term::Var(index=checked_u32(m+r-1-i))  for 0 <= i < m

source = OlderPublic(_):
  source_binder_keys = []
  target_prefix_binder_keys = []
  m = 0
  r = target_context.len
  erased_images = [].
```

The complete source `WeakeningViewV1.images` must equal `erased_images` in
order. Its `sequential_replay` is representative evidence and is erased. Any
checked addition, conversion, length, key, source-tag, or image mismatch is an
invariant abort, not a different quotient value.

### 9.2 QG1a structural normalized-endpoint projection candidate

R2c's frozen normalization frontier fixes the quotient-relevant material order:
canonical payload, indexed inputs, indexed output, and normalized public fields.
Group 2 preserves those four separately typed roots, including their repeated
constructor tags and role-distinct copies.

```text
QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1 ::=
  Formation=0(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    implementation_a:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Abstraction=1(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    b:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    implementation_xi:NormalizedContextV1,
    cut:u32,
    implementation_b:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Aggregation=2(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    b:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    implementation_c:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Transport=3(
    delta:NormalizedContextV1,
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    theta:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    implementation_c:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Comparison=4(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    b:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    comparison:NormalizedComparisonValueV1,
    implementation_a:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    implementation_b:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| DemandCompiler=5(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    activation:NormalizedSectionV1,
    live:NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1,
    implementation_activation:NormalizedSectionV1)
| DischargeTransformer=6(
    delta:NormalizedContextV1,
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    theta:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    activation:NormalizedSectionV1,
    live:NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1,
    discharge:NormalizedDischargeValueV1,
    implementation_discharge:NormalizedDischargeValueV1)

QuotientGroupTwoRealizationsCandidateV1 =
  (canonical_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   indexed_inputs:IndexedInputsV1,
   indexed_output:IndexedOutputV1,
   normalized_public_fields:vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>).
```

For rank-free candidate `C.candidate=(E,X,M_0)`, put:

```text
N = E.normalization.normalized_endpoint
P = N.canonical_payload
I = N.indexed_inputs
O = N.indexed_output
F = N.normalized_public_fields
B = X.seed.checked_built.full_identity.built.
```

`QPayload` copies every already-normalized carrier field by full bytes and
applies exactly the frozen R2c checked-wrapper semantic table:

```text
QPayload(CheckedContextV1 x)   = x.normalized_context
QPayload(CheckedTelescopeV1 x) = x.normalized
QPayload(CheckedSectionV1 x)   = x.normalized
QPayload(CheckedDischargeV1 x) = x.normalized.
```

Thus its seven selected branches are:

```text
Formation:
  (P.gamma,P.a,P.a_raw.normalized)

Abstraction:
  (P.gamma,P.a,P.b,P.xi_raw.normalized_context,
   P.cut,P.b_raw.normalized)

Aggregation:
  (P.gamma,P.a,P.b,P.c_raw.normalized)

Transport:
  (P.delta,P.gamma,P.theta,P.a,P.c_raw.normalized)

Comparison:
  (P.gamma,P.a,P.b,P.comparison,
   P.a_raw.normalized,P.b_raw.normalized)

DemandCompiler:
  (P.gamma,P.grammar,P.scheme,P.activation,P.live,
   P.activation_raw.normalized)

DischargeTransformer:
  (P.delta,P.gamma,P.theta,P.grammar,P.scheme,
   P.activation,P.live,P.discharge,P.discharge_raw.normalized).
```

The complete group projection is:

```text
Q2.canonical_payload        = QPayload(P)
Q2.indexed_inputs           = I
Q2.indexed_output           = O
Q2.normalized_public_fields = F.
```

The tags of `Q2.canonical_payload`, `P`, `I.indices`, `I.nominal_inputs`,
`O`, `N.constructor`, `Q1.constructor`, and `B` all agree. Writing
`Q=Q2.canonical_payload`, the repeated typed roles obey the following full
canonical-byte equations (apart from the displayed checked integer conversion):

```text
Formation:
  Q.gamma = I.indices.Formation.gamma
  I.nominal_inputs.Formation.here = Unit
  Q.a = O.Formation
  Q.implementation_a = O.Formation

Abstraction:
  Q.gamma = I.indices.Abstraction.gamma
  Q.a = I.indices.Abstraction.a
  Q.implementation_xi = I.indices.Abstraction.gamma_a
  I.nominal_inputs.Abstraction.here = Unit
  Q.b = I.nominal_inputs.Abstraction.b
  Q.implementation_b = I.nominal_inputs.Abstraction.b
  Q.cut = checked_u32(Q.gamma.len)
  O.Abstraction = NormalizedFamilyValueV1(Q.gamma,Q.a,Q.b)

Aggregation:
  Q.gamma = I.indices.Aggregation.gamma
  Q.a = I.indices.Aggregation.a
  Q.b = I.indices.Aggregation.b
  I.nominal_inputs.Aggregation.family =
    NormalizedFamilyValueV1(Q.gamma,Q.a,Q.b)
  Q.implementation_c = O.Aggregation

Transport:
  Q.delta = I.indices.Transport.delta
  Q.gamma = I.indices.Transport.gamma
  Q.theta = I.nominal_inputs.Transport.theta
  Q.a = I.nominal_inputs.Transport.a
  Q.theta.delta = Q.delta
  Q.theta.gamma = Q.gamma
  Q.implementation_c = O.Transport

Comparison:
  Q.gamma = I.indices.Comparison.gamma
  Q.a = I.indices.Comparison.a
  Q.b = I.indices.Comparison.b
  I.nominal_inputs.Comparison.left = Q.a
  I.nominal_inputs.Comparison.right = Q.b
  Q.implementation_a = Q.a
  Q.implementation_b = Q.b
  O.Comparison = Q.comparison
  Q.comparison.gamma = Q.gamma
  Q.comparison.left = Q.a
  Q.comparison.right = Q.b
  Q.comparison.route = KernelNormalizedTypeEquality

DemandCompiler:
  Q.gamma = I.indices.DemandCompiler.gamma
  Q.grammar = I.indices.DemandCompiler.grammar
  Q.scheme = I.indices.DemandCompiler.scheme
  Q.activation = I.indices.DemandCompiler.activation
  Q.grammar = I.nominal_inputs.DemandCompiler.grammar
  Q.scheme = I.nominal_inputs.DemandCompiler.scheme
  Q.live = O.DemandCompiler
  Q.implementation_activation = Q.activation

DischargeTransformer:
  Q.delta = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.delta
  Q.gamma = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.gamma
  Q.grammar = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.grammar
  Q.scheme = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.scheme
  Q.activation = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.activation
  Q.live = I.indices.DischargeTransformer.live
  Q.theta = I.nominal_inputs.DischargeTransformer.theta
  Q.discharge = I.nominal_inputs.DischargeTransformer.discharge
  Q.theta.delta = Q.delta
  Q.theta.gamma = Q.gamma
  Q.implementation_discharge = O.DischargeTransformer.
```

The common final-field equation is:

```text
Q2.normalized_public_fields
  = N.normalized_public_fields
  = Fields_c(B).
```

This vector follows
`PublicLeaves(Indices) ++ PublicLeaves(Inputs) ++ PublicLeaves(Output) ++
PublicLeaves(RawRealization)`. Equal values at distinct
`EnvelopeFieldPathV1` roles remain distinct. Field ordinals are contiguous
checked `u32` vector positions; paths and normalized judgments are copied by
full canonical bytes.

A1 field 11 and the checked-built value are normalization-witness and
cross-check inputs only. In particular,
`E.raw_interface_and_implementation.raw_realization` is not a second quotient
source: R2c relates its selected paths to `N` through normalization material
relations, not by a general byte-equality assumption.

Group 2 contains no checked wrapper, source identity, owner reference, binder
key, `RawFieldPathV1`, raw code, decoded payload, replay, receipt, rank, resource
account, or evidence digest. It retains `EnvelopeFieldPathV1` only inside
`NormalizedPublicFieldV1`.

The earlier flattened alternative is rejected for ordinary V1 because it merged
separately typed roots without a frozen reconstruction theorem. Independent
audits have confirmed this structural form's tag agreement, wrapper projection,
role repetitions, Abstraction-cut equation, and public-field census. It remains
`CandidateV1` only because QG2, QG3, root `0xfd`, and final TypeIds remain open.

### 9.3 QG1b normalized boundary and local-context candidate

Group 3 retains normalized boundary values and exact history/source identities,
not the kernel calls that checked them:

```text
QuotientCheckedSignatureBoundaryCandidateV1 =
  (normalized_wire:UncheckedSignature,
   normalized_digest:Digest,
   declaration_count:u64)

QuotientCheckedContextBoundaryCandidateV1 =
  (signature_digest:Digest,
   normalized_context:NormalizedContextV1)

QuotientHistoricalBirthBoundaryCandidateV1 =
  (birth_event_id:GenerativeHistoryEventIdV1,
   birth_event_ordinal:u64,
   normalized_birth_extension:UncheckedSignature,
   predecessor:QuotientCheckedSignatureBoundaryCandidateV1,
   full_successor:QuotientCheckedSignatureBoundaryCandidateV1,
   exact_stage_evidence_digest:Digest)

QuotientCurrentSourceHistoryCandidateV1 =
  (source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
   birth_boundary:QuotientHistoricalBirthBoundaryCandidateV1,
   strict_prefix:QuotientCheckedSignatureBoundaryCandidateV1,
   local_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   strict_prefix_context:QuotientCheckedContextBoundaryCandidateV1,
   opaque_sigma_context:QuotientCheckedContextBoundaryCandidateV1)

QuotientOlderSourceHistoryCandidateV1 =
  (source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
   export:OlderPublicExportV1,
   birth_boundary:QuotientHistoricalBirthBoundaryCandidateV1,
   strict_prefix:QuotientCheckedSignatureBoundaryCandidateV1)

QuotientSourceHistoryCandidateV1 ::=
  Current=0(QuotientCurrentSourceHistoryCandidateV1)
| OlderPublic=1(QuotientOlderSourceHistoryCandidateV1)

QuotientReplayBoundariesAndContextsCandidateV1 =
  (target_birth_boundary:QuotientHistoricalBirthBoundaryCandidateV1,
   opaque_signature:BirthOpaqueSignatureBindingV1,
   selected_source_replays:vector<QuotientSourceHistoryCandidateV1>).
```

The elementary projections are:

```text
QSig(s) =
  (s.normalized_wire,s.normalized_digest,s.declaration_count)

QCtx(c) =
  (c.signature_digest,
   NormalizedContextV1(c.normalized_context))

QBirth(b) =
  (b.birth_event_id,
   b.birth_event_ordinal,
   b.normalized_birth_extension,
   QSig(b.predecessor),
   QSig(b.full_successor),
   b.exact_stage_evidence_digest)

QCurrent(x) =
  (QSourceLeaf(x.source),
   x.occurrence,
   QBirth(x.birth_boundary),
   QSig(x.strict_prefix),
   NormalizedContextV1(x.local_context),
   QCtx(x.strict_prefix_context),
   QCtx(x.opaque_sigma_context))

QOlderPublic(x) =
  (QSourceLeaf(x.source),
   x.export,
   QBirth(x.birth_boundary),
   QSig(x.strict_prefix))

QSourceHistory(Current(x))     = Current(QCurrent(x))
QSourceHistory(OlderPublic(x)) = OlderPublic(QOlderPublic(x)).
```

The `NormalizedContextV1` constructor in `QCurrent` is the semantic-newtype
rewrap of the exact `DependentContext` bytes; it is not a second normalization.
These projections erase `call_role`, `raw_wire`, `raw_context`, every receipt,
and both older-public kernel replays. The normalized older public type and
global value remain in the full `OlderPublicExportV1`; no checked judgment is
needed as a second copy.

Before projection, each source replay must pass these exact identity bindings:

```text
Current x:
  QSourceLeaf(x.source) = SourceRefV1::Current(x.occurrence)
  x.birth_boundary.birth_event_id = x.occurrence.birth_event_id
  x.birth_boundary.birth_event_ordinal = x.occurrence.birth_event_ordinal
  x.birth_boundary.exact_stage_evidence_digest =
    x.occurrence.exact_stage_evidence_digest
  x.strict_prefix.normalized_digest =
    x.occurrence.birth_prefix_signature_digest
  LocalContextDigest(x.local_context) =
    x.occurrence.local_binder_context_digest
  x.strict_prefix_context.signature_digest =
    x.strict_prefix.normalized_digest
  x.strict_prefix_context.normalized_context = x.local_context
  x.opaque_sigma_context.normalized_context = x.local_context

OlderPublic x:
  QSourceLeaf(x.source) =
    SourceRefV1::OlderPublic(
      x.export.export_id,x.export.public_field_path)
  x.birth_boundary.birth_event_id = x.export.export_id.birth_event_id
  x.birth_boundary.birth_event_ordinal =
    x.export.export_id.birth_event_ordinal
  x.birth_boundary.exact_stage_evidence_digest =
    x.export.export_id.exact_stage_evidence_digest
  x.strict_prefix.normalized_digest =
    x.export.export_id.birth_prefix_signature_digest
  x.birth_boundary.full_successor.normalized_digest =
    x.export.export_birth_successor_digest.
```

`LocalContextDigest` is exactly the frozen R1 digest over the complete
oldest-first normalized local context under domain
`law-v2/jg2b2b2a/ordinary-local-binder-context/v1`. Every displayed equality is
full canonical-byte equality; a mismatch aborts projection.

Let `Sigma=N.owned_history.evaluation_binding.full_identity`. The selected
source vector is rebuilt by filtering `Sigma.source_refs`, in its canonical
census order, for exactly:

```text
Current(q) where
  q is in N.implementation or N.prior_and_current.current_output

OlderPublic(export_id,path) where
  (export_id,path) is in N.prior_and_current.prior_support.
```

The original `N.replay_boundaries.selected_source_replays` must have the same
length, tags, and full source identities in that order; applying `QCurrent` or
`QOlderPublic` elementwise yields the quotient vector. No copied
`SourceLeafV1.source_ordinal`, raw traversal position, or footprint sort order
may choose it.

Put `L=N.replay_boundaries.selected_source_replays` and let `SelectedRefs` be
the census filter just displayed. The complete group-3 projection is:

```text
map(source_identity,L) = SelectedRefs

Q3.target_birth_boundary =
  QBirth(N.replay_boundaries.target_birth_boundary)
Q3.opaque_signature = N.replay_boundaries.opaque_signature
Q3.selected_source_replays = map(QSourceHistory,L).
```

Here `source_identity(Current(x))=QSourceLeaf(x.source)` and likewise for
`OlderPublic`. The outer source-history tag, inner `SourceRefV1` tag, and census
tag must agree. In addition:

```text
Q3.opaque_signature = Sigma.opaque_signature
Q3.target_birth_boundary.birth_event_id =
  Q3.opaque_signature.target_birth_event_id
Q3.target_birth_boundary.birth_event_ordinal =
  Q3.opaque_signature.target_birth_event_ordinal
Q3.target_birth_boundary.full_successor.normalized_digest =
  Q3.opaque_signature.exact_full_successor_digest

for every Current(q) entry:
  q.opaque_sigma_context.signature_digest =
    Q3.opaque_signature.opaque_verified_signature_digest.
```

The `opaque_signature` component of the group-1 census and the group-3 copy of
that binding are deliberately role-distinct repeated values and must be
byte-identical. There is no optional, missing, or extra replay row.

The transitive digest whitelist for groups 1--5 is now closed by type and path.
Only the following digest-bearing values survive:

1. digest leaves inside the frozen exact identities retained by group 1,
   source identities, lineage, and footprints: `ProfileManifestIdV1`,
   `EnvelopeParentIdentityV1`, `CompleteHistoryIdentityV1`,
   `BirthSourceCensusV1`, `BirthOpaqueSignatureBindingV1`,
   `GenerativeHistoryEventIdV1`, `OccurrenceId14V1`,
   `NormalizedOccurrenceKeyV1`, `ExportIdV1`, `OlderPublicExportV1`, and
   their exact `GlobalId` leaves;
2. `QSig.normalized_digest`, which is the digest paired by the frozen checked
   signature replay with that exact retained `normalized_wire` and declaration
   count;
3. `QCtx.signature_digest`, which is byte-identical to the exact checked
   signature boundary under which the retained normalized context was replayed;
4. `QBirth.exact_stage_evidence_digest`, which is the R1 exact-authority stage
   manifest digest and must satisfy the current/older identity equations above;
   and
5. the exact R1 digest fields inside the byte-identical opaque-signature
   binding; `GlobalId` leaves at their frozen typed positions inside retained
   `Term`, `DependentContext`, `UncheckedSignature`, and normalized compound
   values; and `GlobalId` under
   `RawDependencyEdgeV1::NormalizationStep::PermittedGlobal`.

Every value in item 1 or 5 is accepted only through its frozen full-value
validation and private history reconstruction. Items 2--4 are rechecked
against the exact R2c replay boundary before erasure of its receipt. None may
decide quotient equality without comparison of the complete decoded key.
`EvidenceCommitmentV1`, digests inside R3 receipts, kernel replays, resource
accounts, normalization/presentation transcripts, exact-evidence transcripts,
or action traces have no admitted path. A digest leaf outside this whitelist is
an invariant abort, not an extension point.

### 9.4 QG1c candidate lineage and footprints

Output lineage and the three footprints are already coordinate-free. The
remaining group-4/group-5 wrappers are:

```text
QuotientOutputLineageEntryCandidateV1 = OutputLineageEntryV1

QuotientCandidateLineageBaseCandidateV1 =
  (principal:QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1,
   fields:vector<QuotientFieldLineageCandidateV1>,
   output:vector<QuotientOutputLineageEntryCandidateV1>)

QuotientFootprintsCandidateV1 =
  (implementation:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   current_output:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   prior_support:ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1).
```

The coordinate erasure functions are exact tuples:

```text
QPrincipal(p) =
  (p.origin_path,
   p.raw_field_path,
   QSourceLeaf(p.principal_leaf),
   p.pre_expansion_occurrence,
   p.exact_anchor)

QFieldLineageEntry(e) =
  (e.raw_leaf_path,
   QSourceLeaf(e.source),
   e.derivation_edge)

QFieldLineage(f) =
  (f.field_ordinal,
   f.field_path,
   map(QFieldLineageEntry,f.entries)).
```

For the same normalized endpoint `N`:

```text
QLineage.principal =
  QPrincipal(
    N.subject_root_projection.source_bundle.principal_lineage)

QLineage.fields = map(QFieldLineage,N.field_lineages)
QLineage.output = N.output_lineage

QFootprints =
  (N.implementation,
   N.prior_and_current.current_output,
   N.prior_and_current.prior_support).
```

The lineage/public-field concordance is:

```text
QLineage.fields.len = N.normalized_public_fields.len

for every checked u32 i in that length:
  QLineage.fields[i].field_ordinal = checked_u32(i)
  QLineage.fields[i].field_ordinal =
    N.normalized_public_fields[i].field_ordinal
  QLineage.fields[i].field_path =
    N.normalized_public_fields[i].path

QLineage.principal.principal_source =
  SourceRefV1::Current(QLineage.principal.exact_anchor).
```

Every entry vector retains its complete R2c order and cardinality. A lineage
source is checked against the exact same `Sigma.source_refs` identity before
its source ordinal is erased. `QPrincipal` and `QFieldLineage` erase only
`SourceLeafV1.source_ordinal`. They retain exact `OriginPathV1`,
`RawFieldPathV1`, `SourceRefV1`, occurrence identity, field/output ordinal,
`EnvelopeFieldPathV1`, and `RawDependencyEdgeV1` values in their original
orders. The quotient output-lineage alias and all three footprint types copy
their source values byte-for-byte. Action-introduced lineage does not occur in
a candidate and remains QG3 work.

## 10. Quotient lift of the match premise

R2c's exact `CandidateMatchPremiseV1(profile,H,Sigma,o,c,raw_ordinal,raw_code)`
denotes the witness-indexed proposition `Eval(H,o,r)=Match(E)`. Removing its
last two fields does not preserve that proposition. The quotient therefore
requires a separately named proposition descriptor:

```text
QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1 =             // candidate schema
  (profile_identity:QuotientProfileIdentityV1,
   history_identity:QuotientHistoryIdentityV1,
   evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   anchor:OccurrenceId14V1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1).
```

Relative to an enclosing semantic candidate descriptor `D`, it is intended to
denote:

Let `R` again denote the fixed pair's `RawEnumerationV1.codes`. Then:

```text
there exist j,r,E such that
  R[j] = r,
  Eval^kappa_c(H,o,r) = Match(E), and
  PreSubjectSemanticProjection(E) = D.
```

`PreSubjectSemanticProjection` must exclude both physically encoded copies of
the premise: `SelectorInputs.match_premise` and
`SourceCandidate.match_premise`. Only after the enclosing descriptor `D` is
fixed may the quotient compiler populate those two structural positions with
byte-identical copies of the displayed admission descriptor. This prevents a
recursive definition. It is a quotient lift, not mutation or reinterpretation
of R2c's exact `SourceCandidate` descriptor. The exact R2c subject and raw
witness remain in representative evidence outside `0xfd`.

The closed R2c compiler names `SelectorInputs.match_premise` as the whole
atomic `MatchE` premise. Before QG2 closes, the complete original-to-quotient
path table must independently prove that no emitted subject path descends into
the premise's raw ordinal or raw code. A path selecting any erased field has no
quotient image and causes invariant abort; generic path syntax cannot invent a
replacement.

### 10.1 QG2 entry audit: obligation survival and typed subject routes

The QG2 entry audit rejects three tempting aliases. The quotient cannot reuse
`DerivedObligationsV1`, `SubjectProjectionSourceBundleV1`/
`SubjectProjectionSourcePathV1`, or `SubjectActionConstantsV1`/
`FormalActionSyntaxV1`. Their transitive graphs admit checked representatives,
owner-local references, raw proposals, raw codes, replay evidence, or source
ordinals even when a particular runtime value happens not to select those
variants. QG2 therefore recompiles semantic obligations and theorem programs;
it does not delete fields from those R2c values.

The obligation container must retain all eight R2c group tags at positions
`0..7`, including an explicit empty `entries` vector if a completed row audit
leaves a group empty. It may not renumber surviving groups. For a future
`QObligations`:

```text
QObligations.constructor = Q1.constructor
QObligations.groups.len = 8
QObligations.groups[i].group = ObligationGroupTagV1(i)
```

Every surviving entry is recompiled in group order. Its group-local and global
ordinals are contiguous in the quotient sequence, and its support path and
symbolic target type are rebuilt against quotient schemas. None of those
coordinates is copied from R2c after a row is filtered. Repeated semantic source
roles stay repeated; no source vector is sorted or deduplicated.

The normative group-level disposition frontier is:

| Group | QG2 entry disposition |
| ---: | --- |
| 0 `ParentReplay` | semantic mirror of all eleven binding rows, retargeted to Q1 versions and exact owner-free identities |
| 1 `RawMembershipAndDecode` | **mixed/closed**: raw membership, length/presentation checks, source-ordinal traversal, and the original paired raw/normalized replay rows have no image; exactly the invariant source-resolution calls receive fresh semantic mirrors |
| 2 `CarrierFormationAndCompatibility` | semantic mirror over Q2 normalized realizations in the frozen carrier preorder |
| 3 `ParticularSubstitutionsAndLifts` | closed receipt-free normalized substitution/composition/iterated-lift mirror in the exact per-constructor role order |
| 4 `IndependentRawOutputCheck` | **mixed/closed**: the original paired replay rows and raw sides have no image; exactly the invariant normalized telescope comparisons and implementation/output agreements receive fresh semantic mirrors |
| 5 `NormalizationSupportAndDisposition` | semantic mirror of the five construction requirements and three quotient preservation requirements |
| 6 `PrincipalCurrentOrigin` | semantic mirror whose `OriginSingleton` payload retains its exact origin path, allowed `RawFieldPathV1`, and occurrence anchor |
| 7 `TheoremEndpointConstruction` | semantic mirror with one well-formedness row per exact quotient subject slot |

No group record is omitted. Representative validation rows disappear because
the admission premise existentially supplies a valid R2c representative; their
absence is not a negative obligation or a claim that the representative check
never ran. Group 1 may have an empty entry vector, and group 3 is empty for
constructor tags `0`, `1`, `2`, and `4`; every other group is nonempty under the
frozen ordinary schemas.

The group-5 preservation requirement must have the semantic shape

```text
(kind:SupportProjectionSubjectKindV1,
 evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
 subject_endpoint_paths:vector<quotient subject-source path>,
 construction_rule:DefinitionRulePathV1).
```

It erases `raw_code` and the evaluation owner index. It must not embed a third
copy of `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1`: section 10 permits
exactly the two physical copies in selector inputs and the source-candidate
descriptor. The requirement is interpreted relative to that enclosing
admission. A future typed path to one canonical premise position is permitted;
another copied premise is not.

The quotient subject-source bundle keeps the ten R2c semantic roles and tags in
the same order, but every payload type changes to its quotient mirror:

| Tag | Quotient role value |
| ---: | --- |
| 0 `EvaluationBinding` | `Q1.evaluation_identity` |
| 1 `SchemaPayload` | `Q2.canonical_payload` |
| 2 `PublicFields` | `Q2.normalized_public_fields` |
| 3 `PrincipalLineage` | `QLineage.principal` |
| 4 `FieldLineages` | `QLineage.fields` |
| 5 `Footprints` | `QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1` |
| 6 `Obligations` | the completed quotient obligation value |
| 7 `SelectorInputs` | `QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1` |
| 8 `ActionConstants` | `QuotientSubjectActionConstantsCandidateV1` |
| 9 `SourceCandidate` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` |

The selector and source-candidate admission fields are byte-identical and are
populated only by section 10's puncture-then-lift construction. The selector's
current domain is `Q1.evaluation_identity.current_sources`; its origin and
anchor agree with `QLineage.principal`. The source-candidate constructor,
payload, principal lineage, and footprints are the byte-identical values in
the enclosing quotient bundle. These equations introduce no third premise
copy and no back-edge from an obligation into the source bundle.

A new quotient subject-source path type is mandatory even though its root tags
are isomorphic to `0..9`. At this R2d compiler-plan cut, translation is the
partial function that is total exactly on the independently compiled symbolic
primary route-occurrence image:

1. independently compile the ordered symbolic R2c route occurrences from the
   frozen `CompileSubjectRootProjection_c`/`CompileSubjectSyntax_c` templates,
   without consuming a representative bundle or numerical `TypeIdV1`;
2. validate each occurrence's root, steps, multiplicity, order, and symbolic
   endpoint against the exact R2c source schema;
3. map the reached named semantic role;
4. re-encode a path under the quotient source schema and independently derive
   its exact symbolic result schema; numerical `TypeIdV1` lowering remains
   deferred to R2e; and
5. abort if the occurrence is off the symbolic compiler image or names a route
   with no quotient image.

Typed steps cannot in general be copied. The generic semantic projection says
that a path to a whole checked wrapper could map to its normalized quotient
value, and that descent through the wrapper's unique normalization field would
drop that wrapper step; every other checked-wrapper child has no image. The
group-2 wrapper table in section 9.2 fixes those semantic relations. However,
none of the frozen primary route occurrences traverses or terminates at a
checked wrapper. Item 3 therefore admits no wrapper route: every such occurrence
is off-image and aborts. A future non-primary census may use a wrapper relation
only after naming and auditing that route separately. Likewise, envelope
`Indices`, `Inputs`, `Output`, and `RawRealization` relations do not create
primary subject-source routes. An R2c `RawField` value projection has no image;
an allowed `RawFieldPathV1` retained as lineage provenance is a different typed
value.

The primary frozen theorem compiler emits direct source paths only to the
whole source candidate, the whole `SelectorInputs.match_premise` atom,
selector current domain and anchor, the two identity action constants, and
the exact schema-payload routes needed by iterated lifts or reindexed derived
components. This finite image, not the generic ten-root path grammar, is the
translation domain. The whole match premise maps to the quotient admission
atom. Its five semantic field equations validate construction of that atom but
do not license five additional primary-compiler path images: every descendant
route is off the reconstructed compiler image and aborts. A future non-primary
path census may admit a field route only by naming and auditing it separately;
generic descent, owner-local indices, `raw_ordinal`, and `raw_code` have no
image.

`SubjectActionConstantsV1` is not reusable. Its `FormalActionSyntaxV1` type
transitively admits raw substitution/context-square proposals. QG2 needs a
representative-free normalized action view whose substitution primitive is
only the canonical normalized `(delta,gamma,images)` and whose square primitive
is only the three normalized substitutions `(rho,sigma,theta_prime)`. The two
identity constants are exactly `(SubstitutionAction,[])` and
`(ContextSquareAction,[])`. Raw proposals, authority/evidence digests,
judgments, and replays have no image. Formal-action projection paths must be
recompiled over this view and the already fixed virtual derived components.

The quotient theorem family retains the exact subject-slot census:

```text
Formation, Abstraction, Aggregation, Comparison, DemandCompiler:
  slots 0..7 = Identity, Composition,
               SelectorExistenceAndAgreement, SelectorNormalization,
               SelectorPresentation, SelectorRenaming,
               SelectorAction, Naturality

Transport, DischargeTransformer:
  the same slots 0..7, then
  8=ExtraCoherenceFirst,
  9=ExtraCoherenceSecond,
  10=ExtraCoherenceThird.
```

Binder order, strict postorder value-node emission, no-common-subexpression
rule, slot-local relocation, proposition suffix order, and final conjunction
remain unchanged; every embedded binder, literal, path, and result type is a
quotient type and is independently rederived. Construction transcripts, slot
spans, node-check receipts, and resource accounts have no group-8 image.
`SupportProjection` binders and `PureSupportFunctionProjection` literals are
reserved for QG3 preservation subjects and are excluded from the primary QG2
slot grammar, preventing a QG2/QG3 cycle.

The absolute no-image set for QG2 is: owner-local indices; source ordinals;
candidate raw ordinal/code; all nested raw codes and raw contexts, raw
`source_term`, and raw/intermediate `reindexed_term` presentations; raw action
proposals; raw membership/length/traversal rows; receipts, replays, R3/evidence
resource and cardinality records, evidence commitments, and
presentation-only digests; support slot/raw/work/owner-map ordinals;
construction transcripts and spans; copied R2c TypeIds; and any generic path
not produced by the reconstructed compiler. Encountering one aborts projection.

The ordered QG2 subfrontier is therefore:

1. close the receipt-free normalized substitution/lift, telescope-comparison,
   normalized output-agreement, and normalized-action views, together with the
   exact group-1/group-4 row disposition tables;
2. close the quotient obligation predicate/source grammar and all eight group
   compilers with rebuilt ordinals, support paths, and symbolic target types;
3. close the ten-role quotient source bundle and total-on-compiler-image path
   translator; and
4. recompile and audit the exact 8/11 subject families and the two-copy
   admission invariant.

No QG2 wire type, root-`0xfd` field, or TypeId is frozen by this entry audit.
The first item above is closed in section 10.2. Section 10.3 closes the second
item's core grammar and stable rows, exposes its three missing exact tables, and
makes the group-2 carrier adjudication the active next cut at that intermediate
stage. Sections 10.4--10.5 close that table, section 10.6 closes O2b completely,
section 10.7 closes O2c, section 10.8 closes item 2's final eight-group compiler,
and section 10.9 closes item 3's source bundle and symbolic path translator.
Section 10.10 closes item 4 and completes QG2; QG3 is now active.

### 10.2 QG2 semantic-view cut: normalized operations and exact group-1/group-4 rows

This cut is **closed at the schema-candidate level**. It fixes the receipt-free
normalized substitution, lift, telescope-comparison, output-agreement, and
action views. It also closes the exact row dispositions for groups 1 and 4.
It freezes no quotient obligation container, source-path sum, root-`0xfd`
field, or numerical `TypeIdV1`. The container, path sum, and symbolic target
types belong to QG2 subfrontier item 2; R2e alone assigns numerical TypeIds.

#### 10.2.1 Normalized substitutions, composition, binder lifts, and section pairing

The quotient reuses R2b's already frozen semantic record exactly:

```text
NormalizedSubstitutionV1 =
  (delta:NormalizedContextV1,
   gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
   images:NormalizedSectionV1).
```

It does not mint an isomorphic quotient copy. For a successful particular
substitution replay `v`, the compile-time projection is

```text
QVerifiedSubstitution(v) =
  NormalizedSubstitutionV1(
    NormalizedContextV1(v.canonical_proposal.domain_context),
    NormalizedContextV1(v.canonical_proposal.codomain_context),
    NormalizedSectionV1(v.canonical_proposal.images)).
```

Before returning that value, the compiler reruns the frozen JG2b2b1 equations:

```text
s.images.len = s.gamma.len

Expected(s,i) =
  PrefixSubstitute(s.gamma[i],s.images[0..i])

for every i:
  s.images[i] is the canonical normalized term checked under s.delta
  at Expected(s,i).
```

Image order is oldest codomain declaration first. The authority binding, raw
proposal, signature wire, raw/canonical judgments, image evidence, digests,
and resource behavior of `v` are representative validation inputs and have no
image. An empty codomain still retains and validates both contexts.

The receipt-free particular composition and lift descriptors are:

```text
QuotientNormalizedSubstitutionCompositionCandidateV1 =
  (outer:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   inner:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   result:NormalizedSubstitutionV1)

QuotientNormalizedBinderLiftStepCandidateV1 =
  (source:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   codomain_parameter:Term,
   reindexed_domain_parameter:Term,
   result:NormalizedSubstitutionV1)

QuotientNormalizedIteratedLiftCandidateV1 =
  (base:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   target:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
   lift_depth:u16,
   steps:vector<QuotientNormalizedBinderLiftStepCandidateV1>,
   result:NormalizedSubstitutionV1).
```

For context `Gamma`, with `n=u64(Gamma.len)`, identity is reconstructed as

```text
QId(Gamma).delta = Gamma
QId(Gamma).gamma = Gamma
QId(Gamma).images.len = n
QId(Gamma).images[i] = Var(checked_u32(n-1-i)).
```

For `theta:Delta->Gamma` and `rho:Xi->Delta`, composition has the frozen
orientation `theta[rho]:Xi->Gamma`:

```text
C = QCompose(theta,rho):
  require theta.delta = rho.gamma
  C.outer = theta
  C.inner = rho
  C.result.delta = rho.delta
  C.result.gamma = theta.gamma
  C.result.images.len = theta.images.len
  C.result.images[i] = NF(CaptureSafeReindex(theta.images[i],rho)).
```

The complete result is revalidated by the substitution equations above. For
one binder lift of `theta` along parameter `A`:

```text
A_theta = NF(CaptureSafeReindex(A,theta))

step.source = theta
step.codomain_parameter = A
step.reindexed_domain_parameter = A_theta
step.result.delta = append_type(Delta,A_theta)
step.result.gamma = append_type(Gamma,A)
step.result.images =
  map(lambda t. CaptureSafeShiftUp(t,1),theta.images) ++ [Var(0)].
```

The lifted result is independently revalidated. Iteration along telescope `T`
is exact:

```text
L = QIteratedLift(base,T)
L.base = base
L.target = T
L.lift_depth = checked_u16(T.len)
L.steps.len = T.len
L.steps[i].source = base                               when i=0
L.steps[i].source = L.steps[i-1].result                when 0<i<T.len
L.steps[i].codomain_parameter = T[i]
each L.steps[i] satisfies the one-lift equations
L.result = base                                        when T.len=0
L.result = L.steps[T.len-1].result                     otherwise.
```

O2b's semantic-operation audit exposed one missing constructor in the original
cut. A normalized section is not itself a substitution into an extended
context. Demand specialization and discharge-contract instantiation require
the canonical context projection and the pairing of a base substitution with a
complete section. Those operations are now frozen here rather than inferred
from a representative replay vector.

For `base:Delta->Gamma` and a normalized telescope `T` over `Gamma`, define the
capture-safe telescope action only from the iterated-lift descriptor:

```text
L = QIteratedLift(base,T)

ReindexTelescope(base,T).len = T.len
ReindexTelescope(base,T)[i] = L.steps[i].reindexed_domain_parameter.
```

The empty telescope gives the empty reindexed telescope. For normalized context
`Gamma` and telescope `T` over it, put `n=u64(Gamma.len)`, `m=u64(T.len)`, and
`GammaT=AppendTelescope(Gamma,T)`. The canonical projection is:

```text
QProject(Gamma,T).delta = GammaT
QProject(Gamma,T).gamma = Gamma
QProject(Gamma,T).images.len = n

for 0 <= i < n:
  QProject(Gamma,T).images[i] =
    Var(checked_u32(checked_sub_u64(
          checked_sub_u64(checked_add_u64(n,m),1),i))).
```

No subtraction is evaluated when `n=0`. Thus projection from an empty base has
zero images, and projection across an empty telescope is byte-identical to
`QId(Gamma)`. The complete result is revalidated by the substitution equations
above; neither equality is assumed from the display.

For `base:Delta->Gamma`, telescope `T` over `Gamma`, and normalized section
term vector `s` over `Delta`, canonical pairing first performs this direct
combined-prefix check:

```text
SectionTerms(NormalizedSectionV1(terms)) = terms
require SectionTerms(s).len = T.len

PairExpected(base,T,s,j) =
  PrefixSubstitute(
    T[j],SectionTerms(base.images) ++ SectionTerms(s)[0..j])

for 0 <= j < T.len:
  SectionTerms(s)[j] is the canonical normalized term checked under
  base.delta at PairExpected(base,T,s,j)

QPair(base,T,s).delta = base.delta
QPair(base,T,s).gamma = AppendTelescope(base.gamma,T)
QPair(base,T,s).images = NormalizedSectionV1(
  SectionTerms(base.images) ++ SectionTerms(s)).
```

This check is in oldest-target-field order, so each appended image is typed
after the complete base image vector and all earlier section images. Merely
checking `s` against a separately computed `ReindexTelescope(base,T)` is not a
substitute: identifying those two expected-type calculations would consume the
generic substitution-composition theorem that this cut does not prove. The
complete result is independently revalidated as one
`NormalizedSubstitutionV1`. Empty `T` requires empty `s` and returns a
byte-identical copy of `base`.

The two derived constructors used by the build-semantic compiler are exactly:

```text
QSectionSubstitution(Gamma,T,s) = QPair(QId(Gamma),T,s)

QActivationSubstitution(Gamma,V,S,a) =
  let GammaE = AppendTelescope(Gamma,V.exports);
  in QSectionSubstitution(GammaE,S.trigger,a).
```

For scheme port `j` with operation anchor `k_j`, parameter telescope
`P_(k_j)`, trigger `X`, earlier derived outputs `Q_0,...,Q_(j-1)`, and normalized
argument section `args_j`, define the dependent left fold

```text
W_0 = X
W_(j+1) = ConcatTelescope(W_j,Q_j)
```

and let

```text
GammaE = AppendTelescope(Gamma,V.exports)
pi_j = QProject(GammaE,W_j)

QPortSubstitution(Gamma,V,S,j) =
  QPair(pi_j,P_(k_j),args_j).
```

This constructor requires `args_j` to pass `QPair`'s direct combined-prefix
check; its separately retained checked-section target is also revalidated by
full bytes and kernel checking, not by an assumed composition law. It then
rechecks that reindexing the selected `R_(k_j)` by the result yields the
retained port output. For a discharge body
`d` of live demand `L`, the contract-instantiation substitution is exactly:

```text
GammaE = AppendTelescope(Gamma,V.exports)
QDischargeSubstitution(Gamma,V,L,d) =
  QSectionSubstitution(
    GammaE,L.demanded_telescope,d.section).
```

`CheckDischarge` constructs one such substitution unconditionally and reuses it
as the operand for every contract of that discharge. Contract count and
termwise uses do not manufacture duplicate operation rows; section 10.6.3
freezes the identity/pairing occurrence and its exact roles.

`QProject`, `QPair`, and their displayed specializations construct only
particular normalized substitutions. They add no new operation-value tag and
assert no generic projection, eta, beta, functoriality, or substitution law.
They are deterministic de Bruijn constructions whose concrete results must
pass the already frozen JG2b2b1 equations.

These descriptors establish only the particular normalized operations that
the frozen representative replay already validated. They do not establish a
generic identity, associativity, congruence, lift-totality, or substitution
naturality theorem.

#### 10.2.2 Representative-free normalized action view

The normalized action type is:

```text
QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitiveCandidateV1 =
  (rho:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   sigma:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
   theta_prime:NormalizedSubstitutionV1)

QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1 ::=
  SubstitutionAction=0(
    primitives:vector<NormalizedSubstitutionV1>)
| ContextSquareAction=1(
    primitives:
      vector<QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitiveCandidateV1>).
```

This sum makes mixed primitive kinds unrepresentable. Projection from a frozen
`CheckedFormalActionSyntaxV1` preserves the exact left-to-right normalized
primitive vector and applies `QVerifiedSubstitution` to every particular
substitution. It erases the evaluation owner, raw postfix program, identity and
compose tokens, replay evidence, judgments, and digests.

The two identities are exactly

```text
QSubstitutionIdentity = SubstitutionAction([])
QContextSquareIdentity = ContextSquareAction([]).
```

Composition is defined only for equal sum tags and is ordered vector
concatenation. It retains no association tree and performs no sort or
deduplication.

A square primitive is valid only relative to the current base substitution
`theta:Delta->Gamma`:

```text
rho.gamma = theta.delta
sigma.gamma = theta.gamma
theta_prime.delta = rho.delta
theta_prime.gamma = sigma.delta

QCompose(theta,rho).result =
  QCompose(sigma,theta_prime).result
```

where the last equality is complete canonical bytes after the two normalized
compositions. This is the representative-free endpoint of the internally
derived pointwise `epsilon`; neither `epsilon`, its judgment, nor its replay is
encoded. The next square uses `theta_prime` as its current base. Empty-square
vertical identities and every pasted endpoint are reconstructed base-
relatively and are not fields of the empty action value.

The virtual constructor-specific components remain outside the primitive
vector: `theta^A` for Abstraction and Aggregation, `theta^E` for
DemandCompiler, and the composite-square, congruence, and reindexed
`G/S/a/L` components for Transport and DischargeTransformer. Formation and
Comparison require no lift. These are recompiled from the views above when the
subject compiler closes.

#### 10.2.3 Group 1: only invariant source-resolution mirrors survive

The source-resolution semantic view is:

```text
QSourceResolutionRequestCandidateV1 ::=
  LocalContextPrefix=0(
    path:RawFieldPathV1,
    source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
    prefix_length:u16)
| TypeView=1(
    path:RawFieldPathV1,
    source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
    target_context:NormalizedContextV1)
| TermView=2(
    path:RawFieldPathV1,
    source:QuotientSourceIdentityV1,
    target_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    expected_type:Term)

QSourceResolutionSuccessCandidateV1 ::=
  LocalContextPrefix=0(value:QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1)
| TypeView=1(value:QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1)
| TermView=2(value:QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1)

QSourceResolutionEntryCandidateV1 =
  (request:QSourceResolutionRequestCandidateV1,
   success:QSourceResolutionSuccessCandidateV1)

QDecodeSourceResolutionViewCandidateV1 =
  (calls:vector<QSourceResolutionEntryCandidateV1>).
```

For each frozen successful call, `QResolve` applies `QSourceLeaf` to the request
source and `QContext`, `QType`, or `QTerm` to the same-tag success. It does not
store `call_ordinal`. Local-prefix length equals the checked `u16` conversion
of the projected context length. Type and term variants recheck every repeated
path, source, target context, and expected type by full bytes. The group-1
census equation of section 9 is rerun before any source ordinal is erased.

The vector is independently compiled from the quotient checked-mirror DAG in
frozen source-call order. The mandatory two-representative equation is

```text
QResolveCalls(E) = QResolveCalls(E')
```

by ordered full bytes whenever the complete retained quotient checked-mirror
coordinates of successful representatives `E` and `E'` agree. A representative
projection must equal that independent compilation. Failure gives that call
an invariant mismatch and aborts the complete quotient projection; the
compiler may not select one representative's vector or delete one call.

The exact row disposition is:

| Frozen R2c group-1 segment | Quotient disposition |
| --- | --- |
| raw-code membership/equality | no row: raw syntax and membership are supplied only by the admission witness |
| every `LengthCheckV1` | no row: path/observed/bound presentation checks have no quotient image |
| every `SourceUse` leaf | no row: source ordinals, bit vectors, and traversal coordinates have no quotient image |
| every source-resolution call | exactly one freshly compiled `QSourceResolutionEntryCandidateV1` row |
| every decode kernel raw/normalized pair | no row: erasing the raw operand destroys the frozen binary `KernelJudgment` predicate |

In particular, retaining the normalized `OpenJudgment` half would invent a
unary relation that neither R2c's relation sum nor A3-C1's expected-judgment
sum defines. It may be rechecked while constructing `QContext`, `QType`, and
`QTerm`; it is not an obligation row.

For `C1=QDecodeSourceResolutionView.calls.len`, group 1 has exactly `C1` rows:

```text
group_local_ordinal(k) = checked_u32(k)
obligation_ordinal(k) = checked_u32(11+k)
next_group_prefix = checked_u32(11+C1)
rule_path = DefinitionRulePathV1(Decode,[VectorElement(1)]).
```

The future quotient predicate sources are request first and the same-tag
success payload second:

```text
QDecodeSourceResolutionView/calls[k]/request

LocalContextPrefix:
  QDecodeSourceResolutionView/calls[k]/success/
    SumPayload(0)/RecordField(0)
    -> QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1
TypeView:
  QDecodeSourceResolutionView/calls[k]/success/
    SumPayload(1)/RecordField(0)
    -> QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1
TermView:
  QDecodeSourceResolutionView/calls[k]/success/
    SumPayload(2)/RecordField(0)
    -> QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1.
```

Their typed paths and symbolic result types are rebuilt against the quotient
request sum and exact same-tag success payload type. The expected judgment is

```text
LocalContextPrefix ->
  StructuralRecord(tau(QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1))
TypeView           -> TypeFormation
TermView           -> HasType(success.value.expected_type).
```

The support path is rebuilt under
`QObligations/groups[1]/entries[k]/predicate`; no R2c ordinal, path, or TypeId
is copied. Group tag 1 remains present with an empty vector if `C1=0`.

#### 10.2.4 Receipt-free telescope comparison

The R2b comparison type cannot be reused because every field contains two
kernel replays. Its semantic projection is:

```text
QuotientNormalizedTelescopeFieldComparisonCandidateV1 =
  (field_ordinal:u32,
   left_prefix:NormalizedContextV1,
   right_prefix:NormalizedContextV1,
   left_normalized_type:Term,
   right_normalized_type:Term)

QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1 =
  (base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
   left:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
   right:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
   left_length:u64,
   right_length:u64,
   fields:
     vector<QuotientNormalizedTelescopeFieldComparisonCandidateV1>).
```

`QTelCmp(base,left,right)` independently rebuilds:

```text
left_length = u64(left.len)
right_length = u64(right.len)
left_length = right_length = u64(fields.len)
fields[i].field_ordinal = checked_u32(i)

LPrefix(0) = base
RPrefix(0) = base
LPrefix(i+1) = append_type(LPrefix(i),left[i])
RPrefix(i+1) = append_type(RPrefix(i),right[i])

fields[i].left_prefix = LPrefix(i)
fields[i].right_prefix = RPrefix(i)
fields[i].left_normalized_type = left[i]
fields[i].right_normalized_type = right[i]

LPrefix(i) = RPrefix(i) by full canonical bytes
left[i] = right[i] by full canonical bytes.
```

There is no fabricated universe judgment, eta rule, proof-irrelevance rule,
digest comparison, or retained formation replay. Group 4 compiles one such row
for Aggregation, Transport, and Comparison, and none for the other four tags:

```text
Aggregation: QTelCmp(Q.gamma,O.Aggregation,Q.implementation_c)
Transport:   QTelCmp(Q.delta,O.Transport,Q.implementation_c)
Comparison:  QTelCmp(Q.gamma,Q.a,Q.b).
```

#### 10.2.5 Normalized implementation/output agreement

Telescope and section newtypes are not interchangeable even though their inner
wires are term vectors. The typed view is:

```text
QuotientNormalizedAgreementIndexCandidateV1 ::=
  Telescope=0(base_context:NormalizedContextV1)
| Section=1(base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
            target:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Discharge=2(index:CarrierIndexValueV1)

QuotientNormalizedAgreementValueCandidateV1 ::=
  Telescope=0(NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| Section=1(NormalizedSectionV1)
| Discharge=2(NormalizedDischargeValueV1)

QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  FormationOutput=0
| AbstractionResult=1
| AggregationOutput=2
| TransportOutput=3
| ComparisonLeft=4
| ComparisonRight=5
| DemandActivation=6
| DischargeOutput=7

QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementRoleCandidateV1,
   index:QuotientNormalizedAgreementIndexCandidateV1,
   implementation:QuotientNormalizedAgreementValueCandidateV1,
   specified:QuotientNormalizedAgreementValueCandidateV1).
```

Write `AppendTelescope(Gamma,A)` for oldest-first extension of normalized
context `Gamma` by telescope `A`. For a DischargeTransformer payload, derive

```text
G_prime = ReindexGrammar(Q.theta,Q.grammar)
S_prime = ReindexScheme(Q.theta,Q.scheme)
theta_E = QIteratedLift(Q.theta,Q.grammar.exports).result
a_prime = ReindexSection(theta_E,Q.activation)
L_prime = Specialize(S_prime,a_prime)

QDischargeIndex(Q) =
  CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex(
    gamma=Q.delta,
    grammar=G_prime,
    scheme=S_prime,
    activation=a_prime,
    live=L_prime).
```

Every named operation is the frozen A2-O/A3-O capture-safe normalized
operation and is rebuilt from section 10.2.1; no checked wrapper or replay is
an operand. The exact per-constructor agreement vector is:

```text
Formation:
  [(FormationOutput,
    Telescope(Q.gamma),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_a),
    Telescope(O.Formation))]

Abstraction:
  [(AbstractionResult,
    Telescope(Q.implementation_xi),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_b),
    Telescope(Q.b))]

Aggregation:
  [(AggregationOutput,
    Telescope(Q.gamma),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_c),
    Telescope(O.Aggregation))]

Transport:
  [(TransportOutput,
    Telescope(Q.delta),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_c),
    Telescope(O.Transport))]

Comparison:
  [(ComparisonLeft,
    Telescope(Q.gamma),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_a),
    Telescope(Q.a)),
   (ComparisonRight,
    Telescope(Q.gamma),
    Telescope(Q.implementation_b),
    Telescope(Q.b))]

DemandCompiler:
  [(DemandActivation,
    Section(AppendTelescope(Q.gamma,Q.grammar.exports),Q.scheme.trigger),
    Section(Q.implementation_activation),
    Section(Q.activation))]

DischargeTransformer:
  [(DischargeOutput,
    Discharge(QDischargeIndex(Q)),
    Discharge(Q.implementation_discharge),
    Discharge(O.DischargeTransformer))].
```

For every row the index and both value tags agree, both values validate at the
displayed index, and `implementation=specified` by full canonical bytes. The
Abstraction telescope is based at `Gamma.A`, not `Gamma`; the Demand activation
is a section over `Gamma.E` at `S.X`; and the Discharge index is the complete
reindexed `G'/S'/a'/L'` index, never a placeholder. The eventual obligation
uses one freshly typed source path to this complete agreement-view record.
That record binds the role, exact index, implementation, and specified value
together; the predicate does not separately copy them or replace the index by
an inferred placeholder. Item 2 must add the acyclic semantic-view source root
needed to reach this record.

#### 10.2.6 Exact group-4 disposition and closure

The exact group-4 transformation is:

| Frozen R2c group-4 segment | Quotient disposition |
| --- | --- |
| every build kernel raw/normalized pair | no row: the binary predicate is destroyed when its raw operand is erased |
| build telescope comparisons | one freshly compiled `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1` row for tags 2, 3, and 4 only |
| raw-output agreement components | one freshly compiled typed agreement row for each displayed role, preserving Comparison left then right |

Thus the quotient group-4 counts are exact:

```text
Formation=1
Abstraction=1
Aggregation=2
Transport=2
Comparison=3
DemandCompiler=1
DischargeTransformer=1.
```

Within group 4, a telescope comparison precedes all output-agreement rows.
Every row has `rule_path=Build[0]`. Group-local ordinals are new contiguous
vector positions; the global ordinal is the checked prefix sum of completed
quotient groups 0--3 plus that position. The support path is rebuilt as
