`QObligations/groups[4]/entries[k]/predicate`. Expected judgment is
`StructuralRecord` with symbolic type `tau` of the exact quotient comparison
or agreement-view record, later injectively lowered to its final nonzero
TypeId by R2e. No R2c coordinate survives.

The transitive no-image closure for this cut includes every verified
substitution evidence wrapper; `CheckedSubstitutionV1`;
`NormalizedTelescopeComparisonV1`; `FormalActionSyntaxV1` and every checked or
proposal action type; raw contexts, telescopes, sections, substitutions,
raw `source_term` and raw/intermediate `reindexed_term` presentations, and
weakening images; source/call/owner/candidate ordinals; both `OpenJudgment`
operands reached through a kernel replay row; epsilon, kernel, substitution, lift, and
formation replays; authority/evidence/presentation digests; R3 resources and
cardinality receipts; transcripts and spans; and copied R2c paths or TypeIds.
Semantic `BinderKeyV1` values retained by the QG1 weakening/context/type/term
mirrors remain lawful; only erased raw/procedural binder coordinates have no
image. The `OpenJudgment` retained as
`NormalizedPublicFieldV1.normalized_judgment` inside Q2 also remains lawful;
it is a semantic public-field value, not a surviving half of a replay-pair
obligation.

The supplied exact-depth-two theory constrains the public primitive arity to
unary action and binary comparison. Sealing-generated higher total open-box
extensions, meaning the missing lid and filler together, require no third
primitive. This is not a fixed-lid or arbitrary closed-boundary uniqueness
claim. The theory does not define this wire, substitution orientation, quotient
equality, row survival, or resource behavior and cannot replace the frozen
ordinary R2/JG2b2b1 contracts.

QG2 subfrontier item 1 is closed. Section 10.3 closes item 2's core grammar and
stable rows and audits the originally non-frozen group-2/group-3/group-5
inputs. Sections 10.4--10.5 close O2a; section 10.6 closes O2b's row ABI,
pairing algebra, roles, operation programs, and exact count vector; section
10.7 closes O2c's primary-route census; and section 10.8 uses those exact inputs
to materialize the final eight-group unrooted obligation value.

### 10.3 QG2 obligation grammar and stable-group compiler cut

This cut is **closed at the unrooted schema-candidate level for the core
grammar and stable rows**. At its intermediate audit, it found three omissions
that could not be repaired by projecting representative replay vectors:

1. R2c names group 2's maximal carrier values and compatibility edges but never
   freezes their constructor-specific occurrence/edge tables, source arities,
   or counts;
2. R2c names group 3's decode/build/nested operation concatenation, while R2b
   stores flat verified-substitution vectors with no semantic role that says
   direct substitution, composition, one lift, or iterated lift; and
3. group 5's three preservation descriptors contain the primary theorem
   compiler's quotient subject-path census, whose constructor/slot routes have
   not yet been enumerated.

Groups 2 and 3 therefore may not be copied from R2c, selected from one
representative, or emitted empty. Group 5 retains eight positions, but its last
three payloads are row skeletons until their repeated endpoint-path vector
closes. This section freezes everything that is independent of those three
adjudications and makes the missing decisions explicit.

#### 10.3.1 Symbolic types and the no-`TypeId` barrier

R2d does not assign `TypeIdV1`. The candidate compiler therefore uses this
finite meta-level type sum:

```text
QuotientObligationSymbolicTypeCandidateV1 ::=
  BindingRequirement=0
| SourceResolutionRequest=1
| CheckedContext=2
| CheckedTypeView=3
| CheckedTermView=4
| CarrierFormationRequirement=5
| CarrierCompatibilityRequirement=6
| NormalizedSubstitution=7
| NormalizedSubstitutionComposition=8
| NormalizedIteratedLift=9
| NormalizedTelescopeComparison=10
| NormalizedOutputAgreement=11
| SupportProjectionRequirement=12
| OriginSingleton=13
| SubjectWellFormed=14
| ObligationPredicate=15.
```

The complete exact schema map is:

| Symbolic variant | Exact schema node |
| --- | --- |
| `BindingRequirement` | `QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `SourceResolutionRequest` | `QSourceResolutionRequestCandidateV1` |
| `CheckedContext` | `QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1` |
| `CheckedTypeView` | `QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1` |
| `CheckedTermView` | `QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1` |
| `CarrierFormationRequirement` | `QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `CarrierCompatibilityRequirement` | `QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `NormalizedSubstitution` | `NormalizedSubstitutionV1` |
| `NormalizedSubstitutionComposition` | `QuotientNormalizedSubstitutionCompositionCandidateV1` |
| `NormalizedIteratedLift` | `QuotientNormalizedIteratedLiftCandidateV1` |
| `NormalizedTelescopeComparison` | `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1` |
| `NormalizedOutputAgreement` | `QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1` |
| `SupportProjectionRequirement` | `QuotientSupportProjectionRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `OriginSingleton` | `QuotientOriginSingletonObligationCandidateV1` |
| `SubjectWellFormed` | `QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1` |
| `ObligationPredicate` | `QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1` |

This is a compiler-plan enum, not a registry node, wire `TypeIdV1`, digest, or
free string. Write `tau(T)` for the unique variant whose right-hand schema node
is `T` in this table.
Only after the complete schema DAG closes may R2e define

```text
lower_type(tau(T)) = the exact nonzero TypeIdV1 assigned to T.
```

Lowering is injective over the displayed schema nodes. A missing node, zero ID,
duplicate ID, or disagreement with the final DAG is an invariant abort. No
candidate record in this section pretends that `lower_type` has already run.

The expected-judgment plan needs only the exact variants reachable from
quotient obligations:

```text
QuotientObligationExpectedJudgmentCandidateV1 ::=
  TypeFormation=0
| HasType=1(normalized_type:Term)
| ContextPrefix=3(normalized_context:NormalizedContextV1,
                  prefix_ordinal:u32)
| CarrierSlot=4(carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1,
                field_ordinal:u32)
| StructuralOccurrence=6(occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1)
| StructuralRecord=8(symbolic_type:
                       QuotientObligationSymbolicTypeCandidateV1)
| ConstructionRule=9(rule_path:DefinitionRulePathV1).
```

The displayed tags are the byte-identical A3-C1 tags; they are never compacted.
`CarrierSlot=4` is reserved for later quotient support traversal but is not a
leading expected judgment on the item-2 row compiler image. A completed item-2
row carrying it aborts. Omitting the other A3-C1 variants from this plan sum
neither renumbers nor narrows the eventual `ExpectedJudgmentV1` sum.

#### 10.3.2 Hoisted bare subject-path grammar

Group 5 forces the bare quotient subject-path shape to occur before the
obligation predicate. The source-bundle child schemas needed by its direct
routes are exact:

```text
QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1 =
  (implementation:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   current_output:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   prior_support:ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1,
   output_lineage:vector<QuotientOutputLineageEntryCandidateV1>)

QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1 =
  (match_premise:QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1,
   current_domain:vector<OccurrenceId14V1>,
   origin_path:OriginPathV1,
   anchor:OccurrenceId14V1)

QuotientSubjectActionConstantsCandidateV1 =
  (substitution_identity:QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1,
   context_square_identity:QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1)

QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1 =
  (match_premise:QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1,
   evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
   canonical_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   principal_lineage:QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1,
   footprints:QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1).
```

The subject-footprint projection is the R2c four-field semantic descriptor. It
is rebuilt from `QFootprints.implementation/current_output/prior_support` and
`QLineage.output`; it is not the three-field `QuotientFootprintsCandidateV1`
alone. Selector inputs use `Q1.evaluation_identity.current_sources` and
`QLineage.principal.origin_path/exact_anchor`. The action constants are exactly
the two empty actions from section 10.2.2. The source candidate is populated by
section 10's puncture-then-lift construction and shares the exact admission
premise with selector inputs.

The ten roots, their order, and the exact symbolic path-result sum are:

```text
QuotientSubjectProjectionSourceRootCandidateV1 ::=
  EvaluationBinding=0
| SchemaPayload=1
| PublicFields=2
| PrincipalLineage=3
| FieldLineages=4
| Footprints=5
| Obligations=6
| SelectorInputs=7
| ActionConstants=8
| SourceCandidate=9

QuotientSubjectProjectionSymbolicTypeCandidateV1 ::=
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor=0
| CandidateAdmissionPremise=1
| OccurrenceVector=2
| Occurrence=3
| NormalizedAction=4
| NormalizedTelescope=5
| NormalizedSubstitution=6
| NormalizedGrammar=7
| NormalizedScheme=8
| NormalizedSection=9
| NormalizedLiveDemand=10
| NormalizedContract=11

QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourceRootCandidateV1,
   steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   result_type:QuotientSubjectProjectionSymbolicTypeCandidateV1).
```

The symbolic result variants name exactly, in order,
`QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1`,
`QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1`,
`vector<OccurrenceId14V1>`, `OccurrenceId14V1`,
`QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1`, `NormalizedTelescopeV1`,
`NormalizedSubstitutionV1`, `NormalizedGrammarValueV1`,
`NormalizedSchemeValueV1`, `NormalizedSectionV1`,
`NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1`, and `NormalizedContractValueV1`. Item 3
validates and preserves this symbolic result while proving that the typed walk
reaches it; root and steps remain byte-identical. R2e alone later lowers the
symbolic result to its exact nonzero `TypeIdV1`.

Only the symbolic path-plan grammar is hoisted here. Section 10.7 attaches the
exact named schema result to every route without faking a numerical
`result_type:TypeIdV1`. Item 3 still owns the ten-role source-bundle value, path
decoding, validation that each typed walk reaches its named result schema, and
proof that translation is total exactly on the primary compiler image. Generic syntactic descent does
not make a path valid. In particular, no primary preservation route descends
through `Obligations`, inside an admission premise, or into any erased raw field.

The six already named primary routes are:

```text
WholeSourceCandidate:
  root=SourceCandidate, steps=[], result_type=EnvelopeObjectDescriptor

SelectorMatchPremise:
  root=SelectorInputs, steps=[RecordField(0)],
  result_type=CandidateAdmissionPremise

SelectorCurrentDomain:
  root=SelectorInputs, steps=[RecordField(1)],
  result_type=OccurrenceVector

SelectorAnchor:
  root=SelectorInputs, steps=[RecordField(3)], result_type=Occurrence

SubstitutionIdentity:
  root=ActionConstants, steps=[RecordField(0)], result_type=NormalizedAction

ContextSquareIdentity:
  root=ActionConstants, steps=[RecordField(1)], result_type=NormalizedAction.
```

The remaining primary routes are exact constructor/slot-specific descendants
of `SchemaPayload`. Section 10.7 closes their finite census; the generic path
type cannot invent them.

#### 10.3.3 Acyclic obligation-source frame

The quotient obligation source roots are new and have this order:

```text
QuotientObligationSourceRootCandidateV1 ::=
  BindingRequirements=0
| DecodeResolution=1
| CarrierRequirements=2
| ParticularOperations=3
| BuildSemanticChecks=4

QuotientObligationSourcePathCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientObligationSourceRootCandidateV1,
   steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   result_type:QuotientObligationSymbolicTypeCandidateV1).
```

The roots are interpreted against, in order:

```text
QuotientBindingRequirementViewCandidateV1
QDecodeSourceResolutionViewCandidateV1
QuotientCarrierRequirementViewCandidateV1
QuotientParticularOperationViewCandidateV1
QuotientBuildSemanticCheckViewCandidateV1.
```

Every value is reconstructed from Q1, Q2, or a section-10.2 semantic view
before `QObligations`. No root reaches `QObligations`, the future ten-role
source bundle, a theorem subject, QG3 support/action data, an R3 type, or
representative evidence. Root tags 2 and 3 are valid only through the exact
closed routes in sections 10.4--10.6; generic descent or any other route still
aborts.

The binding root has one homogeneous record type rather than an ambiguous
heterogeneous `ExactBytes` vector:

```text
QuotientBindingRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  Versions=0
| ProfileIdentity=1
| ParentJg1=2
| ParentJg2a=3
| ParentJg2b2a=4
| ParentJg2b2b0=5
| ParentJg2b2b1=6
| ParentProfile=7
| HistoryIdentity=8
| EvaluationIdentity=9
| Constructor=10

QuotientBindingRequirementValueCandidateV1 ::=
  Versions=0(EnvelopeVersionBindingsV1)
| ProfileIdentity=1(QuotientProfileIdentityV1)
| ParentIdentity=2(value:EnvelopeParentIdentityV1)
| HistoryIdentity=3(QuotientHistoryIdentityV1)
| EvaluationIdentity=4(QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1)
| Constructor=5(ConstructorTagV1)

QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientBindingRoleCandidateV1,
   value:QuotientBindingRequirementValueCandidateV1)

QuotientBindingRequirementViewCandidateV1 =
  (entries:vector<QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1>).
```

Its exact vector is:

```text
[
  (Versions,Versions(Q1.versions)),
  (ProfileIdentity,ProfileIdentity(Q1.profile_identity)),
  (ParentJg1,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.jg1)),
  (ParentJg2a,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.jg2a)),
  (ParentJg2b2a,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.jg2b2a)),
  (ParentJg2b2b0,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.jg2b2b0)),
  (ParentJg2b2b1,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.jg2b2b1)),
  (ParentProfile,ParentIdentity(Q1.parent_identities.profile)),
  (HistoryIdentity,HistoryIdentity(Q1.history_identity)),
  (EvaluationIdentity,EvaluationIdentity(Q1.evaluation_identity)),
  (Constructor,Constructor(Q1.constructor))
].
```

Each role, value tag, and value is independently rederived
from the normalized endpoint and compared before the owner coordinates are
erased. The quotient view itself contains only the displayed semantic values.

The build root uses:

```text
QuotientBuildSemanticCheckCandidateV1 ::=
  TelescopeComparison=0(
    QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1)
| OutputAgreement=1(
    QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1)

QuotientBuildSemanticCheckViewCandidateV1 =
  (entries:vector<QuotientBuildSemanticCheckCandidateV1>).
```

Its vector is exactly the constructor-specific group-4 sequence from section
10.2.6: the optional telescope comparison first, then every agreement role in
displayed order.

#### 10.3.4 Quotient predicate and container candidate

The semantic projection relations are:

```text
QuotientObligationProjectionRelationCandidateV1 ::=
  ExactBinding=0
| SourceResolution=1
| CarrierFormation=2
| CarrierCompatibility=3
| ParticularSubstitution=4
| ParticularComposition=5
| ParticularIteratedLift=6
| TelescopeComparison=7
| NormalizedOutputAgreement=8

QuotientObligationProjectionPredicateCandidateV1 =
  (relation:QuotientObligationProjectionRelationCandidateV1,
   sources:vector<QuotientObligationSourcePathCandidateV1>)

QuotientOriginSingletonObligationCandidateV1 =
  (origin_path:OriginPathV1,
   principal_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1,
   expected_anchor:OccurrenceId14V1)

QuotientSupportProjectionRequirementCandidateV1 =
  (kind:SupportProjectionSubjectKindV1,
   evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   subject_endpoint_paths:
     vector<QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1>,
   construction_rule:DefinitionRulePathV1)

QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1 =
  (subject_slot:u16,
   expected_root_kind:SubjectRootKindV1)

QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1 ::=
  Projection=0(QuotientObligationProjectionPredicateCandidateV1)
| OriginSingleton=1(QuotientOriginSingletonObligationCandidateV1)
| SupportProjectionRequirement=2(
    QuotientSupportProjectionRequirementCandidateV1)
| SubjectWellFormed=3(
    QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1)
| ConstructionRequirement=4(DefinitionRulePathV1).
```

There is no raw membership, length constraint, kernel-judgment pair,
raw-output agreement, profile-rule placeholder, receipt, or proof variant. The
new quotient sum is not an erasure or renumbering of
`DerivedObligationPredicateV1`.

The projection arities are exact:

| Relation | Exact ordered source vector |
| --- | --- |
| `ExactBinding` | one complete `QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `SourceResolution` | request, then the same-tag semantic success payload |
| `CarrierFormation` | one complete carrier-formation requirement |
| `CarrierCompatibility` | one complete carrier-compatibility requirement |
| each particular-operation relation | one complete operation descriptor |
| `TelescopeComparison` | one complete comparison-view record |
| `NormalizedOutputAgreement` | one complete agreement-view record |

Wrong length, order, relation/view tag, or symbolic result type aborts the
complete quotient projection. Historical arity is not predicate-source-vector
length; a future relation may have several typed fields without changing the
exact-depth-two claim.

The unrooted container candidate is:

```text
QuotientObligationSupportPathCandidateV1 =
  (steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   result_type:QuotientObligationSymbolicTypeCandidateV1)

QuotientObligationCandidateV1 =
  (obligation_ordinal:u32,
   group:ObligationGroupTagV1,
   group_local_ordinal:u32,
   rule_path:DefinitionRulePathV1,
   support_path:QuotientObligationSupportPathCandidateV1,
   expected_judgment:QuotientObligationExpectedJudgmentCandidateV1,
   predicate:QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1)

QuotientObligationGroupCandidateV1 =
  (group:ObligationGroupTagV1,
   entries:vector<QuotientObligationCandidateV1>)

QuotientObligationsCandidateV1 =
  (constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
   groups:vector<QuotientObligationGroupCandidateV1>).
```

The self-relative support path for row `k` in group `g` is exactly:

```text
steps = [
  RecordField(1), VectorElement(checked_u32(g)),
  RecordField(1), VectorElement(checked_u32(k)),
  RecordField(6)
]
result_type = tau(QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1).
```

The first `RecordField(1)` selects `groups`, the second selects `entries`, and
`RecordField(6)` selects `predicate`. A later quotient-support path type adds
its exact `Obligations` root. This candidate does not reuse `FullLocalPathV1`,
whose root sum admits `RawCode`, `SubjectProjection`, and action arguments.

#### 10.3.5 Common compiler equations

Let `C_g` be the exact length of quotient group `g`. Define in `u64`:

```text
P[0] = 0
P[g+1] = checked_add_u64(P[g],u64(C_g))
require P[8] <= checked_add_u64(u64(u32::MAX),1)

group_local_ordinal(g,k) = checked_u32(k)
obligation_ordinal(g,k) =
  checked_u32(checked_add_u64(P[g],u64(k))).
```

For every completed value:

```text
constructor = Q1.constructor = Q2.canonical_payload.variant_tag
groups.len = 8
groups[g].group = ObligationGroupTagV1(g)
groups[g].entries[k].group = ObligationGroupTagV1(g).
```

Every group vector, including an empty vector, is present. Rows are
concatenated in group order without sorting or deduplication. The current count
plan in constructor-tag order is:

```text
[11, C1, C2_c, C3_c, M_c, 8, 1, S_c]

Triple(n) = checked_add_u64(n,checked_add_u64(n,n))
Sextuple(n) = checked_add_u64(Triple(n),Triple(n))

C2_c = [5,8,8,7,11,9,13]
C3_c = [
  0,0,0,2,0,
  checked_u32(checked_add_u64(Triple(m_5),3)),
  checked_u32(
    checked_add_u64(
      15,checked_add_u64(p_6,Sextuple(m_6))))
]
M_c = [1,1,2,2,3,1,1]
S_c = [8,8,8,11,8,8,11]

Q_c = Q2.canonical_payload after requiring variant_tag=c
m_5 = u64(Q_5.scheme.ports.len)
p_6 = u64(Q_6.grammar.operations.len)
m_6 = u64(Q_6.scheme.ports.len).
```

Branch-local projection `Q_c` is undefined outside its required constructor
tag; attempting such a projection aborts.

Thus the mathematical shorthand is
`[0,0,0,2,0,3m_5+3,15+p_6+6m_6]`; every operation is checked in `u64`, and each
dynamic final count must convert to `u32`. Section 10.6.3 freezes the generating
programs.
Section 10.7 closes the three group-5 path vectors. This common-equation section
does not itself execute the final eight-group concatenation; section 10.8 now
does so and materializes the `QuotientObligationsCandidateV1` value.

#### 10.3.8 Independence, aborts, and theory boundary

For successful representatives `E,E'` with equal complete retained quotient
semantic coordinates, the mandatory equations are:

```text
QBindingView(E)        = QBindingView(E')
QResolveCalls(E)       = QResolveCalls(E')
QCarrierView(E)        = QCarrierView(E')
QOperationView(E)      = QOperationView(E')
QBuildSemanticView(E)  = QBuildSemanticView(E')
QPrimaryRouteCensus(E) = QPrimaryRouteCensus(E')

CompileQObligations(E) = CompileQObligations(E').
```

Every equality is ordered full canonical-byte equality. A mismatch aborts the
complete quotient projection; the compiler cannot choose one representative,
truncate a vector, copy a replay ordinal, or sort repeated sources.

It also aborts on any group/tag/count mismatch; checked arithmetic failure;
wrong relation arity, source order, typed step, or symbolic target; path outside
the named compiler image; G2 index/value or compatibility failure; G3
substitution/composition/lift failure; G4 comparison/agreement failure; G5
route-census mismatch, sorting, deduplication, or third premise copy; G6
origin/source/anchor mismatch; G7 slot/root-kind mismatch; or any raw code,
raw ordinal, owner index, source ordinal, proposal, replay, receipt, transcript,
span, copied R2c path, or copied/fabricated `TypeIdV1`.

The supplied exact-depth-two theory is only a negative scope guard here. In the
fixed opaque CCHM sealing calculus, unary action and binary comparison suffice
as primitive public operations; sealing-generated higher total open-box
extensions, meaning the missing lid and filler together, add no third primitive
predicate. This does not assert uniqueness for arbitrary fixed-lid or closed-
boundary filler spaces, does not erase any frozen ordinary slot, and does not
derive a quotient equality or generic substitution theorem. In particular,
the three extra-coherence subject slots remain exactly three ordered ordinary
descriptors; their ordinal count is not historical arity.

The broader legacy wording in `tex/pen_paper.tex` that higher fillers are
determined by two-dimensional boundaries or computed from the 2-skeleton is
not consumed by this cut. The refined exact-depth-two appendices and LMCS
record control here: only the sealing-generated total open-box extension is
contractible. Repair of that legacy manuscript sentence remains an A2-C theory
task and cannot silently strengthen this ordinary compiler.

No Genesis label, held-out contextual-internalization prediction, `GCap`
disposition, or desired historical continuation may select an O2a--O2c row.

Section 10.8 closes item 2's final eight-group concatenation and prefix-sum
materialization after O2a1/O2a2 closed the complete group-2 carrier table, O2b
closed group 3, and O2c closed the group-5 primary-route census. Section 10.9
closes item 3's source-bundle value and symbolic path translator; section 10.10
closes item 4's full 8/11 subject programs and completes QG2. QG3 now owns the
active support/action mirror frontier; root `0xfd`, final wire bytes, numerical
TypeIds, and R3 resources remain unmaterialized.

### 10.4 QG2-O2a1 maximal carrier occurrence and pack closure

O2a1 makes the missing representation choice explicitly. It is **closed at
the unrooted compiler-plan level**. A3-O fixes the mathematical carriers but
does not supply a carrier-row traversal: its `PublicLeaves` operation is a
term-leaf traversal and cannot choose group-2 rows. The rule below is therefore
a new quotient representation adjudication. It is selected solely from the
closed nine-carrier sum and Q2's field types, never from a representative's
R2c obligation vector or a desired historical result.

#### 10.4.1 Direct-field maximal-body rule

For constructor tag `c`, scan these four regions in exactly this order:

```text
Q2.canonical_payload
Q2.indexed_inputs.indices
Q2.indexed_inputs.nominal_inputs
Q2.indexed_output.
```

Within each selected sum payload, scan direct fields in declared record order.
Append one occurrence exactly when the direct field has one of the following
nine body types; after appending it, do not descend inside the body:

```text
Unit                                      -> PublicContext
NormalizedTelescopeV1                     -> PublicInterface
NormalizedFamilyValueV1                   -> InterfaceFamily
NormalizedSubstitutionV1                  -> Substitution
NormalizedComparisonValueV1               -> ComparisonWitness
NormalizedGrammarValueV1                  -> SealedPublicGrammar
NormalizedSchemeValueV1                   -> DemandScheme
NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1               -> LiveDemand
NormalizedDischargeValueV1                -> Discharge.
```

`Unit` maps to `PublicContext` only at the physically encoded
`Formation.here` and `Abstraction.here` fields. A bare
`NormalizedContextV1` never synthesizes a context row. A bare
`NormalizedSectionV1`, including an activation or implementation-activation
field, never synthesizes a substitution row. Contexts and activation sections
may supply dependent index components without becoming rows. Composite family,
comparison, grammar, scheme, live-demand, and discharge bodies remain maximal
and are not recursively reopened. `normalized_public_fields` contributes no
row. Equal bodies at distinct retained roles remain distinct.

This inclusion rule rejects both overgeneration from bare indices and
deduplication of role-distinct Q2 copies. It gives every occurrence exactly one
physical body path while allowing its complete dependent index to use an
ordered pack recipe.

#### 10.4.2 Exact path and pack plans

For tag `c`, define these meta-level Q2 path plans:

```text
P_c[f] =
  [RecordField(0),SumPayload(c),RecordField(f)]

X_c[f] =
  [RecordField(1),RecordField(0),
   SumPayload(c),RecordField(f)]

N_c[f] =
  [RecordField(1),RecordField(1),
   SumPayload(c),RecordField(f)]

O_c =
  [RecordField(2),SumPayload(c),RecordField(0)].
```

`P`, `X`, `N`, and `O` mean canonical payload, constructor indices,
nominal inputs, and indexed output. They are compiler provenance, not
obligation-source paths, final typed paths, or wire fields. Every table row
has one `body_path` and a finite ordered index plan. A composite index plan may
read sibling paths or invoke one named normalized reconstruction; it is not
misrepresented as a single path to a complete carrier.

Use these exact pack abbreviations, each displaying first the index and then
the normalized carrier value:

```text
PC(G;u) =
  PublicContextIndex(G),
  PublicContext(gamma=G,here=u)

PI(G;A) =
  PublicInterfaceIndex(G),
  PublicInterface(gamma=G,a=A)

IF(G,A;F) =
  InterfaceFamilyIndex(G,A),
  InterfaceFamily(F)

Sub(D,G;t) =
  SubstitutionIndex(D,G),
  Substitution(t)

Cmp(G,A,B;q) =
  ComparisonWitnessIndex(G,A,B),
  ComparisonWitness(q)

Gram(G;V) =
  SealedPublicGrammarIndex(G),
  SealedPublicGrammar(gamma=G,grammar=V)

Scheme(G,V;S) =
  DemandSchemeIndex(G,V),
  DemandScheme(gamma=G,grammar=V,scheme=S)

Live(G,V,S,a;L) =
  LiveDemandIndex(G,V,S,a),
  LiveDemand(gamma=G,grammar=V,scheme=S,activation=a,live=L)

Dch(G,V,S,a,L;d) =
  DischargeIndex(G,V,S,a,L),
  Discharge(gamma=G,grammar=V,scheme=S,
            activation=a,live=L,discharge=d).
```

`AppendTelescope(G,A)` is the section-10.2.5 oldest-first context extension.
Every pack validates that the carrier tag, index tag, and every index component
repeated inside the value agree by complete canonical bytes.

The compiler-plan type is finite:

```text
QuotientCarrierOccurrencePlanCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientCarrierOccurrenceRoleCandidateV1,
   index_plan:closed row-specific plan below,
   body_path:Q2 path plan above).
```

It is a meta-level plan, not a schema-DAG node. The emitted semantic record
remains the three-field
`QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1` from section 10.3.7. Thus a
group-2 formation obligation still has exactly one source:

```text
CarrierRequirements/
  [RecordField(0),VectorElement(checked_u32(k))]
```

with symbolic result type `CarrierFormationRequirement`. The body and index
plans authenticate construction of that source record; they do not enlarge the
projection predicate's frozen unary arity.

| 0 | `ComparisonPayloadA` | `P_4[1]` | `PI(P_4[0];P_4[1])` |
| 1 | `ComparisonPayloadB` | `P_4[2]` | `PI(P_4[0];P_4[2])` |
| 2 | `ComparisonPayloadWitness` | `P_4[3]` | `Cmp(P_4[0],P_4[1],P_4[2];P_4[3])` |
| 3 | `ComparisonPayloadImplementationA` | `P_4[4]` | `PI(P_4[0];P_4[4])` |
| 4 | `ComparisonPayloadImplementationB` | `P_4[5]` | `PI(P_4[0];P_4[5])` |
| 5 | `ComparisonIndicesA` | `X_4[1]` | `PI(X_4[0];X_4[1])` |
| 6 | `ComparisonIndicesB` | `X_4[2]` | `PI(X_4[0];X_4[2])` |
| 7 | `ComparisonInputLeft` | `N_4[0]` | `PI(X_4[0];N_4[0])` |
| 8 | `ComparisonInputRight` | `N_4[1]` | `PI(X_4[0];N_4[1])` |
| 9 | `ComparisonOutputWitness` | `O_4` | `Cmp(X_4[0],X_4[1],X_4[2];O_4)` |

The two witness bodies remain maximal. Their endpoint telescopes physically
repeated outside those bodies remain separate rows.

**DemandCompiler, tag 5 — `F_5=8`.**

| `k` | Role | Body path | Exact index/value pack |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `DemandPayloadGrammar` | `P_5[1]` | `Gram(P_5[0];P_5[1])` |
| 1 | `DemandPayloadScheme` | `P_5[2]` | `Scheme(P_5[0],P_5[1];P_5[2])` |
| 2 | `DemandPayloadLive` | `P_5[4]` | `Live(P_5[0],P_5[1],P_5[2],P_5[3];P_5[4])` |
| 3 | `DemandIndicesGrammar` | `X_5[1]` | `Gram(X_5[0];X_5[1])` |
| 4 | `DemandIndicesScheme` | `X_5[2]` | `Scheme(X_5[0],X_5[1];X_5[2])` |
| 5 | `DemandInputGrammar` | `N_5[0]` | `Gram(X_5[0];N_5[0])` |
| 6 | `DemandInputScheme` | `N_5[1]` | `Scheme(X_5[0],N_5[0];N_5[1])` |
| 7 | `DemandOutputLive` | `O_5` | `Live(X_5[0],X_5[1],X_5[2],X_5[3];O_5)` |

`P_5[3]` and `X_5[3]` are activation-index sources; `P_5[5]` is the
implementation activation. All three are sections, not standalone carrier
bodies.

**DischargeTransformer, tag 6 — `F_6=12`.**

| `k` | Role | Body path | Exact index/value pack |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `DischargePayloadTheta` | `P_6[2]` | `Sub(P_6[0],P_6[1];P_6[2])` |
| 1 | `DischargePayloadGrammar` | `P_6[3]` | `Gram(P_6[1];P_6[3])` |
| 2 | `DischargePayloadScheme` | `P_6[4]` | `Scheme(P_6[1],P_6[3];P_6[4])` |
| 3 | `DischargePayloadLive` | `P_6[6]` | `Live(P_6[1],P_6[3],P_6[4],P_6[5];P_6[6])` |
| 4 | `DischargePayloadSource` | `P_6[7]` | `Dch(P_6[1],P_6[3],P_6[4],P_6[5],P_6[6];P_6[7])` |
| 5 | `DischargePayloadImplementationOutput` | `P_6[8]` | `DchAt(QDischargeIndex(Q),P_6[8])` |
| 6 | `DischargeIndicesGrammar` | `X_6[2]` | `Gram(X_6[1];X_6[2])` |
| 7 | `DischargeIndicesScheme` | `X_6[3]` | `Scheme(X_6[1],X_6[2];X_6[3])` |
| 8 | `DischargeIndicesLive` | `X_6[5]` | `Live(X_6[1],X_6[2],X_6[3],X_6[4];X_6[5])` |
| 9 | `DischargeInputTheta` | `N_6[0]` | `Sub(X_6[0],X_6[1];N_6[0])` |
| 10 | `DischargeInputSource` | `N_6[1]` | `Dch(X_6[1],X_6[2],X_6[3],X_6[4],X_6[5];N_6[1])` |
| 11 | `DischargeOutput` | `O_6` | `DchAt(QDischargeIndex(Q),O_6)` |

For rows 5 and 11, `DchAt` uses exactly the section-10.2.5 reconstruction:

```text
G_prime = ReindexGrammar(Q.theta,Q.grammar)
S_prime = ReindexScheme(Q.theta,Q.scheme)
theta_E = QIteratedLift(Q.theta,Q.grammar.exports).result
a_prime = ReindexSection(theta_E,Q.activation)
L_prime = Specialize(S_prime,a_prime)

index =
  DischargeIndex(
    gamma=Q.delta,
    grammar=G_prime,
    scheme=S_prime,
    activation=a_prime,
    live=L_prime)

value =
  Discharge(
    gamma=Q.delta,
    grammar=G_prime,
    scheme=S_prime,
    activation=a_prime,
    live=L_prime,
    discharge=body).
```

Its named plan consumes `P_6[0]`, `P_6[2]`, `P_6[3]`, `P_6[4]`, and
`P_6[5]`. The derived `G_prime/S_prime/a_prime/L_prime` values are normalized
operation results, not fabricated path targets.

The exact formation-count vector is therefore:

```text
F_c = [4,7,7,6,10,8,12]
```

in constructor-tag order. Section 10.5 freezes the compatibility count `K_c`
and thereby closes

```text
C2_c[c] = F_c[c] + K_c[c].
```

#### 10.4.4 Formation-row compiler and aborts

For `0 <= k < F_c[c]`, emit row `k` of group 2 with:

```text
relation = CarrierFormation
sources = [
  (root=CarrierRequirements,
   steps=[RecordField(0),VectorElement(checked_u32(k))],
   result_type=CarrierFormationRequirement)
]
rule_path = Build[0]
expected_judgment =
  StructuralRecord(
    tau(QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1)).
```

`StructuralRecord` is exact because the unary source is the complete
requirement descriptor. `CarrierSlot(index,field_ordinal)` cannot be the
leading judgment: a maximal carrier has no unique public-field ordinal. Later
support traversal of `requirement.value` still emits every ordinary
field-specific `CarrierSlot` judgment in the frozen public-leaf order. This
does not promote those leaves into additional group-2 obligations.

The view builder validates for every row:

```text
eval(body_path) has the role's exact body type
eval(index_plan) has the displayed CarrierIndexValueV1 tag
value has the matching NormalizedCarrierValueV1 tag
(index,value) equals the role's displayed complete pack
```

Before scanning, it revalidates every section-9.2 Q2 tag and cross-root
equation. In particular, Abstraction requires
`P_1[3]=X_1[2]=AppendTelescope(P_1[0],P_1[1])` and
`P_1[4]=checked_u32(P_1[0].len)`; Transport and DischargeTransformer require
both substitution endpoints; and every payload/index/input/output copy obeys
the displayed full-byte equation. These are Q2 well-formedness preconditions,
not extra carrier-compatibility rows. All validation is by complete canonical
bytes. The builder preserves table order and repetitions and
aborts on any wrong constructor/role/body/index/value tag; missing, extra,
reordered, or deduplicated row; failed path evaluation, checked conversion,
`AppendTelescope`, or `QDischargeIndex`; index/value endpoint disagreement;
descent into a maximal body; row synthesized from a bare context or section;
public-field promotion; or use of a representative replay position, raw path,
source ordinal, owner coordinate, receipt, evidence value, copied R2c path, or
fabricated TypeId.

For representatives with equal complete Q2 coordinates, evaluation of the
finite plan yields byte-identical ordered formation vectors. O2a1 therefore
closes the occurrence and pack half of `QCarrierView` without selecting a
representative. It does not close O2a2's compatibility semantics, group 2, or
the complete obligation value.

### 10.5 QG2-O2a2 nominal compatibility hypergraph closure

O2a2 is **closed at the unrooted schema-candidate level**. It fixes the
meaning of `CarrierCompatibility` before enumerating edges:

> A quotient carrier-compatibility row is one A3-O nominal constructor
> hyperedge from its ordered nominal carrier inputs to its indexed output.

There is exactly one such hyperedge per constructor. The dependent
carrier-index DAG is already checked by each O2a1 pack. Q2's repeated-copy
equations are QG1 view invariants, and implementation-versus-specified output
agreement is group 4. Neither is duplicated as a carrier-compatibility row.
This separation is normative: merging any of those three relations would
change multiplicities, obscure failure attribution, and double-book facts
already owned by another quotient cut.

#### 10.5.1 Closed role and edge table

The role sum is:

```text
QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  FormationApplication=0
| AbstractionApplication=1
| AggregationApplication=2
| TransportApplication=3
| ComparisonApplication=4
| DemandCompilerApplication=5
| DischargeTransformerApplication=6.
```

Let `R_c[k]` denote O2a1 formation row `k`, including its role, complete
`CarrierIndexValueV1`, and complete `NormalizedCarrierValueV1`. The exact
compatibility table is:

| Constructor `c` | Role | Ordered dependency rows | Target row |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: |
| 0 | `FormationApplication` | `[R_0[2]]` | `R_0[3]` |
| 1 | `AbstractionApplication` | `[R_1[4],R_1[5]]` | `R_1[6]` |
| 2 | `AggregationApplication` | `[R_2[5]]` | `R_2[6]` |
| 3 | `TransportApplication` | `[R_3[3],R_3[4]]` | `R_3[5]` |
| 4 | `ComparisonApplication` | `[R_4[7],R_4[8]]` | `R_4[9]` |
| 5 | `DemandCompilerApplication` | `[R_5[5],R_5[6]]` | `R_5[7]` |
| 6 | `DischargeTransformerApplication` | `[R_6[9],R_6[10]]` | `R_6[11]` |

The dependencies are exactly the A3-O nominal input order, never sorted by
role or carrier tag. The target is exactly the `IndexedOutputV1` occurrence.
Payload, index-copy, and implementation occurrences remain in the formation
vector but do not create extra nominal applications.

#### 10.5.2 Exact hyperedge equations

The notation below unpacks the displayed O2a1 carrier constructors. Every
line is an equality of complete typed values and therefore of full canonical
bytes; a notation mismatch is an abort, not a coercion.

**Formation.** For some normalized context `G` and telescope `A`:

```text
dependencies = [PC(G;Unit)]
target       = PI(G;A).
```

The dependency's `here` field is exactly the encoded `Unit`; the output's
interface index is exactly the dependency context.

**Abstraction.** For normalized `G,A,B`, put
`GA=AppendTelescope(G,A)` and
`F=NormalizedFamilyValueV1(gamma=G,a=A,b=B)`. Then:

```text
dependencies = [PC(GA;Unit), PI(GA;B)]
target       = IF(G,A;F).
```

This simultaneously requires the input interface to live over the complete
extended context and the output family to retain the same `G/A/B` values.

**Aggregation.** For normalized `G,A,B`, let
`F=NormalizedFamilyValueV1(gamma=G,a=A,b=B)` and let
`ConcatTelescope(A,B)` be the frozen oldest-first dependent concatenation of
`A` with `B` over `AppendTelescope(G,A)`. Then:

```text
dependencies = [IF(G,A;F)]
target       = PI(G;ConcatTelescope(A,B)).
```

No association tree, flattening choice, or caller output participates.

**Transport.** For `t:NormalizedSubstitutionV1` with
`t.delta=D`, `t.gamma=G`, and for `A` over `G`, let
`ReindexTelescope(t,A)` be the frozen capture-safe normalized telescope
action. Then:

```text
dependencies = [Sub(D,G;t), PI(G;A)]
target       = PI(D;ReindexTelescope(t,A)).
```

This checks both substitution endpoints and the dependent output index; it is
not a copied particular-substitution receipt.

**Comparison.** For normalized `G,A,B`, let:

```text
q = NormalizedComparisonValueV1(
      gamma=G,
      left=A,
      right=B,
      route=KernelNormalizedTypeEquality).

dependencies = [PI(G;A), PI(G;B)]
target       = Cmp(G,A,B;q).
```

Formation of `q` includes equal telescope length and separately checked
prefix-wise normalized type bytes. It synthesizes no kernel proof term,
universe judgment, path, or equivalence.

**DemandCompiler.** For normalized context `G`, grammar body `V`, scheme body
`S`, and activation `a`, require `a` to be the complete normalized section of
`S.trigger` over `AppendTelescope(G,V.exports)`. Let `L=Specialize(S,a)` by the
frozen dependency-ordered port and contract specialization. Then:

```text
dependencies = [Gram(G;V), Scheme(G,V;S)]
target       = Live(G,V,S,a;L).
```

The activation is a dependent target-index component, not a third nominal
carrier input and not a synthesized substitution row.

**DischargeTransformer.** For `t:NormalizedSubstitutionV1` with
`t.delta=D`, `t.gamma=G`, source grammar/scheme/activation/live values
`V,S,a,L`, and discharge body `d`, reconstruct in order:

```text
V_prime = ReindexGrammar(t,V)
S_prime = ReindexScheme(t,S)
t_E     = QIteratedLift(t,V.exports).result
a_prime = ReindexSection(t_E,a)
L_prime = Specialize(S_prime,a_prime)
d_prime = NormalizedDischargeValueV1(
            section=ReindexSection(t_E,d.section))

dependencies = [Sub(D,G;t), Dch(G,V,S,a,L;d)]
target       = Dch(D,V_prime,S_prime,a_prime,L_prime;d_prime).
```

The rebuilt section is revalidated as a complete section of
`L_prime.demanded_telescope` over `AppendTelescope(D,V_prime.exports)`, and
substitution of that section into every specialized contract is independently
normalized and required equal. This is the frozen A2-O/A3-O capture-safe
discharge action with its ambient index explicit. The target index is byte-
identical to `QDischargeIndex(Q)` from section 10.2.5, and the target body is
byte-identical to `O_6`. No replay, contract receipt, or derivability
disposition is retained.

These seven equations also require every dependency and target to be the
byte-identical O2a1 row named in section 10.5.1. A compiler may not construct
an isomorphic replacement, select a different repeated occurrence, or shorten
the dependency vector.

Every constructor-domain side condition from A3-O remains an admission
precondition and is independently rechecked while building Q2: nonempty and
dependent-formation conditions, the Abstraction cut, Aggregation and Transport
output comparison, Comparison length/normal-form equality, DemandCompiler
trigger/port-or-contract conditions, and DischargeTransformer nonempty demand
and contract formation. They are neither erased nor emitted again as nominal
compatibility hyperedges.

#### 10.5.3 Complete group-2 compiler

The compatibility-count vector is:

```text
K_c = [1,1,1,1,1,1,1].
```

Therefore group 2 is now exact:

```text
C2_c[c] = F_c[c] + K_c[c]
C2_c    = [5,8,8,7,11,9,13].
```

For `0 <= k < F_c[c]`, compile the formation row exactly as section 10.4.4.
Then compile the sole compatibility row at group-local position `F_c[c]`:

```text
relation = CarrierCompatibility
sources = [
  (root=CarrierRequirements,
   steps=[RecordField(1),VectorElement(0)],
   result_type=CarrierCompatibilityRequirement)
]
rule_path = Build[0]
expected_judgment =
  StructuralRecord(
    tau(QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1)).
```

Its `dependencies` and `target` fields are complete byte-identical copies of
the named formation requirements. This preserves the projection predicate's
unary source arity while making the hyperedge self-contained. The compiler
then rebuilds group-local and global ordinals through the section-10.3.5
checked prefix sums. It never places a compatibility row before a formation
row or interleaves the two segments.

In addition to the O2a1 aborts, group 2 aborts on a wrong compatibility role,
dependency count/order/role, target role, constructor tag, pack equation,
normalized operation, or full-byte equality; a duplicate-copy equation
misreported as compatibility; an implementation/output agreement moved out of
group 4; or a representative record used to choose, omit, or reorder an edge.

For equal complete Q2 coordinates, all seven normalized operations above are
deterministic and the O2a1 vectors are byte-identical. The resulting complete
ordered `QCarrierView` and group-2 vector are therefore byte-identical. O2a is
closed without a representative, receipt, owner coordinate, raw path, or new
carrier law. Section 10.6 closes the operation-row ABI,
projection/section-pairing algebra, and exact Decode/Build atomization.

### 10.6 QG2-O2b operation-row ABI and semantic-atomization closure

The O2b audit separates three facts that the earlier placeholder conflated: the
quotient row ABI, the normalized constructors needed to express section-induced
substitutions, and the constructor-specific Decode/Build operation-role census.
A3-O names several compound recipes without atomizing them, R2b stores only
flat replay vectors, and R2c fixes only their high-level concatenation order;
the three cuts below supply the missing quotient representation choices.

#### 10.6.1 O2b1 operation row, result, and complete-prefix ABI — CLOSED

For an operation value `op`, define its substitution result without a caller
branch:

```text
OperationResult(Particular(s))  = s
OperationResult(Composition(c)) = c.result
OperationResult(IteratedLift(l)) = l.result.

CompletePrefixOrdinal(Gamma) = checked_u32(u64(Gamma.len)).
```

`prefix_ordinal` is a prefix-length boundary: zero denotes the empty/base
boundary and `n` denotes all `n` declarations. Consequently `n-1` would name
the boundary before the last declaration and is also undefined for the lawful
empty-codomain case.

For group-local row `k`, let `t` be the tag of
`QOperationView.entries[k].operation`. Its singleton predicate-source vector is
exactly:

```text
ParticularOperations/
  [RecordField(0),VectorElement(checked_u32(k)),RecordField(2),
   SumPayload(t),RecordField(0)].
```

The path selects the complete relation-specific operation payload, not the
enclosing `(role,originating_rule,operation)` entry. This is the exact meaning
of section 10.3.4's “one complete operation descriptor”: the role and origin
are compiler controls that are independently revalidated, while the predicate
source has the exact schema named by its relation. The tag table is:

| `t` | Relation | Symbolic result type |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 0 | `ParticularSubstitution` | `tau(NormalizedSubstitutionV1)` |
| 1 | `ParticularComposition` | `tau(QuotientNormalizedSubstitutionCompositionCandidateV1)` |
| 2 | `ParticularIteratedLift` | `tau(QuotientNormalizedIteratedLiftCandidateV1)` |

Every row then has:

```text
rule_path = QOperationView.entries[k].originating_rule

expected_judgment = ContextPrefix(
  OperationResult(QOperationView.entries[k].operation).gamma,
  CompletePrefixOrdinal(
    OperationResult(QOperationView.entries[k].operation).gamma))

group_local_ordinal = checked_u32(k)
obligation_ordinal = checked_u32(
  checked_add_u64(
    checked_add_u64(
      checked_add_u64(11,u64(C1)),u64(C2_c[c])),
    u64(k))).
```

The final addition is checked rather than wrapping. The self-relative support
path is the section-10.3.4 path for group `3`, row `k`, and `predicate`. A wrong
entry tag, relation, source length/path/type, result codomain, complete-prefix
ordinal, origin, local/global ordinal, or support path aborts the whole quotient
projection.

For representatives with equal complete Q2 coordinates and equal independently
compiled operation views, the complete ordered group-3 rows are byte-identical.
No replay index, representative path, raw ordinal, source ordinal, owner
coordinate, or copied TypeId participates. O2b1 closes this ABI independently;
section 10.6.3 supplies the role compiler and emitted vector.

#### 10.6.2 O2b2a projection and section-pairing algebra — CLOSED

Section 10.2.1 now freezes `ReindexTelescope`, `QProject`, `QPair`,
`QSectionSubstitution`, `QActivationSubstitution`, `QPortSubstitution`, and
`QDischargeSubstitution`. This is the minimum receipt-free algebra needed to
lower an activation section, a port argument section, or a discharge section
to an actual `NormalizedSubstitutionV1`. In particular, `Specialize(S,a)` may
no longer hide an untyped conversion from a section to a substitution.

These constructors are available only after their displayed context,
telescope, section, anchor, output, and JG2b2b1 equations pass. They create no
new operation-value variant: their result is a `Particular` value whose future
role records the exact semantic use. Failure constructs no partial operation
entry.

#### 10.6.3 O2b2b exact Decode/Build operation atomization — CLOSED

An operation occurrence is either an explicit decoded particular or a named
normalized context-morphism constructor, not every term to which its result is
later applied. The compiler emits exactly one row for each explicit decoded-
particular role (`Q.theta` for Transport and DischargeTransformer), one row for
each normative call to `QId`, `QProject`, `QPair`, or `QCompose`, and one row for
each **maximal** `QIteratedLift` call.
Helper specializations are inlined in dependency postorder:

```text
QPortSubstitution       -> QProject; QPair
QActivationSubstitution -> QId; QPair
QDischargeSubstitution  -> QId; QPair.
```

Distinct calls remain distinct rows even when their complete values are byte-
identical. Empty identities/projections/pairings and zero-step maximal lifts
still emit. A lift's individual binder steps remain inside its one complete
descriptor. Applying an already constructed map termwise to a section or
contract emits no additional operation row. Source-resolution weakenings and
field-local `SecCode` expected-type calculations remain group-1 checking
premises, not group-3 morphism constructors.

This syntax-directed rule replaces R2c's underdetermined replay-concatenation
phrase **for the quotient semantic compiler only**. It does not alter the R2c
candidate record. R2b decode/build replays and R2c normalization events remain
independently mandatory validation evidence; they are not projected onto,
zipped with, or cardinality-compared with the semantic entries and never select
a role, multiplicity, phase, or order.

Define the closed role types:

```text
QuotientTransportOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  DecodeTheta=0
| BuildOutputLift=1

QuotientPortFormationOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  PrefixProjection=0(port_ordinal:u32,operation_ordinal:u32)
| ArgumentPairing=1(port_ordinal:u32,operation_ordinal:u32)
| ResultLift=2(port_ordinal:u32,operation_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSpecializationOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  BaseIdentity=0
| ActivationPairing=1
| DemandedTelescopeLift=2

QuotientDischargeOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  BaseIdentity=0
| SectionPairing=1

QuotientDemandCompilerOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  DecodeSchemePort=0(
    QuotientPortFormationOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| BuildSpecialization=1(
    QuotientSpecializationOperationRoleCandidateV1)

QuotientDischargeTransformerOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  DecodeTheta=0
| DecodeSourceSchemePort=1(
    QuotientPortFormationOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DecodeSourceSpecialization=2(
    QuotientSpecializationOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DecodeSourceDischarge=3(
    QuotientDischargeOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DecodeTargetGrammarExportsLift=4
| DecodeTargetGrammarOperationLift=5(operation_ordinal:u32)
| DecodeTargetSchemeBlockLift=6
| DecodeTargetSchemePort=7(
    QuotientPortFormationOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DecodeTargetSpecialization=8(
    QuotientSpecializationOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DecodeTargetRawDischarge=9(
    QuotientDischargeOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| BuildOutputDischarge=10(
    QuotientDischargeOperationRoleCandidateV1)

QuotientParticularOperationRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  Transport=0(QuotientTransportOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DemandCompiler=1(QuotientDemandCompilerOperationRoleCandidateV1)
| DischargeTransformer=2(
    QuotientDischargeTransformerOperationRoleCandidateV1).
```

There is no role for the four constructors with empty operation programs. The
constructor discriminator is exact:

```text
RoleConstructor(Transport(_))            = 3
RoleConstructor(DemandCompiler(_))       = 5
RoleConstructor(DischargeTransformer(_)) = 6

require RoleConstructor(role) = Q2.canonical_payload.variant_tag.
```

Any other pairing aborts.

##### 10.6.3.1 Maximal lift blocks and boundary projection

For a finite dependency-ordered telescope vector, write
`ConcatTelescopes([])=[]` and use the unique left-associated dependent
`ConcatTelescope` fold otherwise. For `L=QIteratedLift(base,T)` and checked
`u64` boundary/slice indices, define:

```text
LiftBoundary(L,0) = L.base
LiftBoundary(L,n) =
  L.steps[checked_u32(checked_sub_u64(n,1))].result
  when 0<n<=u64(T.len)

LiftSlice(L,a,b) = NormalizedTelescopeV1(
  [L.steps[checked_u32(i)].reindexed_domain_parameter | a<=i<b])
  where 0<=a<=b<=u64(T.len).
```

No subtraction is evaluated in the zero branch. All prefix sums and indices
are checked before slicing. The receipt-free normalized term action used below
is:

```text
TermwiseReindexTerms(base,s) =
  require base.gamma is the context in which s was checked;
  NormalizedSectionV1(
    [NF(CaptureSafeReindex(SectionTerms(s)[i],base))
     | 0<=i<SectionTerms(s).len])

TermwiseReindexSection(base,s,target) =
  let r = TermwiseReindexTerms(base,s);
  require SectionTerms(r).len = target.len;
  recheck r as a complete normalized section of target over base.delta;
  return r

TermwiseReindexContract(base,c) =
  require c is a checked contract in base.gamma;
  let r = NormalizedContractValueV1(
    left=NF(CaptureSafeReindex(c.left,base)),
    right=NF(CaptureSafeReindex(c.right,base)),
    ty=NF(CaptureSafeReindex(c.ty,base)));
  recheck r.ty by TypeFormation in base.delta;
  recheck r.left and r.right at r.ty in base.delta;
  return r

TermwiseReindexContractSet(base,cs) =
  canonical_contract_set(map(
    lambda c.TermwiseReindexContract(base,c),cs)).
```

These helpers return normalized terms, sections, or contracts, not context
morphisms, and make no `QId`, `QProject`, `QPair`, `QCompose`, or
`QIteratedLift` call. They emit no group-3 row. A caller using
`TermwiseReindexTerms` must immediately supply an exact target check; the port
program below uses `QPair`'s direct `PairExpected` equations rather than a
hidden `ReindexTelescope` call.

One maximal block is
used wherever the frozen carrier recipe supplies one contiguous dependent
extension:

- Transport: `A`;
- source or target port result: the selected `R_k`;
- specialization: `Q_all=ConcatTelescopes([Q_0,...,Q_(m-1)])`;
- grammar reindexing: `E`, then one `ConcatTelescope(P_k,R_k)` per operation;
- scheme reindexing: one
  `ConcatTelescope(X,Q_all)` block.

Splitting one of these blocks, merging blocks across different operation
ordinals, or deleting a zero-depth block changes the role vector and aborts.
Intermediate maps such as `theta_E`, `theta_EP[k]`, and the activation/port
prefix maps are exact `LiftBoundary` values, not extra rows.

##### 10.6.3.2 Reusable semantic programs

The `emit` labels in this subsection are local roles. Each constructor call
wraps them in exactly the enclosing role alternative named in section 10.6.3.3;
an unwrapped local role never enters the operation view.

For `V=(E,O)`, `S=(X,ports,contracts)`, put
`GammaE=AppendTelescope(Gamma,E)`, `m=S.ports.len`, and let port `j` select
`O[k_j]=(P_(k_j),R_(k_j))`. With `W_0=X` and
`W_(j+1)=ConcatTelescope(W_j,Q_j)`, source-scheme formation emits, for each
ascending `j`:

```text
pi_j    = QProject(GammaE,W_j)
sigma_j = QPair(pi_j,P_(k_j),ports[j].arguments)
K_j     = QIteratedLift(sigma_j,R_(k_j))
Q_j     = LiftSlice(K_j,0,u64(R_(k_j).len))

emit PrefixProjection(j,k_j), Particular(pi_j)
emit ArgumentPairing(j,k_j),  Particular(sigma_j)
emit ResultLift(j,k_j),       IteratedLift(K_j).
```

Every ordinal and selected operation is rechecked, and `Q_j` is byte-identical
to `ports[j].derived_output`. This program has exactly `3m` rows.

`Specialize(Gamma,V,S,a)` emits exactly:

```text
id_E    = QId(GammaE)
alpha_0 = QPair(id_E,X,a)
Q_all   = ConcatTelescopes([Q_0,...,Q_(m-1)])
K_a     = QIteratedLift(alpha_0,Q_all)

emit BaseIdentity,           Particular(id_E)
emit ActivationPairing,      Particular(alpha_0)
emit DemandedTelescopeLift,  IteratedLift(K_a).
```

Checked prefix sums slice `K_a` into the specialized demanded outputs and
recover every port-prefix map with `LiftBoundary`. With
`v=u64(Q_all.len)`, the specialized contract vector is exactly

```text
TermwiseReindexContractSet(
  LiftBoundary(K_a,v),S.contracts).
```

This performs the required canonical full-byte sorting/deduplication and adds
no row. The rebuilt complete live value must equal the retained value.
Specialization therefore has exactly three rows for every `m`, including a
zero-step demanded-telescope lift when `m=0`.

`CheckDischarge(Gamma,V,L,d)` unconditionally emits:

```text
id_E = QId(AppendTelescope(Gamma,V.exports))
beta = QPair(id_E,L.demanded_telescope,d.section)

emit BaseIdentity,    Particular(id_E)
emit SectionPairing,  Particular(beta).
```

For every contract `c` in `L.specialized_contracts`, in stored canonical order,
the compiler constructs `TermwiseReindexContract(beta,c)` and kernel-checks its
normalized `left` and `right` for definitional equality at its normalized `ty`.
Contract cardinality contributes zero additional rows, including when it is
zero. The section and every instantiated contract are still independently
checked.

For `theta:Delta->Gamma`, `ReindexGrammar(theta,V)` first emits
`K_E=QIteratedLift(theta,E)`. Put
`E'=LiftSlice(K_E,0,u64(E.len))` and
`theta_E=LiftBoundary(K_E,u64(E.len))`. For each ascending grammar operation
`k`, emit exactly:

```text
K_O[k] = QIteratedLift(
  theta_E,ConcatTelescope(P_k,R_k)).
```

The split is exact:

```text
q_k         = u64(P_k.len)
r_k         = checked_add_u64(q_k,u64(R_k.len))
P'_k        = LiftSlice(K_O[k],0,q_k)
theta_EP[k] = LiftBoundary(K_O[k],q_k)
R'_k        = LiftSlice(K_O[k],q_k,r_k).
```

The rebuilt `V'` preserves all operation ordinals and has
`V'.operations.len=V.operations.len=p`. Thus grammar reindexing emits `1+p`
lift rows.

The operation trace for the already-frozen public `ReindexScheme(theta,S)`,
after `ReindexGrammar(theta,V)` has rebuilt `V'` and `theta_E`, is named
`ReindexSchemeAfterGrammar(theta_E,V,S,V')`. This internal trace first emits
the single maximal block. It is not a second public overload: its returned
scheme is definitionally the public `ReindexScheme(theta,S)` value for the
ambient indexed grammar `V` and the already rebuilt target grammar `V'`.

```text
K_S = QIteratedLift(
  theta_E,ConcatTelescope(X,Q_all)).
```

Put `DeltaE'=theta_E.delta=AppendTelescope(Delta,E')`. For each ascending port `j`, let
`u_j=u64(W_j.len)` and construct exactly:

```text
W'_j    = LiftSlice(K_S,0,u_j)
b_j     = LiftBoundary(K_S,u_j)
args'_j = TermwiseReindexTerms(b_j,S.ports[j].arguments)
pi'_j   = QProject(DeltaE',W'_j)
sigma'_j = QPair(pi'_j,P'_(k_j),args'_j)
K'_j    = QIteratedLift(sigma'_j,R'_(k_j))

require LiftSlice(K'_j,0,u64(R'_(k_j).len)) =
        LiftSlice(K_S,u_j,u64(W_(j+1).len)).
```

The `QPair` call performs section 10.2.1's direct `PairExpected` check on every
`args'_j` term; no hidden argument-target lift is permitted. The target port
retains `operation_ordinal=k_j`, `arguments=args'_j`, and the common displayed
output slice. The target scheme has `S'.trigger=LiftSlice(K_S,0,u64(X.len))`,
`S'.ports.len=S.ports.len=m`, preserves every port ordinal, and has exactly

```text
S'.contracts = TermwiseReindexContractSet(
  LiftBoundary(K_S,u64(ConcatTelescope(X,Q_all).len)),
  S.contracts).
```

Each target port therefore emits its `PrefixProjection`, `ArgumentPairing`, and
`ResultLift` rows in that order, so scheme reindexing emits exactly `1+3m`
rows.

The later activation action is exactly

```text
a' = TermwiseReindexSection(
  LiftBoundary(K_S,0),a,S'.trigger)
   = TermwiseReindexSection(theta_E,a,S'.trigger).
```

The boundary after `X` is `b_0`, the first port-prefix map; it is not the
activation map. Contract, activation, argument, and discharge term actions
make no context-morphism call and emit no new row.

No base-candidate program calls `QCompose`. Every composition, context-square,
whiskering, and congruence construction belongs to the formal action or
naturality subjects. A Composition-tag operation entry in this group therefore
aborts.

##### 10.6.3.3 Exact seven-constructor programs and counts

Every entry in a Decode segment has
`originating_rule=DefinitionRulePathV1(Decode,[VectorElement(1)])`; every entry
in a Build segment has
`originating_rule=DefinitionRulePathV1(Build,[VectorElement(0)])`. Formation,
Abstraction, Aggregation, and Comparison emit empty views.
Transport emits, in order:

```text
Decode[1]: Particular(Q.theta) with role Transport.DecodeTheta
Build[0]:  IteratedLift(QIteratedLift(Q.theta,Q.a))
           with role Transport.BuildOutputLift.
```

The lift slice is the specified transported output and is independently
compared with the implementation output. Transport has exactly two rows.

DemandCompiler emits source-scheme formation in Decode port order, wrapping
each local role in `DecodeSchemePort`, followed by exactly
`Specialize(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation)` in Build with local roles
wrapped in `BuildSpecialization`. For `m=Q.scheme.ports.len` its count is:

```text
Decode particular/lift = 2m / m
Build  particular/lift = 2  / 1
C3_5 = 3m + 3.
```

DischargeTransformer emits this exact Decode dependency order:

1. `DecodeTheta` as `Particular(Q.theta)`;
2. source-scheme formation, port by port;
3. source specialization;
4. source `CheckDischarge`;
5. target `ReindexGrammar`: exports lift, then operation-block lifts;
6. target `ReindexScheme`: scheme-block lift, then target port triples;
7. target activation termwise reindexing, which emits no row;
8. target specialization; and
9. target-raw `CheckDischarge`.

Steps 4 and 9 are the exact Decode calls

```text
CheckDischarge(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.live,Q.discharge)
CheckDischarge(
  Q.delta,V',L',Q.implementation_discharge).
```

The target `V'/S'/a'/L'` values are Decode-origin because all must exist before
the implementation discharge can be decoded. Build may not relabel or duplicate
them. Build constructs exactly:

```text
d_pred = NormalizedDischargeValueV1(
  section=TermwiseReindexSection(
    theta_E,Q.discharge.section,L'.demanded_telescope))

CheckDischarge(Q.delta,V',L',d_pred).
```

Only that final `CheckDischarge` identity and pairing emit in Build. The
complete `d_pred` must be byte-identical to `Q.implementation_discharge` after
both have passed their independent discharge checks.

For `p=Q.grammar.operations.len` and `m=Q.scheme.ports.len`, the exact kind and
phase counts are:

```text
Decode Particular      = 9 + 4m
Decode IteratedLift    = 4 + p + 2m
Build Particular       = 2
Build IteratedLift     = 0
Composition            = 0

C3_6 = 15 + p + 6m.
```

All arithmetic uses checked `u64` before conversion to `u32`. Contract count,
export count, and telescope lengths affect operation bytes and lift-step
vectors but have coefficient zero in row counts. The exact group-3 vector in
constructor order is therefore:

```text
C3_c = [0,0,0,2,0,3m_5+3,15+p_6+6m_6],

m_5 = u64(Q_5.scheme.ports.len)
p_6 = u64(Q_6.grammar.operations.len)
m_6 = u64(Q_6.scheme.ports.len).
```

Here `Q_c` has the branch-local meaning fixed in section 10.3.5.

As an independent batching invariant, put
`D=sum_{j<m} u64(R_(k_j).len)`, `e=u64(E.len)`, and `x=u64(X.len)`, with checked
sums. DemandCompiler's iterated-lift descriptors contain exactly `2D` binder
steps. DischargeTransformer's Decode iterated-lift descriptors contain exactly

```text
e + sum_{k<p}(u64(P_k.len)+u64(R_k.len)) + x + 5D
```

binder steps, while its Build segment contains zero. Transport's sole lift has
exactly `u64(Q.a.len)` steps. These totals independently cross-check complete
binder-step coverage and multiplicity; the role/count and exact maximal-block
checks above reject splits, merges, and omitted zero-depth descriptors.

##### 10.6.3.4 Complete compiler and independence audit

The view compiler executes the selected program exactly once, appending each
entry in displayed dependency order with its role, forced origin, and operation
tag/value. It then applies O2b1's row compiler without sorting or
deduplication. Role determines origin and value kind; all three must agree.

For equal complete Q2 coordinates, every context, checked prefix sum, selected
operation, maximal block, constructor call, and normalized result above is
deterministic. The ordered roles, origins, operation values, group-3 rows, and
`C3_c` are therefore byte-identical. R2b decode/build replay vectors and R2c
normalization-event vectors are independently revalidated by their already-
frozen traversal equations. They are not projected onto, zipped with, or
cardinality-compared with `entries`. Termwise uses may have replay/events
without operation rows, and a named constructor occurrence may have an
operation row without a one-to-one stored replay. Evidence failure aborts
representative validation, but evidence never adds, deletes, orders,
partitions, or selects a semantic row.

The compiler also aborts on a role/constructor/phase mismatch; wrong port or
operation ordinal; malformed maximal block or slice; wrong projection/pairing
endpoint; missing or extra zero-step call; failed source/target output equality;
contract-use multiplication; a termwise-use row; an individual lift-step row;
any Composition entry; or any representative, receipt, raw/source/owner
ordinal, copied path, or fabricated TypeId used to select a row.

O2b is closed at the unrooted compiler-plan level. The following section closes
O2c's primary-route census. No complete obligation container, root `0xfd`, or
numerical TypeId is materialized by O2b alone.

### 10.7 QG2-O2c primary subject-route census — CLOSED

O2c freezes the exact scan of every physically encoded
`SubjectProjectionSourcePathV1` in the primary R2c endpoint programs. It does
not scan generic paths, proposition suffixes, support-projection subjects, or a
representative-selected path vector. The representation choice is a finite
named compiler-role sum whose lowering produces the symbolic path plans from
section 10.3.2.

#### 10.7.1 Closed route roles and typed path plans

```text
QuotientPrimaryRouteRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  WholeSourceCandidate=0
| SelectorMatchPremise=1
| SelectorCurrentDomain=2
| SelectorAnchor=3
| SubstitutionIdentity=4
| ContextSquareIdentity=5
| AbstractionLiftTargetA=6
| AggregationLiftTargetA=7
| TransportCompositeThetaSource=8
| DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports=9
| DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource=10
| DischargeReindexedGrammarSource=11
| DischargeReindexedSchemeSource=12
| DischargeReindexedActivationSource=13
| DischargeReindexedLiveSource=14
| DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource=15(contract_ordinal:u32).
```

The six roles `0..5` lower exactly to the six direct plans in section 10.3.2.
Writing `SP(c)=SumPayload(c)`, `RF(i)=RecordField(i)`, and
`VE(i)=VectorElement(i)`, the remaining lowering table is exact:

| Role | Root and steps | Symbolic result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `AbstractionLiftTargetA` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(1),RF(1)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `AggregationLiftTargetA` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(2),RF(1)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `TransportCompositeThetaSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(3),RF(2)]` | `NormalizedSubstitution` |
| `DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(5),RF(1),RF(0)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(2)]` | `NormalizedSubstitution` |
| `DischargeReindexedGrammarSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(3)]` | `NormalizedGrammar` |
| `DischargeReindexedSchemeSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(4)]` | `NormalizedScheme` |
| `DischargeReindexedActivationSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(5)]` | `NormalizedSection` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveSource` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(6)]` | `NormalizedLiveDemand` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource(i)` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(6),RF(2),VE(i)]` | `NormalizedContract` |

The contract role is legal only in the DischargeTransformer branch and only
for `i<u64(Q_6.live.specialized_contracts.len)`, with `i` converted exactly to
`u32` before the role or `VE` step is constructed. It targets the canonical
stored order of `Q_6.live.specialized_contracts`, not `Q_6.scheme.contracts`.
The whole scheme already contains the latter; Nat_DT separately retains the
contracts indexing the discharge, which are exactly the specialized contracts
of `L=Q_6.live`.

The child values reached by direct roots are rebuilt exactly as:

```text
QSubjectFootprints =
  (QFootprints.implementation,
   QFootprints.current_output,
   QFootprints.prior_support,
   QLineage.output)

QSelectorInputs.current_domain =
  Q1.evaluation_identity.current_sources
QSelectorInputs.origin_path = QLineage.principal.origin_path
QSelectorInputs.anchor = QLineage.principal.exact_anchor

QActionConstants =
  (QSubstitutionIdentity,QContextSquareIdentity)

QSourceCandidate.match_premise = QSelectorInputs.match_premise
QSourceCandidate.evaluation_identity = Q1.evaluation_identity
QSourceCandidate.constructor = Q1.constructor
QSourceCandidate.canonical_payload = Q2.canonical_payload
QSourceCandidate.principal_lineage = QLineage.principal
QSourceCandidate.footprints = QSubjectFootprints.
```

The two premise fields are populated only after the premise-free source
descriptor is fixed, by section 10's puncture-then-lift construction. These
equations close the child schemas and typed route endpoints but do not yet
construct the complete ten-root source bundle; that remains QG2 item 3.

#### 10.7.2 Exact constructor/slot census

Use the role shorthands:

```text
E  = WholeSourceCandidate
M  = SelectorMatchPremise
U  = SelectorCurrentDomain
o  = SelectorAnchor
Is = SubstitutionIdentity
Iq = ContextSquareIdentity

I(c) = Iq when c in {3,6}
I(c) = Is when c in {0,1,2,4,5}.
```

For every constructor, slots `0..6` are exactly:

```text
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][0] = [E,I(c),E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][1] = [E,E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][2] = [M,U,E,o,E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][3] = [E,E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][4] = [E,E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][5] = [E,E]
QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][6] = [E,E].
```

The constructor-specific formal-action source prefix in naturality slot `7`
is:

```text
S7_0 = []
S7_1 = [AbstractionLiftTargetA]
S7_2 = [AggregationLiftTargetA]
S7_3 = [TransportCompositeThetaSource]
S7_4 = []
S7_5 = [DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports]

h_6 = u64(Q_6.live.specialized_contracts.len)
S7_6 = [
  DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource,
  DischargeReindexedGrammarSource,
  DischargeReindexedSchemeSource,
  DischargeReindexedActivationSource,
  DischargeReindexedLiveSource
] ++ [
  DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource(checked_u32(i))
  | 0<=i<h_6
].

QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][7] = S7_c ++ [E,E].
```

Transport and DischargeTransformer have slots `8..10`, each with an empty role
vector. Those two-whisker programs use only universal binders and
`EarlierBinder` references. The other five constructors have no slots `8..10`;
requesting one aborts rather than returning an empty placeholder.

For every valid constructor/slot pair:

```text
QPrimaryRouteCensus[c][slot] =
  map(RoutePlan,QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][slot])

QPrimaryRouteCensus(c) =
  concat([QPrimaryRouteCensus[c][slot]
          | slot in ascending valid-slot order]).
```

`RoutePlan` is the unique lowering in section 10.3.2 and subsection 10.7.1.
It emits no role tag into the group-5 field. The role is an independently
reconstructed compiler control; the stored vector consists only of complete
symbolic path plans.

Slots `0..6` contribute exactly fourteen `E` occurrences and one each of `M`,
`U`, `o`, and `I(c)`. The common `[E,E]` suffix of slot `7` raises the base to
sixteen `E` occurrences and twenty routes. Hence, in constructor-tag order, the
exact flattened route counts are:

```text
PR_c = [20,21,21,21,20,21,checked_add_u64(25,h_6)].
```

All additions use checked `u64`. Every actual vector-element ordinal converts
exactly to `u32`. Rows 5, 6, and 7 of group 5 each store the byte-identical
`QPrimaryRouteCensus(c)` vector, so group 5 still contains exactly eight
obligations while the three preservation payloads physically contain
`Triple(PR_c[c])` path-plan occurrences. `Triple` is the checked function from
section 10.3.5.

The ordering follows directly from the frozen syntax, not from path sorting.
Within slot 7 every action-argument projection precedes both naturality
endpoints; its nested source path is scanned in literal-field order. The two
endpoint `E` literals then follow. No common-subexpression elimination occurs,
so equal `E` paths and adjacent-slot repeats remain physically repeated.

#### 10.7.3 Empty cases, translation boundary, and independence

An empty Abstraction/Aggregation lift target or empty grammar export telescope
still retains its one target route because the `IteratedLift` descriptor still
physically contains that source path. When `h_6=0`, `S7_6` has only its first
five routes. Slots 8--10 remain empty even when their bound telescopes or
sections are nonempty because their values are sourced solely from universal
binders. `Out_c`, `RawOut_c`, envelope projection paths, and proposition nodes
are not `SubjectProjectionSourcePath` values and add no route.

Every named plan is decoded against the exact quotient child schema, and the
reached schema must equal its symbolic result. The only reachable roots are
`SchemaPayload`, `SelectorInputs`, `ActionConstants`, and `SourceCandidate`.
`Obligations` is unreachable by construction. `SelectorMatchPremise` stops at
the complete admission atom; descent into any of its fields is not a primary
route. A wrong sum tag, field, contract ordinal, result type, constructor/role
pairing, or slot aborts the complete quotient projection.

The independent compiler consumes only the constructor tag, the frozen R2c
subject templates, Q2's normalized payload shape, and `h_6`. It never consumes
an R2c endpoint-path vector to choose its output. At R2d, item 3 independently
compiles the ordered symbolic R2c path-occurrence image, validates each root,
step vector, multiplicity, order, and source schema, translates it through this
closed table, and requires exact equality with the independently compiled
quotient census. R2e later lowers both source and target schema nodes and checks
the physically encoded paths from a supplied validated representative; R4 owns
fixture and mutation replay. An unknown path, root 6, admission-internal
descent, raw/checked-wrapper child, missing repeat, extra path, contract-order
drift, sorting, or deduplication aborts validation but cannot change the
semantic vector.

For representatives with equal complete Q2 coordinates, the constructor,
`h_6`, route roles, checked walks, symbolic results, slot vectors, flattened
census, and all three repeated group-5 payload vectors are byte-identical.
O2c is closed without a representative coordinate, receipt, raw code, source
ordinal, copied TypeId, or new theorem. The following section executes item 2's
final eight-group concatenation and prefix-sum materialization; section 10.9
then executes item 3's ten-role source bundle and symbolic primary-image path
translator. Item 4 remains the exact 8/11 quotient subject compiler.

### 10.8 QG2 item-2 complete unrooted obligation compiler — CLOSED

This section makes no new row-level representation choice. It assembles the
eight already-frozen group compilers, installs one common ordinal/support-path
ABI, and materializes one complete unrooted
`QuotientObligationsCandidateV1`. Item 2 is closed only at this compiler-plan
level: the ten-role source bundle, rooted paths, numerical TypeIds, and root
`0xfd` remain later gates.

#### 10.8.1 Exact group counts and checked prefixes

For branch `c`, let `QResolutionView_c` be the independently compiled value of
schema `QDecodeSourceResolutionViewCandidateV1`, and define the exact source-
resolution count

```text
d_c = u64(QResolutionView_c.calls.len)
```

after independently compiling that view for branch `c`. Let `m_5`, `p_6`, and
`m_6` have their branch-local meanings from section 10.3.5. The complete
eight-position group-count vectors are:

```text
QC_0 = [11,d_0, 5,             0,1,8,1, 8]
QC_1 = [11,d_1, 8,             0,1,8,1, 8]
QC_2 = [11,d_2, 8,             0,2,8,1, 8]
QC_3 = [11,d_3, 7,             2,2,8,1,11]
QC_4 = [11,d_4,11,             0,3,8,1, 8]
QC_5 = [11,d_5, 9,3m_5+3,      1,8,1, 8]
QC_6 = [11,d_6,13,15+p_6+6m_6,1,8,1,11].
```

The displayed multiplication/addition is mathematical shorthand. The compiler
uses `Triple`, `Sextuple`, and checked `u64` addition exactly as sections
10.3.5 and 10.6.3; every source length converts exactly to `u64`. For the
selected branch:

```text
QP_c[0] = 0
QP_c[g+1] = checked_add_u64(QP_c[g],QC_c[g])
require QP_c[8] <= checked_add_u64(u64(u32::MAX),1).
```

Writing `n=d_c`, the exact prefix vectors, including both endpoints, are:

```text
c=0: [0,11,11+n,16+n,16+n,17+n,25+n,26+n,34+n]
c=1: [0,11,11+n,19+n,19+n,20+n,28+n,29+n,37+n]
c=2: [0,11,11+n,19+n,19+n,21+n,29+n,30+n,38+n]
c=3: [0,11,11+n,18+n,20+n,22+n,30+n,31+n,42+n]
c=4: [0,11,11+n,22+n,22+n,25+n,33+n,34+n,42+n]
c=5: [0,11,11+n,20+n,23+n+3m_5,24+n+3m_5,
      32+n+3m_5,33+n+3m_5,41+n+3m_5]
c=6: [0,11,11+n,24+n,39+n+p_6+6m_6,
      40+n+p_6+6m_6,48+n+p_6+6m_6,
      49+n+p_6+6m_6,60+n+p_6+6m_6].
```

These displays are audit equations, not unchecked implementation arithmetic.
Thus the exact total obligation counts are:

```text
QT_c = [
  d_0+34,
  d_1+37,
  d_2+38,
  d_3+42,
  d_4+42,
  d_5+41+3m_5,
  d_6+60+p_6+6m_6
].
```

`h_6=u64(Q_6.live.specialized_contracts.len)` changes only the bytes and length
of each of the three repeated group-5 route-vector payloads. It never changes
`QC_6[5]=8`, any prefix, or `QT_6`.

#### 10.8.2 Closed row-body vectors

Use the compiler-only tuple

```text
QRowBody = (rule_path,expected_judgment,predicate).

QSource(root,steps,result_type) =
  QuotientObligationSourcePathCandidateV1(
    root=root,steps=steps,result_type=result_type)

RF(i) = RecordField(i)
VE(i) = VectorElement(checked_u32(i))
SP(t) = SumPayload(t).
```

It is not a registry node, wire type, source root, or authority. Define the
common projection-body constructor:

```text
ProjectionBody(relation,sources,rule,expected) =
  QRowBody(
    rule,
    expected,
    Projection(
      QuotientObligationProjectionPredicateCandidateV1(
        relation,sources))).
```

The eight body vectors are exactly:

1. `QB_c[0]`: the eleven section-10.3.6 binding rows in displayed binding-role
   order. Row `k` is

   ```text
   ProjectionBody(
     ExactBinding,
     [QSource(
       BindingRequirements,
       [RF(0),VE(k)],
       tau(QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1))],
     PublicLeavesAndLineage[0],
     StructuralRecord(
       tau(QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1))).
   ```

2. `QB_c[1]`: one row per independently compiled resolution call, preserving
   call order. For request/success tag `t`, define

   ```text
   ResolutionResult(0) = tau(QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1)
   ResolutionResult(1) = tau(QuotientCheckedTypeViewCandidateV1)
   ResolutionResult(2) = tau(QuotientCheckedTermViewCandidateV1)

   request_source = QSource(
     DecodeResolution,
     [RF(0),VE(k),RF(0)],
     tau(QSourceResolutionRequestCandidateV1))

   success_source = QSource(
     DecodeResolution,
     [RF(0),VE(k),RF(1),SP(t),RF(0)],
     ResolutionResult(t))

   expected(0) = StructuralRecord(
     tau(QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1))
   expected(1) = TypeFormation
   expected(2) = HasType(
     QResolutionView_c.calls[k].success.value.expected_type).
   ```

   The body is `ProjectionBody(SourceResolution,[request_source,success_source],
   Decode[1],expected(t))`, where `expected(t)` is the exact three-case table in
   sections 10.2.3 and 10.3.6. A tag disagreement aborts rather than coercing
   one branch.

3. `QB_c[2]`: the `F_c[c]` section-10.4 formation bodies followed by exactly
   one section-10.5 compatibility body. Formation row `k` uses

   ```text
   QSource(
     CarrierRequirements,[RF(0),VE(k)],
     tau(QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1)).
   ```

   Its relation is `CarrierFormation`. The final row uses

   ```text
   QSource(
     CarrierRequirements,[RF(1),VE(0)],
     tau(QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1)).
   ```

   Its relation is `CarrierCompatibility`. Both are unary `ProjectionBody`
