   values with rule `Build[0]` and structural expected judgment of their exact
   source schema. The compatibility source selects its self-contained
   hyperedge, never formation element `F_c[c]`.

4. `QB_c[3]`: the section-10.6 operation-view entries in their exact emitted
   order. For entry `k` with operation tag `t`, the unary source is

   ```text
   OperationSymbolicResult(0) =
     tau(NormalizedSubstitutionV1)
   OperationSymbolicResult(1) =
     tau(QuotientNormalizedSubstitutionCompositionCandidateV1)
   OperationSymbolicResult(2) =
     tau(QuotientNormalizedIteratedLiftCandidateV1)

   QSource(
     ParticularOperations,
     [RF(0),VE(k),RF(2),SP(t),RF(0)],
     OperationSymbolicResult(t)).
   ```

   Tag `0`, `1`, or `2` selects, respectively, relation/result
   `ParticularSubstitution/NormalizedSubstitution`,
   `ParticularComposition/NormalizedSubstitutionComposition`, or
   `ParticularIteratedLift/NormalizedIteratedLift`. The rule is the entry's
   independently forced `originating_rule`; expected judgment is the complete-
   codomain `ContextPrefix` from section 10.6.1. The current base programs emit
   no tag-1 Composition row.

5. `QB_c[4]`: the exact section-10.2.6 comparison-first/build-agreement vector.
   For entry `k` with view tag `t`, the unary source is

   ```text
   BuildCheckSymbolicResult(0) =
     tau(QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1)
   BuildCheckSymbolicResult(1) =
     tau(QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1)

   QSource(
     BuildSemanticChecks,
     [RF(0),VE(k),SP(t),RF(0)],
     BuildCheckSymbolicResult(t)).
   ```

   Tag `t` selects relation/result
   `TelescopeComparison/NormalizedTelescopeComparison` or
   `NormalizedOutputAgreement/NormalizedOutputAgreement`. Every row is a
   unary `ProjectionBody` with rule `Build[0]` and structural expected judgment
   of the exact selected schema.

6. `QB_c[5]`: the fixed eight-row vector

   ```text
   ConstructionBody(rule) = QRowBody(
     rule,ConstructionRule(rule),ConstructionRequirement(rule))

   PreservationBody(kind,rule) = QRowBody(
     rule,
     ConstructionRule(rule),
     SupportProjectionRequirement(
       kind=kind,
       evaluation_identity=Q1.evaluation_identity,
       subject_endpoint_paths=QPrimaryRouteCensus(c),
       construction_rule=rule))

   QB_c[5] = [
     ConstructionBody(Normalization[0]),
     ConstructionBody(SupportAndOwnerResolution[0]),
     ConstructionBody(SupportAndOwnerResolution[1]),
     ConstructionBody(SupportAndOwnerResolution[2]),
     ConstructionBody(FieldDisposition[0]),
     PreservationBody(
       NormalizationPreservation,Normalization[0]),
     PreservationBody(
       AdmissibleRenamingPreservation,SupportAndOwnerResolution[2]),
     PreservationBody(
       ReindexingPreservation,SupportAndOwnerResolution[2])].
   ```

   Thus every expected judgment is `ConstructionRule(rule_path)`, every
   construction/preservation rule agrees byte-for-byte with the row rule, and
   the last three contain the byte-identical `QPrimaryRouteCensus(c)` in the
   order fixed by section 10.7.

7. `QB_c[6]`: the singleton

   ```text
   QRowBody(
     Build[0],
     StructuralOccurrence(QLineage.principal.exact_anchor),
     OriginSingleton(
       QLineage.principal.origin_path,
       QLineage.principal.raw_field_path,
       QLineage.principal.exact_anchor)).
   ```

   The two principal-current equations and exact constructor-specific
   origin/leaf pair from section 10.3.6 are mandatory preconditions.

8. `QB_c[7]`: for ascending `0<=k<QC_c[7]`,

   ```text
   QRowBody(
     SubjectConstruction[0],
     StructuralRecord(
       tau(QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1)),
     SubjectWellFormed(
       checked_u16(k),SubjectRootKindV1(k))).
   ```

   Tags `0..7` are the common subject slots; only Transport and
   DischargeTransformer append tags `8..10`.

The compiler requires `QB_c[g].len=QC_c[g]` for every `g`. A missing, extra,
reordered, or deduplicated body aborts the complete construction.

#### 10.8.3 Shared finalizer and complete value

For `0<=g<8` and `0<=k<QC_c[g]`, let `b=QB_c[g][k]`. The unique finalizer is:

```text
GroupTag(0) = ParentReplay
GroupTag(1) = RawMembershipAndDecode
GroupTag(2) = CarrierFormationAndCompatibility
GroupTag(3) = ParticularSubstitutionsAndLifts
GroupTag(4) = IndependentRawOutputCheck
GroupTag(5) = NormalizationSupportAndDisposition
GroupTag(6) = PrincipalCurrentOrigin
GroupTag(7) = TheoremEndpointConstruction.
```

```text
FinalizeQRow(c,g,k,b) = QuotientObligationCandidateV1(
  obligation_ordinal = checked_u32(
    checked_add_u64(QP_c[g],u64(k))),
  group = GroupTag(g),
  group_local_ordinal = checked_u32(k),
  rule_path = b.rule_path,
  support_path = QuotientObligationSupportPathCandidateV1(
    steps = [
      RecordField(1),VectorElement(checked_u32(g)),
      RecordField(1),VectorElement(checked_u32(k)),
      RecordField(6)],
    result_type = tau(QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1)),
  expected_judgment = b.expected_judgment,
  predicate = b.predicate).
```

`GroupTag` is a closed finite table, not an open numeric constructor. The
support path selects `groups[g]/entries[k]/predicate` in the value being
assembled. It is a finite typed path descriptor, not a recursive value edge.

The complete compiler is:

```text
CompileQObligations(Q1,Q2,QLineage,closed quotient views) =
  require c=Q1.constructor=Q2.canonical_payload.variant_tag;
  independently rebuild every view consumed by QB_c;
  compute QC_c, QP_c, and QB_c with checked arithmetic;
  require every body-vector length equation;
  let result = QuotientObligationsCandidateV1(
    constructor=c,
    groups=[
      QuotientObligationGroupCandidateV1(
        group=GroupTag(g),
        entries=[FinalizeQRow(c,g,k,QB_c[g][k])
                 | 0<=k<QC_c[g]])
      | 0<=g<8]);
  for every (g,k), decode result.groups[g].entries[k].support_path
    from result and require it reaches the byte-identical predicate
    with schema tau(QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1);
  return result.
```

All vector reservations are fallible and exact; allocation or checked-
conversion failure returns no partial group and no candidate value. The result
has `groups.len=8`, group tag `g` at physical position `g`, entry count
`QC_c[g]`, contiguous global ordinals in the half-open range `[0,QT_c[c])`, and final endpoint
`QP_c[8]=QT_c[c]`.

Every group record remains present. Group 1 may be empty. Group 3 is empty for
constructors `0`, `1`, `2`, and `4`; `m_5=0` still emits the three specialization
rows. Zero-depth lifts remain rows. `h_6=0` removes only the contract tail from
the repeated route vectors. Empty lift targets retain their physically encoded
route. No other group can be empty under the frozen ordinary schemas.

#### 10.8.4 Audit, independence, and boundary

The compiler aborts on any constructor, branch, group, tag, count, prefix,
ordinal, support path, relation arity, source order, typed step, sum tag,
symbolic result, expected judgment, predicate, or body-vector mismatch. It also
inherits every group-specific carrier, compatibility, operation, agreement,
route, lineage, and subject-slot abort from sections 10.2--10.7. Overflow,
inexact `u64`/`u32`/`u16` conversion, sorting, deduplication, common-
subexpression elimination, omitted empty groups, or changed repeated-path
multiplicity aborts the whole value.

No representative, owner/source/raw ordinal, raw presentation, replay,
receipt, proposal, transcript, span, copied R2c path, or copied/fabricated
`TypeIdV1` may add, remove, partition, order, or select a body. Representative
evidence remains independently mandatory validation evidence and may abort;
it never becomes a compiler input.

For successful representatives `E,E'` with equal complete retained quotient
coordinates:

```text
QBindingView(E)        = QBindingView(E')
QResolveCalls(E)       = QResolveCalls(E')
QCarrierView(E)        = QCarrierView(E')
QOperationView(E)      = QOperationView(E')
QBuildSemanticView(E)  = QBuildSemanticView(E')
QPrimaryRouteCensus(E) = QPrimaryRouteCensus(E')
QC(E) = QC(E')
QP(E) = QP(E')
QB(E) = QB(E')

CompileQObligations(E) = CompileQObligations(E').
```

Every equality is ordered full canonical-byte equality. No Genesis label,
held-out result, desired continuation, `Sem`/SR2 result, `nu`, or `GCap`
disposition is an input. The semantic values used here are typed infrastructure
for the generative-capacity carrier; this cut performs no semantic-novelty
measurement.

QG2 item 2 is now closed. The following section closes item 3's complete
ten-role quotient source bundle and translator over the frozen symbolic primary
route-occurrence image. Neither item 3 nor item 4 may change any row, count,
ordinal, support path, predicate source, symbolic target, or group order frozen
here.

### 10.9 QG2 item-3 quotient source bundle and symbolic path translator — CLOSED

This cut constructs the complete unrooted ten-role source bundle and closes the
source-to-quotient translator at the symbolic compiler-plan level. It does not
reconstruct a raw/evidence-bearing R2c bundle from quotient coordinates and it
does not fabricate either side's numerical `TypeIdV1`. Those operations belong
to R2e and R4 as stated in subsection 10.9.6.

#### 10.9.1 Complete bundle schema and punctured admission descriptor

The missing bundle record is exact:

```text
QuotientSubjectProjectionSourceBundleCandidateV1 =
  (evaluation_binding:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   schema_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   public_fields:vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>,
   principal_lineage:QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1,
   field_lineages:vector<QuotientFieldLineageCandidateV1>,
   footprints:QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1,
   obligations:QuotientObligationsCandidateV1,
   selector_inputs:QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1,
   action_constants:QuotientSubjectActionConstantsCandidateV1,
   source_candidate:QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1).
```

The field order is also the root dispatch. No implementation may infer it from
the payload type:

| Root tag | Bundle field | Exact root schema |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 0 `EvaluationBinding` | `evaluation_binding` | `QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1` |
| 1 `SchemaPayload` | `schema_payload` | `QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1` |
| 2 `PublicFields` | `public_fields` | `vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>` |
| 3 `PrincipalLineage` | `principal_lineage` | `QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1` |
| 4 `FieldLineages` | `field_lineages` | `vector<QuotientFieldLineageCandidateV1>` |
| 5 `Footprints` | `footprints` | `QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1` |
| 6 `Obligations` | `obligations` | `QuotientObligationsCandidateV1` |
| 7 `SelectorInputs` | `selector_inputs` | `QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1` |
| 8 `ActionConstants` | `action_constants` | `QuotientSubjectActionConstantsCandidateV1` |
| 9 `SourceCandidate` | `source_candidate` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` |

The contextual descriptor used by the admission proposition is the following
compiler-only punctured view, not a wire node or an eleventh bundle field:

```text
QuotientPreSubjectDescriptorCompilerViewV1 =
  (evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
   canonical_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   principal_lineage:QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1,
   footprints:QuotientSubjectFootprintProjectionCandidateV1).
```

This is the exact codomain of `PreSubjectSemanticProjection(E)` in section 10.
For a successful R2c representative `E`, the projection is field-by-field:

```text
PreSubjectSemanticProjection(E) =
  QuotientPreSubjectDescriptorCompilerViewV1(
    evaluation_identity=Q1(E).evaluation_identity,
    constructor=Q1(E).constructor,
    canonical_payload=Q2(E).canonical_payload,
    principal_lineage=QLineage(E).principal,
    footprints=(QFootprints(E).implementation,
                QFootprints(E).current_output,
                QFootprints(E).prior_support,
                QLineage(E).output)).
```

Thus it uses exactly the owner-erasure, `QPayload`, `QPrincipal`, and retained
footprint/lineage projections already fixed in sections 8--9. It reads neither
`SelectorInputs.match_premise` nor `SourceCandidate.match_premise`. Hence the
symbol `D` in the admission proposition is now unambiguous and nonrecursive.

#### 10.9.2 Puncture-then-lift compiler and the exact two-copy invariant

`CompileQSourceBundle` consumes only `Q1`, `Q2`, `QLineage`, `QFootprints`, the
closed quotient semantic views, and the independently recompiled item-2 value
`QObligations`. The action identities are constants, not caller inputs. Its
preflight checks are:

```text
c = Q1.constructor
  = variant_tag(Q2.canonical_payload)
  = QObligations.constructor

QObligations =
  CompileQObligations(Q1,Q2,QLineage,closed quotient views)

Q1.anchor.exact = QLineage.principal.exact_anchor
QLineage.fields.len = Q2.normalized_public_fields.len

for every checked u32 i in that length:
  QLineage.fields[i].field_ordinal = checked_u32(i)
  QLineage.fields[i].field_ordinal =
    Q2.normalized_public_fields[i].field_ordinal
  QLineage.fields[i].field_path =
    Q2.normalized_public_fields[i].path.
```

The compiler rechecks every retained-coordinate equation expressible solely
inside Q1, Q2, QLineage, QFootprints, and QObligations: internal profile/
history/evaluation consistency, constructor and payload tags, repeated Q2
roles, field/lineage concordance, footprints, and obligation constructor.
Section-8/9.2 source-projection equations involving representative-side `N`,
`I`, `O`, `B`, parents, owned references, or checked wrappers are not bundle
inputs. R2e independently validates them on a supplied representative and may
only abort; they never select a bundle field. The compiler then constructs, in
this dependency order:

```text
QSubjectFootprints =
  (QFootprints.implementation,
   QFootprints.current_output,
   QFootprints.prior_support,
   QLineage.output)

QPuncturedSelector =
  (current_domain=Q1.evaluation_identity.current_sources,
   origin_path=QLineage.principal.origin_path,
   anchor=QLineage.principal.exact_anchor)

QActionConstants =
  (substitution_identity=QSubstitutionIdentity,
   context_square_identity=QContextSquareIdentity)

D = QuotientPreSubjectDescriptorCompilerViewV1(
  evaluation_identity=Q1.evaluation_identity,
  constructor=c,
  canonical_payload=Q2.canonical_payload,
  principal_lineage=QLineage.principal,
  footprints=QSubjectFootprints)

A = QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1(
  profile_identity=Q1.profile_identity,
  history_identity=Q1.history_identity,
  evaluation_identity=Q1.evaluation_identity,
  anchor=QLineage.principal.exact_anchor,
  constructor=c).
```

`D` is fixed before `A` is interpreted. Relative to that already fixed `D`,
`A` denotes exactly the existential in section 10; `D` is not encoded inside
`A`. Only then are the two premise-bearing children constructed:

```text
QSelectorInputs = QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1(
  match_premise=inline_copy(A),
  current_domain=QPuncturedSelector.current_domain,
  origin_path=QPuncturedSelector.origin_path,
  anchor=QPuncturedSelector.anchor)

QSourceCandidate = QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1(
  match_premise=inline_copy(A),
  evaluation_identity=D.evaluation_identity,
  constructor=D.constructor,
  canonical_payload=D.canonical_payload,
  principal_lineage=D.principal_lineage,
  footprints=D.footprints)

QSourceBundle = QuotientSubjectProjectionSourceBundleCandidateV1(
  evaluation_binding=Q1.evaluation_identity,
  schema_payload=Q2.canonical_payload,
  public_fields=Q2.normalized_public_fields,
  principal_lineage=QLineage.principal,
  field_lineages=QLineage.fields,
  footprints=QSubjectFootprints,
  obligations=QObligations,
  selector_inputs=QSelectorInputs,
  action_constants=QActionConstants,
  source_candidate=QSourceCandidate).
```

`inline_copy` means an independently encoded structural field. Canonical wire
encoding contains no references or interning, so a local compiler variable is
not a third physical copy and common-subexpression elimination cannot replace
either stored occurrence. A typed traversal of the completed bundle schema must
find `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` at exactly these positions:

```text
SelectorInputs/[RecordField(0)]
SourceCandidate/[RecordField(0)].
```

The two reached values are byte-identical. A third typed occurrence, a missing
occurrence, a shared-reference encoding, or unequal bytes aborts the complete
construction. This is the bundle-local two-copy theorem. Item 4 must separately
prove the transitive invariant for every recompiled 8/11 subject family.

The completed bundle additionally obeys, by full canonical bytes:

```text
QSourceBundle.selector_inputs.current_domain =
  QSourceBundle.evaluation_binding.current_sources
QSourceBundle.selector_inputs.origin_path =
  QSourceBundle.principal_lineage.origin_path
QSourceBundle.selector_inputs.anchor =
  QSourceBundle.principal_lineage.exact_anchor

QSourceBundle.source_candidate.match_premise =
  QSourceBundle.selector_inputs.match_premise
QSourceBundle.source_candidate.evaluation_identity =
  QSourceBundle.evaluation_binding
QSourceBundle.source_candidate.constructor =
  variant_tag(QSourceBundle.schema_payload)
QSourceBundle.source_candidate.canonical_payload =
  QSourceBundle.schema_payload
QSourceBundle.source_candidate.principal_lineage =
  QSourceBundle.principal_lineage
QSourceBundle.source_candidate.footprints = QSourceBundle.footprints

QSourceBundle.obligations.constructor =
  QSourceBundle.source_candidate.constructor
QSourceBundle.selector_inputs.match_premise.constructor =
  QSourceBundle.source_candidate.constructor.
```

`QObligations` is built first and embedded once. Its group-5 values contain only
root/step/symbolic-result path-plan data, never `QSourceBundle` by value. All
three preservation vectors statically exclude root 6 `Obligations`; therefore
embedding the completed obligation value introduces no value cycle.

#### 10.9.3 Symbolic R2c primary occurrence image

R2d cannot construct or byte-compare the complete R2c subject value at this
cut. Its source paths contain numerical `TypeIdV1` values assigned only by R2e,
and its bundle contains erased representative data absent from Q1/Q2. Item 3
instead closes this compiler-only, non-wire source-schema sum and occurrence
plan:

```text
R2cPrimarySourceSymbolicTypeCompilerV1 ::=
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor=0
| CandidateMatchPremise=1
| OccurrenceVector=2
| Occurrence=3
| FormalActionSyntax=4
| NormalizedTelescope=5
| NormalizedSubstitution=6
| NormalizedGrammar=7
| NormalizedScheme=8
| NormalizedSection=9
| NormalizedLiveDemand=10
| NormalizedContract=11

R2cPrimaryPathOccurrenceCompilerV1 =
  (subject_slot:u16,
   value_node_ordinal:u32,
   nested_path_ordinal:u32,
   role:QuotientPrimaryRouteRoleCandidateV1,
   source_root:SubjectProjectionSourceRootV1,
   steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   source_result:R2cPrimarySourceSymbolicTypeCompilerV1).
```

The locator distinguishes physically repeated equal paths. For source slot
`slot`, local physical value-node position `j`, and the zero-based source-path
leaf position `k` produced by the recursion below within that node:

```text
subject_slot        = checked_u16(slot)
value_node_ordinal  = checked_u32(j)
nested_path_ordinal = checked_u32(k).
```

`k` resets to zero at each value node. Locators are unique and emitted in
lexicographic `(subject_slot,value_node_ordinal,nested_path_ordinal)` order; any
conversion failure, duplicate, or order drift aborts. Within each path-bearing
value node, the emitted `k` values are contiguous from zero; direct literal tags
0--3 therefore use `k=0`. Subject-slot and value-node positions may have gaps in
the occurrence list because nodes with no source path emit no locator. The
locator is compiler control and is not emitted into a quotient path or bundle.
`CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(c,Q2)` returns one occurrence vector per valid
subject slot. It compiles the frozen R2c endpoint value templates symbolically
and scans exactly one canonical occurrence domain:
`SubjectRootProjection.subjects`, in ascending subject-slot, local value-node,
and nested literal-field order. The byte-identical initial value block in
`SubjectSyntax` is governed by the R2c correspondence theorem; it is not scanned
as a second route domain.

This symbolic compiler allocates neither `SubjectProjectionSourcePathV1` nor
the R2c `value_node_types` vector. It independently emits each path constructor's
root and steps and derives `source_result` by walking the frozen R2c schema. R2e
later checks that lowering this schema yields the numerical result type in the
physical path.

Only a `SubjectNodeV1::Literal` can contribute. Literal tags 0--3 append the
root/steps of their single source-path constructor. For tag 4, the scanner
descends into the `FormalActionProjectionPathV1` root payload and uses this exact
recursion:

```text
IteratedLift(_,target,_)       -> append target
OrderedComposite(left,right)  -> scan left, then scan right
  SourceProjection(path)      -> append path
  PrimitiveArgument(_)        -> append nothing
  EarlierDerived(_)           -> append nothing
ReindexedPayloadComponent(p)  -> append p
ReconstructedEpsilon          -> append nothing
EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint     -> append nothing.
```

Literal tag 5 and every nonliteral node append nothing. Binders are not a source
path domain. Encountering a source path anywhere not generated by this recursion
is an extra occurrence and aborts.

For each slot, the occurrence roles are exactly
`QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][slot]` from subsection 10.7.2. The compiler requires the
same length and attaches roles positionally, preserving every repeat. Slots
8--10 for Transport and DischargeTransformer compile empty occurrence vectors;
those slots are invalid for the other constructors. The flattened role count is
again

```text
[20,21,21,21,20,21,checked_add_u64(25,h_6)],
```

where `h_6` is read only from the DischargeTransformer payload and every actual
contract ordinal admits the exact checked `u32` conversion.

#### 10.9.4 Closed paired route table and quotient typed walks

Write `SP(c)=SumPayload(c)`, `RF(i)=RecordField(i)`, and
`VE(i)=VectorElement(checked_u32(i))`. The exact paired table is:

| Role | Legal constructor | R2c root and steps | R2c source result | Quotient result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `WholeSourceCandidate` | all | `SourceCandidate/[]` | `EnvelopeObjectDescriptor` | `EnvelopeObjectDescriptor` |
| `SelectorMatchPremise` | all | `SelectorInputs/[RF(0)]` | `CandidateMatchPremise` | `CandidateAdmissionPremise` |
| `SelectorCurrentDomain` | all | `SelectorInputs/[RF(1)]` | `OccurrenceVector` | `OccurrenceVector` |
| `SelectorAnchor` | all | `SelectorInputs/[RF(3)]` | `Occurrence` | `Occurrence` |
| `SubstitutionIdentity` | `0,1,2,4,5` | `ActionConstants/[RF(0)]` | `FormalActionSyntax` | `NormalizedAction` |
| `ContextSquareIdentity` | `3,6` | `ActionConstants/[RF(1)]` | `FormalActionSyntax` | `NormalizedAction` |
| `AbstractionLiftTargetA` | `1` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(1),RF(1)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `AggregationLiftTargetA` | `2` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(2),RF(1)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `TransportCompositeThetaSource` | `3` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(3),RF(2)]` | `NormalizedSubstitution` | `NormalizedSubstitution` |
| `DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports` | `5` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(5),RF(1),RF(0)]` | `NormalizedTelescope` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource` | `6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(2)]` | `NormalizedSubstitution` | `NormalizedSubstitution` |
| `DischargeReindexedGrammarSource` | `6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(3)]` | `NormalizedGrammar` | `NormalizedGrammar` |
| `DischargeReindexedSchemeSource` | `6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(4)]` | `NormalizedScheme` | `NormalizedScheme` |
| `DischargeReindexedActivationSource` | `6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(5)]` | `NormalizedSection` | `NormalizedSection` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveSource` | `6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(6)]` | `NormalizedLiveDemand` | `NormalizedLiveDemand` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource(i)` | `6`, `i<h_6` | `SchemaPayload/[SP(6),RF(6),RF(2),VE(i)]` | `NormalizedContract` | `NormalizedContract` |

The R2c source names in this table denote, respectively,
`EnvelopeObjectDescriptorV1`, `CandidateMatchPremiseV1`,
`vector<OccurrenceId14V1>`, `OccurrenceId14V1`, `FormalActionSyntaxV1`, and the
seven displayed normalized schema types. For every occurrence, a symbolic walk
from the named R2c root through the independently emitted steps must reach
exactly that source schema.

All sixteen current roles independently re-emit the same root tag and step bytes
on the quotient side; they do not copy the R2c path object. A second symbolic
walk starts from the root schema in subsection 10.9.1 and must reach the exact
schema named by `QuotientSubjectProjectionSymbolicTypeCandidateV1`. The only
type-changing endpoint relations are:

```text
EnvelopeObjectDescriptorV1
  -> QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1
CandidateMatchPremiseV1
  -> QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1
FormalActionSyntaxV1
  -> QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1.
```

The whole source candidate is rebuilt from `D` and `A`; the raw-witness premise
is replaced by the context-relative admission atom; and the two R2c identity
syntaxes normalize to `QSubstitutionIdentity` and `QContextSquareIdentity`.
Occurrence values and all seven normalized payload-result kinds agree by full
canonical bytes with their quotient positions.

No row in the paired table reaches a checked wrapper. Consequently the generic
Q2 wrapper-normalization relation contributes zero item-3 cases. A path ending
at a wrapper, descending through its unique normalization child, or selecting
any other wrapper child is off the primary image and aborts. The symbolic result
sum is not expanded to admit such a path.

#### 10.9.5 Exact-on-image translator

The translator is internal and occurrence-indexed; it is not a public generic
path recognizer:

```text
TranslateQPrimaryOccurrence(QSourceBundle,occurrence) =
  require occurrence is the exact value at its locator in
    CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(c,Q2);
  require occurrence.role is legal for c;
  require the paired table reproduces occurrence.source_root,
    occurrence.steps, and occurrence.source_result;
  independently emit q = RoutePlan(occurrence.role);
  decode q.root and q.steps against QSourceBundle;
  require the reached schema equals q.result_type;
  require q has its unique canonical encoding;
  return q.
```

For every valid slot, translation is applied in ascending occurrence-locator
order. Both directions are mandatory: every occurrence in the symbolic primary
image translates exactly once, and no other occurrence is accepted. Root/step
equality alone is insufficient because repeated `WholeSourceCandidate` paths
are physically indistinguishable without their locators.

Writing `I_c=CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(c,Q2)`, the complete internal
translation is:

```text
TranslateQPrimaryImage(QSourceBundle,c,Q2) =
  concat([
    [TranslateQPrimaryOccurrence(QSourceBundle,occ)
     | occ in I_c[slot] in ascending locator order]
    | slot in ascending valid-slot order]).
```

The per-slot translated vectors and their flattened concatenation must equal,
by ordered full canonical bytes:

```text
map(RoutePlan,QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][slot])
QPrimaryRouteCensus[c][slot]
QPrimaryRouteCensus(c).
```

Finally, the compiler decodes every stored group-5 path plan against the
completed bundle and requires the `subject_endpoint_paths` vectors in group-5
local rows 5, 6, and 7 each to equal `QPrimaryRouteCensus(c)`. This validation
does not sort, deduplicate, share, or rescan `SubjectSyntax`.

#### 10.9.6 Aborts, deferrals, and independence

Bundle construction aborts without a partial value on any item-2 mismatch;
constructor/tag disagreement; Q1 identity disagreement; Q2 equation failure;
public-field/lineage or anchor mismatch; footprint mismatch; missing, unequal,
or extra admission occurrence; back-edge; allocation or canonical-encoding
failure; or any representative, evidence, receipt, raw code, raw ordinal,
owner-local coordinate, copied TypeId, `Sem`/SR2 result, `nu`, or `GCap`
disposition used to choose a field.

Translation aborts on a locator, count, order, repeat, slot, role, root, step,
sum-tag, vector-bound, source-schema, target-schema, or symbolic-result mismatch;
failed checked conversion; occurrence/image/vector allocation or canonical-
encoding failure; an unknown literal/nested-field case; a merely well-typed
generic path; any root outside `SchemaPayload`, `SelectorInputs`,
`ActionConstants`, and `SourceCandidate`; root 6 `Obligations`; a descendant of
the admission atom, current-domain vector, anchor, action identity, or source
candidate; a checked-wrapper route; a raw-field value or raw presentation;
source/owner/raw ordinals; receipt, replay, transcript, span, resource, or
evidence material; a copied numerical TypeId; or disagreement with any of the
three group-5 vectors. Retained `RawFieldPathV1` and `SourceRefV1` provenance
inside `QLineage` remains lawful data and is not a subject-source path target.

R2e must assign and lower both the R2c and quotient nonzero TypeIds, reconstruct
the actual R2c compiler values from a supplied validated representative, and
check every physical source/target path encoding and numerical result type
against this symbolic occurrence plan. R4 must regenerate canonical fixtures
and reject path, multiplicity, TypeId, and admission-copy mutations. Neither
later check is an input to the R2d compiler.

For successful representatives `E,E'` with equal complete retained quotient
coordinates, the mandatory equalities are:

```text
Q1(E) = Q1(E')
Q2(E) = Q2(E')
QLineage(E) = QLineage(E')
QFootprints(E) = QFootprints(E')
QObligations(E) = QObligations(E')
D(E) = D(E')
A(E) = A(E')
CompileQSourceBundle(E) = CompileQSourceBundle(E')
CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(Q1(E).constructor,Q2(E)) =
  CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(Q1(E').constructor,Q2(E'))
TranslateQPrimaryImage(
  QSourceBundle(E),Q1(E).constructor,Q2(E)) =
  TranslateQPrimaryImage(
    QSourceBundle(E'),Q1(E').constructor,Q2(E')) =
  QPrimaryRouteCensus(c).
```

Every equality is ordered full canonical-byte equality for encoded candidate
values and exact structural equality for compiler-only symbolic controls. A
representative may later make R2e validation abort, but it cannot add, delete,
order, select, or alter a bundle field or route.

The bundle-local two-copy traversal, all ten root dispatches, both symbolic
walks for every occurrence, the exact-on-image biconditional, and all three
group-5 comparisons have passed independent audits. Item 3 is closed at the
compiler-plan level. The result remains unrooted: it contains no root byte,
schema version, root `0xfd`, final path wire, numerical TypeId, theorem subject,
QG3 support or action, representative, evidence, `GCap`, `gamma`, semantic SR2
result, or `nu`. The following subsection closes item 4's exact 8/11 quotient
subject families and their full transitive admission invariant.

### 10.10 QG2 item-4 exact quotient-primary subject compiler — CLOSED

This subsection closes the primary theorem-program family at the same
compiler-plan boundary as items 2 and 3. It also records the narrow R2a/R2c C2
corrigenda forced by the audit: an exact formal history-renaming reference,
an exact proposition sort marker, exact source `Out_c`/`RawOut_c` paths, and
named receipt-free results for the two virtual square components, and one
append-only dependent-discharge proposition opcode. No binder, slot, path
occurrence, clause/node count, or existing node tag changes.

The item-4 output is a fresh quotient-only syntax graph. It does not reuse
`SubjectBinderV1`, `SubjectIndexRefV1`, `CoreProjectionLiteralV1`,
`FormalActionProjectionPathV1`, `TypedProjectionPathV1`, `SubjectNodeV1`, or
their numerical `TypeIdV1` fields. Their transitive source schemas admit raw,
support, proposal, or evidence-bearing branches that are not quotient-primary
data.

The exact compiler-plan result sum is:

```text
QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1 ::=
```

The variants name, in order, the exact leaf schemas:

| Variant | Exact schema |
| --- | --- |
| `Unit` | `Unit` |
| `EnvelopeObjectDescriptor` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` |
| `CandidateAdmissionPremise` | `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` |

| `HistoryRenamingRef` | `SubjectHistoryRenamingRefV1` |

This sum is an encoded symbolic type plan, not a local registry ID. R2e alone
defines the injective lowering from each variant to its exact nonzero
`TypeIdV1`. A zero, duplicate, guessed, representative-copied, or schema-
inconsistent lowering aborts.

`SubjectHistoryRenamingRefV1=newtype u32` and
`SubjectPropositionSortV1=newtype Unit` are the source-and-quotient-safe leaf
types fixed by the R2c subsection 5.3.0a corrigendum. A renaming reference is
the checked global ordinal of the exact formal `HistoryRenaming` binder. It is
not a bijection value. `RenameHistory` interprets it relative to the enclosing
source bundle as a universally quantified admissible same-history identifier
bijection. It has no literal, path, composition, equality, owner, or runtime-
execution form. The proposition sort is only the inferred result marker for
logical nodes; proposition content remains the node expression and no proof is
stored.

The item-3 source result sum embeds by this total table:

```text
EnvelopeObjectDescriptor -> EnvelopeObjectDescriptor
CandidateAdmissionPremise -> CandidateAdmissionPremise
OccurrenceVector          -> OccurrenceVector
Occurrence                -> Occurrence
NormalizedAction          -> NormalizedAction
NormalizedTelescope       -> NormalizedTelescope
NormalizedSubstitution    -> NormalizedSubstitution
NormalizedGrammar         -> NormalizedGrammar
NormalizedScheme          -> NormalizedScheme
NormalizedSection         -> NormalizedSection
NormalizedLiveDemand      -> NormalizedLiveDemand
NormalizedContract        -> NormalizedContract.
```

No source-path result can select `Unit`, context, family, comparison,
discharge, either square subject, the formal renaming reference, or the
proposition marker.

#### 10.10.2 Quotient-primary binder, action-path, literal, and node schemas

The quotient-primary binder sum preserves every emitted R2c tag. R2a requires
contiguous sum tags, so non-image positions are explicit payload-free reserved
variants rather than missing positions or aliases for QG3:

```text
QuotientPrimarySubjectBinderSortCandidateV1 ::=
  ReservedCorePayload=0
| DependentContext=1
| ReservedTelescope=2
| ReservedSection=3
| Substitution=4(domain_binder:u32,codomain_binder:u32)
| Carrier=5(index:SubjectCarrierIndexV1)
| Occurrence=6
| HistoryRenaming=7
| FormalAction=8(action_kind:ActionKindV1,
                 cardinality:FormalActionCardinalityV1,
                 base:QuotientPrimarySubjectActionBaseCandidateV1)
| ReservedQG3SupportProjection=9
| PresentedEnvelope=10(constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
                       evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1)
| NonemptyPublicInterface=11(context_binder:u32)
| SchemeActivation=12(context_binder:u32,
                      grammar_binder:u32,
                      scheme_binder:u32)
| DischargeAtSpecialization=13(context_binder:u32,
                               grammar_binder:u32,
                               scheme_binder:u32,
                               activation_binder:u32)

QuotientPrimarySubjectActionBaseCandidateV1 ::=
  SourceCandidate=0
| PriorActionResult=1(action_binder:u32)

QuotientPrimarySubjectBinderCandidateV1 =
  (sort:QuotientPrimarySubjectBinderSortCandidateV1).
```

Reserved tags `0`, `2`, `3`, and `9` are valid schema variants only so the sum
is structurally lawful. They are invalid compiler values and abort if decoded
in a completed primary family. In particular tag 9 is never interpreted as a
support binder. QG3 uses separate support/action subject schemas.

Every ordinal in a nonreserved binder payload points strictly backward after
checked global relocation and selects the exact required sort. The result
type table is:

```text
DependentContext                -> NormalizedContext
Substitution                    -> NormalizedSubstitution
Occurrence                      -> Occurrence
HistoryRenaming                 -> HistoryRenamingRef
FormalAction                    -> NormalizedAction
PresentedEnvelope               -> EnvelopeObjectDescriptor
NonemptyPublicInterface         -> NormalizedTelescope
SchemeActivation                -> NormalizedSection
DischargeAtSpecialization       -> NormalizedDischarge.
```

For `Carrier(index)`, exact carrier-body results by
`SubjectCarrierIndexV1` tag are:

```text
PublicContext        -> Unit
PublicInterface      -> NormalizedTelescope
InterfaceFamily      -> NormalizedFamily
Substitution         -> NormalizedSubstitution
ComparisonWitness   -> NormalizedComparison
SealedPublicGrammar -> NormalizedGrammar
DemandScheme        -> NormalizedScheme
LiveDemand          -> NormalizedLiveDemand
Discharge           -> NormalizedDischarge.
```

Only `SealedPublicGrammar` and `DemandScheme` occur in current primary
families. The complete table prevents arbitrary typing of a malformed other
tag before rejection.

The formal-action mirror is fresh and TypeId-free:

```text
QuotientFormalActionComponentRefCandidateV1 ::=
  SourceProjection=0(
    QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| PrimitiveArgument=1(primitive_ordinal:u32)
| EarlierDerived=2(derived_component_ordinal:u32)

QuotientFormalActionDerivedComponentCandidateV1 ::=
  IteratedLift=0(
    primitive_ordinal:u32,
    target:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1,
    lift_depth:u16)
| OrderedComposite=1(
    left:QuotientFormalActionComponentRefCandidateV1,
    right:QuotientFormalActionComponentRefCandidateV1)
| ReconstructedEpsilon=2
| ReindexedPayloadComponent=3(
    source:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint=4

QuotientFormalActionProjectionRootCandidateV1 ::=
  NormalizedAction=0
| PrimitiveArgument=1(primitive_ordinal:u32)
| DerivedComponent=2(
    component_ordinal:u32,
    component:QuotientFormalActionDerivedComponentCandidateV1)

QuotientFormalActionProjectionPathCandidateV1 =
  (action_binder:u32,
   root:QuotientFormalActionProjectionRootCandidateV1,
   result_type:QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1).
```

Every emitted action projection selects a complete primitive or virtual
component, so no generic step vector exists. A descendant projection is not
part of the item-4 grammar. `EarlierDerived(j)` requires `j<i`; a source path
inside a component is translated at its exact item-3 occurrence locator.

The primary literal sum retains emitted R2c tags and has no support branch:

```text
QuotientPrimaryCoreProjectionLiteralCandidateV1 ::=
  CorePayloadProjection=0(
    path:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| ReservedCarrierPayloadProjection=1
| ExactOccurrenceProjection=2(
    path:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| ReservedNormalizedOccurrenceProjection=3
| FormalActionArgumentProjection=4(
    path:QuotientFormalActionProjectionPathCandidateV1).
```

Tags 1 and 3 are never emitted and abort in a complete value. Terminal R2c
tag 5 is absent rather than reserved: the sum is already contiguous through
tag 4. Thus `PureSupportFunctionProjection` and its result type are not in the
transitive quotient-primary graph.

The new semantic `Project` payload is defined next. Node and container schemas
are:

```text
QuotientPrimarySubjectNodeCandidateV1 ::=
  Binder=0(binder_ordinal:u32)
| Literal=1(QuotientPrimaryCoreProjectionLiteralCandidateV1)
| Project=2(source_node:u32,

| Normalize=3(source_node:u32)
| Reindex=4(value_node:u32,action_node:u32)
| Compose=5(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| RenameHistory=6(value_node:u32,renaming_node:u32)
| Selector=7(value_node:u32)
| Origin=8(value_node:u32,origin_path:OriginPathV1)
| ApproxOrd=9(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| ApproxEnvelope=10(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| Equality=11(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| Membership=12(element_node:u32,set_node:u32)
| Conjunction=13(conjunct_nodes:vector<u32>)
| Implication=14(premise_node:u32,conclusion_node:u32)
| ExistsUnique=15(binder_ordinal:u32,
                  domain_node:u32,predicate_node:u32)
| DependentDischargeEquality=16(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)

QuotientSubjectEndpointProgramCandidateV1 =
  (subject_kind:SubjectRootKindV1,
   binders:vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectBinderCandidateV1>,
   value_nodes:vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectNodeCandidateV1>,

     vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1>)

QuotientSubjectRootProjectionCandidateV1 =
  (constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
   source_bundle:QuotientSubjectProjectionSourceBundleCandidateV1,
   subjects:vector<QuotientSubjectEndpointProgramCandidateV1>)

QuotientSubjectSyntaxCandidateV1 =
  (binders:vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectBinderCandidateV1>,
   nodes:vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectNodeCandidateV1>,
   root_node:u32)

QuotientEnvelopeSubjectsCandidateV1 =
  (syntax:QuotientSubjectSyntaxCandidateV1,
   root_projection:QuotientSubjectRootProjectionCandidateV1).
```

`Membership=12` is retained as a safe logical opcode but is not emitted by the
closed templates. No type above contains a construction transcript, span,
receipt, replay, resource account, checked wrapper, raw action, support value,
representative, or numerical type ID.

#### 10.10.3 Finite semantic projections and square subjects

An R2c `EnvelopeFieldPathV1` is not meaningful against the quotient descriptor:
the latter has neither `Output` nor `RawRealization` as a structural child.
The quotient compiler therefore uses this complete nine-role semantic plan:

```text
QuotientSubjectSemanticProjectionRoleCandidateV1 ::=
  FormationOutput=0
| AbstractionOutput=1
| AggregationOutput=2
| TransportOutput=3
| ComparisonOutput=4
| DemandCompilerOutput=5
| DischargeTransformerOutput=6
| TransportNormalizedImplementation=7
| DischargeTransformerNormalizedImplementation=8

QuotientSubjectSemanticProjectionPlanCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientSubjectSemanticProjectionRoleCandidateV1,
   result_type:QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1).
```

For a source node denoting descriptor `X`, let `Q=X.canonical_payload`.
Decoding the role is exactly:

| Role | Payload tag | Reconstructed value | Result |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `FormationOutput` | 0 | `Q.a` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `AbstractionOutput` | 1 | `NormalizedFamilyValueV1(Q.gamma,Q.a,Q.b)` | `NormalizedFamily` |
| `AggregationOutput` | 2 | `ConcatTelescope(Q.a,Q.b)` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `TransportOutput` | 3 | `ReindexTelescope(Q.theta,Q.a)` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `ComparisonOutput` | 4 | `Q.comparison` | `NormalizedComparison` |
| `DemandCompilerOutput` | 5 | `Q.live` | `NormalizedLiveDemand` |
| `DischargeTransformerOutput` | 6 | `ReindexDischargeEndpoint(Q.theta,Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live,Q.discharge).discharge` | `NormalizedDischarge` |
| `TransportNormalizedImplementation` | 3 | `Q.implementation_c` | `NormalizedTelescope` |
| `DischargeTransformerNormalizedImplementation` | 6 | `Q.implementation_discharge` | `NormalizedDischarge` |

The final two roles are distinct plan values. For an unacted base descriptor,
Q2 construction has already checked its stored implementation bytes against the
independently reconstructed nominal output; the projection does not repeat or
encode that proof. For a formal reindexed descriptor, the two projections return
the independently rebuilt values without an equality guard: their comparison is
the naturality subject being constructed. The final two roles are quotient
mirrors of the two emitted R2c raw-realization projections and contain no raw
path or checked wrapper. `Out_c(X)` uses role `c` in the first seven rows.
`W_c(X)` exists only for `c=3,6` and uses row 7 or 8. An incorrect role, payload
tag, or symbolic result aborts. No step vector, generic record descent, support-
local path, other constructor-role pair, or projection-time naturality premise
is accepted.

The missing square-component results reuse the neutral receipt-free subject
leaves frozen by the R2c C2 corrigendum:

```text
SubjectNormalizedSubstitutionEqualityV1
SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1
SubjectIndexedDischargeEndpointV1
SubjectCarrierIndexEqualityV1
SubjectHeterogeneousDischargeEqualityV1
SubjectTransportSquareEndpointV1
SubjectDischargeSquareEndpointV1.
```

They are proposition descriptors, not equality proofs. This reuse introduces
no R2c-to-R2d dependency and no duplicate registry type.

For square primitive `(rho,sigma,theta_prime)` over payload substitution
`theta`, derive:

```text
LC = QCompose(theta,rho)
RC = QCompose(sigma,theta_prime)
epsilon = SubjectNormalizedSubstitutionEqualityV1(
  left=LC.result,right=RC.result).
```

Both composition descriptors are independently reconstructed by the exact
section-10.2.1 operation before their results populate `epsilon`. `epsilon`
denotes `LC.result approx_Sub RC.result`; it neither stores nor claims a receipt.
Admissibility of an instantiated square requires that relation, and the later
action-rebuild operation checks it independently. Item 4 constructs only its
formal subject descriptor.

For Transport, retain the reindexed implementation, both nested actions, both
direct-composite actions, all four adjacent comparison subjects, and the end-
to-end naturality subject:

```text
require rho.delta = theta_prime.delta = LC.result.delta = RC.result.delta
base_context = LC.result.delta

theta_stage    = ReindexTelescope(theta,Q.a)
sigma_stage    = ReindexTelescope(sigma,Q.a)
reindexed_implementation = ReindexTelescope(rho,Q.implementation_c)
nested_left    = ReindexTelescope(rho,theta_stage)
composite_left = ReindexTelescope(LC.result,Q.a)
composite_right = ReindexTelescope(RC.result,Q.a)
nested_right   = ReindexTelescope(theta_prime,sigma_stage)

transport_square = SubjectTransportSquareEndpointV1(
  epsilon=epsilon,
  reindexed_implementation=reindexed_implementation,
  nested_left=nested_left,
  composite_left=composite_left,
  composite_right=composite_right,
  nested_right=nested_right,
  implementation_agreement=SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1(
    base_context,reindexed_implementation,nested_left),
  left_association=SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1(
    base_context,nested_left,composite_left),
  epsilon_congruence=SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1(
    base_context,composite_left,composite_right),
  right_association=SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1(
    base_context,composite_right,nested_right),
  naturality=SubjectNormalizedTelescopeEqualityV1(
    base_context,reindexed_implementation,nested_right)).
```

For DischargeTransformer, the discharge value alone is not enough to determine
its dependent target. Define the receipt-free indexed action, for
`t:Delta->Gamma`, explicitly from the complete source tuple:

```text
ReindexDischargeEndpoint(t,Gamma,V,S,a,L,d) =
  require t.gamma = Gamma;
  V_prime = ReindexGrammar(t,V);
  S_prime = ReindexScheme(t,S);
  t_E = QIteratedLift(t,V.exports).result;
  a_prime = TermwiseReindexSection(t_E,a,S_prime.trigger);
  L_prime = Specialize(t.delta,V_prime,S_prime,a_prime);
  d_prime = NormalizedDischargeValueV1(
    section=TermwiseReindexSection(
      t_E,d.section,L_prime.demanded_telescope));
  CheckDischarge(t.delta,V_prime,L_prime,d_prime);
  return SubjectIndexedDischargeEndpointV1(
    index=CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex(
      gamma=t.delta,
      grammar=V_prime,
      scheme=S_prime,
      activation=a_prime,
      live=L_prime),
    discharge=d_prime)

ReindexDischargeEndpointAgain(t,x) =
  require x.index is CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex(
    gamma,V,S,a,L);
  ReindexDischargeEndpoint(t,gamma,V,S,a,L,x.discharge)

theta_stage = ReindexDischargeEndpoint(
  theta,Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live,Q.discharge)
sigma_stage = ReindexDischargeEndpoint(
  sigma,Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live,Q.discharge)
implementation_stage = SubjectIndexedDischargeEndpointV1(
  index=theta_stage.index,
  discharge=Q.implementation_discharge)
require implementation_stage.index is
  CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex(gamma_i,V_i,S_i,a_i,L_i)
CheckDischarge(
  gamma_i,V_i,L_i,implementation_stage.discharge)
reindexed_implementation =
  ReindexDischargeEndpointAgain(rho,implementation_stage)
nested_left = ReindexDischargeEndpointAgain(rho,theta_stage)
composite_left = ReindexDischargeEndpoint(
  LC.result,Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live,Q.discharge)
composite_right = ReindexDischargeEndpoint(
  RC.result,Q.gamma,Q.grammar,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live,Q.discharge)
nested_right = ReindexDischargeEndpointAgain(theta_prime,sigma_stage)

HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(x,y) =
  SubjectHeterogeneousDischargeEqualityV1(
    left=x,
    right=y,
    index_equality=SubjectCarrierIndexEqualityV1(
      left=x.index,right=y.index))

discharge_square = SubjectDischargeSquareEndpointV1(
  epsilon=epsilon,
  reindexed_implementation=reindexed_implementation,
  nested_left=nested_left,
  composite_left=composite_left,
  composite_right=composite_right,
  nested_right=nested_right,
  implementation_agreement=HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(
    reindexed_implementation,nested_left),
  left_association=HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(
    nested_left,composite_left),
  epsilon_congruence=HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(
    composite_left,composite_right),
  right_association=HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(
    composite_right,nested_right),
  naturality=HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(
    reindexed_implementation,nested_right)).
```

`Specialize` rebuilds the complete demanded telescope and canonical retained
contract set, while `CheckDischarge` rechecks the section and every instantiated
contract. Every one of the five discharge endpoints therefore retains its own
complete dependent index and body. A context/index/type mismatch aborts, but
the compiler does not require any adjacent endpoints or indices to be equal.
Each index pair is retained by its explicit heterogeneous dependent-equality
subject; none is fed to homogeneous `ApproxOrd` unless the indices first become
byte-identical. The four adjacent equalities are precisely the unproved
implementation-agreement, association, and congruence subjects retained by the
descriptor; the fifth proposition field is the unproved end-to-end naturality
subject.

#### 10.10.4 Exact formal-action mirror and symbolic node typing

In naturality slot 7 the sole action binder has global binder ordinal `6`.
For an exactly-one substitution action, primitive 0 is `theta`. For an
exactly-one square action, its sole square primitive is flattened only for
projection as primitive arguments `0=rho`, `1=sigma`, and `2=theta_prime`.
The derived vectors are exactly:

```text
Formation:  []
Comparison: []

Abstraction:
  [IteratedLift(
     0,Translate(AbstractionLiftTargetA),
     checked_u16(Q.a.len))]

Aggregation:
  [IteratedLift(
     0,Translate(AggregationLiftTargetA),
     checked_u16(Q.a.len))]

DemandCompiler:
  [IteratedLift(
     0,Translate(DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports),
     checked_u16(Q.grammar.exports.len))]

Transport:
  [ReconstructedEpsilon,
   OrderedComposite(
     SourceProjection(Translate(TransportCompositeThetaSource)),
     PrimitiveArgument(0)),
   OrderedComposite(PrimitiveArgument(1),PrimitiveArgument(2)),
   EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint]

DischargeTransformer:
  [ReconstructedEpsilon,
   OrderedComposite(
     SourceProjection(Translate(
       DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource)),
     PrimitiveArgument(0)),
   OrderedComposite(PrimitiveArgument(1),PrimitiveArgument(2)),
   ReindexedPayloadComponent(Translate(
     DischargeReindexedGrammarSource)),
   ReindexedPayloadComponent(Translate(
     DischargeReindexedSchemeSource)),
   ReindexedPayloadComponent(Translate(
     DischargeReindexedActivationSource)),
   ReindexedPayloadComponent(Translate(
     DischargeReindexedLiveSource)),
   ReindexedPayloadComponent(Translate(
     DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource(i)))
       for ascending 0<=i<h_6,
   EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint].
```

Every `Translate` is item-3 occurrence-indexed translation at the exact
locator where its component is emitted, not a role-only lookup. Lift depths
use checked exact `u16` conversion and zero-depth lifts remain. Route-bearing
derived-prefix counts are `[0,1,1,1,0,1,5+h_6]`.

Complete action-projection result types are:

```text
NormalizedAction root                    -> NormalizedAction
each primitive                           -> NormalizedSubstitution
IteratedLift                             -> NormalizedSubstitution
OrderedComposite                         -> NormalizedSubstitution
ReconstructedEpsilon                     -> SubstitutionEqualitySubject
reindexed G/S/a/L/contract               ->
  NormalizedGrammar/NormalizedScheme/NormalizedSection/
  NormalizedLiveDemand/NormalizedContract
Transport congruence                     -> TransportCongruenceEndpoint
DischargeTransformer congruence          -> DischargeCongruenceEndpoint.
```

The exact action-argument prefix lengths emitted before naturality endpoints
are `[1,2,2,7,1,2,11+h_6]`.

Node result types are independently inferred; a stored symbolic vector never
chooses them. Closed rules on the compiler image are:

```text
Binder(b)          -> exact BinderType(b)
Literal(p)         -> exact path/result table above
Project(x,p)       -> p.result_type after the nine-role check

Normalize(x)       -> same type for
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor, NormalizedTelescope, NormalizedFamily,
  NormalizedComparison, NormalizedLiveDemand, NormalizedDischarge

Reindex(E,q)       -> EnvelopeObjectDescriptor
Reindex(v,q)       -> the same normalized carrier kind as v
  for the exact constructor/action pair

Compose(q1,q2)     -> NormalizedAction for equal action tags
Compose(s1,s2)     -> NormalizedSubstitution
Compose(context,telescope) -> NormalizedContext
Compose(telescope,telescope) -> the exact template-selected one of
  NormalizedFamily, NormalizedTelescope, NormalizedComparison
Compose(scheme,section) -> NormalizedLiveDemand

RenameHistory(E,h) -> EnvelopeObjectDescriptor
RenameHistory(o,h) -> Occurrence
Selector(E)         -> Occurrence
Origin(E,root_c)    -> Occurrence

ApproxOrd(x,y)      -> PropositionSort
ApproxEnvelope(E,F) -> PropositionSort
Equality(x,y)       -> PropositionSort
Membership(o,U)     -> PropositionSort
Conjunction(ps)     -> PropositionSort
Implication(p,q)    -> PropositionSort
ExistsUnique(b,U,p) -> PropositionSort
DependentDischargeEquality(x,y) -> PropositionSort.
```

`RenameHistory` requires its second operand to be the direct binder value
`SubjectHistoryRenamingRefV1(b)` for the exact earlier sort; a computed or
projected value is invalid. `Origin` requires the constructor's frozen
`OriginPathV1`. Equality operands have identical symbolic type.
`CandidateAdmissionPremise` and `SubstitutionEqualitySubject` are proposition
descriptors and may occur where a premise is required, while logical node
results carry `PropositionSort`. Every operand ordinal is strictly earlier.
The exact template role disambiguates the three telescope/telescope compose
rows; a caller-supplied result tag cannot do so.

`DependentDischargeEquality(x,y)` is admitted only when independent expression
typing derives exact `SubjectIndexedDischargeEndpointV1` values `X,Y` for the
two normalized discharge operands. It denotes
`HeterogeneousDischargeSubject(X,Y)` and neither compares nor requires the two
indices or bodies. In DischargeTransformer slot 7, the derived subject must be
byte-identical to the `naturality` field of that slot's already emitted
`EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint` virtual component. This cross-check binds the outer
clause to the complete five-endpoint square without assuming it.

#### 10.10.5 Exact slots, emission arithmetic, and locators

The compiler maps R2c templates homomorphically: binder order, node opcodes,
left-to-right syntax trees, and proposition order are unchanged; source paths,
action paths, literals, and semantic projections are replaced by the
quotient-primary forms above. Common clauses remain exactly:

| Slot | Binders | Quotient clause |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 0 | empty | `Reindex(E,id_c) approx_env E` |
| 1 | `q1`, then `q2` over `PriorActionResult(q1)` | `Reindex(E,Compose(q1,q2)) approx_env Reindex(Reindex(E,q1),q2)` |
| 2 | `q:Occurrence` | `MatchE -> exists! q in U. Origin(Normalize(E),root_c)=q and q=o and Selector(Normalize(E))=q` |
| 3 | empty | `Selector(Normalize(E))=Selector(E)` |
| 4 | `E':PresentedEnvelope` | `E approx_env E' -> Selector(E)=Selector(E')` |
| 5 | `h:HistoryRenaming` | `Selector(RenameHistory(E,h))=RenameHistory(Selector(E),h)` |
| 6 | arbitrary normalized `q` | `Selector(Reindex(E,q))=Selector(E)` |
| 7 | exactly-one `q` | the constructor row below |

The naturality rows use the nine-role projection plan and remain:

| Constructor | Left | Right |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Formation | `N(O(R(E,q)))` | `N(R(O(E),q))` |
| Abstraction | `N(R(O(E),q))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |
| Aggregation | `N(R(O(E),q))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |
| Transport | `N(W(R(E,q)))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |
| Comparison | `N(R(O(E),q))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |
| DemandCompiler | `N(R(O(E),q))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |
| DischargeTransformer | `N(W(R(E,q)))` | `N(O(R(E,q)))` |

The clause opcode is `ApproxOrd` for the first six rows, including Transport.
For DischargeTransformer it is `DependentDischargeEquality`; its derived
heterogeneous subject is the square component's end-to-end `naturality` field.

Transport and DischargeTransformer alone append slots `8..10`, with the exact
independently repeated R2c binder vectors and `T0~T1`, `T1~T2`, `T2~T3` or
`D0~D1`, `D1~D2`, `D2~D3` clauses. Transport uses `ApproxOrd`; DischargeTransformer
uses `DependentDischargeEquality` so no mismatched dependent index is fed to a
homogeneous relation. Their four endpoints are rebuilt by the same strict-
postorder formulas from R2c subsection 5.3.4. No binder is shared between these
slots and no placeholder is encoded for another constructor.

Common binder counts for slots `0..7` are:

```text
[0,2,1,0,1,1,1,1]
prefixes [0,0,2,3,3,4,5,6]
total 7.
```

Transport's three extra slots each bind eight values and start at global
ordinals `[7,15,23]`, for total `31`. DischargeTransformer's each bind eleven
and start at `[7,18,29]`, for total `40`.

Strict left-to-right postorder with a fresh node for every occurrence gives:

```text
common value counts slots 0..6 = [4,10,12,5,6,8,6]
common value starts            = [0,4,14,26,31,37,45]
slot-7 value start             = 51
slot-7 counts by constructor   = [11,12,12,17,11,12,21+h_6]
each extra-slot value count    = 16.
```

Transport's extra value starts are `[68,84,100]`.
DischargeTransformer's are `[72+h_6,88+h_6,104+h_6]`.
Clause-node counts for common slots are
`[1,1,6,1,3,1,1,1]`, with prefixes
`[0,1,2,8,9,12,13,14]` and local clause-root offsets
`[0,0,5,0,2,0,0,0]`; each extra slot contributes one clause node.

The complete checked census is:

| Constructor | Binders | Value nodes | Clause nodes | Full nodes including conjunction |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Formation | 7 | 62 | 15 | 78 |
| Abstraction | 7 | 63 | 15 | 79 |
| Aggregation | 7 | 63 | 15 | 79 |
| Transport | 31 | 116 | 18 | 135 |
| Comparison | 7 | 62 | 15 | 78 |
| DemandCompiler | 7 | 63 | 15 | 79 |
| DischargeTransformer | 40 | `120+h_6` | 18 | `139+h_6` |

The final conjunction ordinal is `V_c+15` for five eight-slot families and
`V_c+18` for the two eleven-slot families. Every count and addition is checked
in `u64`; every stored binder/node/contract position admits exact `u32`
conversion before reservation or emission, and every slot admits exact `u16`
conversion.

All primary source-path leaves have nested locator ordinal zero. Exact local
value-node locators are:

```text
slot 0: 0:E, 1:id_c, 3:E
slot 1: 0:E, 5:E
slot 2: 0:MatchE, 1:U, 2:E, 7:o, 8:E
slot 3: 0:E, 3:E
slot 4: 0:E, 2:E
slot 5: 0:E, 4:E
slot 6: 0:E, 4:E

slot 7 Formation:
  1:E, 6:E
slot 7 Abstraction:
  1:AbstractionLiftTargetA, 2:E, 7:E
slot 7 Aggregation:
  1:AggregationLiftTargetA, 2:E, 7:E
slot 7 Transport:
  4:TransportCompositeThetaSource, 7:E, 12:E
slot 7 Comparison:
  1:E, 6:E
slot 7 DemandCompiler:

slot 7 DischargeTransformer:
  4:DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource,
  6:G, 7:S, 8:a, 9:L,

  11+h_6:E, 16+h_6:E.
```

Slots `8..10` have empty source-path vectors. These are item-3 local physical
value-node coordinates, not globally relocated operand ordinals stored in
nodes. Every locator consumes exactly the matching occurrence from
`CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan`; rebuilding a path from its role alone is
invalid because equal repeated routes have distinct physical occurrences.

#### 10.10.6 Complete compiler, route partition, and admission theorem

The compiler is:

```text
CompileQPrimaryEndpoint(c,slot,QSourceBundle,Q2) =
  require slot is valid for c;
  let image = CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(c,Q2)[slot];
  emit the exact binder template with checked global relocation;
  emit the exact value template by strict postorder;
  at each source-path locator:
    consume the next exact image occurrence;
    emit TranslateQPrimaryOccurrence(QSourceBundle,occurrence);
  infer every symbolic node type independently;
  require stored value_node_types equal inferred value-node types;
  require image is consumed exactly once with no extra path;
  require the emitted ordered path vector equals
    map(RoutePlan,QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][slot]);
  return the endpoint program.

CompileQPrimarySubjects(c,Q1,Q2,QLineage,QFootprints,QObligations) =
  require QObligations = CompileQObligations(...);
  let bundle = CompileQSourceBundle(...,QObligations);
  let programs =
    [CompileQPrimaryEndpoint(c,slot,bundle,Q2)
     | slot in the exact valid ascending slot range];
  require programs[slot].subject_kind = SubjectRootKindV1(slot);
  require every program matches the corresponding group-7
    SubjectWellFormed slot and root-kind row one-for-one;
  let root_projection =
    QuotientSubjectRootProjectionCandidateV1(c,bundle,programs);
  scan only root_projection.subjects and require the exact ordered result
    QPrimaryRouteCensus(c) and all three group-5 vectors;
  let syntax.binders = concatenate every program binder vector in slot order;
  let syntax.nodes = concatenate every globally relocated value-node block in
    slot order, then every proposition suffix in slot order, then one
    Conjunction of clause roots in slot order;
  let syntax.root_node = checked_u32(V_c+C_c);
  require syntax.root_node selects the final Conjunction;
  infer and validate every proposition-suffix and final-Conjunction node by the
    section-10.10.4 symbolic typing rules;
  require syntax's initial value block is byte-identical to the
    concatenation of root_projection endpoint value nodes;
  return QuotientEnvelopeSubjectsCandidateV1(syntax,root_projection).
```

The endpoint-program scan, and only that scan, is item 3's canonical route
domain. The byte-identical syntax prefix is a required copy but is not rescanned
or added to group 5. Let

```text
PR_c = [20,21,21,21,20,21,25+h_6].
```

A typed traversal of the complete item-4 value partitions every embedded
`QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1` occurrence as:

```text
3 * PR_c  in the three group-5 preservation rows inside QObligations
1 * PR_c  in root_projection.subjects
1 * PR_c  in the byte-identical syntax value prefix
0         everywhere else.
```

Thus full transitive totals are
`[100,105,105,105,100,105,125+5h_6]`. Equal paths are not deduplicated,
interned, or common-subexpression eliminated. Semantic projection plans are a
distinct type and do not enter this census.

The completed `QuotientEnvelopeSubjectsCandidateV1` contains the source bundle
exactly once, as `root_projection.source_bundle`. Syntax contains descriptors
and path plans only. A typed value-schema traversal must find
`QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` values at exactly:

```text
root_projection.source_bundle.selector_inputs.match_premise
root_projection.source_bundle.source_candidate.match_premise.
```

The two values are byte-identical to item 3's `A`. There is no bundle per
program, standalone emitted projection, construction transcript, syntax-side
bundle, or group-7 back-edge. A symbolic result tag or path that denotes the
admission schema is not an embedded admission value. Consequently the full
family, not merely its bundle, has exactly two physical admission values.

#### 10.10.7 Aborts, independence, phase boundary, and closure

Construction aborts atomically on any item-2 or item-3 mismatch; constructor,
payload, slot, root-kind, group-7, action-kind, cardinality, base, binder,
component, component-result, projection-role, symbolic-type, operand, source-
image, locator, route, repeat, count, prefix, node, clause, conjunction, syntax-
prefix, or admission-census mismatch. It also aborts on a nonbackward or
cross-slot reference; a reserved binder/literal tag; `Membership` in a
completed template; a nonempty formal-action descendant path; a generic or
off-image source path; any overflow or inexact `u16`/`u32` conversion;
allocation failure; or failed canonical decode/re-encode equality. No partial
program or family is returned.

No support binder or literal, support seed/subject, support-local projection,
computed support, action image/trace/key, checked action, raw presentation,
raw code/ordinal, source/owner ordinal, representative, evidence, receipt,
replay, transcript, span, resource account, numerical TypeId, Genesis label,
held-out result, `Sem`/SR2 result, `nu`, `GCap` disposition, or `gamma` may
select or alter a compiler value. Retained normalized implementation values
and provenance paths already present in closed quotient coordinates remain
lawful data; they do not authorize a raw path.

For successful representatives `E,E'` with equal complete retained quotient
coordinates:

```text
CompileQObligations(E) = CompileQObligations(E')
CompileQSourceBundle(E) = CompileQSourceBundle(E')
CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(E) =
  CompileR2cPrimaryPathImagePlan(E')
CompileQPrimarySubjects(E) = CompileQPrimarySubjects(E').
```

Encoded candidate equalities are ordered full canonical-byte equalities;
compiler controls are exact structural equalities. Representative-side
validation may later make R2e abort but cannot add, delete, reorder, or choose
a subject component.

R2e must assign all nonzero type IDs, reconstruct the supplied representative's
physical R2c programs, verify the R2c corrigendum paths and every quotient
symbolic lowering, and perform the implementation-source join. R4 must
regenerate fixtures and reject slot, binder, node, component, projection-role,
path, multiplicity, type, syntax-prefix, and admission-copy mutations. QG3
alone adds support/action subject mirrors; it may not retroactively change this
primary family.

The exact 8/11 slot compiler, formal-action vectors, nine semantic projection
roles, two square-subject schemas, node typing, arithmetic, locator table,
one-domain route scan, full `5*PR_c` path partition, and transitive two-copy
admission theorem have passed independent audits. QG2 item 4 is closed at the
compiler-plan level. QG2 is complete; the active R2d frontier moves to QG3's
semantic support/disposition and action-subject mirrors required by the
generative-capacity representation.

### 10.11 QG3-S1a projection inventory, seven-root boundary, and disposition mirror — CLOSED

This cut closes the projection decision and the ordinary field-disposition
mirror. It does **not** yet close the complete quotient-support compiler.
In particular, S1b2's finite candidate symbolic table and exact direct seven-
root traversal, S1b3's candidate owner graph and closure, and the later formal-
action locus remain separate QG3 subgates. This distinction is necessary:
`OrdinarySupportV1` cannot be turned into a quotient value by deleting receipts.
Its paths, owner alignment, structural type identities, and coverage
coordinates are representative-side values and must be recompiled over the
quotient schema.

#### 10.11.1 Exact R2c field disposition

The complete `OrdinarySupportV1` audit is:

| R2c material | Quotient disposition |
| --- | --- |
| `constructor` | retain semantically, reconstructed as `Q1.constructor` |
| `subject_root_projection` | reconstruct as the closed QG2 quotient root projection; never copy R2c syntax |
| `principal_lineage` | use `QLineage.principal` as owner-resolution context; do not repeat it in group 6 |
| `field_lineages` | use `QLineage.fields` as owner-resolution context; do not repeat them in group 6 |
| `roots` | no image; replace by the seven semantic compiler roots below |
| `coverage` | no record erasure; rebuild the fresh semantic graph, owner map, classes, and coverage |
| `projection` | rebuild from fresh semantic support keys |
| `footprint_agreement` | retain a fresh receipt-free role-distinct copy, independently recomputed |
| `preservation_subjects` | semantic claim survives, but the three subjects require fresh QG3 syntax in the next cut |
| `resource_account` | forbidden R3 material |

The nested `SupportCoverageV1` audit is:

| R2c coverage field | Quotient disposition |
| --- | --- |
| `shape_slots`, `resolved_direct_slots` | procedural evidence; no image |
| `root_counts` | the node/shape/resolution certificate has no image; replace it by an exact partition of fresh semantic dependencies by quotient root |
| `raw_dependencies` | rebuild as fresh semantic dependencies |
| `closure_worklist` | procedural evidence; omit it while retaining every rebuilt closure dependency and edge |
| `owner_map` | rebuild exact semantic owners; erase raw ordinals and owner-resolution hop paths |
| `support_classes` | rebuild by sorting fresh complete semantic keys |
| `raw_to_class` | replace by a fresh semantic-dependency-to-class map |
| `all_resolved` | redundant certificate; no image |
| `shape_cardinality`, `resolution_cardinality` | forbidden R3 receipts |
| `resource_account` | forbidden R3 material |

Within each `RawDependencyV1`, `raw_ordinal` and
`owner_resolution_path` have no image. The edge enum itself is semantic and
safe to reuse, but the containing path, referent, judgment, owner, and support
key are not. `FullLocalPathV1` names the R2c root universe;
`ExpectedJudgmentV1::StructuralRecord` carries a numerical `TypeIdV1`; and
`BinderKeyV1` and `FormalActionIdV1` point into the R2c subject-path universe.
Consequently every one of those enclosing quotient types must be fresh.

There is therefore no lawful function

```text
DropReceipts : OrdinarySupportV1 -> quotient support.
```

The lawful direction consumes the already projected Q1--Q5 and QG2 semantic
coordinates and never reads or copies `E.structural_support`. Slot `9[0]`
still consumes the admitted rank-free R2c representative from which those
coordinates are projected. R2e/R4 later cross-check the independently rebuilt
support against the representative-side support and physical path bytes.

#### 10.11.2 Seven semantic support roots

The quotient support source universe is exactly:

```text
QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CanonicalPayload=0
| IndexedInputs=1
| IndexedOutput=2
| PublicFields=3
| Obligations=4
| PrimarySubjects=5
| ActionArguments=6
```

Its compiler-only frame is:

```text
QuotientSupportRootFramePlanCandidateV1 =
  (canonical_payload:
     QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   indexed_inputs:IndexedInputsV1,
   indexed_output:IndexedOutputV1,
    public_fields:vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>,
    obligations:QuotientObligationsCandidateV1,
    primary_program_skeleton:
      vector<QuotientSubjectEndpointProgramCandidateV1>,
    action_arguments:QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1).
```

For the already closed candidate coordinates:

```text
frame.canonical_payload = Q2.canonical_payload
frame.indexed_inputs    = Q2.indexed_inputs
frame.indexed_output    = Q2.indexed_output
frame.public_fields     = Q2.normalized_public_fields
frame.obligations       = QObligations
frame.primary_program_skeleton =
  CompileSupportPrimaryProgramSkeleton(Q1,Q2)

I(c) = QContextSquareIdentity
       when c in {Transport,DischargeTransformer}
I(c) = QSubstitutionIdentity
       when c in {Formation,Abstraction,Aggregation,
                  Comparison,DemandCompiler}

candidate frame.action_arguments = I(c).
```

Root 5 is the footprint-erased endpoint-program skeleton at the exact physical
coordinates of the closed `root_projection.subjects`. It contains no combined
`syntax` or source bundle. Subsection 10.13.3 fixes its compiler and finite
source-route evaluator. During S1b3, `Q1` authenticates
the private occurrence graph/evaluator, `QLineage` supplies exact raw-path-to-
`SourceRefV1` provenance, and that erased frame supplies typed structural
resolution context. `QFootprints` is only the independently postcomputed
equality target; it may not select an owner, add closure, supply a referent, or
enter a resolver sidecar. None of these contexts is an additional support root.

Root 6 is always present as a same-tag normalized action value. A candidate and
its total identity action therefore have the same tagged empty primitive vector
and the same seven-root shape. A nonempty action later supplies the one complete
flattened normalized vector under the same constructor-fixed tag. An optional
root, repeated action roots, a mixed action kind, or a stepwise action tree is
invalid.

The first four roots deliberately preserve their distinct group-2 semantic
roles even when equal normalized values repeat. Root traversal is fixed as
`0..6`. No `RawCode` root, checked wrapper, raw action proposal, or
representative subject program has a quotient-support path.

The frame is an input to the compiler, not a duplicate value stored in group 6.
Group 6 stores only the semantic graph/projection, its receipt-free footprint
agreement, and—after S1b3 and the subsequent preservation cut—the three
separate preservation subjects.

#### 10.11.3 Fresh graph ABI and the remaining compiler subgate

These leaves and closed enums may be reused:

- exact current/older/global identities and normalized term/carrier values;
- `RawDependencyEdgeV1`, whose payload contains no raw value;
- `ReconstructionTagV1`, `NormalizationRuleV1`, and
  `ParameterOrResultV1`; and
- `DefinitionRulePathV1`.

The quotient path, symbolic result schema, binder/formal-action locator,
referent, expected judgment, current-owner location, owner, and support key
must all be fresh. Section 10.12 closes the corrected path/locus shape, the
symbolic-schema generator, and the expected-judgment case family; S1b2 must
freeze the generated finite candidate symbolic manifest. The other exact
finite sums remain later QG3 work. Candidate values reject every formal-action referent/owner/key until
the fresh formal locus is frozen; QG3 may not reuse or reinterpret
`SubjectPathV1`.

The outer graph ABI, including the subsection-10.12 virtual-locus corrigendum,
is now fixed:

```text
QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalCandidateV1 = newtype u32
QuotientSupportClassOrdinalCandidateV1      = newtype u32

QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Direct=0(
    steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| Dereferenced=1(
    descriptor_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| PrimaryProject=2(
    project_node_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| PrimaryProjectGrammar=3(
    project_node_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)

QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   locus:QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1,
   result_schema:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportDependencyPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalCandidateV1,
   path:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1,
   referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanCandidateV1,
   expected_judgment:
     QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportOwnerMapEntryPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalCandidateV1,
   owner:QuotientSupportOwnerPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportClassEntryPlanCandidateV1 =
  (class_ordinal:QuotientSupportClassOrdinalCandidateV1,
   key:QuotientSupportKeyPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportDependencyClassEntryPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalCandidateV1,
   class_ordinal:QuotientSupportClassOrdinalCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportRootCoveragePlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   dependency_ordinals:
     vector<QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalCandidateV1>)

QuotientSupportCoveragePlanCandidateV1 =
  (roots:vector<QuotientSupportRootCoveragePlanCandidateV1>,
   dependency_to_class:
     vector<QuotientSupportDependencyClassEntryPlanCandidateV1>)

QuotientSupportProjectionPlanCandidateV1 =
  (keys:vector<QuotientSupportKeyPlanCandidateV1>)

QuotientSupportGraphPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependencies:vector<QuotientSupportDependencyPlanCandidateV1>,
   owner_map:vector<QuotientSupportOwnerMapEntryPlanCandidateV1>,
   classes:vector<QuotientSupportClassEntryPlanCandidateV1>,
   coverage:QuotientSupportCoveragePlanCandidateV1,
   projection:QuotientSupportProjectionPlanCandidateV1).
```

The two new ordinals are canonical coordinates of the reconstructed semantic
graph. They are not R2c raw ordinals or source ordinals and are never copied
from a representative. Their equations are:

```text
for every checked u32 i:
  dependencies[i].dependency_ordinal = i
  owner_map[i].dependency_ordinal    = i

K_i =
  QuotientSupportKey(
    owner_map[i].owner,
    dependencies[i].expected_judgment)

classes =
  enumerate_with_checked_class_ordinals(
    sort_unique([K_i | i in dependencies]))

dependency_to_class[i] =
  (dependency_ordinal=i,
   class_ordinal=the unique j with classes[j].key=K_i)

projection.keys = map(.key,classes).
```

Until the final formal key constructor and shared encoding close, the
candidate `sort_unique` above uses the semantic order
`Current < Older < Structural`, followed by complete payload-byte order within
each case. It does not sort by an as-yet-unfrozen outer sum tag. The eventual
shared comparator must extend this restriction exactly.

Direct dependencies are emitted in root order, quotient value-traversal
preorder, and the frozen local-edge order. The closure suffix is then rebuilt
in canonical complete-occurrence order. Repeated semantic dependencies remain
repeated. `coverage.roots` has exactly tags `0..6`; each ordinal vector is
strictly ascending; the seven vectors are disjoint; their union is exactly
`[0,dependencies.len)`; and an ordinal occurs in row `r` exactly when its
dependency path has root `r`. `dependency_to_class` is total and has the same
length and order as `dependencies`.

“Owner-independent” means independent of arena positions, raw dependency
ordinals, and owner-resolution hop transcripts. It does not mean owner-erased.
A3 requires each class key to retain its exact semantic
current/older/structural/formal owner and complete expected judgment.

The receipt-free footprint agreement shape is:

