```text
QuotientSupportFootprintAgreementPlanCandidateV1 =
  (current_projection:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   older_projection:ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1,
   output_projection:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1).
```

The current and older projections are independently recomputed from the fresh
owner map. The output projection is independently recomputed from the final
quotient output lineage, not by filtering whichever graph rows have the
`IndexedOutput` root. The three values must equal, respectively,
`QFootprints.implementation`, `QFootprints.prior_support`, and
`QFootprints.current_output`.

This outer ABI closes the coordinate and coverage decision, but no final
`QuotientSemanticSupportCandidateV1` is declared at this cut. After the
section-10.12 S1b1 closure, QG3 must still close:

1. the exact typed traversal/path compiler, including public-field,
   obligation-dereference, and primary-subject traversal, together with the
   generated finite symbolic-type manifest;
2. the candidate-restricted referent/owner/key subdomain and no-formal graph;
3. normalization semantics for the fresh preservation subject's
   `NormalizedSeed` endpoint;
4. output-footprint recomputation for a nonidentity action result; and
5. the unique quotient formal-action subject locus used by action support.

The first item is the immediate S1b2 frontier; item 2 is S1b3. Their no-formal
candidate subdomain may close before item 5, but the final shared binder,
referent, owner, and key sums may not: their formal payload is fixed only after
the unique action locus closes. Items 3--5 join the subsequent preservation/
action cut. Guessing a QG2 node ordinal, copying an R2c `TypeIdV1`, appending a
sum variant after freeze, or installing a payload-free reserved formal tag is
invalid.

#### 10.11.4 Closed ordinary field-disposition mirror

The ordinary replacement grammar has exactly zero rules. Its quotient
classification therefore retains the field and the semantic branch result,
but none of the negative-search certificate:

```text
QuotientPublicFieldDispositionKindCandidateV1 ::=
  ReservedDerived=0
| Primitive=1

QuotientPublicFieldDispositionEntryCandidateV1 =
  (field:NormalizedPublicFieldV1,
   disposition:QuotientPublicFieldDispositionKindCandidateV1)

QuotientPublicFieldDispositionsCandidateV1 =
  (entries:vector<QuotientPublicFieldDispositionEntryCandidateV1>).
```

`ReservedDerived=0` preserves the R2c discriminant assignment, but is not a
valid completed ordinary-V1 value. `Primitive=1` has no payload. Let
`F=Q2.normalized_public_fields`. The compiler is exactly:

```text
CompileQFieldDispositions(Q1,Q2):
  require Q1 is bound to ordinary profile V1 and its exact empty grammar;
  require the closed Q1/Q2 constructor and field-census equations;
  require F[i].field_ordinal = checked_u32(i) for every i;
  return
    (entries=
      [(field=F[i],disposition=Primitive)
       | i in the exact Q2 field order]).
```

That order is the frozen
`Indices ++ Inputs ++ Output ++ RawRealization` public-field order. Equal
normalized judgments at distinct field paths remain distinct; there is no sort
or deduplication.

For an admitted R2c representative with disposition vector `D`, R2e/R4 later
checks:

```text
D.entries.len = F.len
D.entries[i].field = F[i]
D.entries[i].disposition = Primitive(C_i)
C_i.field = F[i]

EraseDisposition(D).entries[i] = (F[i],Primitive)
EraseDisposition(D) = CompileQFieldDispositions(Q1,Q2).
```

The quotient result is nevertheless built only from `Q1,Q2`. The duplicate
certificate field, lower public boundary, profile identity, grammar version,
zero rule count, empty attempted-rule vector, `search_completed=True`, and
fixed completeness-rule tag are independently implied or checked and then
omitted. The empty-enumeration receipt, resource account, typing replays,
replacement evidence, and search transcript are forbidden.

Wrong profile or grammar identity; a Q1/Q2 constructor mismatch; a malformed
field ordinal/path/judgment; a missing, extra, reordered, or deduplicated
entry; tag 0; any payload on `Primitive`; any `Derived` representative value;
overflow; allocation failure; or canonical decode/re-encode mismatch aborts.
An incomplete search or resource failure never constructs `Primitive`.

For an action result, group 7 is rebuilt from the final action-side `Q2`; it is
not inherited from the base candidate. Identity gives literal equality.
Every tag remains `Primitive`, while a nonempty action may change the retained
normalized fields.

This classification means only “no replacement exists in the pinned empty
ordinary field-replacement grammar.” It asserts no absolute primitiveness,
semantic novelty, present underivability, cubical depth, paid support,
generative provenance, SR2 result, `nu`, `GCap` disposition, or preferred
historical continuation.

#### 10.11.5 Action-shape correction and phase boundary

The earlier shorthand that a nonidentity action “differs only at the
lineage/action-subject positions” is false if read as a byte-difference
restriction. A3-O `CanonicalActionResult` requires a nonempty action to rebuild
the normalized realization, fields, obligations, endpoints, support,
footprints, and dispositions. Those common-group bytes may therefore change.

The correct invariant is structural:

- candidate and action use one identical eight-group schema with no outer
  candidate/action sum tag;
- the only additional top-level action-owned fields are group 4's
  `action_introduced` vector and group 8's optional instantiated action
  subject;
- the total flattened identity action sets those positions to `[]` and
  `None` and reproduces all eight candidate groups byte-for-byte; and
- a nonempty total action rebuilds every common position from the original
  base and the one complete flattened action, then populates the two
  action-owned positions. No other action-only field is admitted.

Thus “same shape” does not mean “only two byte ranges may differ.” The plan
ledger is corrected to use the structural statement.

Writing `QAction(B,A)` for deterministic reconstruction from original base
`B` and total normalized action `A`, identity is:

```text
QAction(B,[])      = QCandidate(B)
QAction(B,A ++ []) = QAction(B,A).
```

The second equation matters for an existing nonempty image: appending a new
identity action preserves that image's nonempty lineage, nested action-support
values, and `Some(action_subject)`. It does not reset the result to the base
candidate.

This cut adds no field or path to the frozen QG2 primary family. Its 8/11 slot
census, exactly two admission values, and exact `5*PR_c` route-copy partition
remain byte-for-byte unchanged. QG3 remains active; root `0xfd` and the final
shared candidate/action projection remain unmaterialized.

### 10.12 QG3-S1b1 typed locus and symbolic-universe kernel — CLOSED

The first S1b audit finds one necessary correction to subsection 10.11.3 and
then closes the schema-naming and expected-judgment decisions on which the
exact traversal depends. It does not yet claim that the no-formal traversal,
its finite symbolic-type manifest, or the candidate support graph is closed.

#### 10.12.1 Typed virtual locus corrigendum

The former path shape `(root,steps,result_schema)` was sufficient only for a
value physically nested below one of the seven roots. It was not sufficient
for either of the two required virtual traversal forms:

- an obligation or primary-subject descriptor selects a semantic value from a
  rebuilt quotient view or the primary source bundle; and
- a primary `Project` node constructs a semantic value from an earlier node
  and one of the nine frozen projection roles.

Subsection 10.11.3 therefore now uses the four-way
`QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1` displayed there. Its semantics are
exactly:

1. `Direct(steps)` decodes `steps` from the selected root value and reaches
   the dependency locus by an ordinary typed physical walk.
2. `Dereferenced(descriptor_steps,reached_steps)` first decodes
   `descriptor_steps` from the selected root and requires the reached value to
   have exactly one of these two nominal schemas:

   ```text
   QuotientObligationSourcePathCandidateV1
   QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1.
   ```

   The physical descriptor type selects the already frozen obligation-view or
   subject-source-bundle resolver. The resolver evaluates that descriptor at
   this occurrence; `reached_steps` then walks inside the selected semantic
   value.
3. `PrimaryProject(project_node_steps,reached_steps)` decodes
   `project_node_steps` to one exact root-5 `Project` value node, evaluates its
   checked earlier-node reference and exact nine-role semantic projection,
   and then walks `reached_steps` inside the reconstructed result.
4. `PrimaryProjectGrammar(project_node_steps,reached_steps)` decodes that same
   exact root-5 `Project` node, requires its frozen projection role to be
   `DemandCompilerOutput`, evaluates its checked strict-earlier source to an
   exact `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1`, requires the descriptor
   payload tag to be `DemandCompiler=5`, selects the descriptor's exact
   `canonical_payload.grammar`, and walks `reached_steps` inside that
   `NormalizedGrammarValueV1`. Thus a reindexed earlier descriptor supplies its
   reindexed grammar; the compiler never substitutes the base descriptor's
   grammar or reconstructs a grammar from live-demand bytes.

For an obligation-source descriptor, symbolic typing is exactly
`ObligationResult(descriptor.result_type)` and must equal the nominal schema
reached by the five-view resolver. For a subject-source descriptor, symbolic
typing first applies the exact total
subsection-10.10.1 embedding:

```text
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor) = EnvelopeObjectDescriptor
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(
  CandidateAdmissionPremise) = CandidateAdmissionPremise
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(OccurrenceVector) = OccurrenceVector
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(Occurrence) = Occurrence
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedAction) = NormalizedAction
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedTelescope) = NormalizedTelescope
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedSubstitution) = NormalizedSubstitution
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedGrammar) = NormalizedGrammar
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedScheme) = NormalizedScheme
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedSection) = NormalizedSection
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedLiveDemand) = NormalizedLiveDemand
EmbedSubjectProjectionResult(NormalizedContract) = NormalizedContract.
```

The left side is `QuotientSubjectProjectionSymbolicTypeCandidateV1`; the right
side is `QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1`. A direct root-5
literal requires the embedded result to equal its enclosing
`value_node_types[j]`. A source descriptor nested in a formal-action component
instead requires the component-specific type fixed by the closed QG2
recursion. A root-4 preservation endpoint uses the embedded result directly.
Any mismatch aborts before `TypeAt` or `Canon` is evaluated.

The locus grammar is necessary but never sufficient admission. S1b2's exact
event image is the only valid path domain. Roots 0--3 admit `Direct` only.
Root 4 admits `Direct` only for the leading stored predicate incidence and
`Dereferenced` only for the exact projection-source and preservation-endpoint
descriptors. Root 5 admits only the exact `Dereferenced` and `PrimaryProject`
dependency occurrences. `PrimaryProjectGrammar` is admitted only as the
generated `GrammarCompanion` with `reached_steps=[]` or matching
`OperationRecordCompanion` with
`reached_steps=[RecordField(1),VectorElement(k)]` while walking a
`DemandCompilerOutput` project; it is never a dependency role. In particular,
root 5 has no direct walk into its embedded source bundle. Candidate root 6
emits no dependency path because its same-tag vector is empty. A root-4
`ObligationPremise` uses the direct predicate locus. A source-backed root-5
`TheoremEndpoint` uses the descriptor locus with empty `reached_steps`; its
nested semantic dependencies use the same descriptor occurrence with nonempty
descendants. A primary project emits no theorem-endpoint edge.

The path stores the descriptor's physical occurrence, not a copy of its
bytes. Consequently two equal descriptors at different obligation/source or
subject/node positions remain two dependency occurrences. Resolution context
is not an eighth support root, and no selected value is pretended to be a
by-value child of the descriptor. For every valid path `p`,
`p.result_schema` must name the exact nominal schema of the value reached after
the complete locus evaluation. A malformed step, wrong physical descriptor
type, off-image route, ambiguous resolution, or result-schema mismatch aborts.

This corrigendum changes no frozen QG2 value, route, node, binder, or count. It
also does not reuse `FullLocalPathV1`, `SubjectPathV1`, an R2c type ID, or a
representative-side path.

#### 10.12.2 Type-free nominal schema locator

Seven by-value root schemas alone cannot name every support result. Root 4
contains symbolic references to obligation compiler values that are not
embedded below `QObligations`; root 5 likewise names virtual endpoint result
schemas. The two QG2 symbolic tables already close those external families.
They become schema anchors only, never additional support roots or coverage
rows:

```text
QuotientSupportSchemaAnchorPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Root=0(
    root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1)
| ObligationResult=1(
    symbolic_type:QuotientObligationSymbolicTypeCandidateV1)
| PrimaryResult=2(
    symbolic_type:QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportSchemaStepPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  RecordField=0(field_ordinal:u16)
| VectorElementType=1
| SumPayload=2(variant_tag:u8)
| OptionPayload=3
| TermChildType=4(child_edge_tag:u8)

QuotientSupportSchemaLocatorPlanCandidateV1 =
  (anchor:QuotientSupportSchemaAnchorPlanCandidateV1,
   steps:vector<QuotientSupportSchemaStepPlanCandidateV1>).
```

The root-anchor map is exact:

| Root | Exact nominal anchor schema |
| --- | --- |
| `CanonicalPayload` | `QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1` |
| `IndexedInputs` | `IndexedInputsV1` |
| `IndexedOutput` | `IndexedOutputV1` |
| `PublicFields` | `vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>` |
| `Obligations` | `QuotientObligationsCandidateV1` |
| `PrimarySubjects` | `QuotientSubjectRootProjectionCandidateV1` |
| `ActionArguments` | `QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1` |

`ObligationResult(t)` and `PrimaryResult(t)` resolve by the complete exact
schema maps in subsections 10.3.1 and 10.10.1, respectively. An overlap such
as `NormalizedSubstitutionV1` denotes one nominal schema node, not two support
types.

Schema-step evaluation is against the frozen nominal R2a--R2d schema graph,
before numerical registry assignment. `RecordField`, `SumPayload`, and
`OptionPayload` require the exact selected child to exist;
`VectorElementType` selects the element schema and deliberately has no value
ordinal. `TermChildType` is valid only for the frozen inherited JG2b2b0 term
edge and returns its exact term child schema. A semantic newtype remains a
distinct zero-step node, but is transparently unwrapped only while consuming
a following structural step. Aliases share one node; generic instantiations
retain their exact argument node; and anonymous payload records retain their
enclosing declaration and variant identity. Nominally distinct but
wire-isomorphic records never collapse.

For a nominal schema node `T`, define

```text
Routes(T) =
  every simple QuotientSupportSchemaLocatorPlanCandidateV1
  whose exact schema evaluation reaches T

Canon(T) =
  min Routes(T)
  under (anchor canonical bytes, steps.len, step canonical bytes).
```

Only simple routes participate. Thus the local acyclic schema graph remains
finite and inherited recursive `Term` children cannot manufacture infinitely
many aliases for the same term schema. Every node admitted below must have a
nonempty `Routes(T)`; zero or multiple evaluations of one locator abort. The
three anchor variants and their order are frozen here and may not later be
extended to rename an already canonical node.

This locator is a compiler generator, not a runtime type name. In particular,
it is not a free string, digest, schema ordinal, numerical `TypeIdV1`, or an
open path value accepted in `result_schema`.

#### 10.12.3 Smallest finite admitted symbolic universe

S1b2 is necessarily a two-pass nominal compiler. Before the symbolic enum
exists, pass A emits this compiler-only tuple for every direct incidence. A
bare path contains its root because a companion may live under a different
root from the dependency:

```text
QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   locus:QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportRecipePathBindingsPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1,
   grammar_companion:Option<QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1>,
   operation_record_companion:
     Option<QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1>)

NominalDirectIncidence =
  (root,
   bare_locus,
   path_bindings:
     QuotientSupportRecipePathBindingsPlanCandidateV1,
   nominal_result_schema,
   edge,
   nominal_expected_judgment,
   referent_recipe,
   owner_resolution_recipe).
```

The two recipes are closed finite semantic directives, not encoded referents,
owners, hop transcripts, or final sum tags. They expose every deterministic
companion path that S1b3 may need. In particular, an operation incidence
exposes its selected dependency locus, complete grammar companion, and, where
distinct, the selected operation-record companion. This prevents a later
scheme/live selector from inventing `opath=gpath/operations[k]` after the
manifest has frozen.

For a nominal incidence stream `I`, define

```text
NoFormalPathCensus(I) =
  every bare-path role reference in
    I.path_bindings.dependency,
    I.nominal_expected_judgment,
    I.referent_recipe, or
    I.owner_resolution_recipe,
  expanded through I.path_bindings and preserving incidence order and
  recipe-local role order.
```

Equal path bytes at different role occurrences remain separate census events;
the following type set alone deduplicates their nominal result schemas. Let
`NoFormalDirectImage` range over pass-A streams for every valid input in all
seven constructor families. It is the static compiler image, not the traversal
of one selected candidate:

```text
U_nf =
  { TypeAt(p)
    | p occurs in NoFormalPathCensus(I)
      for some I in NoFormalDirectImage }
  union
  { T
    | NominalStructuralRecord(T) occurs in I
      for some I in NoFormalDirectImage }

M_nf = sort_unique([Canon(T) | T in U_nf]).
```

`TypeAt` evaluates the fresh four-way bare locus and the nominal schema graph;
it does not read a claimed result schema to determine its own answer. S1b3 may
consume only path occurrences already exposed by `NoFormalPathCensus`; it may
not introduce a new support path or symbolic schema while materializing a
referent or resolving an owner. Every closure dependency inherits one of those
complete paths from its forcing row, carries only
`StructuralOccurrence(added_occurrence)`, and therefore adds no path,
`StructuralRecord` schema, or symbolic type to `M_nf`.

To preserve subsection 10.11.3's closed-finite-sum requirement, S1b2 must
generate and freeze

```text
QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  sum [Node_a | a in M_nf in canonical order]

SchemaOf(Node_a) = ResolveSchema(a).
```

Every `Node_a` is payload-free. If `m=M_nf.len`, then

```text
1 <= m <= 256
tag(Node_(M_nf[i])) = checked_u8(i)  for 0 <= i < m.
```

These are candidate compiler-plan tags only, never final registry IDs. The one
table is common to all constructors and candidate values. Its exact contents
and cardinality are normative. The barrier equations are:

```text
ResolveSchema(Canon(T)) = T
Canon(ResolveSchema(a)) = a                       for a in M_nf
a != b  => ResolveSchema(a) != ResolveSchema(b)  for a,b in M_nf

lower_nominal_path(root,locus,T).result_schema =
  Node_(Canon(TypeAt(root,locus)))
require TypeAt(root,locus) = T.
```

Pass B first freezes `M_nf` and its tags, then lowers every nominal schema in
the incidence stream. A claimed `result_schema` never selects pass-A
traversal. A locator outside `M_nf`, an omitted member, a duplicate nominal
node, or a tag/table mismatch aborts.

R2e later lowers `SchemaOf(Node_a)` injectively to a final nonzero `TypeIdV1`.
The lowering need not be monotone in `Node_a` tag order, and there is no cast,
arithmetic, comparison, registry lookup, or ordering equation between a
symbolic tag and a `TypeIdV1`.

`M_nf` and `QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1` are explicitly
candidate-restricted compiler artifacts. The later nonempty action root and
instantiated action subject must define their own `M_action` and a final shared
manifest together with an order-preserving injective candidate embedding,
unless they first prove `U_action` is a subset of `U_nf`. They may not append
to or renumber this candidate enum. The final shared wire type and candidate/
action encoding remain unmaterialized.

This sequencing is intentional. Freezing an arbitrary locator record now
would admit unused descendant schemas and disguise an open schema path as a
closed symbolic enum. Freezing the generated table before the virtual
traversal would risk omitting exactly the external result schemas for which
the two non-root anchors are needed.

#### 10.12.4 Exact no-formal expected-judgment surface

The no-formal compiler image has exactly nine admitted expected-judgment
cases:

```text
QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  TypeFormation=0
| HasType=1(
    normalized_type:Term)
| DefinitionallyEqualAt=2(
    normalized_type:Term)
| ContextPrefix=3(
    normalized_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    prefix_ordinal:u32)
| CarrierSlot=4(
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1,
    field_ordinal:u32)
| OperationOrdinal=5(
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
    operation_ordinal:u32)
| StructuralOccurrence=6(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1)
| StructuralRecord=8(
    symbolic_type:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1)
| ConstructionRule=9(
    rule_path:DefinitionRulePathV1).
```

This display is a meta-level restricted case family, not yet an R2a
`SchemaTypeNodeV1::Sum`. The eventual stored fresh sum must have all ten
contiguous A3-C1 positions and insert the payloadful schema branch

```text
NormalizationSubject=7(rule:NormalizationRuleV1)
```

between tags 6 and 8. Quotient support validation admits no value of that
branch. Thus the schema remains contiguous without inventing a payload-free
reserved variant, while the semantic compiler image remains exactly the nine
displayed cases.

The displayed tags are the byte-identical A3-C1 tags and are not compacted.
The complete normalized context, term, carrier index, occurrence, symbolic
schema, and rule path are retained. `OperationOrdinal` uses the fresh quotient
grammar locus, never `FullLocalPathV1`. The later formal-action locus must use
one of these same nine complete judgments; it may not populate tag 7, append a
case, or renumber a tag. The actual stored sum remains unmaterialized until its
final shared path and symbolic-schema payload types close.

All nine variants are required across the seven-constructor family:

- type and term roles require tags 0 and 1;
- comparison and contract endpoints require tag 2;
- context/telescope prefixes require tag 3;
- indexed carrier fields require tag 4;
- grammar declarations and scheme/live operation selectors require tag 5;
- the principal-origin obligation and closure suffix require tag 6;
- structurally typed obligation and reached-value roles require tag 8; and
- the group-5 construction and preservation requirements require tag 9.

Tag `NormalizationSubject=7` has no admitted quotient-support value. The seven
quotient roots carry normalized semantic values but no normalization-event
label. A primary `Normalize` value node is an opcode and contains no
`NormalizationRuleV1`. Emitting expected tag 7 would therefore invent
provenance rather than retain quotient semantics.

The same boundary fixes the no-formal direct images of
`RecordReconstruction=22` and `NormalizationStep=23` as empty. The R2c
reconstruction labels belong to its raw/Build presentation recipe and may not
be inherited implicitly; every surviving quotient construction requirement
is already an explicit root-4 obligation. Edges 24--26 remain closure-suffix
only, and edges 27--28 remain action-only. `SourceLeaf=0` also has no direct
quotient image because no semantic root contains an R2c `SourceLeafV1`.

Consequently the candidate edge admission boundary is exact:

```text
direct candidate edge tags  subset of {1,...,21}
candidate closure edge tags exactly where emitted in {24,25,26}
candidate forbidden tags    {0,22,23,27,28}.
```

S1b2 fixes the exact subset and multiplicities inside `1..21`; this display is
not permission to emit every tag. Later action rebuilding may admit 27 and 28.
Tags 0, 22, and 23 remain absent from quotient support. Reusing the larger
`RawDependencyEdgeV1` code type or its payload enums does not authorize a
forbidden variant.

#### 10.12.5 Phase boundary

QG3-S1b1 closes exactly:

1. the provisional physical-versus-virtual three-way support locus and its root
   restrictions, with the bounded projected-grammar omission corrected and
   appended by S1b2 in subsection 10.13;
2. the complete root/external-result schema anchors, schema-step grammar, and
   canonical nominal naming rule;
3. the exact definition of the smallest no-formal manifest `M_nf` and its
   generated finite-sum discipline;
4. the nine-case expected-judgment compiler surface, the contiguous ten-way
   eventual wire shape, and the semantic exclusion of tag 7; and
5. the empty no-formal direct image of R2c presentation-only source,
   reconstruction, and normalization edges.

S1b itself remains active. S1b2 must now freeze the exact root-0--6 direct
nominal traversal and path census, evaluate and publish the complete ordered
candidate `M_nf` table and cardinality, lower the nominal incidences, and fill
every remaining local semantic incidence. It must preserve descriptor
occurrences and repeated reached values, keep root 5 restricted to the root
projection, and keep the candidate root-6 same-tag action vector empty. S1b3
then closes the candidate referent/owner/key subdomain, resolves every direct
owner, defines and evaluates the quotient-internal closure suffix, and closes
the no-formal support graph.

No final shared binder, referent, owner, or key sum, support-preservation
subject, formal-action locus, action-introduced lineage, instantiated action
subject, eight-group projection, or root `0xfd` is declared here.

### 10.13 QG3-S1b2 direct nominal traversal and candidate manifest — CLOSED

This cut closes the no-formal direct compiler, not the candidate support graph.
It freezes the exact root walks, every support-path role needed by later
referent and owner resolution, the complete pass-A nominal incidence image,
and the one project-wide candidate symbolic-type enum generated from that
image. S1b3 still has to materialize referents and owners, evaluate closure,
and construct classes, coverage, projection, and footprint agreement.

The completed walk exposes one bounded omission in S1b1's provisional
three-locus algebra. A projected Demand live value can contain operation
selectors while its exact, possibly reindexed grammar is not a descendant of
the projected result. The corrected locus sum therefore appends
`PrimaryProjectGrammar=3`. It is compiler-only, is admitted solely for the two
companion bindings displayed below, and changes no existing locus tag, schema
anchor, manifest node, or dependency incidence.

#### 10.13.1 Prefix-field and inherited-schema adjudications

The R2c phrase “telescope prefix” did not determine whether a quotient
dependency should name a whole context/telescope container or the field whose
formation depends on the prefix. The quotient rule is now exact:

```text
PrefixLocus(base,X,i) = the Term at X.Field(i).Type

PrefixExpected(base,X,i) =
  ContextPrefix(
    normalized_context=NormalizedContext(base ++ X),
    prefix_ordinal=checked_u32(base.len + i))
```

Here `base` and `X` are oldest-first, `base ++ X` is the complete normalized
dependent context in which the selected field occurs, and the ordinal is the
absolute pre-field boundary. For a context root, `base=[]`; for a telescope,
`base` is its exact ambient context. The direct locus is the value path ending
in the selected `.../VectorElement(i) : Term`, never the whole
`NormalizedContextV1`, `NormalizedTelescopeV1`, or inherited
`DependentContext`. The field incidence is emitted before recursively walking
that term. Equal term bytes at different fields remain different incidences.

This choice is semantic rather than cardinality-driven: the dependency is the
particular field whose well-formedness consumes the prefix, whereas the full
context and boundary are already retained without loss in the expected
judgment. It also prevents a context container from collapsing several
field-local dependencies.

Value paths and schema locators have different inherited-codec boundaries.
A checked `TypedPathStepV1::VectorElement(i)` may walk an inherited
`DependentContext` using its bound predecessor codec. The local schema graph,
however, treats `Inherited(DependentContext)` as opaque. Newtype transparency
only changes the current node to that inherited node; it does not reveal a
local `vector<Term>` child.

The quotient value walker makes that claim executable through this closed
predecessor-codec adapter, evaluated only after the exact inherited binding and
runtime value have been validated:

```text
QuotientInheritedValuePathAdapterV1:
  Inherited(DependentContext) / VectorElement(i)
    -> Term
    iff i is in bounds in the canonically decoded oldest-first context

  Inherited(OpenJudgment::TypeFormation) / SumPayload(0)
    -> inherited TypeFormation payload view
  inherited TypeFormation payload view / RecordField(0)
    -> DependentContext
  inherited TypeFormation payload view / RecordField(1)
    -> Term

  Inherited(OpenJudgment::HasType) / SumPayload(1)
    -> inherited HasType payload view
  inherited HasType payload view / RecordField(0)
    -> DependentContext
  inherited HasType payload view / RecordField(1)
    -> Term
  inherited HasType payload view / RecordField(2)
    -> Term.
```

`TypeAt` consults this adapter while evaluating a **value** path. The two
payload views are compiler-only views of the frozen predecessor codec, not
schema nodes, anchors, manifest members, or wire variants. The root-3 compiler
rejects `DefinitionallyEqual` before path evaluation, so no tag-2 adapter is
admitted. The already frozen inherited-Term `TermChild(e) -> Term` rule remains
the only other inherited descendant rule. Every other inherited binding, step,
field, sum tag, or vector access aborts. Canonical decode/re-encode of the
predecessor value is required before and after the walk.

This adapter does not participate in `Routes` or `Canon`, which are evaluated
only in the local nominal schema graph. Therefore

```text
Root(CanonicalPayload)/SumPayload(0)/RecordField(0)/VectorElementType
```

is **not** a valid schema locator. The unique canonical `Term` locator is

```text
Root(CanonicalPayload)/SumPayload(0)/RecordField(1)/VectorElementType,
```

because Formation field 1 is
`NormalizedTelescopeV1 = newtype vector<Term>`. Formation field 2 gives a
second valid but lexically later route. This distinction is checked, not
inferred from equal wire bytes.

Root 3 receives one further explicit inherited-codec rule. A valid
`OpenJudgment` is walked in kernel field order, always context first:

```text
TypeFormation: context, term
HasType:       context, term, type
```

The corresponding physical paths are displayed below. A root-3
`DefinitionallyEqual` value is off the closed Q2 public-field image and aborts;
the quotient compiler does not silently add its left/right/type walk.

#### 10.13.2 Bare-path census and finite recipe algebra

Write a bare quotient path as `(root,locus)`, before attaching its generated
symbolic result tag. For a suffix `s`, descent is total only on a valid reached
value and is defined by

```text
Direct(p) / s                 = Direct(p ++ s)
Dereferenced(d,p) / s         = Dereferenced(d,p ++ s)
PrimaryProject(n,p) / s       = PrimaryProject(n,p ++ s)
PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,p) / s = PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,p ++ s).
```

The descriptor or project occurrence never changes during descent. The exact
compiler-only path-role grammar is:

```text
QuotientSupportRecipePathRolePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Dependency=0
| GrammarCompanion=1
| OperationRecordCompanion=2.
```

Every incidence exposes one `Dependency` role occurrence. Edges
`OperationAnchor=8`, `OperationParameter=9`, and `OperationResult=10` expose,
in that order after the dependency occurrence, one `GrammarCompanion` and one
`OperationRecordCompanion`. Role occurrences remain distinct even when two
complete paths are byte-identical. No fourth support-path role is admitted.

The role tags are references into the row's explicit
`QuotientSupportRecipePathBindingsPlanCandidateV1`, not implicit instructions
to reconstruct a path:

```text
for every row r:
  r.path_bindings.dependency = (r.root,r.bare_locus)

for r.edge in {OperationAnchor,OperationParameter,OperationResult}:
  r.path_bindings.grammar_companion = Some(gpath)
  r.path_bindings.operation_record_companion = Some(opath)

for every other direct edge:
  r.path_bindings.grammar_companion = None
  r.path_bindings.operation_record_companion = None.
```

Here `gpath` and `opath` are complete bare quotient paths, possibly under a
different root or virtual descriptor occurrence from the dependency. Every
recipe role lookup is total against these fields; requesting `GrammarCompanion`
or `OperationRecordCompanion` from `None` aborts. Pass B lowers and retains all
three binding fields through recipe execution. The descent equations above are
compiler composition rules, not an open path admission: a
`PrimaryProjectGrammar` binding has exactly `reached_steps=[]` for
`GrammarCompanion` or `[RF(1),VE(k)]` for the matching
`OperationRecordCompanion`; every other suffix or role aborts. S1b3 may compare
a bound path but may not recompute or replace it.

After the semantic checks above, the only new `TypeAt` equations are

```text
TypeAt(PrimarySubjects,PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,[])) =
  NormalizedGrammarValueV1

TypeAt(PrimarySubjects,
       PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,[RF(1),VE(k)])) =
  NormalizedGrammarOperationV1.
```

They resolve to the already present grammar and operation nodes. `Canon`, the
three schema-anchor families, `U_nf`, the final `M_nf`, and its tags are not
extended by this locus correction.

Pass A also uses a nominal expected-judgment view to avoid referring to the
not-yet-generated finalized path or symbolic enum:

```text
NominalizeExpected(OperationOrdinal(gpath,k)) =
  NominalOperationOrdinal(
    grammar_path_role=GrammarCompanion,
    operation_ordinal=k)

NominalizeExpected(StructuralRecord(T)) =
  NominalStructuralRecord(nominal_schema=T)

NominalizeExpected(other_no_path_case) = other_no_path_case.
```

`NominalOperationOrdinal` resolves `GrammarCompanion` through the row's path
bindings and checks that the bound path is `gpath`. Pass B alone constructs the
stored `OperationOrdinal(QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,k)`.

The complete pass-A referent directives are:

```text
QuotientSupportReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CandidateBinderFromValue=0(path:Dependency)
| KernelGlobalFromValue=1(path:Dependency)
| RecordSlot=2(
    path:Dependency | OperationRecordCompanion)
| OperationSlot=3(
    grammar_path:GrammarCompanion,
    operation_ordinal:u32,
    parameter_or_result:ParameterOrResultV1,
    field_ordinal:u32,
    selected_field_path:Dependency,
    operation_record_path:OperationRecordCompanion).
```

These are finite compiler instructions, not the still-unfrozen referent sum.
Their edge dispatch is exact:

| Direct edge | Referent recipe |
| --- | --- |
| `LocalBinderUse=1` | `CandidateBinderFromValue(Dependency)` |
| `GlobalUse=2` | `KernelGlobalFromValue(Dependency)` |
| `TelescopePrefix=3` through `GrammarExport=7` | `RecordSlot(Dependency)` |
| `OperationAnchor=8` | `RecordSlot(OperationRecordCompanion)` |
| `OperationParameter=9` | `OperationSlot(...,Parameter,...)` |
| `OperationResult=10` | `OperationSlot(...,Result,...)` |
| `SchemeTrigger=11` through `TermChild=21` | `RecordSlot(Dependency)` |

The owner directive exposes every later support-path use without encoding an
R2c hop transcript:

```text
QuotientSupportOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  HistoryOwner=0(
    terminal:QuotientSupportHistoryTerminalRecipePlanCandidateV1,
    current_location:QuotientSupportCurrentLocationRecipePlanCandidateV1)
| StructuralTerminal=1(
    terminal_path:QuotientSupportRecipePathRolePlanCandidateV1,
    terminal_referent:QuotientSupportReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportHistoryTerminalRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CurrentOccurrenceFromValue=0
| OlderExportFromValue=1
| SourceIdentityFromValue=2
| KernelGlobalFromValue=3
| CandidateBinderFromValue=4
| LineageSource=5(QuotientSupportLineageSemanticLocatorPlanCandidateV1)
| ResolvedPrimarySourceFromDependencyDescriptor=6

QuotientSupportCurrentLocationRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  PublicFieldLocation=0(EnvelopeFieldPathV1)
| AuxiliaryDependencyPath=1

QuotientSupportLineageSemanticLocatorPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  RawSource=0(raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1)
| FieldContribution=1(
    field_path:EnvelopeFieldPathV1,
    raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1,
    derivation_edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1)
| OutputContribution=2(
    field_path:EnvelopeFieldPathV1,
    raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1).
```

The lineage locator contains no source ordinal or entry-vector ordinal.
Repeated matching entries are valid only when every match selects one
byte-identical `SourceRefV1`. Candidate preflight classifies a genuinely absent
semantic source as false before the successful quotient coordinates are frozen;
distinct sources are malformed and abort. Once S1b3 begins after successful
preflight, zero matches, several distinct sources, or a result different from
the preflight result is an invariant abort.
`EnvelopeFieldPathV1`, `RawFieldPathV1`, obligation/source descriptors, and
primary source routes are resolver coordinates, not quotient support paths,
and do not enlarge the symbolic manifest.

`ResolvedPrimarySourceFromDependencyDescriptor` is valid only when the
dependency locus is `Dereferenced` through an exact root-5 direct literal. It
uses that locus's retained physical descriptor occurrence and decoded
`QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1`; it stores neither a
second descriptor path nor a source ordinal.

The public-versus-auxiliary location is also structural, not a byte search.
Define `EnclosingPublicField(root,locus)` by the following complete role map,
then append the exact `PublicLeaves` descendant inside the selected carrier
field:

| Support root | Exact envelope-field origin |
| --- | --- |
| `CanonicalPayload`, Formation | `gamma -> Indices`; `a -> Output`; `implementation_a -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, Abstraction | `gamma,a -> Indices`; `b -> Inputs`; `implementation_xi,implementation_b -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, Aggregation | `gamma,a,b -> Indices`; `implementation_c -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, Transport | `delta,gamma -> Indices`; `theta,a -> Inputs`; `implementation_c -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, Comparison | `gamma,a,b -> Indices`; `comparison -> Output`; `implementation_a,implementation_b -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, DemandCompiler | `gamma,grammar,scheme,activation -> Indices`; `live -> Output`; `implementation_activation -> RawRealization` |
| `CanonicalPayload`, DischargeTransformer | `delta,gamma,grammar,scheme,activation,live -> Indices`; `theta,discharge -> Inputs`; `implementation_discharge -> RawRealization` |
| `IndexedInputs` | `RF(0) -> Indices`, `RF(1) -> Inputs`, preserving the selected same-tag payload and carrier descendant |
| `IndexedOutput` | `Output`, preserving the selected same-tag payload and carrier descendant |
| `PublicFields` | the byte-identical stored `public_fields[i].path` selected by the leading `VE(i)` |
| `Obligations`, `PrimarySubjects`, `ActionArguments` | no physical enclosing public field |

The arrows name Q2 projection roles, not equal-value matches. A direct locus at
one exact `PublicLeaves` term leaf or below it through checked term-child/typed
descendant steps inherits that leaf's enclosing field; the descendant suffix is
stripped before the role lookup. A selected scalar, wrapper, operation record,
or other locus outside every such leaf subtree has no public-field result even
when it contains or equals public bytes. A history terminal uses
`PublicFieldLocation(EnclosingPublicField(...))` exactly when this function is
defined and otherwise `AuxiliaryDependencyPath`. A lineage terminal uses the
same role to select the fixed QLineage entry; zero or several role matches
abort rather than falling back to a different copy.

Augment that same finite semantic-role walk with its already frozen lineage
coordinate; do not inspect a `SourceRefV1` while constructing the recipe:

```text
QuotientSupportNonemptyLineageOriginPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Raw=0(
    raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1)
| Field=1(
    field_path:EnvelopeFieldPathV1,
    raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1,
    derivation_edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1)
| Output=2(
    field_path:EnvelopeFieldPathV1,
    raw_leaf_path:RawFieldPathV1).

LineageOrigin(authority,row)
  : Option<QuotientSupportNonemptyLineageOriginPlanCandidateV1>.
```

There is no encoded `None` tag. The compiler builds an erased annotation in
product with every already closed semantic resolver:

```text
OriginFrame(T) = total function
  (valid_typed_descendant_steps_of_T,RawDependencyEdgeV1)
    -> Option<QuotientSupportNonemptyLineageOriginPlanCandidateV1>

wrapper/scalar node -> None at that node
record/sum/vector   -> None at the wrapper and inject each child frame
exact PublicLeaves role -> PublicOrigin(role,descendant,edge).

PublicOrigin(RawRealization,leaf,e) =
  Some(Raw(exact Decode/Build raw_leaf_path))
PublicOrigin(Indices|Inputs,leaf,e) =
  Some(Field(exact EnvelopeFieldPath,
             exact Decode/Build raw_leaf_path,e))
PublicOrigin(Output,leaf,e) =
  Some(Output(exact EnvelopeFieldPath,
              exact Decode/Build raw_leaf_path))
PublicOrigin(non_leaf,_,_) = None.
```

Root 3 selects the public role from the root of its stored
`EnvelopeFieldPathV1`. For every other direct value, the exact role comes from
the subsection-10.4.3 occurrence pack and the finite public-field table above.
The `raw_leaf_path` is the coordinate produced by the frozen constructor-
specific Decode/Build lineage-role walk for this exact public leaf and semantic
edge; it is not selected by source equality or vector position. A missing
coordinate or two distinct coordinates for one semantic role aborts.

Virtual resolution is also total. A frame can never be attached afterwards by
byte search. The following helper ABI is compiler-only and total exactly on the
closed S1b2 image:

```text
ResolveDirectRootDecorated(authority,root,direct_steps)
  -> QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,T)

DecodeExactObligationDescriptor(authority,descriptor_steps)
  -> the unique physical descriptor in authority.obligations

ResolveQObligationSourceDecorated(
  authority,descriptor,reached_steps)
  -> QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,T)

DecodeExactQPrimaryDescriptor(authority,descriptor_steps)
  -> the unique physical descriptor in
     authority.support_frame.primary_resolver.program_skeleton

DecodeExactProjectOccurrence(authority,project_node_steps)
  -> (subject_slot,value_node_ordinal,project_node,enclosing_program)

EvaluateCheckedEarlierDescriptorCore(
  authority,enclosing_program,value_node_ordinal,source_node)
  -> the unique strictly earlier footprint-erased descriptor core

EvaluatePrimaryProjectionDecorated(authority,descriptor_core,projection)
  -> QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,R)

WalkDecorated(decorated_state,reached_steps)
  -> QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,T).
```

Every decoder validates the exact physical occurrence, slot/node bounds,
strict-earlier operand, result-type table, and program-skeleton identity before
returning. `PrimarySchemaOf` is the exact subsection-10.10.1 symbolic-type
table. `F(s,e)` means application of the displayed `OriginFrame` function; no
separate first-match `OriginAt` operation exists. The lineage function is:

```text
LineageOrigin(authority,row) = match row.bare_locus:
  Direct(p) ->
    state = ResolveDirectRootDecorated(authority,row.root,p)
    require state.view=Semantic(v)
    require TypeOf(v)=TypeAt(row.path_bindings.dependency)
    state.origin_frame(p,row.edge)

  Dereferenced(descriptor_steps,reached_steps) ->
    match row.root:
      Obligations ->
        d = DecodeExactObligationDescriptor(authority,descriptor_steps)
        state = ResolveQObligationSourceDecorated(
                  authority,d,reached_steps)
      PrimarySubjects ->
        d = DecodeExactQPrimaryDescriptor(authority,descriptor_steps)
        state = ResolveSupportQPrimarySourceDecorated(
                  authority,d,reached_steps)
      otherwise -> abort
    match state.view:
      SchemaOnly ->
        require reached_steps=[]
        require SupportViewSchema(state.view)=
                TypeAt(row.path_bindings.dependency)
        None
      Semantic(v) ->
        require TypeOf(v)=TypeAt(row.path_bindings.dependency)
        state.origin_frame(reached_steps,row.edge)

  PrimaryProject(project_node_steps,reached_steps) ->
    (slot,j,n,P) = DecodeExactProjectOccurrence(
                     authority,project_node_steps)
    X = EvaluateCheckedEarlierDescriptorCore(
          authority,P,j,n.source_node)
    root_state = EvaluatePrimaryProjectionDecorated(
                   authority,X,n.path)
    require root_state.view=Semantic(v0)
    require TypeOf(v0)=PrimarySchemaOf(n.path.result_type)
    require n.path.result_type =
      P.value_node_types[j]
    state = WalkDecorated(root_state,reached_steps)
    require state.view=Semantic(v)
    require TypeOf(v)=TypeAt(row.path_bindings.dependency)
    state.origin_frame(reached_steps,row.edge)

  PrimaryProjectGrammar(_,_) -> abort. // companion, never dependency
```

The obligation decorated resolver uses this complete root table:

| Obligation source root | Exact origin-frame rule |
| --- | --- |
| `BindingRequirements` | all `None` |
| `DecodeResolution` | each normalized semantic descendant of the exact call request/success uses `Raw(call.request.path)`; structural and source metadata use `None` |
| `CarrierRequirements` | each formation value uses its exact subsection-10.4.3 occurrence role; compatibility wrappers are `None` and inject those exact child frames |
| `ParticularOperations` | product-evaluate the exact subsection-10.6.3 role program; every newly constructed `QId`, `QProject`, `QPair`, `QIteratedLift`, or `QCompose` wrapper and result subtree is `None`, while only an exact injected Q2 argument projection carries its fixed public-role frame |
| `BuildSemanticChecks` | wrapper `None`; comparison/agreement descendants use the exact named Q2 roles in the group-4 table |

The primary-source decorated resolver uses this complete route partition:

| Primary route | Exact origin-frame rule |
| --- | --- |
| `WholeSourceCandidate` | descriptor wrapper `None`; its canonical-payload child uses the root-0 public-field role table |
| `SelectorMatchPremise`, `SelectorCurrentDomain`, `SelectorAnchor`, `SubstitutionIdentity`, `ContextSquareIdentity` | all `None` |
| `AbstractionLiftTargetA`, `AggregationLiftTargetA`, `DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports`, every Discharge reindexed grammar/scheme/activation/live/contract route | exact `Indices` contribution role from the paired route table |
| `TransportCompositeThetaSource`, `DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource` | exact `Inputs` contribution role from the paired route table |

`EvaluatePrimaryProjectionDecorated` first reconstructs the subsection-10.10.3
value and then selects exactly one frame:

| Projection role | Required payload tag | Exact frame |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| `FormationOutput` | 0 | Formation `Output` |
| `AbstractionOutput` | 1 | Abstraction `Output` |
| `AggregationOutput` | 2 | Aggregation `Output` |
| `TransportOutput` | 3 | Transport `Output` |
| `ComparisonOutput` | 4 | Comparison `Output` |
| `DemandCompilerOutput` | 5 | DemandCompiler `Output` |
| `DischargeTransformerOutput` | 6 | DischargeTransformer `Output` |
| `TransportNormalizedImplementation` | 3 | Transport `RawRealization` |
| `DischargeTransformerNormalizedImplementation` | 6 | DischargeTransformer `RawRealization` |

A wrong route, payload tag, value type, result type, or frame aborts. Equal
Formation output/implementation bytes and Comparison endpoint copies cannot
exchange origins. The frame is erased immediately after the owner recipe is
emitted; it is not a support path, wire field, or S1b3 input.

```text
LineageLocator(Raw(r))         = RawSource(r)
LineageLocator(Field(f,r,e))   = FieldContribution(f,r,e)
LineageLocator(Output(f,r))    = OutputContribution(f,r)

OwnerLocation(row) =
  PublicFieldLocation(EnclosingPublicField(row.root,row.bare_locus))
    if EnclosingPublicField(row.root,row.bare_locus) is defined
  AuxiliaryDependencyPath
    otherwise

SupportNodeView(T) ::= SchemaOnly | Semantic(value:T)

SupportViewSchema(view:SupportNodeView(T)) = T.

ValueTerminal(v) =
  v is Term::Var               -> Some(CandidateBinderFromValue)
  v is Term::Global            -> Some(KernelGlobalFromValue)
  v is OccurrenceId14V1        -> Some(CurrentOccurrenceFromValue)
  v is OlderPublicExportV1     -> Some(OlderExportFromValue)
  v is SourceRefV1             -> Some(SourceIdentityFromValue)
  otherwise                    -> None.

SupportValueTerminal(Semantic(v)) = ValueTerminal(v)
SupportValueTerminal(SchemaOnly) = None.
```

The complete owner-recipe emitter is then:

```text
EmitOwner(authority,row):
  R = row.referent_recipe
  match row.edge:
    LocalBinderUse ->
      HistoryOwner(CandidateBinderFromValue,OwnerLocation(row))

    GlobalUse ->
      HistoryOwner(KernelGlobalFromValue,OwnerLocation(row))

    OperationAnchor ->
      StructuralTerminal(
        OperationRecordCompanion,
        RecordSlot(OperationRecordCompanion))

    ObligationPremise ->
      StructuralTerminal(Dependency,RecordSlot(Dependency))

    TheoremEndpoint -> match TypeAt(row.path_bindings.dependency):
      OccurrenceId14V1 ->
        require row.root=PrimarySubjects and row.bare_locus is an exact
                direct-literal Dereferenced(descriptor,[]);
        HistoryOwner(
          ResolvedPrimarySourceFromDependencyDescriptor,
          AuxiliaryDependencyPath)

      QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1 |
      QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1 |
      vector<OccurrenceId14V1> |
      QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1 ->
        StructuralTerminal(Dependency,RecordSlot(Dependency))

      otherwise -> abort

    e in {3..7,9..18,21} ->
      if LineageOrigin(authority,row)=Some(o):
        HistoryOwner(
          LineageSource(LineageLocator(o)),
          OwnerLocation(row))
      else if SupportValueTerminal(
                SupportViewAtBare(
                  authority,row.path_bindings.dependency))=Some(t):
        HistoryOwner(t,OwnerLocation(row))
      else:
        StructuralTerminal(Dependency,R)

    otherwise -> abort.
```

The theorem-endpoint branch is a static result-schema partition; it does not
resolve an owner in S1b2. Structural fallback retains the row's exact referent,
so edges 9 and 10 keep their `OperationSlot` recipe. Edge 8 is the sole
operation-record-path exception. When a public origin is present, failure to
resolve that exact lineage locator aborts; structural fallback is forbidden.

At S1b3 execution, `RawSource(r)` selects all matching raw-path entries from
`QLineage.principal` and `QLineage.fields`, requires one byte-identical
`SourceRefV1`, and then uses Q1 only to authenticate a selected `Current(q)`
against the history occurrence evaluator. `FieldContribution(f,r,e)` selects
entries of the unique QLineage field at `f` matching `(r,e)`; and
`OutputContribution(f,r)` selects matching QLineage output entries and
interprets their occurrence as `SourceRefV1::Current`. Repetitions are
preserved, and all matches must already denote one byte-identical terminal
identity. Sorting, deduplication, first-match selection, or fallback is
forbidden.

This dispatch is by the exact locus and semantic route, never by edge tag or a
value-equality search alone. `QFootprints` occurs nowhere in any recipe. S1b3
may use the Q1-authenticated occurrence evaluator, `QLineage`, and the quotient
frame to execute these directives, but may introduce no new support path.

#### 10.13.3 Total ambient-pack compiler

The local edge rules are not executable from a reached value alone. Telescope
prefixes require a base context, sections require a base substitution and
target telescope, carrier-slot judgments require a complete carrier index and
full `PublicLeaves` ordinal, operation selectors require an exact grammar
companion, and term variables require the exact binder context. These values
are now a closed compiler-only state algebra:

```text
QuotientSupportTraversalAmbientPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ContextAt(context:NormalizedContextV1)
| TelescopeAt(
    base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| SubstitutionAt(substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1)
| SectionAt(
    base_substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    target_telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| ActivationSectionAt(
    base_substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    target_telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| FamilyAt(
    family:NormalizedFamilyValueV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| ComparisonAt(comparison:NormalizedComparisonValueV1)
| GrammarAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| SchemeAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| ActivationAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| LiveAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    activation:NormalizedSectionV1,
    live:NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1)
| DischargeAt(
    index:CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex,
    discharge:NormalizedDischargeValueV1)
| ContractAt(
    base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    contract:NormalizedContractValueV1)
| TermAt(
    context:NormalizedContextV1,
    expectation:TypeFormation | HasType(expected_type:Term)).
```

This is erased state, not an encoded sum, manifest node, support path, owner,
or receipt. A reached semantic value must have exactly one applicable state of
the required kind.

Every context in that algebra is additionally decorated by an exact
oldest-first binder frame:

```text
QuotientSupportBinderFramePlanCandidateV1 =
  (identity:BinderKeyV1,
   complete_type:Term)

QuotientSupportBinderEnvironmentPlanCandidateV1 =
  (context:NormalizedContextV1,
   binders:vector<QuotientSupportBinderFramePlanCandidateV1>)

require binders.len = context.len
require map(.complete_type,binders) = ContextTerms(context)
require every identity is HistoricalBinder or SyntheticBinder.
```

Here
`ContextTerms(NormalizedContextV1(DependentContext(v)))=v` is the checked
double-newtype projection, `EmptyContext=NormalizedContextV1(DependentContext([]))`, and
`ContextTelescope(C)=NormalizedTelescopeV1(ContextTerms(C))` is an explicit
same-term rewrap. `NormalizedContextV1` and `NormalizedTelescopeV1` remain
distinct newtypes; no equation below passes one where the other is required.

`FormalBinder` is rejected before traversal. In the formulas below,
`ContextAt(C)`, a telescope base `G`, and `TermAt(C,J)` abbreviate this complete
environment, not context bytes alone. `TelescopeAt(G,T)` also carries the exact
introduced binder frame for every field of `T`; field `i` is checked in the
base environment extended by frames `0..i`, and its frame is appended only
after its type checks. Every other context-valued field of an ambient, including
both ends of a substitution, carries the corresponding complete frame. All
context operations transport the identity and complete type in lockstep.

The decoration is reconstructed by route. A QG1 checked context/telescope uses
its retained binder-key vector after the no-formal check. A direct Q2
`PublicLeaves` field uses the exact QLineage raw path and source and reruns
`IntroducedBinderKey`: the historical case requires the exact current
parameter-type child and body edge; every other case yields the exact synthetic
`(profile_version,constructor,raw_field_path)` key. `QId`, `QProject`, `QPair`,
`QIteratedLift`, telescope concatenation, reindexing, specialization, and every
primary-project evaluator transport or append those complete frames by their
frozen semantic operations. They never identify binders by type bytes. A
missing key, formal key, length/type mismatch, or two nonidentical keys for one
boundary aborts. The restricted key is compiler state; S1b3 must translate it
into its fresh candidate binder sum before storing a referent.

Writing `F_C` for the oldest-first binder frame of context `C` and `F_T` for
the introduced frame of telescope `T`, the frame lift is executable rather
than an appeal to type equality:

```text
F_EmptyContext = []
F_AppendTelescope(G,T) = F_G ++ F_T
F_ConcatTelescope(T,U) = F_T ++ F_U

ReindexFrame(theta,(k,A)) =
  (k,NF(CaptureSafeReindex(A,theta)))

QId(G):
  F_delta=F_G; F_gamma=F_G

QProject(G,T):
  F_delta=F_G++F_T; F_gamma=F_G

QPair(base,T,s):
  F_delta=F_(base.delta)
  F_gamma=F_(base.gamma)++F_T

QCompose(theta,rho):
  F_delta=F_(rho.delta)
  F_gamma=F_(theta.gamma)

one lift of theta along telescope frame (k,A):
  A_theta=NF(CaptureSafeReindex(A,theta))
  F_result.delta=F_(theta.delta)++[(k,A_theta)]
  F_result.gamma=F_(theta.gamma)++[(k,A)].
```

`QIteratedLift` applies the last equation in telescope order, using the exact
identity already attached to each target field. `ReindexTelescope` uses
`ReindexFrame` at the corresponding lifted step. Copy, projection of an
already stored endpoint, specialization, grammar/scheme reindexing, and
primary-project evaluation preserve or product-compose these equations; no
operation chooses `k` from type bytes. The same identity appears on the two
sides of a lift even though its complete type is reindexed.

Term-body binders require a separate provenance product; a normalized term's
bytes alone do not identify the binder introduced by one of its constructors:

```text
QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1 =
  (term:Term,
   source_occurrence_at_node:
     total map from every valid JG2b2b0 term path of term
       to Option<OccurrenceId14V1>)
```

This map is compiler-only and is required to have the same constructor tree as
`term`. Decoding `Current(q)` supplies the exact Q1-authenticated occurrence at
the root and the exact authenticated descendant occurrence at every child
path. Decoding `OlderPublic` supplies only `Global(x)` and `None`. A Q2 or
QLineage-backed normalized term receives the product while its semantic route
is evaluated; equal term bytes never acquire provenance by search.

The product algebra is exhaustive. Capture-safe shift and reindex retain the
source occurrence at every constructor they transport and insert the complete
provenance product of a substituted image. Telescope concatenation and copies
retain their products. `QId` and `QProject` manufacture only `Var` nodes with
`None`; one lift adds only its transported images and a fresh `Var(0)`;
`QPair` concatenates already decorated images. `BetaApply` erases the selected
application/lambda constructors and capture-safely substitutes the decorated
argument into the decorated body; first/second projection erases its projection
and pair wrappers and selects the decorated component; and
`PermittedGlobal(g)` starts from the exact same-birth Q1 body occurrence and
its authenticated descendant map. These are the complete frozen NF rules.
No candidate operation in this section manufactures a `Pi`, `Sigma`, or
`Lambda` constructor.

Canonical contract sets lift this product through their otherwise
provenance-blind sort/dedup step:

```text
DecoratedContract =
  (value:NormalizedContractValueV1,
   left:QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1,
   right:QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1,
   ty:QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1)

require left.term=value.left
require right.term=value.right
require ty.term=value.ty

DecoratedCanonicalContractSet(v):
  sort by full canonical bytes of .value exactly as canonical_contract_set;
  for each adjacent equal-.value run:
    require every complete (left,right,ty) provenance triple is identical;
    retain the value and that one common triple;
  return the resulting decorated vector.
```

The initial checked-scheme projection and every
`TermwiseReindexContractSet`/specialization/grammar-or-scheme reindexing call
use `DecoratedCanonicalContractSet` in the support compiler. A byte-equal run
with zero provenance or two different provenance triples is checked false at
the outer candidate boundary and is an invariant abort if it recurs after
successful preflight. Source position, sort stability, first-element choice,
or equal contract bytes may not choose an origin product.

It follows inductively that every candidate-reachable `Pi`, `Sigma`, or
`Lambda` node has exactly one source occurrence `q`, with Q1 authenticating the
matching node kind and each displayed body edge. Its body frame appends
`HistoricalBinder(q,PiBody|SigmaBody|LambdaBody)` and the complete parameter or
parameter-type term. Telescope declarations, by contrast, append their exact
A3 `IntroducedBinderKey`, which may be historical or synthetic. Thus the two
admitted `BinderKeyV1` cases are total without minting a structural binder. A
binding constructor with `None`, two distinct source occurrences, a mismatched
node kind/body edge, or provenance lost or duplicated by an operation is an
invariant abort after successful no-formal preflight.

Both the binder frame and term-provenance product are erased annotations over
already admitted values and JG2b2b0 child paths. They add no quotient support
path, path-binding role, direct incidence, symbolic schema, or member of the
seventeen-node `M_nf`.

The decorated resolver state is therefore the explicit compiler-only product

```text
QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,T) =
  (view:SupportNodeView(T),
   traversal_ambient:
     Option<QuotientSupportTraversalAmbientPlanCandidateV1>,
   binder_environment:
     Option<QuotientSupportBinderEnvironmentPlanCandidateV1>,
   term_provenance:
     Option<QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1>,
   origin_frame:OriginFrame(R))
```

where `R` is the selected semantic root schema and `T` is the reached schema.
For `Semantic(v)` at `T=Term`, `term_provenance=Some(P)` with `P.term=v`; for
every other reached schema it is `None`. `SchemaOnly` requires both binder and
term products to be `None`. A present binder environment is exactly the one
decorating the context boundary in `traversal_ambient`; it is never recovered
from term bytes. `WalkDecorated` maps a state at `(R,T)` and one valid typed
descendant step to the state at `(R,U)`, preserving the same origin frame while
transporting the ambient, binder environment, and complete term-provenance
product by the exhaustive algebra above. Iteration defines a reached-step walk.
Dropping any product and reconstructing it later by value equality is invalid.

Define, with all arithmetic checked,

```text
GE(G,V) = AppendTelescope(G,V.exports)

W_0(S) = S.trigger
W_(j+1)(S) =
  ConcatTelescope(W_j(S),S.ports[j].derived_output)

k_j = S.ports[j].operation_ordinal
P_j = V.operations[k_j].parameters
R_j = V.operations[k_j].results
pi_j = QProject(GE(G,V),W_j(S)).
```

The semantic descent algebra is exact:

```text
ContextAt(C):
  interpret as TelescopeAt(EmptyContext,ContextTelescope(C))

TelescopeAt(G,T), field i:
  emit PrefixExpected(G,T,i)
  child -> TermAt(G ++ T[0..i],TypeFormation)

SubstitutionAt(s), image i:
  child -> TermAt(s.delta,HasType(Expected(s,i)))

SectionAt(base,T,a), image i:
  child -> TermAt(base.delta,HasType(PairExpected(base,T,a,i)))

ActivationSectionAt(base,T,a):
  descend as SectionAt(base,T,a) and mark every image for the
  ActivationImage overlay

FamilyAt(f,InterfaceFamilyIndex(f.gamma,f.a)):
  f.a -> TelescopeAt(f.gamma,f.a)
  f.b -> TelescopeAt(AppendTelescope(f.gamma,f.a),f.b)

ComparisonAt(q):
  q.left  -> TelescopeAt(q.gamma,q.left)
  q.right -> TelescopeAt(q.gamma,q.right)
  validate q.route; do not walk q.gamma or route metadata

GrammarAt(G,V,gpath,SealedPublicGrammarIndex(G)):
  exports -> TelescopeAt(G,V.exports)
  operation k parameters -> TelescopeAt(GE(G,V),P_k)
  operation k results ->
    TelescopeAt(AppendTelescope(GE(G,V),P_k),R_k)

SchemeAt(G,V,gpath,S,DemandSchemeIndex(G,V)):
  trigger -> TelescopeAt(GE(G,V),S.trigger)
  port j arguments -> SectionAt(pi_j,P_j,S.ports[j].arguments)
  port j derived_output ->
    TelescopeAt(AppendTelescope(GE(G,V),W_j(S)),
                S.ports[j].derived_output)
  contract -> ContractAt(
    AppendTelescope(GE(G,V),W_m(S)),contract)

ActivationAt(G,V,gpath,S,a):
  descend as ActivationSectionAt(QId(GE(G,V)),S.trigger,a)

LiveAt(G,V,gpath,S,a,L):
  first validate ActivationAt(G,V,gpath,S,a)
  demanded_telescope -> TelescopeAt(GE(G,V),L.demanded_telescope)
  specialized contract -> ContractAt(
    AppendTelescope(GE(G,V),L.demanded_telescope),contract)

DischargeAt(DischargeIndex(G,V,S,a,L),d):
  d.section ->
    SectionAt(QId(GE(G,V)),L.demanded_telescope,d.section)

ContractAt(C,c):
  c.ty    -> TermAt(C,TypeFormation)
  c.left  -> TermAt(C,HasType(c.ty))
  c.right -> TermAt(C,HasType(c.ty)).
```

The contract support incidences remain, in their separate local edge order,
`TypeFormation`, `DefinitionallyEqualAt(c.ty)`, and
`DefinitionallyEqualAt(c.ty)`. The term-checking state above authorizes the
children; it does not change those support expected judgments.

Only edges 6, 7, and 11--13 consume carrier slots. Their full A3 ordinals are
not vector-local ordinals:

```text
FamilyIndex:
  index = InterfaceFamilyIndex(f.gamma,f.a)
  ordinal(a[i]) = checked_u32(i)
  ordinal(b[i]) = checked_u32(a.len+i)

GrammarExport:
  index = SealedPublicGrammarIndex(G)
  ordinal(export[i]) = checked_u32(i)

SchemeTrigger/PortArgument/PortDerivedOutput:
  index = DemandSchemeIndex(G,V)
  port_base(j) =
    S.trigger.len
    + sum_(r<j)(S.ports[r].arguments.len
                + S.ports[r].derived_output.len)
  ordinal(trigger[i]) = checked_u32(i)
  ordinal(port[j].arguments[i]) = checked_u32(port_base(j)+i)
  ordinal(port[j].derived_output[i]) =
    checked_u32(port_base(j)+S.ports[j].arguments.len+i).
```

Every sum is evaluated in checked `u64` before `u32` conversion. The flattened
ordinal map must be exactly the A3 `PublicLeaves` map of the complete indexed
carrier; a missing, repeated, or record-local replacement aborts.

**Root-0 seeds.** Let `L=[SP(c)]`. The following is a total field map; skipped
scalar metadata has no state:

```text
c=0 Formation:
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(1) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,a)
  L/RF(2) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,implementation_a)

c=1 Abstraction:
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(1) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,a)
  L/RF(2) -> TelescopeAt(gamma ++ a,b)
  L/RF(3) -> ContextAt(implementation_xi)
  L/RF(5) -> TelescopeAt(implementation_xi,implementation_b)

c=2 Aggregation:
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(1) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,a)
  L/RF(2) -> TelescopeAt(gamma ++ a,b)
  L/RF(3) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,implementation_c)

c=3 Transport:
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(delta)
  L/RF(1) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(2) -> SubstitutionAt(theta)
  L/RF(3) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,a)
  L/RF(4) -> TelescopeAt(delta,implementation_c)

c=4 Comparison:
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(1) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,a)
  L/RF(2) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,b)
  L/RF(3) -> ComparisonAt(comparison)
  L/RF(4) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,implementation_a)
  L/RF(5) -> TelescopeAt(gamma,implementation_b)

c=5 DemandCompiler, gpath=(CanonicalPayload,Direct(L/RF(1))):
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(1) -> GrammarAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,
                       SealedPublicGrammarIndex(gamma))
  L/RF(2) -> SchemeAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,
                      DemandSchemeIndex(gamma,grammar))
  L/RF(3) -> ActivationAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,activation)
  L/RF(4) -> LiveAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,activation,live)
  L/RF(5) -> ActivationAt(
    gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,implementation_activation)

c=6 DischargeTransformer,
    gpath=(CanonicalPayload,Direct(L/RF(3))):
  L/RF(0) -> ContextAt(delta)
  L/RF(1) -> ContextAt(gamma)
  L/RF(2) -> SubstitutionAt(theta)
  L/RF(3) -> GrammarAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,
                       SealedPublicGrammarIndex(gamma))
  L/RF(4) -> SchemeAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,
                      DemandSchemeIndex(gamma,grammar))
  L/RF(5) -> ActivationAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,activation)
  L/RF(6) -> LiveAt(gamma,grammar,gpath,scheme,activation,live)
  L/RF(7) -> DischargeAt(
    DischargeIndex(gamma,grammar,scheme,activation,live),discharge).
```

For `c=6,L/RF(8)`, reconstruct exactly

```text
G_prime = ReindexGrammar(theta,grammar)
S_prime = ReindexScheme(theta,scheme)
theta_E = QIteratedLift(theta,grammar.exports).result
a_prime = ReindexSection(theta_E,activation)
L_prime = Specialize(S_prime,a_prime)
```

and seed

```text
DischargeAt(
  DischargeIndex(delta,G_prime,S_prime,a_prime,L_prime),
  implementation_discharge).
```

No edge inside a discharge body requires a grammar path.

**Root-1 and root-2 seeds.** Every maximal body consumes its exact
subsection-10.4.3 occurrence pack, equivalently:

```text
PI(G;A)       -> TelescopeAt(G,A)
PC(G;Unit)    -> terminal after complete index/value validation
IF(G,A;f)     -> FamilyAt(f,InterfaceFamilyIndex(G,A))
Sub(D,G;s)    -> SubstitutionAt(s)
Cmp(G,A,B;q)  -> ComparisonAt(q)
Gram(G;V)     -> GrammarAt(
  G,V,exact body path,SealedPublicGrammarIndex(G))
Scheme(G,V;S) -> SchemeAt(
  G,V,exact selected grammar path,S,DemandSchemeIndex(G,V))
Live(G,V,S,a;L) ->
  LiveAt(G,V,exact selected grammar path,S,a,L)
Dch(G,V,S,a,L;d) ->
  DischargeAt(DischargeIndex(G,V,S,a,L),d).
```

For root 1, an indices grammar declaration uses its own exact body path; an
indices scheme/live/activation uses that indices grammar; a nominal-input
grammar declaration uses its own body path; and a nominal-input scheme uses the
selected indices grammar. Every telescope base is the displayed pack base,
including Abstraction `b` over `gamma_a`. For root 2, Formation/Aggregation
outputs are over selected indices `gamma`; Abstraction uses `FamilyAt`;
Transport output is over `delta`; Comparison endpoints are over `gamma`;
Demand output uses the exact root-1 indices grammar path; and Discharge output
uses the reconstructed `G_prime/S_prime/a_prime/L_prime` index above. No
equal-byte search chooses a repeated copy.

More explicitly, every root-1 direct `NormalizedContextV1` field, including
Abstraction `gamma_a`, seeds `ContextAt(the stored context)` and every `Unit` is
terminal. Demand indices activation seeds
`ActivationAt(gamma,grammar,indices_gpath,scheme,activation)`. Discharge indices
activation and live seed, respectively,
`ActivationAt(gamma,grammar,indices_gpath,scheme,activation)` and
`LiveAt(gamma,grammar,indices_gpath,scheme,activation,live)`. The Discharge
nominal source seeds
`DischargeAt(DischargeIndex(gamma,grammar,scheme,activation,live),discharge)`.
These are the only root-1 direct section/live exceptions to the maximal-body
pack table.

**Root-3 and term frames.** An explicit `OpenJudgment` context `C` seeds:

```text
TypeFormation:
  context -> ContextAt(C)
  term    -> TermAt(C,TypeFormation)

HasType:
  context -> ContextAt(C)
  term    -> TermAt(C,HasType(type))
  type    -> TermAt(C,TypeFormation).
```

`WalkTerm(L,TermAt(C,J))` uses the frozen JG2b2b0 child-frame function:

```text
Var(i):
  require i selects exactly one declaration in C
  expected = HasType(
    exact capture-safe lookup/weakening of that declaration type)

Global(o):
  require the exact Q1-authenticated Sigma(H,o) declaration
  expected = HasType(its complete normalized type)

TermChild(e):
  child state = JG2ChildFrame(C,J,term,e).
```

`JG2ChildFrame` extends `C` only for a `Pi` body, `Sigma` body, or `Lambda`
body. A parameter/type child and every nonbinding child use the unextended
frame. For a body child, let `q` be the unique source occurrence attached to
the parent node by the term-provenance product above; append, respectively,

```text
(HistoricalBinder(q,PiBody),     complete Pi parameter)
(HistoricalBinder(q,SigmaBody),  complete Sigma parameter)
(HistoricalBinder(q,LambdaBody), complete Lambda parameter type).
```

Q1 must authenticate the parent kind, the parameter sibling, the body child,
and this exact binder-entering edge. The complete type is transported with the
identity through the same capture-safe operation that produced the child term.
A missing or second distinct `q`, frame/type length mismatch, or
non-kernel-checkable child state aborts.

**Root-4 virtual seeds.** The leading direct predicate has no ambient child.
An exact `Projection` descriptor dispatches on its closed obligation result:

- `LocalContextPrefix` request has no term child; `TypeView` request seeds
  `ContextAt(target_context)`; and `TermView` request seeds both
  `ContextAt(target_context)` and
  `TermAt(target_context,TypeFormation)` for its `expected_type`;
- checked context uses `ContextAt(normalized_context)`;
- checked type view uses its target context and `TermAt(context,TypeFormation)`;
- checked term view uses its target context, validates the expected type under
  `TypeFormation`, and uses `TermAt(context,HasType(expected_type))` for the
  normalized term;
- normalized substitution/composition recursively uses `SubstitutionAt` for
  each stored substitution;
- an iterated lift uses `TelescopeAt(base.gamma,target)`, checks each codomain
  parameter under `step.source.gamma`, each reindexed domain parameter under
  `step.source.delta`, and uses `SubstitutionAt` for every stored substitution;
- carrier formation uses `CarrierPackSeed`: it selects exactly the
  subsection-10.4.3 role body inside `requirement.value`, applies the complete
  nine-case pack table above (including terminal `PC(G;Unit)`), and treats the
  index and every sibling field as validation/ambient data rather than blind
  traversal children;
- carrier compatibility visits dependencies and then target, each through its
  stored formation pack;
- telescope comparison uses the stored base and exact left/right prefix
  contexts;
- output agreement uses `TelescopeAt(base,_)`, the exact `DischargeIndex`
  components, or, for `DemandActivation`,
  `ActivationSectionAt(QId(base),target,_)` for both `implementation` and
  `specified`; and
- binding-only and scalar metadata add no ambient child.

A root-4 `SupportProjectionRequirement` uses the primary-route table below for
each physical descriptor occurrence. It never emits edge 20.

**Footprint-erased primary resolver.** Neither S1b2 nor S1b3 may consume the
full `QPrimarySubjects.root_projection`: its source candidate contains the
four-field subject-footprint projection. Instead compile this exact erased
resolver state directly from the retained non-footprint coordinates:

```text
QuotientSupportEnvelopeCorePlanCandidateV1 =
  (match_premise:QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1,
   evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
   canonical_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   principal_lineage:QuotientPrincipalRootLineageCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportPrimaryResolverPlanCandidateV1 =
  (source_candidate_core:QuotientSupportEnvelopeCorePlanCandidateV1,
   selector_inputs:QuotientSelectorSubjectInputsCandidateV1,
   action_constants:QuotientSubjectActionConstantsCandidateV1,
   program_skeleton:
     vector<QuotientSubjectEndpointProgramCandidateV1>)

QuotientSupportNoFootprintRootFramePlanCandidateV1 =
  (canonical_payload:QuotientNormalizedSchemaPayloadCandidateV1,
   indexed_inputs:IndexedInputsV1,
   indexed_output:IndexedOutputV1,
   public_fields:vector<NormalizedPublicFieldV1>,
   obligations:QuotientObligationsCandidateV1,
   primary_resolver:QuotientSupportPrimaryResolverPlanCandidateV1,
   action_arguments:QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1).

QuotientSupportPrivateHistoryAuthorityPlan(Q1) =
  a borrowed, nonencoded capability owned by one VerifiedEnvelopeCensusV1
  whose exact quotient binding projection is Q1 and which exposes only:
    Sigma(H,o) declaration/type lookup;
    current-occurrence and descendant authentication;
    binder-entering occurrence and binding-origin lookup;
    strictly older public DeclarationField export authentication; and
    the exact ClStep_b and least-fixed-point Cl_b occurrence relations.

QuotientSupportResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1 =
  (q1:QuotientBindingCandidateV1,
   q2:QuotientGroupTwoRealizationsCandidateV1,
   lineage:QuotientCandidateLineageBaseCandidateV1,
   obligations:QuotientObligationsCandidateV1,
   private_history:QuotientSupportPrivateHistoryAuthorityPlan(q1),
   support_frame:QuotientSupportNoFootprintRootFramePlanCandidateV1)

require support_frame =
  CompileSupportNoFootprintRootFrame(q1,q2,lineage,obligations)

CompileSupportNoFootprintRootFrame(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QObligations):
  require the closed Q1/Q2/QLineage/QObligations consistency equations;
  c = Q1.constructor
    = variant_tag(Q2.canonical_payload)
    = QObligations.constructor;
  construct support_frame with:

support_frame.canonical_payload = Q2.canonical_payload
support_frame.indexed_inputs    = Q2.indexed_inputs
support_frame.indexed_output    = Q2.indexed_output
support_frame.public_fields     = Q2.normalized_public_fields
support_frame.obligations       = QObligations
support_frame.primary_resolver  =
  CompileSupportPrimaryResolver(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QObligations)
support_frame.action_arguments  = I(c);
return support_frame.
```

`private_history` is a capability, not a canonical record, handle ordinal, or
caller-supplied value. Its owner validates the complete profile, parent,
history, evaluation, anchor, and constructor identities against `q1` before
exposing any operation. The `VerifiedEnvelopeCensusV1` ownership invariant says
that two such capabilities with byte-identical complete `q1` identities expose
the same typed relations; a digest or lookup hint cannot establish that
