equality. The capability is erased before publication and contains no
`QFootprints`, representative support, R2c support coordinate, receipt, or R3
account.

`CompileSupportPrimaryResolver(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QObligations)` reconstructs the
admission premise, descriptor core, selector inputs, and two empty actions by
the existing QG2 equations with the footprint field omitted. It recompiles the
endpoint templates, source-path literal occurrences, value-node types, and
strict-earlier project graph directly from `c`, `Q2`, and the frozen
`QPrimaryRouteRoles`; it does not call `CompileQSourceBundle` or
`CompileQPrimarySubjects`. It requires the same ordered route census and
physical descriptor multiplicities. The skeleton contains path plans and
symbolic node types, never a resolved source bundle or descriptor value.

```text
CompileSupportPrimaryProgramSkeleton(Q1,Q2)
  : vector<QuotientSubjectEndpointProgramCandidateV1> =
  require c=Q1.constructor=variant_tag(Q2.canonical_payload);
  for every valid slot in ascending order:
    emit the exact closed binder template;
    emit the exact value-node template in strict postorder;
    replace each source locator, in occurrence order, by
      RoutePlan(the next QPrimaryRouteRoles[c][slot] member);
    infer and validate every value_node_type;
    require the route vector, subject kind, strict-earlier operands,
            value count, and binder count equal the closed QG2 equations;
  return the ordered program vector.
```

This compiler performs no source dereference. The resolver record requires
`program_skeleton=CompileSupportPrimaryProgramSkeleton(Q1,Q2)` byte-for-byte.
Binder templates use `Q1.evaluation_identity` exactly where the closed QG2
template requires `PresentedEnvelope`; no source bundle is consulted.

`ResolveSupportQPrimarySourceDecorated(authority,d,reached_steps)` returns the
exact `QuotientSupportDecoratedNodeStatePlanCandidateV1(R,T)` fixed above. It
is total on the compiler image and is not a stored function field of the
resolver record. `authority.support_frame.primary_resolver`
selects the physical route, while `authority.q1/q2/lineage/obligations` supply
the retained semantic value and lineage coordinate and
`authority.private_history` authenticates every occurrence, descendant,
binder entry, and older export under the dependent Q1 identity. Together they
supply the field/output origin frame, binder frame, and term provenance. Its
ambient and origin-frame components are the exact product lifts fixed elsewhere
in this subsection.

The route evaluator has this exhaustive observation boundary:

```text
WholeSourceCandidate at reached_steps=[] ->
  SchemaOnly at QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1
WholeSourceCandidate at reached_steps=[RF(3)]++s ->
  Semantic(the exact descendant s of Q2.canonical_payload)
WholeSourceCandidate at every other reached path -> abort

SelectorMatchPremise at [] ->
  SchemaOnly at QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1
SelectorCurrentDomain at [] ->
  SchemaOnly at vector<OccurrenceId14V1>
SelectorAnchor at [] -> Semantic(Q1 anchor)
SubstitutionIdentity|ContextSquareIdentity at [] ->
  SchemaOnly at QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1

every normalized telescope/substitution/grammar/scheme/section/live/contract
route -> Semantic(the exact value reconstructed by its closed Q2 operation)
every other route or descendant -> abort.
```

Every `SchemaOnly` branch first validates the complete underlying
non-footprint source equation, but does not construct or retain that value.
`SupportSchemaAt` returns its displayed type. `SupportValueAt` is defined only
on `Semantic(v)` and returns `v`; applying it to `SchemaOnly` aborts.
`LineageOrigin` and `ValueTerminal` are `None` for an admitted `SchemaOnly`
view. The occurrence endpoint is deliberately semantic because its exact `q`
is needed for history ownership. A project consumes only
`source_candidate_core.canonical_payload` and the decorated operation
products; it never materializes the omitted footprint field. Thus no resolver,
sidecar, ambient, or graph-core input contains `QFootprints` or a whole
footprint-bearing descriptor.

For every admitted bare support path `p`, define the total indexed resolver

```text
SupportViewAtBare(
  authority,
  p:QuotientSupportBarePathPlanCandidateV1)
  : SupportNodeView(TypeAt(p.root,p.locus)).

SupportViewAtLowered(
  authority,
  p:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1)
  : SupportNodeView(SchemaOf(p.result_schema)) =
  require SchemaOf(p.result_schema)=TypeAt(p.root,p.locus);
  return SupportViewAtBare(authority,(p.root,p.locus)).
```

Roots 0--4 return `Semantic` values from the displayed footprint-free frame
fields and their decorated obligation resolver. Root 5 uses the exact literal,
project, and `PrimaryProjectGrammar` functions above; the grammar companion is
always `Semantic`. Root 6 validates its identity action and has no admitted
dependency path. The function has no default case. `SupportValueAt` is the
projection of `x.dependency_view`, after requiring it equals
`SupportViewAtLowered(coordinates,Bound(r,Dependency))` in the matching
preflight vector.

**Root-5 dereferenced seeds.** `AmbientOfPrimaryRoute` is total over that
footprint-erased resolver:

| Exact primary route | Ambient state |
| --- | --- |
| `WholeSourceCandidate` | walk its `canonical_payload` by the root-0 table, prefixing every reached path by descriptor `RF(3)` |
| match premise, occurrence vector, occurrence | terminal |
| either identity action | terminal empty action |
| `AbstractionLiftTargetA` | `TelescopeAt(Q.gamma,Q.a)` |
| `AggregationLiftTargetA` | `TelescopeAt(Q.gamma,Q.a)` |
| `TransportCompositeThetaSource` | `SubstitutionAt(Q.theta)` |
| `DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports` | `TelescopeAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar.exports)` |
| `DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource` | `SubstitutionAt(Q.theta)` |
| `DischargeReindexedGrammarSource` | `GrammarAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,(descriptor_root,Dereferenced(this_descriptor,[])),SealedPublicGrammarIndex(Q.gamma))` |
| `DischargeReindexedSchemeSource` | `SchemeAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,gpath_G,Q.scheme,DemandSchemeIndex(Q.gamma,Q.grammar))` |
| `DischargeReindexedActivationSource` | `ActivationAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,gpath_G,Q.scheme,Q.activation)` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveSource` | `LiveAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,gpath_G,Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live)` |
| `DischargeReindexedLiveContractSource(i)` | `ContractAt(AppendTelescope(GE(Q.gamma,Q.grammar),Q.live.demanded_telescope),Q.live.specialized_contracts[i])` |

Here `gpath_G` is the exact dereferenced occurrence corresponding to the same
slot-7, value-node-6, nested-path-0
`DischargeReindexedGrammarSource` locator. Under root 5 it is that program's
physical descriptor; under a root-4 preservation vector it is the exact vector
ordinal generated from the same locator in that preservation-row copy. This
mapping also covers root-5 formal-action descriptor nodes 7, 8, 9, and `10+i`.
It is never chosen by grammar-byte equality.

**Root-5 project seeds.** Let `X` be the exact value of the project's checked
strict-earlier envelope expression and `Q=X.canonical_payload`. The nine roles
map exhaustively:

| Project role | Ambient state |
| --- | --- |
| `FormationOutput` | `TelescopeAt(Q.gamma,Q.a)` |
| `AbstractionOutput` | `FamilyAt(NormalizedFamilyValueV1(Q.gamma,Q.a,Q.b),InterfaceFamilyIndex(Q.gamma,Q.a))` |
| `AggregationOutput` | `TelescopeAt(Q.gamma,ConcatTelescope(Q.a,Q.b))` |
| `TransportOutput` | `TelescopeAt(Q.delta,ReindexTelescope(Q.theta,Q.a))` |
| `ComparisonOutput` | `ComparisonAt(Q.comparison)` |
| `DemandCompilerOutput` | `LiveAt(Q.gamma,Q.grammar,(PrimarySubjects,PrimaryProjectGrammar(project_node,[])),Q.scheme,Q.activation,Q.live)` |
| `DischargeTransformerOutput` | `DischargeAt(DischargeIndex(Q.delta,G_prime,S_prime,a_prime,L_prime),reindexed discharge)` |
| `TransportNormalizedImplementation` | `TelescopeAt(Q.delta,Q.implementation_c)` |
| `DischargeTransformerNormalizedImplementation` | `DischargeAt(DischargeIndex(Q.delta,G_prime,S_prime,a_prime,L_prime),Q.implementation_discharge)` |

The primed values are reconstructed by the same exact equations used for the
root-0 Discharge implementation. Thus every root-0--5 direct, dereferenced, and
projected semantic occurrence has a deterministic ambient. Resolution aborts
on zero or several states; a wrong index/tag/base/target; a missing primary
locator or grammar companion; nonidentical repeated companions; malformed port
ordinal or `W_j`; failed `QProject`, `PairExpected`, reindex, specialization,
kernel check, or arithmetic; or any equality search used in place of an
occurrence token. Root 6 has no emitted incidence and requires no ambient.

The decorated virtual resolvers used by `LineageOrigin` therefore return the
product `(view,ambient,binder_environment,term_provenance,origin_frame)`.
Only a matched `Semantic(value)` view projects a value; `SchemaOnly` carries
only its indexed schema. Any earlier shorter tuple notation is a projection of
this one after the omitted components have been carried, checked, and retained;
no component is reconstructed after resolution.

#### 10.13.4 Closed local semantic walker

For value paths in this subsection, abbreviate

```text
RF(i) = RecordField(i)
VE(i) = VectorElement(checked_u32(i))
SP(t) = SumPayload(t)
TC(e) = TermChild(e).
```

Entering a selected sum payload, record, vector, vector element, carrier node,
or term constructor conceptually emits `Visit`. Scalar tags, ordinals, vector
lengths, and path metadata do not. At each visited node the compiler emits its
complete local incidence vector, preserving multiplicity, and only then walks
children in declared record, element, or JG2b2b0 term-edge order. There is no
default case, sorting, semantic deduplication, or equality-guided descent.

At a term locus `L`, the table is exact:

| Term node | Local incidences, followed by child order |
| --- | --- |
| `Var` | one `LocalBinderUse=1`; no child |
| `Global` | one `GlobalUse=2`; no child |
| `Pi` | `TermChild=21(PiParameter)`, then `TermChild=21(PiBody)`; recurse parameter, body |
| `Sigma` | `TermChild=21(SigmaParameter)`, then `TermChild=21(SigmaBody)`; recurse parameter, body |
| `Lambda` | `TermChild=21(LambdaParameterType)`, then `TermChild=21(LambdaBody)`; recurse parameter type, body |
| `Apply` | `TermChild=21(ApplyFunction)`, then `TermChild=21(ApplyArgument)`; recurse function, argument |
| `Pair` | `TermChild=21(PairSigmaType)`, `TermChild=21(PairFirst)`, `TermChild=21(PairSecond)`; recurse in that order |
| `First` | one `TermChild=21(FirstPair)`; recurse pair |
| `Second` | one `TermChild=21(SecondPair)`; recurse pair |
| `Sort`, `UnitType`, `Unit` | empty |

Every `TermChild(e)` dependency is located at `L/TC(e)`, not the parent. Its
expected judgment is the unique frozen JG2b2b0 kernel expectation for that
child. A missing or multiply applicable expectation aborts. Each child is then
visited separately, so a child `Var` or `Global` produces its own later row.

Contexts and telescopes use the prefix-field rule of subsection 10.13.1. For
each oldest-first field `i`, they emit edge 3 at `L/VE(i)` and then walk that
term. A family `f=(gamma,a,b)` has an additional record-local vector:

1. emit `FamilyIndex=6` at every `f.a/VE(i)`, oldest first;
2. emit `FamilyIndex=6` at every `f.b/VE(i)`, oldest first; and
3. recurse through `a` and then `b`.

`gamma` is validated as ambient/index data and is not walked from inside the
family record; no `gamma` field receives an incidence there. Each edge-6
judgment is

```text
CarrierSlot(
  InterfaceFamilyIndex(f.gamma,f.a),
  the exact full A3 PublicLeaves flattened ordinal).
```

Thus all family edge-6 rows precede the descendant edge-3 and term rows at the
same physical term loci.

Every section image receives `SectionExpectedType=4`, in image order, by the
ambient state that admitted its parent:

```text
SubstitutionAt(s):
  HasType(Expected(s,i))

SectionAt(base,T,a) or ActivationSectionAt(base,T,a):
  HasType(PairExpected(base,T,a,i)).
```

The exact substitution or base substitution, target telescope, and length are
mandatory. Edges 5, 14, and 18 copy the corresponding edge-4 judgment
byte-for-byte. Additional
roles follow the parent/child boundary rather than a global re-sort:

- a substitution record emits all `SubstitutionImage=5` rows before descending
  into its section, whose child visit later emits edge 4;
- an activation section carries both roles locally, so its stable local merge
  is all edge-4 rows followed by all `ActivationImage=14` rows;
- a port record emits its selector edge 8, then all `PortArgument=12` and
  `PortDerivedOutput=13` rows before descending into its argument section and
  derived telescope; and
- a discharge record emits every `DischargeImage=18` row before descending
  into its section, whose child visit later emits edge 4.

Consequently every section image retains edge 4 as well as any substitution,
activation, port, or discharge overlay.

The remaining normalized records have these exact local vectors and walks:

- a substitution validates `delta` and `gamma` as endpoints, emits every
  `SubstitutionImage=5` at `images/VE(i)`, and walks only the `images` section;
  it does not recursively walk either endpoint context;
- a comparison validates `gamma` and the singleton route, then walks `left`
  followed by `right`; it does not recursively walk `gamma` or route metadata;
- a grammar emits `GrammarExport=7` for all exports in order, then walks the
  export telescope; for each operation ordinal it emits the operation-local
  rows below and walks parameters then results;
- a scheme emits `SchemeTrigger=11` for all trigger fields and walks the
  trigger; each port emits its retained selector edge 8, all edge-12 argument
  rows, and all edge-13 derived-output rows before the child walks; contracts
  follow ports;
- a live demand emits one selector edge 8 at each
  `inherited_operation_ordinals[j]` scalar, in vector order, then walks the
  demanded telescope and specialized contracts;
- a contract emits `ContractType=15` at `RF(2)`, `ContractLeft=16` at `RF(0)`,
  and `ContractRight=17` at `RF(1)`, in that edge order, then walks type, left,
  and right; their expected judgments are `TypeFormation`,
  `DefinitionallyEqualAt(type)`, and `DefinitionallyEqualAt(type)`.

The discharge walk is exactly the earlier overlay rule; this paragraph adds no
second edge-18 vector.

Edges 7 and 11--13 use the complete carrier index and exact full A3
`PublicLeaves` flattened ordinal. A record-local or vector-local ordinal cannot
replace it. All normalized contexts, telescopes, sections, substitutions,
families, comparisons, grammars, schemes, live demands, contracts, discharges,
and terms not reached by the root tables below are off-image; this list is an
exhaustive dispatch, not a permissive recursive-record convention.

The nominal expected-judgment dispatch is total:

| Direct edge | Exact nominal expected judgment |
| --- | --- |
| `LocalBinderUse=1` | `HasType(the complete binder type derived at this term path)` |
| `GlobalUse=2` | `HasType(the complete normalized global type from the Q1-authenticated signature view)` |
| `TelescopePrefix=3` | `PrefixExpected(base,X,i)` from subsection 10.13.1 |
| `SectionExpectedType=4` | the exact image `HasType` displayed above |
| `SubstitutionImage=5`, `ActivationImage=14`, `DischargeImage=18` | the byte-identical edge-4 image `HasType` at the same term locus |
| `FamilyIndex=6` | `CarrierSlot(InterfaceFamilyIndex(gamma,a),full flattened ordinal)` |
| `GrammarExport=7` | `CarrierSlot(SealedPublicGrammarIndex(gamma),full flattened ordinal)` |
| `OperationAnchor=8`, `OperationParameter=9`, `OperationResult=10` | `NominalOperationOrdinal(GrammarCompanion,k)` |
| `SchemeTrigger=11`, `PortArgument=12`, `PortDerivedOutput=13` | `CarrierSlot(DemandSchemeIndex(gamma,grammar),full flattened ordinal)` |
| `ContractType=15` | `TypeFormation` |
| `ContractLeft=16`, `ContractRight=17` | `DefinitionallyEqualAt(the complete contract type)` |
| `ObligationPremise=19` | `LiftQExpected(entry.expected_judgment)` |
| `TheoremEndpoint=20` | the exact five-row root-5 table below |
| `TermChild=21(e)` | the unique frozen JG2b2b0 child expectation |

The binder/global type is computed in the same checked typing environment that
authorizes the selected normalized term; a de Bruijn lookup, global lookup, or
normal-form mismatch aborts. The three image-overlay equations are byte
equality requirements, not independently chosen expected types.

#### 10.13.5 Exact operation companions

For a grammar at bare path `gpath` and checked operation ordinal `k`, define

```text
opath = gpath / [RF(1),VE(k)].
```

A grammar declaration emits at the operation record, before walking its
fields:

```text
OperationAnchor=8:
  Dependency               = opath
  GrammarCompanion          = gpath
  OperationRecordCompanion = opath
  nominal_expected          = NominalOperationOrdinal(
                                GrammarCompanion,k).
```

It then emits one `OperationParameter=9` at
`opath/[RF(0),VE(i)]` per parameter and one `OperationResult=10` at
`opath/[RF(1),VE(i)]` per result. Each row exposes the same `gpath` and `opath`,
uses `OperationSlot(gpath,k,Parameter|Result,i)`, and retains
`NominalOperationOrdinal(GrammarCompanion,k)`. Parameter rows precede result rows; their child
terms are visited afterwards.

A scheme port selector or live-demand inherited selector has its exact scalar
field as `Dependency`, but the same complete `gpath` and derived `opath` as
companions. Selector repeats remain repeated dependencies. Companion selection
is closed:

1. a grammar declaration uses its enclosing grammar;
2. a complete carrier value uses its local grammar child;
3. root-0 Demand uses `[SP(5),RF(1)]`;
4. root-0 Discharge uses `[SP(6),RF(3)]`;
5. root-1 Demand/Discharge indices use their selected indices grammar;
6. a root-1 nominal-input scheme uses that selected indices grammar;
7. a root-2 Demand output uses that selected indices grammar; and
8. a `Dereferenced` virtual route preserves its exact semantic origin:
   schema-payload roles use the payload grammar, indexed-input/output roles use
   the selected indices grammar, complete carrier requirements use their local
   grammar, and a Discharge reindexed-live route uses the separately occurring
   reindexed-grammar descriptor fixed by the paired primary-route table; and
9. a `PrimaryProject` route emits an operation selector only for
   `DemandCompilerOutput`. If its physical project-node path is `n`, bind

   ```text
   gpath = (PrimarySubjects,PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,[]))
   opath = (PrimarySubjects,
            PrimaryProjectGrammar(n,[RF(1),VE(k)])).
   ```

   Both bindings evaluate the same checked earlier descriptor expression as
   the project, including any formal reindexing. No other project role admits a
   grammar companion.

All available companion copies must agree by canonical bytes. A missing
companion, multiple nonidentical copies, wrong constructor/tag, out-of-bounds
`k`, a nonempty/other `PrimaryProjectGrammar` suffix, or a selected operation
record unequal to `operations[k]` aborts. A pure scheme or live-demand body is
never allowed to reconstruct its ambient grammar from the body bytes.

#### 10.13.6 Exact seven-root pass-A traversal

The root stream is the concatenation for root tags `0..6`. Within a root it is
the preorder and local incidence order fixed above. Every checked `u32`
conversion occurs before the affected incidence is emitted; overflow produces
no prefix of a stream.

**Root 0 — `CanonicalPayload`.** For constructor `c`, enter `SP(c)` and select
the following `RF(f)` children in displayed order:

| `c` | Exact selected semantic fields in record order |
| ---: | --- |
| `Formation=0` | `gamma`, `a`, `implementation_a` |
| `Abstraction=1` | `gamma`, `a`, `b`, `implementation_xi`, skip scalar `cut`, `implementation_b` |
| `Aggregation=2` | `gamma`, `a`, `b`, `implementation_c` |
| `Transport=3` | `delta`, `gamma`, `theta`, `a`, `implementation_c` |
| `Comparison=4` | `gamma`, `a`, `b`, `comparison`, `implementation_a`, `implementation_b` |
| `DemandCompiler=5` | `gamma`, `grammar`, `scheme`, `activation`, `live`, `implementation_activation` |
| `DischargeTransformer=6` | `delta`, `gamma`, `theta`, `grammar`, `scheme`, `activation`, `live`, `discharge`, `implementation_discharge` |

The field ordinal is its declaration ordinal in subsection 9.2; skipping
`cut=RF(4)` does not renumber Abstraction's later field. Every maximal carrier
body uses the exact occurrence role and index/value pack of subsection 10.4.3.
No ambient index or companion may be reconstructed from body bytes alone.

**Root 1 — `IndexedInputs`.** Walk the complete indices branch before the
nominal-input branch:

```text
indices        at [RF(0),SP(c),RF(f)]
nominal inputs at [RF(1),SP(c),RF(f)].
```

| `c` | Indices fields | Nominal-input fields |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| `0` | `gamma` | `here:Unit` |
| `1` | `gamma`, `a`, `gamma_a` | `here:Unit`, `b` |
| `2` | `gamma`, `a`, `b` | `family` |
| `3` | `delta`, `gamma` | `theta`, `a` |
| `4` | `gamma`, `a`, `b` | `left`, `right` |
| `5` | `gamma`, `grammar`, `scheme`, `activation` | `grammar`, `scheme` |
| `6` | `delta`, `gamma`, `grammar`, `scheme`, `activation`, `live` | `theta`, `discharge` |

Both inner sum tags must equal `c`. The exact subsection-10.4.3 packs and all
cross-root byte equalities are checked before traversal.

**Root 2 — `IndexedOutput`.** The selected output value is reached directly at
`[SP(c)]` and walked by this table:

| `c` | Exact output body |
| ---: | --- |
| `0` | public-interface `NormalizedTelescopeV1` |
| `1` | `NormalizedFamilyValueV1` |
| `2` | public-interface `NormalizedTelescopeV1` |
| `3` | public-interface `NormalizedTelescopeV1` |
| `4` | `NormalizedComparisonValueV1` |
| `5` | `NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1` |
| `6` | `NormalizedDischargeValueV1` |

Each `IndexedOutputV1` variant carries its displayed prior type directly; no
anonymous one-field record or trailing `RF(0)` exists. Output roles and packs
are exactly subsection 10.4.3.

**Root 3 — `PublicFields`.** For public-field ordinal `i`, let

```text
J_i = [VE(i),RF(2)].
```

Require the exact Q2 field count and order,
`public_fields[i].field_ordinal=checked_u32(i)`, and the exact stored
`EnvelopeFieldPathV1` and normalized-judgment bytes. The ordinal and path
fields validate the role; neither is recursively scanned. The only walks are:

```text
TypeFormation=0:
  context = J_i/[SP(0),RF(0)]
  term    = J_i/[SP(0),RF(1)]
  walk context, then term under TypeFormation

HasType=1:
  context = J_i/[SP(1),RF(0)]
  term    = J_i/[SP(1),RF(1)]
  type    = J_i/[SP(1),RF(2)]
  walk context, term under HasType(type), then type under TypeFormation.
```

`DefinitionallyEqual=2`, a missing/extra field, reordered path, mismatched
judgment, or any other inherited `OpenJudgment` form aborts.

**Root 4 — `Obligations`.** For group `g` and its entry `k`, define

```text
E(g,k) = [RF(1),VE(g),RF(1),VE(k)]
Q(g,k) = E(g,k)/RF(6).
```

Groups and entries are walked in stored order after rechecking the frozen
eight-group tags, counts, ordinals, and support path. Every entry first emits
exactly one `ObligationPremise=19`:

```text
root                  = Obligations
bare_locus            = Direct(Q(g,k))
nominal_result_schema = QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1
referent_recipe       = RecordSlot(Dependency)
owner_recipe          = StructuralTerminal(
                          Dependency,
                          RecordSlot(Dependency))
nominal_expected      = LiftQExpected(entry.expected_judgment).
```

`LiftQExpected` preserves tags and complete payloads. Its
`StructuralRecord(t)` case replaces the obligation symbolic tag by the exact
nominal node from subsection 10.3.1; it does not assign either candidate
symbolic tags or numerical type IDs. The exact group image is:

| Group | Required leading nominal expected judgment |
| ---: | --- |
| `0` | `StructuralRecord(QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1)` |
| `1` | `StructuralRecord(QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1)`, `TypeFormation`, or `HasType(expected_type)`, according to the closed call kind |
| `2` | `StructuralRecord(QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1)` or `StructuralRecord(QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1)` |
| `3` | `ContextPrefix(operation_result.gamma,CompletePrefixOrdinal(operation_result.gamma))` |
| `4` | `StructuralRecord(QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1)` or `StructuralRecord(QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1)` |
| `5` | `ConstructionRule(entry.rule_path)` |
| `6` | `StructuralOccurrence(exact_anchor)` |
| `7` | `StructuralRecord(QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1)` |

Only two predicate tags have a secondary walk:

```text
Projection=0:
  descriptor_j = Q(g,k)/[SP(0),RF(1),VE(j)]
  resolve through the corresponding exact five-view obligation source
  walk each reached value as Dereferenced(descriptor_j,reached_steps)

SupportProjectionRequirement=2:
  descriptor_j = Q(g,k)/[SP(2),RF(2),VE(j)]
  resolve through CompileSupportPrimaryResolver's exact route evaluator
  walk each reached value as Dereferenced(descriptor_j,reached_steps).
```

`OriginSingleton=1`, `SubjectWellFormed=3`, and
`ConstructionRequirement=4` have no secondary walk. A preservation descriptor
emits no edge 20: after the leading edge 19, only incidences found while
walking its reached semantic value are emitted. Descriptors remain repeated by
physical occurrence. There is no general descent into a predicate record,
source-path metadata, the evaluation identity, or the construction rule.

**Root 5 — `PrimarySubjects`.** Walk the footprint-erased `program_skeleton`,
whose physical coordinates must equal the corresponding closed QG2 root-
projection coordinates. For subject slot `s` and value-node ordinal `j`, define

```text
N(s,j) = [RF(2),VE(s),RF(2),VE(j)].
```

Walk skeleton subjects and then their value nodes in order. Do not independently
scan the forbidden full `source_bundle`, binders, `value_node_types`, the combined syntax graph,
proposition opcodes, or node-ordinal metadata.

For a `Literal=1` node, a direct source descriptor is

```text
descriptor = N(s,j)/[SP(1),SP(t),RF(0)].
```

Literal tags `CorePayloadProjection=0` and
`ExactOccurrenceProjection=2` emit `TheoremEndpoint=20` at
`Dereferenced(descriptor,[])` and then walk the reached value with descendant
reached steps. Tags 1 and 3 abort. The direct endpoint image is exactly:

| Reached nominal schema | Edge-20 nominal expected judgment |
| --- | --- |
| `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` | `StructuralRecord(the same schema)` |
| `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` | `StructuralRecord(the same schema)` |
| `vector<OccurrenceId14V1>` | `StructuralRecord(the same schema)` |
| `OccurrenceId14V1` | `StructuralOccurrence(the reached occurrence)` |
| `QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1` | `StructuralRecord(the same schema)` |

No sixth direct endpoint type is admitted.

For `FormalActionArgumentProjection=4`, let

```text
F = N(s,j)/[SP(1),SP(4),RF(0)]
C = F/[RF(1),SP(2),RF(1)].
```

Only the closed QG2 source-descriptor recursion is scanned:

| Derived component | Exact descriptor occurrences, in order |
| --- | --- |
| `IteratedLift=0` | `C/[SP(0),RF(1)]` |
| `OrderedComposite=1` | left then right; a `SourceProjection=0` component reference uses `C/[SP(1),RF(b),SP(0),RF(0)]`, `b=0,1` |
| `ReconstructedEpsilon=2` | none |
| `ReindexedPayloadComponent=3` | `C/[SP(3),RF(0)]` |
| `EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint=4` | none |

`PrimitiveArgument` and `EarlierDerived` component references add no source
descriptor. Every listed descriptor is dereferenced and semantically walked,
but formal-action literal tag 4 emits no theorem-endpoint or action edge.

For a `Project=2` node, require its source ordinal to select a strict earlier
node, re-evaluate the exact one of nine semantic projection roles, and require
the result to equal `value_node_types[j]`. Walk the reconstructed result as

```text
PrimaryProject(N(s,j),reached_steps).
```

If and only if that role is `DemandCompilerOutput`, every operation-selector
row reached inside the projected live demand binds its grammar and operation
record exactly as

```text
GrammarCompanion =
  (PrimarySubjects,PrimaryProjectGrammar(N(s,j),[]))
OperationRecordCompanion(k) =
  (PrimarySubjects,
   PrimaryProjectGrammar(N(s,j),[RF(1),VE(k)])).
```

The project evaluator checks that both companions come from the same evaluated
earlier descriptor and that the selected ordinal is the live value's inherited
operation ordinal. No other `Project` result emits an operation row or admits
this locus.

The project-node occurrence `N(s,j)` is retained even if its reconstructed
bytes equal a literal result. It emits no edge 20. Every other opcode validates
its operands and inferred result type but has no materialized child traversal.

**Root 6 — `ActionArguments`.** Require the constructor-fixed identity:

```text
SubstitutionAction([]) for c in {0,1,2,4,5}
ContextSquareAction([]) for c in {3,6}.
```

The selected sum and empty vector may be visited for shape validation, but
they emit no dependency, support path, or path-census role. A wrong sum tag,
mixed kind, or nonempty primitive vector aborts. This is the candidate identity
root only; it says nothing about the later nonempty action image.

#### 10.13.7 Complete pass-A stream and path census

Each emitted row is exactly the subsection-10.12.3 tuple

```text
NominalDirectIncidence(
  root,
  bare_locus,
  path_bindings,
  nominal_result_schema=TypeAt(root,bare_locus),
  edge,
  nominal_expected_judgment,
  referent_recipe,
  owner_resolution_recipe).
```

`TypeAt` is computed by the typed locus evaluator; no caller claim or symbolic
tag selects traversal. Local expected judgments use the exact normalized
typing context, companion type, carrier pack, grammar path, occurrence, record
schema, or construction-rule path displayed above. Zero or several applicable
static-expected cases abort.

For one incidence, `NoFormalPathCensus` expands complete bare paths in this
field order:

1. the dependency locus;
2. support paths in the nominal expected judgment, in payload field order;
3. support-path role references in the referent recipe, in declaration order;
   and
4. support-path role references in the owner recipe, recursively in declaration
   order.

Thus an operation row records the dependency before every grammar/operation
reference made by its expected judgment, referent, and owner directive. Named
roles guarantee that all such references are byte-identical to the one exposed
companion value, but they do not deduplicate census occurrences. Equal complete
path bytes at different fields, recipes, or incidences remain repeated events.

The direct stream is enumerated with checked contiguous fresh dependency
ordinals only in pass B. It is never sorted. Across the static seven-
constructor compiler image, the direct edge-tag image is exactly
`{1,...,21}`. For one candidate, multiplicities are determined solely by its
frozen vector lengths, term shapes, obligation rows, source descriptors, and
primary endpoint nodes. Tags `0`, `22`, `23`, and `27..28` have no pass-A
image; tags `24..26` remain S1b3 closure-only.

#### 10.13.8 Generated candidate manifest: exactly seventeen nodes

For this table only, use schema-locator abbreviations

```text
sRF(i) = QuotientSupportSchemaStepPlanCandidateV1::RecordField(i)
sVE    = QuotientSupportSchemaStepPlanCandidateV1::VectorElementType
sSP(t) = QuotientSupportSchemaStepPlanCandidateV1::SumPayload(t)

P_s = sRF(1)/sVE/sRF(1)/sVE/sRF(6).
```

These are type-graph steps: unlike value paths, `sVE` carries no element
ordinal. Evaluating the complete static pass-A image and applying
`sort_unique(Canon(T))` produces `m=17` exactly:

| Tag | Generated payload-free variant | Canonical schema locator | Exact nominal schema |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | `U32` | `Root(CanonicalPayload)/sSP(1)/sRF(4)` | `u32` |
| `1` | `NormalizedGrammar` | `Root(CanonicalPayload)/sSP(5)/sRF(1)` | `NormalizedGrammarValueV1` |
| `2` | `Term` | `Root(CanonicalPayload)/sSP(0)/sRF(1)/sVE` | `Term` |
| `3` | `NormalizedGrammarOperation` | `Root(CanonicalPayload)/sSP(5)/sRF(1)/sRF(1)/sVE` | `NormalizedGrammarOperationV1` |
| `4` | `QuotientObligationPredicate` | `Root(Obligations)/P_s` | `QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1` |
| `5` | `QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligation` | `Root(Obligations)/P_s/sSP(3)` | `QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1` |
| `6` | `Occurrence` | `Root(Obligations)/sRF(1)/sVE/sRF(1)/sVE/sRF(5)/sSP(6)/sRF(0)` | `OccurrenceId14V1` |
| `7` | `OccurrenceVector` | `Root(Obligations)/P_s/sSP(2)/sRF(1)/sRF(6)` | `vector<OccurrenceId14V1>` |
| `8` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptor` | `Root(PrimarySubjects)/sRF(1)/sRF(9)` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` |
| `9` | `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremise` | `Root(PrimarySubjects)/sRF(1)/sRF(7)/sRF(0)` | `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` |
| `10` | `QuotientNormalizedAction` | `Root(PrimarySubjects)/sRF(1)/sRF(8)/sRF(0)` | `QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1` |
| `11` | `QuotientBindingRequirement` | `ObligationResult(BindingRequirement)/[]` | `QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `12` | `QuotientCheckedContext` | `ObligationResult(CheckedContext)/[]` | `QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1` |
| `13` | `QuotientCarrierFormationRequirement` | `ObligationResult(CarrierFormationRequirement)/[]` | `QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `14` | `QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirement` | `ObligationResult(CarrierCompatibilityRequirement)/[]` | `QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `15` | `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparison` | `ObligationResult(NormalizedTelescopeComparison)/[]` | `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1` |
| `16` | `QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreement` | `ObligationResult(NormalizedOutputAgreement)/[]` | `QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1` |

Accordingly the generated candidate enum is normatively:

```text
QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  U32=0
| NormalizedGrammar=1
| Term=2
| NormalizedGrammarOperation=3
| QuotientObligationPredicate=4
| QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligation=5
| Occurrence=6
| OccurrenceVector=7
| QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptor=8
| QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremise=9
| QuotientNormalizedAction=10
| QuotientBindingRequirement=11
| QuotientCheckedContext=12
| QuotientCarrierFormationRequirement=13
| QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirement=14
| QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparison=15
| QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreement=16.
```

Every variant is payload-free and `SchemaOf` is the rightmost table column.
The order follows anchor bytes, route length, and route bytes exactly; it is not
an explanatory type-family order. In particular, the invalid inherited-context
route cannot precede the displayed `Term` route.

Exhaustiveness is a finite edge partition:

- direct tags 1--7, 9--18, and 21 have exact `Term` dependency loci; the
  prefix-field adjudication is essential for tag 3;
- tag 8 has either an operation-record or `u32` selector locus and exposes the
  grammar and operation-record companions;
- tag 19 has the predicate locus and the eight-group-family leading expected
  schemas, of which the six external structural targets and the physically
  rooted subject-well-formed target are exactly the rows above;
- tag 20 has exactly the five root-5 endpoint schemas;
- no direct source, reconstruction, normalization, closure, or action edge
  contributes another type; and
- closure will inherit a forcing path and use `StructuralOccurrence`, so it
  cannot extend the table.

This proves both inclusion directions. No `NormalizedContextV1`,
`NormalizedTelescopeV1`, inherited `DependentContext`, `OpenJudgment`,
`NormalizedSectionV1`, or other normalized wrapper is a direct dependency
locus or structural-record target. Complete contexts and types remain present
inside expected-judgment payloads; their presence does not make their schemas
members of `M_nf`.

#### 10.13.9 Pass-B lowering, aborts, and phase boundary

Pass B freezes the table above once for the whole project and all seven
constructors, then lowers the already completed nominal stream:

```text
LowerPath(p=(root,locus)) =
  let T = TypeAt(root,locus);
  require Canon(T) in M_nf;
  return
    (root=root,
     locus=locus,
     result_schema=Node_(Canon(T)))

LowerResultSchema(T) =
  require Canon(T) in M_nf;
  Node_(Canon(T))

LowerExpected(NominalStructuralRecord(T),lowered_bindings) =
  StructuralRecord(LowerResultSchema(T))

LowerExpected(NominalOperationOrdinal(role,k),lowered_bindings) =
  OperationOrdinal(lowered_bindings[role],k)

LowerExpected(other_without_quotient_path,lowered_bindings) =
  other_without_quotient_path.
```

The exact pass-B row type is:

```text
QuotientSupportLoweredRecipePathBindingsPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
   grammar_companion:Option<QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1>,
   operation_record_companion:
     Option<QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1>)

QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   bare_locus:QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1,
   path_bindings:
     QuotientSupportLoweredRecipePathBindingsPlanCandidateV1,
   result_schema:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanCandidateV1,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1,
   expected_judgment:
     QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1,
   referent_recipe:QuotientSupportReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1,
   owner_resolution_recipe:
     QuotientSupportOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1).
```

Define the total-on-valid-pass-A-image row lowering:

```text
LowerBindings(B) =
  (dependency=LowerPath(B.dependency),
   grammar_companion=map(LowerPath,B.grammar_companion),
   operation_record_companion=
     map(LowerPath,B.operation_record_companion))

LowerPassA(n):
  B = LowerBindings(n.path_bindings)
  return QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1(
    root=n.root,
    bare_locus=n.bare_locus,
    path_bindings=B,
    result_schema=LowerResultSchema(n.nominal_result_schema),
    edge=n.edge,
    expected_judgment=LowerExpected(
      n.nominal_expected_judgment,B),
    referent_recipe=n.referent_recipe,
    owner_resolution_recipe=n.owner_resolution_recipe).
```

For every pass-A row, pass B lowers each present path binding with `LowerPath`,
lowers `nominal_result_schema` with `LowerResultSchema`,
and lowers `nominal_expected_judgment` with the already lowered bindings. It
retains `root`, `bare_locus`, `edge`, and the two finite recipes byte-for-byte.
It also requires

```text
lowered.path_bindings.dependency.root  = lowered.root
lowered.path_bindings.dependency.locus = lowered.bare_locus
lowered.result_schema =
  lowered.path_bindings.dependency.result_schema.
```

Referent and owner recipes remain path-role instructions; pass B does not
materialize either final sum. Dependency ordinals are assigned as checked
contiguous `u32` positions in the lowered direct stream. Every role path is
lowered independently and must resolve to the same nominal node found in pass
A. A claimed symbolic tag never selects `TypeAt`, a companion, or a route.

The compiler aborts without a quotient support value on any:

- off-image root-4 or root-5 descriptor/project route, ambiguous
  dereference, primary-result mismatch, or attempt to descend through root-5
  source-bundle storage directly;
- malformed public-field ordinal/path/judgment, root-3 definitional equality,
  root-5 reserved literal, or nonempty/wrong-kind root-6 action;
- missing ambient context, section target, carrier pack, binder, global,
  grammar/operation companion, static expected judgment, or owner recipe;
- invalid typed step, schema locator, vector/term child, checked arithmetic,
  canonical decode/re-encode, or pass-A/pass-B type equality;
- omitted or duplicate manifest node, tag reordering, locator alias collapse,
  default dispatch, sorting, or semantic deduplication of direct incidences;
- copied R2c `FullLocalPathV1`, `SubjectPathV1`, `TypeIdV1`, raw/source/owner
  ordinal, hop transcript, representative support, receipt, replay, resource
  account, evidence target, or presentation field; or
- use of `QFootprints`, semantic SR2, `nu`, a later registry, or an R2e/R4
  comparison to select any pass-A or pass-B value.

QG3-S1b2 therefore closes exactly the direct nominal traversal, companion-path
census, `m=17` candidate manifest, and its lowering. It does not declare the
candidate binder/referent/current-location/owner/key sums, resolve an owner,
emit a closure row, construct a class or coverage vector, recompute a
footprint, define an action manifest, or materialize a stored support graph.

The next S1b cut was QG3-S1b3. It had to consume this lowered direct stream and
its frozen recipes, using only the Q1-bound private occurrence authority,
`QLineage`, and quotient-frame resolver context, then
close the candidate-restricted no-formal graph in
the order already fixed in subsection 10.11.3. Candidate `M_nf` is immutable;
Later action work must prove a subset relation or define a separate action and
final shared manifest without appending to or renumbering these tags.

### 10.14 QG3-S1b3 no-formal candidate support graph — CLOSED

This cut consumes the frozen S1b2 pass-B stream. It materializes the smallest
candidate-only binder/referent/owner/key image, resolves every direct owner,
computes the exact quotient-internal occurrence closure, and constructs the
classes, coverage, projection, and receipt-free footprint agreement. It does
not define the later shared candidate/action sums, support-preservation
subjects, action-introduced lineage, instantiated action subject, or root
`0xfd`.

#### 10.14.1 Exact candidate-only semantic sums

The stored no-formal image is exactly:

```text
QuotientSupportBinderPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Historical=0(
    parent:OccurrenceId14V1,
    body_edge:HistoricalBodyEdgeV1)
| Synthetic=1(
    profile_version:u16,
    constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
    raw_field_path:RawFieldPathV1)

QuotientSupportReferentPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CurrentOccurrence=0(OccurrenceId14V1)
| KernelGlobal=1(GlobalId)
| CandidateBinder=2(QuotientSupportBinderPlanCandidateV1)
| RecordSlot=3(QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1)
| OperationSlot=4(
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
    operation_ordinal:u32,
    parameter_or_result:ParameterOrResultV1,
    field_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  PublicField=0(EnvelopeFieldPathV1)
| AuxiliaryPath=1(QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportOwnerPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Current=0(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
    location:QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanCandidateV1)
| Older=1(
    export_id:ExportIdV1,
    public_field_path:PublicFieldPathV1::DeclarationField)
| Structural=2(
    terminal_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
    terminal_referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportKeyPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Current=0(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
    location:QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanCandidateV1,
    expected_judgment:
      QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1)
| Older=1(
    export_id:ExportIdV1,
    public_field_path:PublicFieldPathV1::DeclarationField,
    expected_judgment:
      QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1)
| Structural=2(
    terminal_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanCandidateV1,
    terminal_referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanCandidateV1,
    expected_judgment:
      QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanCandidateV1).
```

These are candidate-only encoded types, not prefixes of the later shared
candidate/action types. Their image is exact:

```text
direct referents =
  {KernelGlobal,CandidateBinder,RecordSlot,OperationSlot}
closure referents = {CurrentOccurrence}

Structural.terminal_referent in {RecordSlot,OperationSlot}.

edge 1                    -> CandidateBinder
edge 2                    -> KernelGlobal
edges 9,10                -> OperationSlot
every other direct edge   -> RecordSlot.
```

`OlderExport`, `NormalizationNode`, `FormalAction`, a formal binder, and any
reserved variant are absent. An older terminal is injected directly into
`Owner::Older`; it is never stored as a referent. A direct global or binder must
resolve to a history owner and cannot terminate structurally. The later shared
sums must preserve every candidate semantic payload by an injective,
order-preserving embedding whose comparator extends the candidate comparator.
The candidate encoding itself is immutable; the later shared encoding need not
reuse these bytes or make this sum a tag prefix.

The action cut may add a payloadful `Formal` binder only after its unique
quotient locus is frozen. It does not reserve a candidate `Structural` binder;
any future operation that genuinely manufactures a binding constructor without
source provenance would require a new action-only semantic case and proof, not
a retroactive change to this candidate sum.

Translation of the erased S1b2 binder environment has an explicit checked
outcome:

```text
QBinder(Q1,HistoricalBinder(p,e)) = Success(Historical(p,e))
QBinder(Q1,SyntheticBinder(v,c,r)) =
  require (v,c)=(Q1.versions.profile_version,Q1.constructor);
  Success(Synthetic(v,c,r))
QBinder(Q1,FormalBinder(_,_)) = CheckedFalse.

CheckedQBinder(Q1,x,k):
  require x records the successful preflight result Success(b) for k;
  require QBinder(Q1,k)=Success(b) again;
  return b.
```

`Success`/`CheckedFalse` is compiler control, not an encoded sum nested inside
`CandidateBinder`. No-formal preflight must unwrap `Success(b)`. After that
preflight, `CheckedQBinder` reruns `QBinder`, requires the same `Success(b)`,
and returns only `b`; a changed result or `CheckedFalse` is an invariant abort.
A synthetic profile/constructor mismatch is malformed and aborts. The raw path
is retained semantic lineage, not a source ordinal or R2c support coordinate.

#### 10.14.2 Direct referent materialization and owner resolution

S1b3 does not recover an ambient from a path after the fact. Step 1 reruns the
closed S1b2 traversal and constructs this erased parallel vector:

```text
QuotientSupportPreflightCoordinatesPlanCandidateV1 =
  QuotientSupportResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1

CompilerControlOutcome<T> ::= Success(value:T) | CheckedFalse | Abort

QuotientSupportPreflightRowContextPlanCandidateV1(
  r:QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1) =
  (source_nominal_row:NominalDirectIncidence,
   dependency_view:SupportNodeView(SchemaOf(r.result_schema)),
   traversal_ambient:
     Option<QuotientSupportTraversalAmbientPlanCandidateV1>,
   binder_environment:
     Option<QuotientSupportBinderEnvironmentPlanCandidateV1>,
   binding_origin_frame:
     Option<QuotientSupportTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1>,
   lineage_origin:
     Option<QuotientSupportNonemptyLineageOriginPlanCandidateV1>,
   value_terminal:
     Option<QuotientSupportHistoryTerminalRecipePlanCandidateV1>,
   resolved_binder:Option<QuotientSupportBinderPlanCandidateV1>,
   resolved_history_terminal:Option<SourceRefV1>)

Preflight(
  lowered_rows:vector<
    QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1>,
  coordinates:QuotientSupportPreflightCoordinatesPlanCandidateV1)
  : CompilerControlOutcome<
      dependent ordered vector
        [QuotientSupportPreflightRowContextPlanCandidateV1(
           lowered_rows[i])
         | checked i in [0,lowered_rows.len)]> =
  rerun pass A and decorated pass B in their frozen order;
  on success bind the returned dependent vector as preflight;
  require preflight.len=lowered_rows.len;
  for checked loop position i:
    require LowerPassA(preflight[i].source_nominal_row)
              = lowered_rows[i] by full canonical value equality;
    state_i = DecoratedStateForNominalRow(
                coordinates,preflight[i].source_nominal_row);
    require preflight[i].dependency_view=state_i.view;
    require preflight[i].dependency_view=SupportViewAtLowered(
              coordinates,lowered_rows[i].path_bindings.dependency);
    require preflight[i].traversal_ambient=
              state_i.traversal_ambient;
    require preflight[i].binder_environment=
              state_i.binder_environment;
    require preflight[i].binding_origin_frame=
              state_i.term_provenance;
    require every present component is exactly the product component emitted
            while visiting that dependency occurrence;
  return Success(preflight).
```

`CompilerControlOutcome` is control flow, not an encoded or publishable sum.
`CheckedFalse` is restricted to the well-formed semantic inadmissibility cases
listed in subsection 10.14.5; every invariant violation returns `Abort`.

```text
SupportSchemaAt(coordinates,r,x):
  p = Bound(r,Dependency)
  require x.dependency_view=SupportViewAtLowered(coordinates,p)
  return SchemaOf(r.result_schema).

SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x):
  SupportSchemaAt(coordinates,r,x)
  match x.dependency_view:
    Semantic(v) -> return v
    SchemaOnly  -> abort.
```

Thus a value is returned only after the authority-indexed view equality is
rechecked; a `SchemaOnly` view never fabricates one.

`DecoratedStateForNominalRow(authority,row)` dispatches on the same exact
`Direct`/`Dereferenced`/`PrimaryProject` locus and physical occurrence as
`LineageOrigin`, invokes the corresponding decorated helper once, and returns
its full five-component state. It has no value-byte lookup or fallback.
`LineageOrigin`, `SupportViewAtBare`, and the three displayed preflight sidecars
are projections or independent reruns of that same product and must agree
byte-for-byte. In particular `binding_origin_frame` is exactly
`state_i.term_provenance`; despite its historical field name it is not a second
origin-frame relation.

The option is absent where that component is inapplicable, not where
reconstruction failed. Every row with
`value_terminal=CandidateBinderFromValue` requires `TermAt`, its complete
binder environment, the binding-origin restriction at that exact `Var`, and
the unique successful `resolved_binder`; this is not limited to edge 1. Every
row with `value_terminal=KernelGlobalFromValue` likewise requires the exact
term state. A term child requires the corresponding decorated child frame. A
row whose dependency view is not `Term` but carries either term-only component
aborts. A `HistoryOwner` row requires exactly one authenticated
`resolved_history_terminal`; a structural row requires `None`.

Cached `lineage_origin` and `value_terminal` must equal the subsection-10.13
functions on the same source nominal row; `EmitOwner` and `ExecOwner` consume
those same values. Every execution-time terminal rerun must equal
`resolved_history_terminal`. No sidecar field may add a path absent from that
row's bindings and path census.
Whenever both an ambient and binder environment are present, the latter must
be byte-for-byte the decoration of every context boundary used by that ambient;
a second independently chosen environment aborts.

`preflight` is an ordered vector, never a map keyed by row bytes: repeated
byte-identical incidences remain distinct loop positions. The checked `i` is
erased compiler position, not an encoded or copied R2c ordinal. The vector is
neither part of the lowered stream nor stored in the graph. It is a
deterministic recomputation from
`(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QObligations,the footprint-erased support frame)`, the sole
private-history capability owned under Q1, and the frozen S1b2 compiler. A
caller cannot supply or mutate either the vector or that capability, and both
are erased before publication.

For lowered S1b2 row `r`, `Bound(r,role)` returns the already pass-B-lowered
complete path in `r.path_bindings`. It requires the role to be present, to have
the exact pass-A nominal value, and to satisfy `TypeAt` and `result_schema`
again. It never derives `OperationRecordCompanion` from a grammar path.

Referent execution is:

```text
ResolveBinderAtDependency(coordinates,r,x):
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=Term::Var(i)
  require x supplies the unique decorated
    S1b2 TermAt/BinderEnvironment(C,B) and binding-origin frame
  require B.len=C.len and map(.complete_type,B)=ContextTerms(C) and i<C.len
  j = checked_sub_u64(checked_sub_u64(C.len,1),i)
  T = the exact capture-safe lookup/weakening of B[j].complete_type
  b = CheckedQBinder(
        coordinates.q1,x,B[checked_u32(j)].identity)
  return (b,T)

ResolveGlobalAtDependency(coordinates,r,x):
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=Term::Global(g)
  require x supplies the unique decorated S1b2 TermAt state
  require coordinates.private_history returns the exact Sigma(H,o)
          declaration and complete normalized type T under coordinates.q1
  require the term checker validates Global(g) at T in that state
  return (g,T)

ExecReferent(coordinates,r,x) =
  ExecReferentRecipe(coordinates,r,x,r.referent_recipe)

ExecReferentRecipe(coordinates,r,x,recipe):
  match recipe:
  CandidateBinderFromValue(Dependency) ->
    require r.edge=LocalBinderUse
    (b,T) = ResolveBinderAtDependency(coordinates,r,x)
    require r.expected_judgment=HasType(T)
    CandidateBinder(b)

  KernelGlobalFromValue(Dependency) ->
    require r.edge=GlobalUse
    (g,T) = ResolveGlobalAtDependency(coordinates,r,x)
    require r.expected_judgment=HasType(T)
    KernelGlobal(g)

  RecordSlot(role) ->
    require exactly one of:
      r.edge in {TelescopePrefix..GrammarExport,
                 SchemeTrigger..TermChild}
        and role=Dependency;
      r.edge=OperationAnchor
        and role=OperationRecordCompanion
    RecordSlot(Bound(r,role))

  OperationSlot(G,k,side,i,D,O) ->
    require (r.edge,side) is
      (OperationParameter,Parameter) or (OperationResult,Result)
    g = Bound(r,G); d = Bound(r,D); o = Bound(r,O)
    require the exact grammar, operation, selected field, bounds,
      `d` locus, `o` record, and `OperationOrdinal(g,k)` expectation
    OperationSlot(g,k,side,i)

  otherwise -> abort.
```

The checked subtraction is never evaluated after a failed nonempty/bounds
guard. `RecordSlot(OperationRecordCompanion)` therefore materializes edge 8's
operation record exactly, while edges 9 and 10 retain `OperationSlot`.
Equivalently, the executable recipe/edge partition is edge 1 only for
`CandidateBinderFromValue`, edge 2 only for `KernelGlobalFromValue`, edges
3--7 and 11--21 only for `RecordSlot(Dependency)`, edge 8 only for
`RecordSlot(OperationRecordCompanion)`, and edges 9--10 only for the matching
`OperationSlot` side. Any other recipe/edge pair is an invariant abort.

Location execution binds the recipe to the actual dependency path:

```text
ExecLocation(r,x,PublicFieldLocation(f)) =
  p = Bound(r,Dependency)
  require EnclosingPublicField(p.root,p.locus)=f
  PublicField(f)

ExecLocation(r,x,AuxiliaryDependencyPath) =
  p = Bound(r,Dependency)
  require EnclosingPublicField(p.root,p.locus) is undefined
  AuxiliaryPath(p).
```

The descendant-inheritance rule of subsection 10.13.2 is part of that first
equality. A virtual root-4/root-5 dependency can retain a public lineage origin
while using `AuxiliaryPath`; semantic origin and physical current-owner location
remain separate functions.

Every history terminal returns exactly one `SourceRefV1`:

```text
DecodeExactQPrimaryDescriptorFromBoundRow(coordinates,r,x):
  p = Bound(r,Dependency)
  require p.root=PrimarySubjects
  require p.locus=Dereferenced(descriptor_steps,[])
  require r.edge=TheoremEndpoint
  d = DecodeExactQPrimaryDescriptor(coordinates,descriptor_steps)
  require x.dependency_view=SupportViewAtLowered(coordinates,p)
  require d is the literal's translated paired source route in
          coordinates.support_frame.primary_resolver.program_skeleton
  return d.

ExecTerminal(coordinates,CurrentOccurrenceFromValue,r,x,R):
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=OccurrenceId14V1 q
  require coordinates.private_history authenticates q under coordinates.q1
  return Current(q)

ExecTerminal(coordinates,OlderExportFromValue,r,x,R):
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=OlderPublicExportV1 y
  require y.public_field_path is DeclarationField
  return AuthenticateSource(
    coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,
    OlderPublic(y.export_id,y.public_field_path))

ExecTerminal(coordinates,SourceIdentityFromValue,r,x,R):
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=SourceRefV1 s
  return AuthenticateSource(
    coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,s)

ExecTerminal(coordinates,KernelGlobalFromValue,r,x,R):
  (g,_) = ResolveGlobalAtDependency(coordinates,r,x)
  if r.edge=GlobalUse:
    require R=KernelGlobal(g)
  return ResolveSigmaGlobalOwner(
    coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,g)

ExecTerminal(coordinates,CandidateBinderFromValue,r,x,R):
  (b,_) = ResolveBinderAtDependency(coordinates,r,x)
  if r.edge=LocalBinderUse:
    require R=CandidateBinder(b)
  match b:
    Historical(p,e) ->
      q = BinderEnteringOccurrence(
            coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,p,e)
      return Current(q)
    Synthetic(v,c,raw_path) ->
      require (v,c)=(coordinates.q1.versions.profile_version,
                     coordinates.q1.constructor)
      s = UniqueQLineageSourceAtIntroducedBinder(
            coordinates.lineage,raw_path)
      return AuthenticateSource(
        coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,s)

ExecTerminal(coordinates,LineageSource(locator),r,x,R):
  s = the exact subsection-10.13.2 resolver result from
      coordinates.lineage
  return AuthenticateSource(
    coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,s)

ExecTerminal(
  coordinates,ResolvedPrimarySourceFromDependencyDescriptor,r,x,R):
  require the exact root-5 direct-literal descriptor occurrence
  require SupportValueAt(coordinates,r,x)=OccurrenceId14V1 q
  d = DecodeExactQPrimaryDescriptorFromBoundRow(coordinates,r,x)
  state = ResolveSupportQPrimarySourceDecorated(coordinates,d,[])
  require state.view=Semantic(v)
  require its declared result type is Occurrence
  require v=q byte-for-byte
  return AuthenticateSource(
    coordinates.private_history,coordinates.q1,Current(q)).
```

`ResolveSigmaGlobalOwner` returns the exact same-birth declaration occurrence
or the unique older public `DeclarationField`. A later, private, missing, or
nonpublic global is checked false during preflight; distinct ambiguity is an
invariant abort. `UniqueQLineageSourceAtIntroducedBinder` scans the
closed QLineage semantic raw-path relation, requires all matches to be one
byte-identical `SourceRefV1`, and may return either a Q1-authenticated
`Current(q)` or the unique authenticated older public `DeclarationField`.
`AuthenticateSource(private_history,Q1,s)` validates `Current(q)` with the
owned occurrence evaluator and validates
`OlderPublic(e,DeclarationField)` with the exact strictly older public export
table after checking the capability's dependent `Q1` identity. A private-body
or other public-field path is false during outer preflight. It does not use Q1
as a raw-path index.

Owner execution is then:

```text
ExecOwner(coordinates,r,x,R):
  recipe = r.owner_resolution_recipe
  require recipe = EmitOwner(
    coordinates,x.source_nominal_row), using x.lineage_origin and
    x.value_terminal after their independent equality checks

  match recipe:
  HistoryOwner(t,l) ->
    checked_location = ExecLocation(r,x,l)
    s = ExecTerminal(coordinates,t,r,x,R)
    require x.resolved_history_terminal=Some(s)
    match s:
      Current(q) -> Current(q,checked_location)
      OlderPublic(e,p) -> Older(e,p)

  StructuralTerminal(role,referent_recipe) ->
    require x.resolved_history_terminal=None
    p = Bound(r,role)
    R_terminal = ExecReferentRecipe(
                   coordinates,r,x,referent_recipe)
    require R_terminal=R
    require exactly one of:
      r.edge=OperationAnchor
        and role=OperationRecordCompanion
        and R_terminal=RecordSlot(p);
      r.edge=ObligationPremise
        and role=Dependency
        and R_terminal=RecordSlot(p);
      r.edge=TheoremEndpoint
        and role=Dependency
        and R_terminal=RecordSlot(p)
        and TypeAt(p.root,p.locus) is one of
          QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1,
          QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1,
          vector<OccurrenceId14V1>,
          QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1;
      r.edge in {TelescopePrefix..GrammarExport,
                 OperationParameter..ContractRight,
                 DischargeImage,TermChild}
        and role=Dependency
        and x.lineage_origin=None
        and x.value_terminal=None
    Structural(p,R_terminal).
```

The last set is exactly edges 3--7, 9--18, and 21. A syntactic
`ValueTerminal=Some(_)` whose required history resolution fails never falls
back to `Structural`. Edges 1--2 and the occurrence-valued theorem endpoint
have no structural branch. No hop, source ordinal, raw ordinal, first-match
rule, or caller owner is constructed. `KeyOf(owner,J)` is the tag-for-tag
mapping to the corresponding key case with the complete `J` payload.

Let the lowered S1b2 stream have length `d`. Direct construction is one
transaction in row order:

```text
for checked_u32 i in [0,d):
  r = lowered_S1b2_rows[i]
  x = preflight[i]
  p = r.path_bindings.dependency
  J = r.expected_judgment
  R = ExecReferent(coordinates,r,x)
  O = ExecOwner(coordinates,r,x,R)

  D[i] = QuotientSupportDependencyPlanCandidateV1(
    dependency_ordinal=i,path=p,edge=r.edge,
    referent=R,expected_judgment=J)
  Owners[i] =
    QuotientSupportOwnerMapEntryPlanCandidateV1(i,O).
```

Every conversion and all row outputs are validated before any prefix is
published.

#### 10.14.3 Exact quotient-internal closure suffix

Closure consumes only `D`, `Owners`, and
`coordinates.private_history` after its dependent Q1 identity check. Put:

```text
C0 = { q | some direct Owners[i].owner = Current(q,_) }
C  = Cl_b(coordinates.private_history,C0)
A  = sort_unique_full_occurrence_bytes(C \ C0)

ord_A(q) = checked_u32(checked_add_u64(d,position_A(q)))

first(q) =
  least direct i with Owners[i].owner=Current(q,_), if one exists
  ord_A(q), otherwise

ClosureWitness(q) =
  (q_force:OccurrenceId14V1,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1)
  such that q_force in C and
    ClStep_b(coordinates.private_history,q_force,edge,q)

for q in A:
W(q) =
  the least ClosureWitness(q), ordered by
    (first(.q_force),tag(.edge)).
```

`C=Cl_b(coordinates.private_history,C0)` is the least fixed point of exactly
`ClosureAncestor=24`, `ClosureBinderSibling=25`, and
`ClosureSameBirth=26` under the frozen history relation. `first` is injective:
one row has one owner, and the direct/suffix ordinal ranges are disjoint. For a
fixed target `q`, the minimizing pair therefore identifies one exact
`q_force,edge`; multiple witnesses with the same pair must be byte-identical.

Define the inherited path recursively by the semantic `ClStep_b` measure:

```text
q_force = W(q).q_force

P(q) =
  D[first(q_force)].path, when first(q_force)<d
  P(q_force),              otherwise.
```

Every recursive step must decrease the frozen semantic measure. Evaluation is
topological in that measure even when the full-byte order of `A` places
`q_force` later than `q`; a cycle or nondecreasing witness aborts. Emission is
nevertheless exactly `A` order. For `j=position_A(q)`, append:

```text
D[d+j] = (
  dependency_ordinal=ord_A(q),
  path=P(q),
  edge=W(q).edge,
  referent=CurrentOccurrence(q),
  expected_judgment=StructuralOccurrence(q))

Owners[d+j] = (
  dependency_ordinal=ord_A(q),
  owner=Current(q,AuxiliaryPath(P(q)))).
```

The witness, forcing ordinal, fixed-point work order, and recursive memo table
are compiler-only and erased. A suffix row adds no path or candidate symbolic
type: its complete path is byte-identical to the forcing lineage's inherited
S1b2 path. Checked overflow, allocation failure, a missing/ambiguous witness,
an occurrence outside the exact closure, a duplicate `A` entry, or any R2c
raw/work/source/owner coordinate aborts the transaction.

#### 10.14.4 Classes, coverage, projection, and footprint agreement

For every completed dependency ordinal `i`, form

```text
K_i = KeyOf(Owners[i].owner,D[i].expected_judgment).
```

Candidate key comparison is semantic case rank
`Current < Older < Structural`, then lexicographic full canonical payload bytes
within the case, including location, terminal path/referent, and complete
expected judgment. Recipe tags, a future shared tag, a digest, normalized-term
equivalence, and owner erasure do not participate. Then:

```text
classes = enumerate_with_checked_class_ordinals(
  sort_unique([K_i | 0 <= i < D.len]))

dependency_to_class[i] =
  (dependency_ordinal=i,
   class_ordinal=the unique j with classes[j].key=K_i)

projection.keys = map(.key,classes).
```

Coverage is generated by one ascending dependency scan:

```text
coverage.roots = [
  (root=t,
   dependency_ordinals=[i | 0<=i<D.len and D[i].path.root=t])
  | t in [CanonicalPayload,IndexedInputs,IndexedOutput,PublicFields,
          Obligations,PrimarySubjects,ActionArguments] ]

coverage.dependency_to_class = dependency_to_class.
```

Thus every root vector is strictly ascending, the seven vectors are disjoint,
their union is `[0,D.len)`, and an ordinal belongs exactly to its path root.
Closure rows participate under their inherited roots; they do not alter an
earlier direct count certificate because no such certificate is stored.

The receipt-free footprints are independently computed only after the complete
owner map exists:

```text
current_projection = ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1(sort_unique([
  q | entry in Owners and entry.owner=Current(q,_)]))

older_projection = ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1(sort_unique([
  OlderPublicFootprintEntryV1(e,p)
  | entry in Owners and entry.owner=Older(e,p)]))

OutputSeeds =
  { entry.occurrence | entry in final candidate QLineage.output,
    after checking its output ordinal, field path, raw leaf path,
    and private-history-authenticated occurrence under Q1 }

output_projection = ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1(
  sort_unique(Cl_b(coordinates.private_history,OutputSeeds))).
```

Output projection is not a filter of root 2 or of whichever closure rows
inherited that root. It is a separate traversal of final candidate output
lineage through the same exact `Cl_b`. Only after all three values exist does
the compiler compare, in this order:

```text
current_projection = QFootprints.implementation
older_projection   = QFootprints.prior_support
output_projection  = QFootprints.current_output.
```

`QFootprints` is an equality target only. It cannot select a recipe, terminal,
closure seed/witness, class, coverage row, or output seed. The stored
`QuotientSupportFootprintAgreementPlanCandidateV1` contains the three
independently computed left sides.

The compiler returns the pair:

```text
(graph:QuotientSupportGraphPlanCandidateV1,
 footprint_agreement:
   QuotientSupportFootprintAgreementPlanCandidateV1)

graph.dependencies = D
graph.owner_map     = Owners
graph.classes       = classes
graph.coverage      = coverage
graph.projection    = projection.
```

This is a compiler return shape, not a newly frozen final semantic-support
record. The enclosing support record remains open until preservation and the
shared candidate/action sums close.

#### 10.14.5 Transaction boundary and independence theorem

The exact stage order is:

1. rerun pass A and pass B from the frozen quotient inputs, validate the
   canonical S1b2 stream, bindings, manifest, and lowering byte-for-byte, and
   complete the no-formal semantic preflight;
2. materialize every direct referent in row order;
3. resolve every direct owner in row order without `QFootprints`;
4. compute `C0,C,A,W,P` and append the closure suffix;
5. construct keys/classes and dependency-to-class mapping;
6. construct root coverage and projection;
7. recompute all three footprints; and
8. compare them to `QFootprints` and publish one complete value.

Before `GraphCore` is invoked, a well-formed but inadmissible formal binder, a
later/private/nonpublic global, a genuinely absent semantic terminal, or a
forbidden formal terminal is checked false. A binding constructor with no
authenticated origin, or an equal-contract run with missing or nonfunctional
provenance, is likewise checked false at this outer candidate boundary.
Distinct ambiguity in an already authenticated Q1/QLineage relation is never a
candidate choice and is an invariant abort.
After that preflight succeeds, every terminal and route is rerun as an
invariant: zero matches, a second distinct match, or a changed result in Q1,
QLineage, the binder environment, or any recipe resolver aborts.

Malformed frozen Q1/Q2/QLineage/QObligation data or recompiled primary-
resolver/program-skeleton state; wrong
recipe/edge/path role; missing binding; `TypeAt`, schema, ambient,
binder-environment, referent, owner, closure, ordering, or canonical-encoding
mismatch; bounds/arithmetic/allocation failure; a final footprint mismatch; or
any use of `QFootprints` before step 8 is an invariant abort. Neither checked
false nor abort publishes a partial graph, receipt, or R3 account.

The compiler reads no representative support, `FullLocalPathV1`, R2c raw/work/
source/owner ordinal, owner hop, closure worklist, resolution transcript,
cardinality receipt, evidence target, presentation, semantic SR2 result, or
`nu`. For successful representatives `E,E'`, equality of the sufficient
retained quotient coordinates

```text
(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QFootprints,QObligations)(E)
  =
(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QFootprints,QObligations)(E')
```

and the `VerifiedEnvelopeCensusV1` ownership invariant supplies the unique
private-history capabilities indexed by those complete Q1 identities. The
capabilities themselves are neither compared nor encoded; their extensional
relations are equal because the full authenticated identities are equal. These
facts imply byte-identical S1b2 streams and bindings, direct referents/owners,
`C0/C/A/W/P`, suffix, keys/classes, coverage, projection, computed footprint
agreement, and complete no-formal graph. QFootprints equality affects only the
same final validation result. This is the S1b3 representative-independence
theorem.

More strongly,

```text
QuotientSupportGraphCoreResolverContextPlanCandidateV1(
  lowered_S1b2_rows:vector<
    QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1>) =
  (authority:QuotientSupportResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1,
   preflight:
     dependent ordered vector
       [QuotientSupportPreflightRowContextPlanCandidateV1(
          lowered_S1b2_rows[i])
        | checked i in [0,lowered_S1b2_rows.len)] )

require resolver_context.authority.support_frame =
  CompileSupportNoFootprintRootFrame(
    resolver_context.authority.q1,
    resolver_context.authority.q2,
    resolver_context.authority.lineage,
    resolver_context.authority.obligations)

require Preflight(
  lowered_S1b2_rows,
  resolver_context.authority) = Success(resolver_context.preflight)

GraphCore(
  lowered_S1b2_rows:vector<
    QuotientSupportLoweredDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1>,
  resolver_context:
    QuotientSupportGraphCoreResolverContextPlanCandidateV1(
      lowered_S1b2_rows)):
  coordinates = resolver_context.authority;
  preflight   = resolver_context.preflight;
  Q1          = coordinates.q1;
  Q2          = coordinates.q2;
  QLineage    = coordinates.lineage;
  QObligations= coordinates.obligations;
  HistoryAuthority = coordinates.private_history;
  execute steps 2--7 above and return the graph and computed footprints.
```

Here `resolver_context` is the unique compiler-only product of the resolver
authority and ordered `preflight` vector. The privately owned census selects
the sole capability whose complete identity projection is Q1; the frozen S1b2
compiler recomputes every other component from
`(Q1,Q2,QLineage,QObligations,the footprint-erased support frame)`. Neither the
capability nor the support frame is an independent caller argument or encoded
value. The support frame includes all typed operation companions and the exact
primary resolver state. `GraphCore` has no `QFootprints` or
`QPrimarySubjects` argument. Holding those inputs fixed
while changing only a final comparison target cannot change the graph or any
of the three recomputed footprints; it can only change whether step 8 succeeds.

QG3-S1b3 is therefore closed. Section 10.15 takes its next lawful input and
closes the preservation kernel without changing candidate `M_nf` or this
no-formal graph. Section 10.16 then audits the semantic owner, corrects it to
the canonical instantiated naturality subject, and closes the subject skeleton
and primitive spine without guessing the final shared sums.

### 10.15 QG3 preservation kernel and generic subject template — CLOSED

This cut closes the quotient-preservation kernel without guessing the later
action-capable support-key sum. It fixes a fresh preservation-path type, one
minimal contextual seed per subject, the four endpoint-mode tags, the exact
clause wire, the reflexive quotient normal-form rule, and the cross-check
against QG2's three frozen group-5 requirements. It does **not** declare a
final preservation-subject aggregate or bind a support-projection literal to
the candidate-only S1b3 key type.

#### 10.15.1 Dependency cut and fresh preservation routes

For constructor `c`, write

```text
B_c =
  (Q1,Q2,QLineage,QFootprints,QObligations,QPrimarySubjects,G0)
```

for the already validated outputs of QG1, QG2, and S1b3 for the same admitted
candidate. `G0` is the successful no-formal graph from subsection 10.14.5.
`B_c` is compiler context, not an encoded record, a caller argument, or an
additional copy of any component. Every use occurs inside the same verified
transaction and may use only the unique Q1-bound private-history authority
fixed in subsection 10.14.2. In particular,

```text
c = Q1.constructor
  = variant_tag(Q2.canonical_payload)
  = QObligations.constructor
  = QPrimarySubjects.root_projection.constructor

QPrimarySubjects.root_projection.source_bundle.evaluation_binding
  = Q1.evaluation_identity.
```

Any disagreement aborts before a preservation value is emitted.

QG3 does not reuse
`QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1`. That type belongs to the
frozen QG2 family and its full transitive census must remain exactly
`5*PR_c`. The preservation family has this fresh, isomorphic plan grammar:

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationSourceRootPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  EvaluationBinding=0
| SchemaPayload=1
| PublicFields=2
| PrincipalLineage=3
| FieldLineages=4
| Footprints=5
| Obligations=6
| SelectorInputs=7
| ActionConstants=8
| SourceCandidate=9

QuotientSupportPreservationSourceResultPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor=0
| CandidateAdmissionPremise=1
| OccurrenceVector=2
| Occurrence=3
| NormalizedAction=4
| NormalizedTelescope=5
| NormalizedSubstitution=6
| NormalizedGrammar=7
| NormalizedScheme=8
| NormalizedSection=9
| NormalizedLiveDemand=10
| NormalizedContract=11

QuotientSupportPreservationSourcePathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportPreservationSourceRootPlanCandidateV1,
   steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   result_schema:
     QuotientSupportPreservationSourceResultPlanCandidateV1).
```

The twelve result variants name exactly the same twelve semantic leaf schemas,
in the same order, as the table in subsection 10.3.2. This is a distinct plan
type, not a type alias or a QG2 value reference.

`CompileQG3PreservationRouteCensus(c,Q2.canonical_payload)` independently
reruns the frozen symbolic primary-route generator and the exact semantic-role
translation of subsection 10.9.4, but emits the fresh root and result tags
above. The payload supplies the checked constructor-specific vector shapes,
including `m_5`, `p_6`, and `h_6`; constructor tag alone is insufficient. The
compiler does not read any stored `QPrimaryRouteCensus(c)` vector. Define the
compiler-only structural relabeling

```text
RelabelToQG2Path:
  QuotientSupportPreservationSourcePathPlanCandidateV1
  -> QuotientSubjectProjectionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1
```

by the displayed tag identities and byte-identical step vector. Admission
requires

```text
map(RelabelToQG2Path,
    CompileQG3PreservationRouteCensus(c,Q2.canonical_payload))
  = QPrimaryRouteCensus(c)
```

one-for-one in full canonical bytes. This is an independent cross-check, not
the construction rule. It preserves slot order, strict-postorder value-node
order, literal-field order, and every repeated route. It never sorts,
deduplicates, interns, or selects from QG2.

#### 10.15.2 Minimal contextual seed and factored endpoint modes

The exact seed is

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationSeedPlanCandidateV1 =
  (evaluation_identity:QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1,
   subject_endpoint_paths:
     vector<QuotientSupportPreservationSourcePathPlanCandidateV1>).

Seed_c =
  (evaluation_identity=Q1.evaluation_identity,
   subject_endpoint_paths=
     CompileQG3PreservationRouteCensus(c,Q2.canonical_payload)).
```

The constructor, action kind, Q2, lineage, obligations, primary subjects, and
candidate graph are dependent compiler context and are not repeated in the
seed. In particular the seed contains no raw code, source bundle, admission
premise, support graph or projection, footprint, proof, receipt, numerical
`TypeIdV1`, or compiler-authority handle.

R2c stores the same seed once inside each endpoint expression and then requires
the two copies to be byte-identical. The quotient factors that invariant into
one subject-local seed plus two ordered modes:

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationEndpointModePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ProjectSeed=0
| NormalizedSeed=1
| RenamedSeed=2(renaming_binder:u32)
| ReindexedSeed=3(action_binder:u32).
```

The modes contain no path vector. Left versus right is carried by field
position and cannot be swapped. There is one physical seed in each subject,
not one seed per endpoint and not one seed shared by all three subjects.

This factorization also fixes the later representative-side relation. R2e may
accept an R2c preservation subject only after it independently checks:

1. `SeedOf(left)=SeedOf(right)` by complete R2c seed bytes;
2. both R2c seeds name the one enclosing R2c source bundle;
3. the outer and proposition binder vectors are byte-identical;
4. the R2c path vector translates, occurrence-for-occurrence, to the fresh
   quotient vector above; and
5. the erased raw code, evaluation owner, and source-bundle fields agree with
   the admitted representative and project to the fixed `B_c`.

Those checks authorize erasure; none of their representative fields becomes a
QG3 compiler input. QG3 constructs `Seed_c` directly from quotient context.

#### 10.15.3 Fresh clause wire

`SupportProjectionSubjectKindV1` is already the safe three-case semantic enum
used by QG2 and is reused unchanged. Every containing binder, literal, node,
path, mode, and subject type is fresh.

The binder grammar retains only the exact R2c discriminants needed by these
clauses. Tags `0..6` are reserved schema positions and are invalid in every
completed preservation subject:

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationActionBasePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SourceCandidate=0

QuotientSupportPreservationBinderSortPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ReservedCorePayload=0
| ReservedDependentContext=1
| ReservedTelescope=2
| ReservedSection=3
| ReservedSubstitution=4
| ReservedCarrier=5
| ReservedOccurrence=6
| HistoryRenaming=7
| FormalAction=8(action_kind:ActionKindV1,
                 cardinality:FormalActionCardinalityV1,
                 base:
                   QuotientSupportPreservationActionBasePlanCandidateV1)
| SupportProjection=9
```
