```text
QuotientSupportPreservationBinderPlanCandidateV1 =
  (sort:QuotientSupportPreservationBinderSortPlanCandidateV1).
```

The support-function path has exactly one projection mode and one symbolic
result sort:

```text
QuotientSupportFunctionProjectionModePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Full=0

QuotientSupportPreservationSymbolicSortPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SupportProjectionSort=0

QuotientSupportFunctionProjectionPathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (support_binder:u32,
   projection:QuotientSupportFunctionProjectionModePlanCandidateV1,
   result_sort:QuotientSupportPreservationSymbolicSortPlanCandidateV1).
```

`Full` is the only valid preservation projection. There is no candidate-only
`Current`, `Older`, or `OutputRoot` alternative.

The literal and node sums retain their R2c discriminants. Every `Reserved...`
variant is a schema position only and aborts in a completed subject:

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationLiteralPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ReservedCorePayloadProjection=0
| ReservedCarrierPayloadProjection=1
| ReservedExactOccurrenceProjection=2
| ReservedNormalizedOccurrenceProjection=3
| ReservedFormalActionArgumentProjection=4
| PureSupportFunctionProjection=5(
    path:QuotientSupportFunctionProjectionPathPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportPreservationNodePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ReservedBinder=0
| Literal=1(QuotientSupportPreservationLiteralPlanCandidateV1)
| ReservedProject=2
| ReservedNormalize=3
| ReservedReindex=4
| ReservedCompose=5
| ReservedRenameHistory=6
| ReservedSelector=7
| ReservedOrigin=8
| ReservedApproxOrd=9
| ReservedApproxEnvelope=10
| Equality=11(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)

QuotientSupportPreservationPropositionPlanCandidateV1 =
  (nodes:vector<QuotientSupportPreservationNodePlanCandidateV1>,
   root_node:u32)

QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectTemplatePlanCandidateV1 =
  (kind:SupportProjectionSubjectKindV1,
   seed:QuotientSupportPreservationSeedPlanCandidateV1,
   binders:vector<QuotientSupportPreservationBinderPlanCandidateV1>,
   left_mode:QuotientSupportPreservationEndpointModePlanCandidateV1,
   right_mode:QuotientSupportPreservationEndpointModePlanCandidateV1,
   proposition:QuotientSupportPreservationPropositionPlanCandidateV1).
```

The quotient stores the binder vector once. This is the second lawful
factorization of the R2c invariant
`subject.binders=subject.proposition.binders`; the R2e relation above checks
the two R2c copies before erasing one.

For every subject, the proposition is exactly

```text
nodes = [
  Literal(PureSupportFunctionProjection(
    support_binder=0,
    projection=Full,
    result_sort=SupportProjectionSort)),
  Literal(PureSupportFunctionProjection(
    support_binder=0,
    projection=Full,
    result_sort=SupportProjectionSort)),
  Equality(left_node=0,right_node=1)
]
root_node = 2.
```

The two equal literals are distinct physical node occurrences. Node 0 is
positionally bound to `(seed,left_mode)` and node 1 to `(seed,right_mode)`.
They cannot be swapped, shared, or redirected. No proof value is stored.

#### 10.15.4 Exact three-row template and QG2 cross-check

Let `a_c` be `SubstitutionAction` for constructor tags `0,1,2,4,5` and
`ContextSquareAction` for tags `3,6`. The three subject templates are uniquely:

```text
Normalization:
  kind       = NormalizationPreservation
  seed       = a fresh physical Seed_c
  binders    = [SupportProjection]
  left_mode  = ProjectSeed
  right_mode = NormalizedSeed

Admissible renaming:
  kind       = AdmissibleRenamingPreservation
  seed       = a fresh physical Seed_c
  binders    = [SupportProjection,HistoryRenaming]
  left_mode  = ProjectSeed
  right_mode = RenamedSeed(renaming_binder=1)

Reindexing:
  kind       = ReindexingPreservation
  seed       = a fresh physical Seed_c
  binders    = [
    SupportProjection,
    FormalAction(action_kind=a_c,
                 cardinality=ArbitraryNormalized,
                 base=SourceCandidate)]
  left_mode  = ProjectSeed
  right_mode = ReindexedSeed(action_binder=1).
```

Each row uses the exact proposition in subsection 10.15.3. Binder ordinal 0 is
always `SupportProjection`; ordinal 1 is the sole `HistoryRenaming` or
`FormalAction` binder in the applicable row. This freezes the reindexing
clause's syntactic formal-action position. It does not yet freeze the semantic
support locus, formal referent/owner/key payload, or action result generated by
that binder.

Define

```text
RuleFor(NormalizationPreservation) = Normalization[0]

RuleFor(AdmissibleRenamingPreservation)
  = RuleFor(ReindexingPreservation)
  = SupportAndOwnerResolution[2]

RequirementOf(subject) =
  QuotientSupportProjectionRequirementCandidateV1(
    kind=subject.kind,
    evaluation_identity=subject.seed.evaluation_identity,
    subject_endpoint_paths=
      map(RelabelToQG2Path,subject.seed.subject_endpoint_paths),
    construction_rule=RuleFor(subject.kind)).
```

For the three rows in the displayed order, `RequirementOf(subject)` must equal
the decoded `SupportProjectionRequirement` payload at group-5 local rows
`5,6,7`, respectively, by complete bytes. The requirement is a cross-check;
it is neither stored in the subject nor followed as a pointer. Construction of
each fresh seed independently reruns the QG3 route compiler before this
comparison.

Consequently, with

```text
PR_c = [20,21,21,21,20,21,25+h_6],
```

the frozen QG2 family still contains exactly `5*PR_c` occurrences of its QG2
path-plan type. The three QG3 seeds separately contain exactly `3*PR_c`
occurrences of the fresh preservation path-plan type. Every repetition remains
physical and ordered. Endpoint modes and proposition syntax contain zero such
source paths, and the preservation family contains zero admission-premise
values. The combined quotient family therefore still has exactly the two
physical admission values already proved in subsection 10.10.6.

#### 10.15.5 Shared result parameter and `NormalizedSeed`

The endpoint literals cannot return
`QuotientSupportProjectionPlanCandidateV1`: that S1b3 type contains only
candidate keys, while the reindexing endpoint must admit later formal/action
keys. Let `K` range over a future shared action-capable support-key schema and
let

```text
iota_candidate:
  QuotientSupportKeyPlanCandidateV1 -> K
```

be total, injective, and order-preserving for the exact candidate comparator
`Current < Older < Structural` fixed in subsection 10.14.4. Define only the
following parametric schema and embedding at this cut:

```text
SharedSupportProjectionPlan<K> =
  (keys:vector<K>)

SchemaOf_K(SupportProjectionSort) =
  SharedSupportProjectionPlan<K>

EmbedCandidateProjection_K(P).keys =
  map(iota_candidate,P.keys).
```

The map preserves the input vector exactly. It never sorts, deduplicates, or
recomputes classes. A concrete `K`, `iota_candidate`, wire schema, and numerical
`TypeIdV1` remain open.

`NormalizedSeed` is a distinct quotient endpoint mode whose interpreter invokes
the reflexive quotient normal-form eliminator. It is not an alias for the
`ProjectSeed` tag and not a normalization of projection bytes. `ProjectSeed`
and `NormalizedSeed` are the two endpoint modes of R2c's normalization-
preservation subject. Separately, the enclosing R2c normalization evidence
relates the complete raw and normalized envelope endpoints through the exact
`NormalizationMaterialPaths_c` frontier and validates second-pass idempotence.
Root `0xfd` deliberately removes the raw endpoint, material paths,
normalization witness, and replay evidence; `B_c` contains only the already-
normalized quotient coordinates. There is therefore no lawful second quotient
endpoint and no generic normalizer over the twelve preservation-route result
schemas.

Define the quotient normal-form eliminator on the admitted contextual domain:

```text
NF_Q_c:
  AdmittedQuotientCandidateContext_c
  -> AdmittedQuotientCandidateContext_c

NF_Q_c(B_c) = B_c.
```

`AdmittedQuotientCandidateContext_c` denotes exactly the dependent subtype of
the tuples `B_c` fixed in subsection 10.15.1 that have passed all QG1, QG2, and
S1b3 validation. It is compiler-only domain notation, not a new encoded schema
or an additional field.

This is definitional elimination into the already-normalized quotient context,
not an invocation of the R2c raw-to-normalized compiler, a runtime check, or a
stored proof. It introduces no raw presentation, normalization evidence,
receipt, representative, second graph, or preservation back-edge. The mode
interpreter must still dispatch the distinct `NormalizedSeed` tag and apply
`NF_Q_c`; it may not rewrite the encoded mode to `ProjectSeed`.

For every later admissible `(K,iota_candidate)`, the closed denotations are

```text
Denote_K(ProjectSeed,Seed_c;B_c) =
  EmbedCandidateProjection_K(B_c.G0.projection)

Denote_K(NormalizedSeed,Seed_c;B_c) =
  EmbedCandidateProjection_K(NF_Q_c(B_c).G0.projection)
  = EmbedCandidateProjection_K(B_c.G0.projection).
```

Thus the two denotations are equal in the parametric
`SharedSupportProjectionPlan<K>` while their clause syntax remains distinct;
canonical-byte identity follows only after the concrete `K` wire is frozen.
This is not a claim that the R2c raw endpoint was definitionally normalized.
When R2e later relates a representative subject to this template, it performs
the checks in subsection 10.15.2 and may rely on the enclosing R2c value's
already completed normalization/idempotence validation. Those checks authorize
erasure only: they cannot change `NF_Q_c`, add a quotient endpoint, enter
`B_c`, or select a kernel field. Collapsing the two mode tags or normalizing,
sorting, or deduplicating the projection vector is invalid. The normalization
subject remains a proposition descriptor rather than stored evidence.

The remaining endpoint equations are obligations on the next cut:

```text
Denote_K(RenamedSeed(h),Seed_c;B_c) =
  Pi_shared_K(QCandidate(Rename_Q(B_c,h)))

Denote_K(ReindexedSeed(q),Seed_c;B_c) =
  Pi_shared_K(QAction(B_c,q)).
```

`Rename_Q`, `QAction`, `Pi_shared_K`, and their formal support payloads are not
defined by this kernel. The formal-action locus and shared sums must close
before these equations become a concrete typed subject family.

#### 10.15.6 Transaction boundary, independence, and next cut

Kernel construction aborts atomically on any context, constructor, route,
translation, result-schema, repeat, order, count, seed, requirement, rule,
binder, mode, ordinal, literal, node, positional association, or checked-
arithmetic mismatch. It also aborts on a reserved tag; a non-`Full`
projection; an endpoint-local seed or path payload; sharing a seed across
subjects; an alternate action kind/cardinality/base; a numerical or guessed
TypeId; or any attempted QG2 mutation. No partial template is returned.

No R2c source bundle, raw code or ordinal, owner coordinate, checked wrapper,
presentation, representative, normalization witness, receipt, replay,
construction transcript, R3 account, evidence target, semantic SR2 result,
`nu`, `GCap`, `gamma`, or semantic-novelty datum can select a kernel field.
Holding the complete retained quotient context fixed while changing only such
forbidden material leaves the fresh route census, three seeds, modes, clause
syntax, normal-form denotation, and QG2 cross-check unchanged.

This closes the preservation route/seed factorization, exact quotient
`NormalizedSeed` meaning, fresh binder/literal/node wire template, parametric
shared-result contract, three-row shape, `3*PR_c` QG3 path census, zero-new-
admission theorem, and representative independence at the compiler-plan level.
It does not close concrete subject typing, a concrete `SchemaOf_K`
instantiation, final TypeId lowering, renaming/reindexing denotation, the
preservation aggregate, or the enclosing support record.

The preservation kernel itself closes here. The subsequent source audit in
subsection 10.16 corrects one phase-order statement formerly attached to this
boundary: the reindexing template's binder ordinal 1 fixes only the quantified
action parameter of the preservation proposition. It is not the semantic
owner of action support. The formal owner must instead be rooted in the
separate canonical instantiated naturality subject required by A3 and R2c.
This correction changes no preservation schema or denotation above. Candidate
`M_nf`, `G0`, every QG2 byte, QG2's `5*PR_c` census, and the two-copy admission
theorem remain immutable.

### 10.16 QG3-S1c0 instantiated-action anchor and primitive spine — CLOSED

The first post-preservation audit finds a genuine ordering error in the former
frontier description. This subsection corrects it and closes the largest
action prefix that does not guess the still-open canonical-composite subject
program. It does not close that program, the final formal-ID occurrence wire,
the shared symbolic manifest, or the shared support graph.

#### 10.16.1 Ownership-source corrigendum

Three frozen facts decide the owner:

1. R2c's pure reindexing-preservation subject represents the arbitrary action
   only by its formal binder and `ReindexedSeed`; its seed contains no action-
   component census, `FormalActionIdV1`, checked action, or action root.
2. R2c binds every nonempty action-root entry's `formal_id` to the final action
   trace and instantiated subject locus.
3. A3 fixes the final key's instantiated naturality entry as the canonical
   `SubjectSyntaxV1` built once for the complete canonical composite.

Subsection 10.15.4 already records the same boundary locally: preservation
binder ordinal 1 freezes the clause's syntactic action position but not its
semantic support locus. Therefore no quotient compiler may define

```text
FormalActionOwner = preservation.Reindexing.binders[1]
```

or use that binder as an alias, fallback, or occurrence coordinate. The
preservation subject consumes an action in its proposition; it does not own
the formal dependencies of that action. QG3 also continues to forbid reuse or
reinterpretation of `SubjectPathV1` and QG2's globally relocated naturality
binder ordinal 6.

The corrected dependency is:

```text
retained base quotient context + total normalized action A
  -> canonical instantiated naturality-subject skeleton
  -> canonical-total-action component and node compiler
  -> exact formal occurrence IDs and nonempty root-6 traversal
  -> generated M_action and final shared sums
  -> concrete preservation denotations
  -> action support, lineage, footprints, and eight-group closure.
```

No arrow may be reversed. In particular, support classes, action-introduced
lineage, or the result projection cannot select the subject that owns them.

#### 10.16.2 Standalone subject skeleton and semantic binder anchor

Let `A` be the complete constructor-kind-matching normalized action after
flattening every prior and newly appended action, and let `n=A.primitives.len`.
The group-8 option obeys exactly:

```text
InstantiatedActionSubject(B,A) = None       iff n=0
InstantiatedActionSubject(B,A) = Some(S_A)  iff n>0

InstantiatedActionSubject(B,A ++ []) =
  InstantiatedActionSubject(B,A).
```

Thus a candidate and a total identity action have `None`; appending identity
to an existing nonempty action preserves the same `Some` result. There is no
latest-step subject, vector of subjects, or per-primitive subject.

Before its complete value-node program is generated, `S_A` is governed by this
compiler-plan skeleton:

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectRolePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CanonicalNaturality=0

QuotientInstantiatedActionBasePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SourceCandidate=0

QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderSortPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  FormalAction=0(
    action_kind:ActionKindV1,
    cardinality:FormalActionCardinalityV1,
    base:QuotientInstantiatedActionBasePlanCandidateV1)

QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectSkeletonPlanCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectRolePlanCandidateV1,
   subject_kind:SubjectRootKindV1,
   binders:
     vector<QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderSortPlanCandidateV1>)

QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder=0.
```

The only admitted skeleton has `role=CanonicalNaturality`,
`subject_kind=Naturality=7`, and

```text
binders = [
  FormalAction(
    action_kind=a_c,
    cardinality=ArbitraryNormalized,
    base=SourceCandidate)
].
```

Its standalone local binder ordinal is exactly 0. `ExactlyOnePrimitive` is the
frozen QG2 slot-7 premise and is invalid for an arbitrary nonempty composite;
`PriorActionResult` and every other base are also invalid. The semantic locus
`CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder` resolves only to

```text
group_8.instantiated_action_subject
  / Some
  / canonical_naturality_program
  / binders[0]
```

and, after the final syntax is built, to its required byte-identical syntax-
binder copy. It names the binder declaration, not a guessed value-node
ordinal. Subsection 10.17 derives the required finer
`CanonicalPrimitive(i)` occurrence locus from the completed component program;
this skeleton itself neither collapses nor distinguishes occurrences by
assumption.

The subject is interpreted in the unique base environment already present in
the enclosing QG2 primary family. The quotient does not copy that source
bundle into `S_A`: the compiler receives

```text
BaseSubjectEnvironment(B) =
  B.QPrimarySubjects.root_projection.source_bundle
```

as nonencoded dependent context and rechecks every subject path against it.
R2e must verify the representative's repeated base bundle before erasing the
copy. This is a factorization, not an omission or pointer: it prevents a second
pair of embedded admission-premise values while retaining the exact base
semantics. The rebuilt final-image source bundle, final payload, final key,
support graph, and action lineage are forbidden subject environments.

The factorization theorem is exact. Let `E_A` be a supplied validated R2c
action image, let `E_0=Base(E_A)` be its original candidate, and let
`RepresentativeActionSubjectBaseBundle(E_A)` denote the exact source-side
bundle which the frozen R2c continuation requires the reconstructed action
subject to retain. This notation is an R2e compiler projection, not a field of
the R2d quotient. For the quotient base context `B`, require

```text
RepresentativeActionSubjectBaseBundle(E_A)
  = E_0.subjects.root_projection.source_bundle

CompileQSourceBundle(E_0)
  = B.QPrimarySubjects.root_projection.source_bundle
  = BaseSubjectEnvironment(B).
```

All equalities are complete typed bytes. Relative to the enclosing `B`, the
erasure is reversible by reinserting `BaseSubjectEnvironment(B)`; hence two
admitted action subjects cannot be identified by dropping a bundle on which
they differed. The first equality discharges R2c's byte-identical-retention
requirement, the second uses the already frozen item-3/item-4 translator, and
neither equation permits an action-side bundle. Failure of either equality
aborts before the quotient subject is published. The encoded quotient family
therefore retains exactly the original QG2 bundle once and still contains
exactly its two physical admission-premise values.

When the eight-group record is eventually materialized, its primary-subject
field must be byte-identical to `B.QPrimarySubjects`, and its action subject is
interpreted against `BaseSubjectEnvironment(B)`. That later encoding equality
is an output cross-check, not an input or a back-edge into this cut.

The eventual `Some` value has exactly one naturality program. Its endpoint
topology is the constructor's frozen QG2 slot-7 row applied once to the total
canonical composite; its one clause is `ApproxOrd`, except that
DischargeTransformer uses `DependentDischargeEquality`. Its syntax begins with
the byte-identical program value block, appends that one clause, and ends with
`Conjunction([clause])`. The exact action-component nodes, node counts, result
types, root ordinal, and final bytes remain outputs of the next cut rather
than open fields of this skeleton.

#### 10.16.3 Exact root-6 primitive spine

The part of the nonempty action traversal independent of those later node
bytes is already exact. Write

```text
T_sub = NormalizedSubstitutionV1
T_sq  = QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitiveCandidateV1

P_sub(i) =
  (root=ActionArguments,
   locus=Direct([
     SumPayload(0),RecordField(0),VectorElement(checked_u32(i))]),
   nominal_result_schema=T_sub)

P_sq(i) =
  (root=ActionArguments,
   locus=Direct([
     SumPayload(1),RecordField(0),VectorElement(checked_u32(i))]),
   nominal_result_schema=T_sq).
```

`nominal_result_schema` is pass-A schema notation, not a guessed shared
symbolic tag. The final path stores its generated shared symbolic variant only
after `M_action` closes.

For `SubstitutionAction(primitives)` the compiler emits one leading
`ActionArgument=28` incidence at `P_sub(i)` for every vector position `i`. For
`ContextSquareAction(primitives)` it emits one at `P_sq(i)`. A square primitive
is one record containing `rho`, `sigma`, and `theta_prime`; it is not three
action arguments. The three substitutions are visited later as record
children in field order. Both `n` and every `i` must admit exact checked `u32`
conversion before any path or incidence is reserved. The exact root-6 order is

```text
for i in [0,n):
  require checked_u32(i);
  emit the one ActionArgument incidence for primitive i;
  traverse primitive i semantically in typed field order.
```

The incidence's pass-A nominal judgment is
`NominalStructuralRecord(T_sub)` or `NominalStructuralRecord(T_sq)`. Its final formal
referent, owner, and key must resolve through
`CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder` and the exact occurrence locator produced
by subsection 10.17. That audit selects one distinct semantic
`CanonicalPrimitive(i)` locus per complete root-6 primitive and one checked
three-node projection block, not three formal IDs, for a square. This
subsection alone deliberately makes no such choice.

The identity action has zero root-6 incidences and zero primitive-element
visits; validating its empty same-tag sum/vector wrappers emits no row. Every
nonempty action has exactly `n` leading edge-28 incidences, in primitive order,
with no missing, extra, deduplicated, or repeated-by-caller row. QG2 virtual
lifts, composites, epsilon, reindexed payload components, and congruence
endpoints are not root-6 primitive entries. `ActionIntroduced=27` is likewise
absent from this spine; its exact lineage-owned dispatcher remains later work.

Concrete `Global(g)` dependencies encountered below a primitive continue to
resolve under the retained history to current or older owners. `FormalGlobal`
has no quotient image. A formal-binder dependency must resolve through the
same instantiated-subject binder anchor selected for its enclosing primitive;
formal owners seed no `Cl_b` suffix and enter none of the implementation,
current-output, or prior-support footprints.

#### 10.16.4 Primitive schema delta and the generated-manifest target

Let `U_primitive` be the static union of leading root-6 spine result schemas
over every valid nonempty action in all seven constructor families. It forces
exactly two nominal structural-record schemas not present in candidate `U_nf`:

```text
U_primitive \ U_nf = {T_sub,T_sq}.
```

For one selected action the difference is the corresponding singleton
`{T_sub}` or `{T_sq}`; the two-element equation is the project-wide compiler
image, not a claim that one constructor admits both action kinds.

Their canonical schema locators are:

```text
Canon(T_sub) =
  Root(CanonicalPayload)/sSP(3)/sRF(2)

Canon(T_sq) =
  Root(PrimarySubjects)/sRF(1)/sRF(8)/sRF(1)/
  sSP(1)/sRF(0)/sVE.
```

These are canonical nominal-schema routes, not live dependency paths and not
extra support roots. The candidate seventeen-node enum remains immutable.

Subsection 10.17 proves that the complete canonical-composite subject and the
edge-28 prefix add repetitions only of `U_nf` beyond the displayed primitive
delta. S1c2 must prove the same inclusion for all remaining descendants and
edge-27 action incidences. If that inclusion holds, the exact manifest generator

```text
M_target =
  sort_unique([Canon(T) | T in U_nf union U_primitive])
```

has exactly 19 nodes, with `T_sub` inserted after candidate tag 0 and `T_sq`
after candidate tag 10; the candidate symbolic embedding is then

```text
0      -> 0
1..10  -> old_tag + 1
11..16 -> old_tag + 2.
```

This is the exact target lemma, not a frozen final shared enum. If the remaining
edge-27/action traversal discovers another nominal schema, the compiler
must regenerate the shared manifest and this conditional map does not apply.
It may not hide the schema, append it to candidate `M_nf`, or preserve the
number 19 by aliasing two nominal types.

#### 10.16.5 Transaction, independence, and next cut

Skeleton/spine construction aborts atomically on a wrong constructor/action
kind, a `Some`/`None` mismatch, preservation-binder ownership, `FormalGlobal`,
a nonzero local binder ordinal, wrong cardinality/base, more or fewer than one
subject, an alternate subject kind or clause family, a copied final-image
environment, an extra admission copy, a malformed root-6 path, missing/extra/
reordered edge-28 row, square-to-three-arguments flattening, result-schema or
judgment mismatch, formal entry into `Cl_b` or a history footprint, checked-
arithmetic overflow, allocation failure, or any partial publication. A caller
action that fails typing produces the frozen checked action-false result before
a canonical `A` exists; a mismatch after canonical `A` is accepted is an
invariant abort.

The skeleton, semantic binder anchor, and primitive spine are functions only
of the retained base quotient context and total canonical `A`. Raw postfix or
association syntax, a newly appended stepwise endpoint, action trace or replay,
representative path/support/owner ordinals, receipts, resource accounts,
numerical `TypeIdV1`, `QFootprints` as a selector, R3, a semantic SR2 result,
`nu`, `GCap`, `gamma`, or semantic-novelty data cannot select them. Holding the
base context and total `A` fixed while changing only such material leaves this
subcut unchanged.

This closes the ownership-source correction, empty/nonempty option law,
standalone one-binder naturality skeleton, semantic binder anchor, exact
one-per-primitive root-6 paths and edge-28 spine, square-primitive atomicity,
footprint exclusion, and the two-node primitive schema delta. It changes no
QG2 value, candidate `M_nf`, S1b3 graph, preservation schema, route count, or
admission value.

Subsection 10.17 now closes the fresh canonical-total-action component
compiler: right-associated substitution composites and composite lifts, square
vertical composites and the canonical pasted epsilon, the complete
multi-primitive action-projection prefix, strict-postorder node bytes and
counts, exact occurrence locators, and the finished `Some` subject. The
following combined subject/action pass-A census generates `M_action` and the
final shared manifest; only after that may R2d close the shared formal sums,
candidate embedding, concrete preservation denotations, nonempty support,
action-introduced lineage, footprints, and the eight-group value.

### 10.17 QG3-S1c1 canonical-total-action component and subject compiler -- CLOSED

This cut completes the one-program `Some` value left open by subsection 10.16.
It compiles the total flattened action from the original base, not the latest
step and not an intermediate action image. It freezes the component forest,
the ten-node naturality endpoint suffix, all node/count/source locators, and a
fresh primitive-occurrence locus. It does not yet freeze the shared support
sums or the final action manifest: `ActionIntroduced=27` and the complete
nonempty support traversal remain inputs to the next pass-A cut.

Two choices which A3 leaves implicit for actions of length greater than two are
made explicit here:

1. substitution composition is the already required right-associated suffix
   comb;
2. square vertical composition is a left prefix fold, matching the required
   left-to-right pointwise paste.

The second item is a fresh R2d canonicalization choice. It is not attributed to
an upstream associativity theorem. Both choices are deterministic presentations
of the flattened primitive vector and assert no associativity, functoriality,
naturality, or equality proof.

#### 10.17.1 Fresh source paths and component grammar

Every source path in the standalone program is independently compiled against
`BaseSubjectEnvironment(B)` from subsection 10.16.2. It uses a fresh nominal
wrapper, so no stored QG2 path occurrence or preservation path is reused:

```text
Q_B = BaseSubjectEnvironment(B).schema_payload

QuotientInstantiatedActionSourceRootPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  EvaluationBinding=0
| SchemaPayload=1
| PublicFields=2
| PrincipalLineage=3
| FieldLineages=4
| Footprints=5
| Obligations=6
| SelectorInputs=7
| ActionConstants=8
| SourceCandidate=9

QuotientInstantiatedActionSourceResultPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  EnvelopeObjectDescriptor=0
| CandidateAdmissionPremise=1
| OccurrenceVector=2
| Occurrence=3
| NormalizedAction=4
| NormalizedTelescope=5
| NormalizedSubstitution=6
| NormalizedGrammar=7
| NormalizedScheme=8
| NormalizedSection=9
| NormalizedLiveDemand=10
| NormalizedContract=11

QuotientInstantiatedActionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientInstantiatedActionSourceRootPlanCandidateV1,
   steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
   result_schema:QuotientInstantiatedActionSourceResultPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientInstantiatedSquareFieldPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Rho=0 | Sigma=1 | ThetaPrime=2

QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveComponentPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SubstitutionPrimitive=0(primitive_ordinal:u32)
| SquarePrimitiveField=1(
    primitive_ordinal:u32,
    field:QuotientInstantiatedSquareFieldPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SourceProjection=0(
    QuotientInstantiatedActionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| PrimitiveArgument=1(
    QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveComponentPlanCandidateV1)
| EarlierDerived=2(derived_component_ordinal:u32)

QuotientInstantiatedActionDerivedComponentPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  IteratedLift=0(
    source:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1,
    target:QuotientInstantiatedActionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1,
    lift_depth:u16)
| OrderedComposite=1(
    left:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1,
    right:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1)
| CanonicalPastedEpsilon=2(primitive_count:u32)
| ReindexedPayloadComponent=3(
    source:QuotientInstantiatedActionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint=4(
    epsilon:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1,
    left_composite:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1,
    right_composite:QuotientInstantiatedActionComponentRefPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientInstantiatedActionProjectionRootPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  NormalizedAction=0
| PrimitiveArgument=1(
    QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveComponentPlanCandidateV1)
| DerivedComponent=2(
    component_ordinal:u32,
    component:
      QuotientInstantiatedActionDerivedComponentPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientInstantiatedActionProjectionPathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (action_binder:u32,
   root:QuotientInstantiatedActionProjectionRootPlanCandidateV1,
   result_type:QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1).
```

The fresh root/result tags name the same ten source roles and twelve semantic
leaf schemas, in the same order, as their QG2 counterparts; they are neither
aliases nor copied QG2 values. `CompileActionSource(role)` independently emits
and typed-walks this fresh triple, then embeds its result through the same exact
leaf-schema table into the action node's primary symbolic result.

The sole valid `action_binder` is local ordinal 0. `EarlierDerived(j)` requires
`j` to be strictly smaller than the ordinal of its containing derived
component. Every primitive/component ordinal and `primitive_count` is checked
in `u64` and converts exactly to `u32` before the enclosing value is allocated.
`SquarePrimitiveField(i,...)` is a subject-level projection of one field of
square primitive `i`; it does not split the root-6 action argument, which
remains the one complete square record fixed in subsection 10.16.3.

`IteratedLift` is intentionally generalized from QG2: its source is the final
composite reference, not necessarily primitive 0. `CanonicalPastedEpsilon`
reconstructs the unique final receipt-free equality subject from the complete
square vector. It stores neither the primitive epsilon subjects nor the
left-to-right paste tree. Those compiler locals are independently rebuilt and
checked, then erased, exactly as A3 requires for intermediate association and
pasting traces. `EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint` names only the final five-endpoint
semantic square from subsection 10.10.3 and stores no proof.

The action literal is a fresh tag-preserving mirror of the quotient-primary
literal:

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionCoreProjectionLiteralPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CorePayloadProjection=0(
    path:QuotientInstantiatedActionSourcePathPlanCandidateV1)
| ReservedCarrierPayloadProjection=1
| ReservedExactOccurrenceProjection=2
| ReservedNormalizedOccurrenceProjection=3
| FormalActionArgumentProjection=4(
    path:QuotientInstantiatedActionProjectionPathPlanCandidateV1).
```

The instantiated-action node is the following fresh nominal, tag-for-tag
mirror of `QuotientPrimarySubjectNodeCandidateV1`, with only its `Literal=1`
payload replaced by the fresh literal above:

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectNodePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Binder=0(binder_ordinal:u32)
| Literal=1(
    QuotientInstantiatedActionCoreProjectionLiteralPlanCandidateV1)
| Project=2(
    source_node:u32,
    path:QuotientSubjectSemanticProjectionPlanCandidateV1)
| Normalize=3(source_node:u32)
| Reindex=4(value_node:u32,action_node:u32)
| Compose=5(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| RenameHistory=6(value_node:u32,renaming_node:u32)
| Selector=7(value_node:u32)
| Origin=8(value_node:u32,origin_path:OriginPathV1)
| ApproxOrd=9(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| ApproxEnvelope=10(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| Equality=11(left_node:u32,right_node:u32)
| Membership=12(element_node:u32,set_node:u32)
| Conjunction=13(conjunct_nodes:vector<u32>)
| Implication=14(premise_node:u32,conclusion_node:u32)
| ExistsUnique=15(
    binder_ordinal:u32,domain_node:u32,predicate_node:u32)
| DependentDischargeEquality=16(left_node:u32,right_node:u32).
```

The compiler image admits exactly
`Binder=0`, `Literal=1`, `Project=2`, `Normalize=3`, `Reindex=4`,
`ApproxOrd=9`, `Conjunction=13`, and
`DependentDischargeEquality=16`; every other node variant and every reserved
literal aborts in a completed instantiated-action subject. Node result types
reuse the closed 22-way `QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1`; this
cut adds no primary-subject symbolic type.

The completed records are:

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionEndpointProgramPlanCandidateV1 =
  (subject_kind:SubjectRootKindV1,
   binders:
     vector<QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderSortPlanCandidateV1>,
   value_nodes:
     vector<QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectNodePlanCandidateV1>,
   value_node_types:
     vector<QuotientPrimarySubjectSymbolicTypeCandidateV1>)

QuotientInstantiatedActionSyntaxPlanCandidateV1 =
  (binders:
     vector<QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderSortPlanCandidateV1>,
   nodes:vector<QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectNodePlanCandidateV1>,
   root_node:u32)

QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectPlanCandidateV1 =
  (role:QuotientInstantiatedActionSubjectRolePlanCandidateV1,
   program:QuotientInstantiatedActionEndpointProgramPlanCandidateV1,
   syntax:QuotientInstantiatedActionSyntaxPlanCandidateV1).
```

The role, subject kind, and one binder are exactly the skeleton in subsection
10.16.2. `program.binders=syntax.binders` in complete bytes. The syntax node
vector begins with the byte-identical complete `program.value_nodes`, then the
one clause, then the singleton conjunction. The contextual source environment
is not a fourth field and is not encoded again.

#### 10.17.2 Right-associated substitution compiler

For

```text
A = SubstitutionAction([theta_0,...,theta_(n-1)]), n>=1,
theta_i : Gamma_(i+1) -> Gamma_i,
```

define, using the frozen `QCompose(outer,inner)=outer[inner]` orientation,

```text
RA_(n-1) = theta_(n-1)
RA_k     = QCompose(theta_k,RA_(k+1)).result
             for descending k=n-2,...,0
TotalSub(A) = RA_0.
```

This is the fixed right-associated presentation
`theta_0[theta_1[...[theta_(n-1)]]]`. It is not obtained by folding the
constructor payload through intermediate action images.

The prefix first emits primitive projections at nodes `0..n-1`. For
`0<=j<n-1`, let `k=n-2-j` and emit derived component `D_j` at node `n+j`:

```text
D_j = OrderedComposite(
  left=PrimitiveArgument(SubstitutionPrimitive(k)),
  right=if j=0
        then PrimitiveArgument(SubstitutionPrimitive(n-1))
        else EarlierDerived(j-1)).
```

Thus `D_0` is the rightmost two-primitive suffix and `D_(n-2)` is the total
composite. Define

```text
TotalSubRef(A) =
  PrimitiveArgument(SubstitutionPrimitive(0))  if n=1,
  EarlierDerived(n-2)                          if n>1.
```

Formation and Comparison stop there. Abstraction, Aggregation, and
DemandCompiler append exactly one derived `IteratedLift` of `TotalSubRef(A)` at
component ordinal `n-1` and subject node `2n-1`. Its target/depth are exactly:

```text
Abstraction:
  (CompileActionSource(AbstractionLiftTargetA),
   checked_u16(Q_B.a.len))

Aggregation:
  (CompileActionSource(AggregationLiftTargetA),
   checked_u16(Q_B.a.len))

DemandCompiler:
  (CompileActionSource(DemandCompilerLiftTargetExports),
   checked_u16(Q_B.grammar.exports.len)).
```

The paths are independently generated at the exact base-payload routes from
subsection 10.9.4. The total-component reference, target, depth, and evaluated
lift are cross-checked against the action applied once to the original base.
No primitive-by-primitive payload fold, lift composition theorem, or caller
association tree is retained.

The exact substitution prefix lengths are

```text
P_Formation(n)          = 2n-1
P_Abstraction(n)        = 2n
P_Aggregation(n)        = 2n
P_Comparison(n)         = 2n-1
P_DemandCompiler(n)     = 2n.
```

At `n=1` there is no composite component. The first and fourth rows contain
only `theta_0`; the other rows contain `theta_0` and its one total lift. This
specializes to the exact QG2 slot-7 component order and prefix lengths without
aliasing the QG2 `ExactlyOnePrimitive` binder or wire.

#### 10.17.3 Composite-square compiler

For Transport or DischargeTransformer write

```text
A = ContextSquareAction([Q_0,...,Q_(n-1)]), n>=1
Q_i = (rho_i,sigma_i,theta_(i+1)),
theta_0 = the substitution projected from the base payload.
```

The compiler first rechecks, for every ascending `i`,

```text
rho_i.gamma       = theta_i.delta
sigma_i.gamma     = theta_i.gamma
theta_(i+1).delta = rho_i.delta
theta_(i+1).gamma = sigma_i.delta

QCompose(theta_i,rho_i).result
  = QCompose(sigma_i,theta_(i+1)).result
```

in complete canonical bytes. Primitive field projections then occupy nodes
`3i`, `3i+1`, and `3i+2` in exact `rho`, `sigma`, `theta_prime` order.

The two total vertical maps use the fresh left-prefix convention:

```text
R_0 = rho_0
R_i = QCompose(R_(i-1),rho_i).result       for ascending 1<=i<n

S_0 = sigma_0
S_i = QCompose(S_(i-1),sigma_i).result     for ascending 1<=i<n.
```

For `0<=j<n-1`, the `rho` component at derived ordinal `j` constructs
`R_(j+1)` from primitive `rho_0` when `j=0`, otherwise from
`EarlierDerived(j-1)`, and primitive `rho_(j+1)`. The `sigma` component at
derived ordinal `n-1+j` analogously constructs `S_(j+1)`; for `j>0` its prior
reference is `EarlierDerived(n+j-2)`. Therefore

```text
TotalRhoRef = PrimitiveArgument(SquarePrimitiveField(0,Rho))
                if n=1
              EarlierDerived(n-2) otherwise

TotalSigmaRef = PrimitiveArgument(SquarePrimitiveField(0,Sigma))
                  if n=1
                EarlierDerived(2n-3) otherwise.
```

The internal paste checker forms

```text
epsilon_i = SubjectNormalizedSubstitutionEqualityV1(
  left=QCompose(theta_i,rho_i).result,
  right=QCompose(sigma_i,theta_(i+1)).result)

Paste_0 = epsilon_0
Paste_i = PointwiseLeftPaste(Paste_(i-1),epsilon_i)
            for ascending 1<=i<n,
```

where `Paste_i` has endpoints
`QCompose(theta_0,R_i).result` and
`QCompose(S_i,theta_(i+1)).result`. Each intermediate is independently typed
and checked. `PointwiseLeftPaste` is not an encoded operation or witness: after
the boundary checks it returns exactly the
`SubjectNormalizedSubstitutionEqualityV1(left,right)` record with those two
displayed endpoints. Only `Paste_(n-1)` is emitted, as
`CanonicalPastedEpsilon(checked_u32(n))` at derived ordinal `2n-2`.
Primitive epsilon leaves, intermediate paste nodes, association endpoints,
proofs, and replay are compiler locals and have no encoded child. Given the
base and flattened primitive vector, rerunning these equations reconstructs
them uniquely; this is an exact factorization, not an evidence erasure that
could identify two different final subjects.

The final composites are emitted next:

```text
CompositeThetaSource_Transport = TransportCompositeThetaSource
CompositeThetaSource_DischargeTransformer =
  DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource

derived 2n-1:
  LC = OrderedComposite(
    SourceProjection(CompileActionSource(CompositeThetaSource_c)),
    TotalRhoRef)

derived 2n:
  RC = OrderedComposite(
    TotalSigmaRef,
    PrimitiveArgument(SquarePrimitiveField(n-1,ThetaPrime))).
```

The compiler requires the final pasted-epsilon endpoints to be byte-identical
to `LC.result` and `RC.result`. For DischargeTransformer, let
`h=u64(Q_B.live.specialized_contracts.len)` and `r=4+h`; derived ordinals
`2n+1+j`, `0<=j<r`, then emit reindexed `G`, `S`, `a`, `L`, and contracts in
the frozen payload/ascending-contract order. All are rebuilt with total
`S_(n-1)` from their independently generated base source paths. Transport has
`r=0` and emits none.

Finally, derived ordinal `2n+1+r` is

```text
EpsilonCongruenceEndpoint(
  epsilon=EarlierDerived(2n-2),
  left_composite=EarlierDerived(2n-1),
  right_composite=EarlierDerived(2n)).
```

Its value is exactly the subsection-10.10.3 five-endpoint Transport or
DischargeTransformer square with

```text
rho=R_(n-1), sigma=S_(n-1), theta_prime=theta_n,
epsilon=Paste_(n-1), LC=theta_0.R_(n-1), RC=S_(n-1).theta_n.
```

For DischargeTransformer the outer naturality endpoint must equal the
congruence component's `naturality` field in complete bytes. Neither family
requires any adjacent or end-to-end proposition to be inhabited.

The exact square prefix lengths are

```text
P_Transport(n) = 3n + 2(n-1) + 1 + 2 + 1
               = 5n+2

P_DischargeTransformer(n,h) = 5n+2 + 4+h
                            = 5n+6+h.
```

At `n=1`, both vertical-composite ranges are empty and the sequence is exactly
the frozen QG2 order

```text
rho, sigma, theta_prime, epsilon, LC, RC,
[G,S,a,L,contracts], congruence.
```

Thus Transport specializes to prefix 7 and DischargeTransformer to prefix
`11+h`.

#### 10.17.4 Exact endpoint suffix, node bytes, and counts

Let `P=P_c(n)` be the appropriate prefix above. Every endpoint occurrence is
fresh; the compiler performs no common-subexpression elimination. Projection
roles are exactly

```text
OutputRole_Formation             = FormationOutput
OutputRole_Abstraction           = AbstractionOutput
OutputRole_Aggregation           = AggregationOutput
OutputRole_Transport             = TransportOutput
OutputRole_Comparison            = ComparisonOutput
OutputRole_DemandCompiler        = DemandCompilerOutput
OutputRole_DischargeTransformer  = DischargeTransformerOutput

ImplementationRole_Transport = TransportNormalizedImplementation
ImplementationRole_DischargeTransformer =
  DischargeTransformerNormalizedImplementation.
```

These are the nine-role values from subsection 10.10.3, not new projection
roles. For the exact result types, define

```text
X_Formation            = NormalizedTelescope
X_Abstraction          = NormalizedFamily
X_Aggregation          = NormalizedTelescope
X_Transport            = NormalizedTelescope
X_Comparison           = NormalizedComparison
X_DemandCompiler       = NormalizedLiveDemand
X_DischargeTransformer = NormalizedDischarge

Types(A_X) = [EnvelopeObjectDescriptor,NormalizedAction,
              EnvelopeObjectDescriptor,X,X]
Types(B_X) = [EnvelopeObjectDescriptor,X,NormalizedAction,X,X].
```

The stored `value_node_types` vector is exactly the component-prefix projection
result types in emission order, followed by `Types(A_X)||Types(B_X)` for
Formation, `Types(B_X)||Types(A_X)` for Abstraction/Aggregation/Comparison/
DemandCompiler, or `Types(A_X)||Types(A_X)` for Transport and
DischargeTransformer. The first square block uses the implementation role and
the second the output role; both have the displayed common result `X`.
No stored symbolic tag selects or overrides this inference. Formation's
ten-node suffix is

```text
P+0  Literal(E)
P+1  Binder(0)
P+2  Reindex(P+0,P+1)
P+3  Project(P+2,FormationOutput)
P+4  Normalize(P+3)

P+5  Literal(E)
P+6  Project(P+5,FormationOutput)
P+7  Binder(0)
P+8  Reindex(P+6,P+7)
P+9  Normalize(P+8).
```

Abstraction, Aggregation, Comparison, and DemandCompiler use

```text
P+0  Literal(E)
P+1  Project(P+0,OutputRole_c)
P+2  Binder(0)
P+3  Reindex(P+1,P+2)
P+4  Normalize(P+3)

P+5  Literal(E)
P+6  Binder(0)
P+7  Reindex(P+5,P+6)
P+8  Project(P+7,OutputRole_c)
P+9  Normalize(P+8).
```

Transport and DischargeTransformer use

```text
P+0  Literal(E)
P+1  Binder(0)
P+2  Reindex(P+0,P+1)
P+3  Project(P+2,ImplementationRole_c)
P+4  Normalize(P+3)

P+5  Literal(E)
P+6  Binder(0)
P+7  Reindex(P+5,P+6)
P+8  Project(P+7,OutputRole_c)
P+9  Normalize(P+8).
```

In every row, `P+4` is the left endpoint and `P+9` the right. Node `P+10` is
`ApproxOrd(P+4,P+9)` except for DischargeTransformer, where it is
`DependentDischargeEquality(P+4,P+9)`. Node `P+11` is
`Conjunction([P+10])` and is the syntax root. The program stores exactly nodes
`0..P+9` and their independently inferred types; the syntax appends exactly the
last two nodes.

Each `Binder(0)` denotes the complete normalized action. Its `Reindex`
interpretation is cross-checked against `TotalSub(A)` or the complete square
`(R_(n-1),S_(n-1),theta_n,Paste_(n-1))`; otherwise the entire subject aborts.
This prevents the component prefix from becoming unauthenticated decoration.

The checked census is:

| Constructor family | Prefix `P` | Value nodes | Clause nodes | Full nodes | Root |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Formation, Comparison | `2n-1` | `2n+9` | 1 | `2n+11` | `2n+10` |
| Abstraction, Aggregation, DemandCompiler | `2n` | `2n+10` | 1 | `2n+12` | `2n+11` |
| Transport | `5n+2` | `5n+12` | 1 | `5n+14` | `5n+13` |
| DischargeTransformer | `5n+6+h` | `5n+16+h` | 1 | `5n+18+h` | `5n+17+h` |

Every expression is evaluated with checked `u64`; `n>0`; each stored binder,
component, primitive, contract, value-node, clause, and root ordinal converts
exactly to its stated wire width before reservation. The `n=1` value-node
counts are exactly QG2's `[11,12,12,17,11,12,21+h]`; the standalone full-node
counts add the one clause and one conjunction. Every subtractive expression is
branch-guarded: `n-1`, `n-2`, and `2n-3` are evaluated only in their stated
nonempty or `n>1` branch, and the `n=0` option law returns `None` before this
arithmetic begins.

#### 10.17.5 Exact source and primitive-occurrence locators

The canonical source-occurrence domain scans `program.value_nodes` once, as in
the QG2 item-3 correspondence rule; it does not rescan the byte-identical
syntax prefix as a second semantic occurrence domain. The exact local program
value-node locators, in emission order, are:

```text
Formation, Comparison:
  P:E, P+5:E

Abstraction, Aggregation, DemandCompiler:
  P-1:the constructor's total-lift target,
  P:E, P+5:E

Transport:
  5n-1:TransportCompositeThetaSource,
  P:E, P+5:E

DischargeTransformer:
  5n-1:DischargeTransformerCompositeThetaSource,
  5n+1:G, 5n+2:S, 5n+3:a, 5n+4:L,
  5n+5+i:contract(i) for 0<=i<h,
  P:E, P+5:E.
```

Nested source-path ordinal is zero in every listed node. Hence the canonical
program-path counts are respectively `2`, `3`, `3`, and `7+h`, independent of
action length. The syntax prefix contains a second byte-identical copy of each
path-plan field, so the encoded subject has twice the corresponding number of
physical path-plan values and exactly `2P` physical action-projection paths;
the equality cross-check prevents those copies from becoming a second route
or owner domain. The compiler generates all routes from the subsection-10.9.4
role table and checks their complete paths/results; it does not copy the QG2
route vector. The internal primitive-epsilon and paste evaluation emits no
source path, admission atom, or evidence occurrence. The standalone action
subject therefore adds zero admission values.

The semantic binder declaration and the physical primitive occurrence are
distinct coordinates. Freeze the compiler-only locus

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveOccurrencePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  CanonicalPrimitive=0(primitive_ordinal:u32)

QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1 =
  (binder:QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1,
   primitive:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveOccurrencePlanCandidateV1).
```

For primitive `i`, `binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder` and the exact
projection block is

```text
SubstitutionAction: [i,i+1)
ContextSquareAction: [3i,3i+3)
```

in the program value block. The first block contains exactly
`SubstitutionPrimitive(i)`; the second contains exactly
`SquarePrimitiveField(i,Rho)`, then `Sigma`, then `ThetaPrime`. The byte-
identical syntax prefix is cross-checked but is not a second locus.

The representative-to-quotient factorization is an explicit compiler check,
not prose identification. For a validated R2c action image `E_A`, let
`ArgEntry(E_A,i)` be position `i` of its unique final tag-7
`CheckedActionArgumentRootV1`, and let
`RepresentativeInstantiatedActionSubject(E_A)` denote the exact final
canonical instantiated naturality subject required by A3. Both are R2e
compiler projections, not fields copied into the quotient. Let
`ResolveRepresentativeFormalBinder(E_A,subject_path)` be the R2e-owned typed
resolver into `E_A`'s final instantiated-action subject. It returns a subject,
compiler-derived subject role/kind, program, binder declaration, and semantic
primitive projection fiber; it does not return or expose the representative's
numerical path coordinates to the quotient. Define

```text
QuotientPrimitiveBlock(A,i) =
  [i,i+1)    if A is SubstitutionAction
  [3i,3i+3)  if A is ContextSquareAction

CheckRepresentativePrimitiveFiber(E_A,A,i):
  require ArgEntry(E_A,i).action_argument_ordinal = checked_u32(i)
  require ProjectCheckedPrimitive(ArgEntry(E_A,i).checked_primitive)
            = A.primitives[i]
  require ArgEntry(E_A,i).formal_id = FormalBinder(sp,0)

  r = ResolveRepresentativeFormalBinder(E_A,sp)
  require r.subject = RepresentativeInstantiatedActionSubject(E_A)
  require r.subject_role = CanonicalNaturality
  require r.subject_kind = Naturality
  require r.binder = r.program.binders[0]
  require r.binder =
    FormalAction(a_c,ArbitraryNormalized,SourceCandidate)
  require r.semantic_primitive_ordinal = checked_u32(i)
  require TranslateRepresentativePrimitiveFiber(r) =
    QuotientPrimitiveBlock(A,i)

EraseRepresentativeFormalActionId(E_A,A,i) =
  QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1(
    binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder,
    primitive=CanonicalPrimitive(checked_u32(i))).
```

`ProjectCheckedPrimitive` is exactly the section-10.2.2 representative-free
projection: `QVerifiedSubstitution` for a substitution primitive and the
ordered three-field quotient square primitive for a square. It carries no
checked wrapper, trace, or evidence into the result.
`TranslateRepresentativePrimitiveFiber` independently reads the resolved R2c
formal-action projection roles and accepts exactly one substitution-primitive
projection or the ordered `rho/sigma/theta_prime` triple for one square. It
then returns their positions in the fresh program prefix; no stored source
ordinal or claimed target block selects the result.

The resolver accepts only the program copy of the canonical total-action
naturality entry. A path into QG2 primary slot 7, a preservation subject,
another subject/slot, the syntax copy, another binder, or a component outside
the selected primitive fiber is invalid. `FormalGlobal`, any nonzero binder
ordinal, and an absent/extra/reordered root-7 entry are likewise invalid.

The fiber theorem is exact:

```text
for every 0<=i<n, CheckRepresentativePrimitiveFiber(E_A,A,i) succeeds

i != j  =>
  QuotientPrimitiveBlock(A,i) intersect
  QuotientPrimitiveBlock(A,j) = empty

concat_i QuotientPrimitiveBlock(A,i) =
  [0,n)   for SubstitutionAction
  [0,3n)  for ContextSquareAction.
```

Thus the fibers are total over the primitive-projection prefix, pairwise
disjoint, and one-to-one with root-6 action arguments even when two checked
primitive values have equal bytes. The syntax-prefix copy creates neither a
second fiber nor a second quotient locus. R2e must validate every displayed
equation against the supplied representative before erasing `sp`; failure
aborts before any action subject, formal owner, or support row is published.

Root-6 action argument `i` uses this one `CanonicalPrimitive(i)` locus. Thus
all entries share one semantic binder declaration while distinct primitive
ordinals remain distinct formal occurrences; a square still has one formal
occurrence rather than three field occurrences. This is the fresh quotient
factorization of the representative `FormalActionIdV1`, now defined by
`EraseRepresentativeFormalActionId`, not a reuse of
`SubjectPathV1` and not an arbitrary choice of the square's `rho` node. The
final shared formal-ID wire will wrap this now-closed locus in the next shared-
sum cut. `FormalGlobal` remains uninhabited, and concrete globals below every
primitive continue to resolve through retained history.

#### 10.17.6 Schema image, transaction, and next cut

Every component result is in the already closed quotient-primary symbolic
family:

```text
primitive fields, vertical/substitution composites, lifts, LC, RC
  -> NormalizedSubstitution
canonical pasted epsilon
  -> SubstitutionEqualitySubject
reindexed G/S/a/L/contract
  -> NormalizedGrammar/NormalizedScheme/NormalizedSection/
     NormalizedLiveDemand/NormalizedContract
final congruence
  -> TransportCongruenceEndpoint or DischargeCongruenceEndpoint.
```

The endpoint suffix uses only `EnvelopeObjectDescriptor`, `NormalizedAction`,
the constructor's output/implementation type, and `PropositionSort`. Formal-
action projections are bound component declarations and emit no support
incidence. Their fresh source dereferences have the same nominal schema image
already audited for QG2 slot 7. Together with the root-6 spine, the static
S1c1 pass-A result is therefore

```text
U_S1c1 \ U_nf = {T_sub,T_sq}
```

project-wide, or the matching singleton for one selected action kind. This
proves the conditional nineteen-node target through the completed subject and
edge-28 prefix. It does not yet prove it through `ActionIntroduced=27`, the
remaining primitive descendants, action lineage, or the final shared graph;
therefore `M_target` remains a generated target and no final enum/tag is frozen
by this subsection.

Construction aborts atomically on an empty action entering this compiler,
wrong action kind, nonsequential substitution/square, malformed square,
alternate association/paste order, wrong component/ref/order/result type,
non-earlier derived reference, source-path drift, copied source bundle or QG2
path occurrence, endpoint/component mismatch, wrong clause, formal-locus/block
mismatch, missing/extra/reordered node, arithmetic/conversion/allocation
failure, or partial publication. A validated representative mismatch is an
invariant abort; no subject or support fragment survives it.

The result is a function only of the retained base quotient context and total
flattened normalized action. Identity postfixes, caller parentheses, stepwise
payloads/endpoints, raw paste syntax, replay, receipts, resources, owner/raw
ordinals, final action support, `QFootprints` as a selector, R3, semantic SR2,
`nu`, `GCap`, `gamma`, and semantic-novelty data cannot select any byte. Hence

```text
CompileInstantiatedActionSubject(B,A ++ []) =
  CompileInstantiatedActionSubject(B,A)
```

and `n=0` remains exactly `None` with no component, node, path, occurrence, or
root-6 entry.

This closes QG3-S1c1: the generalized component ABI, total substitution and
square compilers, completed one-program `Some` subject, exact program/syntax
bytes and counts, source occurrence census, and primitive formal-occurrence
loci are fixed. The next lawful cut is QG3-S1c2's combined subject/action
pass-A traversal. It must close every post-prefix action incidence, especially
`ActionIntroduced=27`, prove the complete `U_action`, generate `M_action` and
the final shared manifest, and only then freeze the shared symbolic and formal
sums plus the candidate embedding.

### 10.18 QG3-S1c2 combined action pass-A and shared manifest -- CLOSED

This cut closes the last nominal-schema uncertainty before the shared support
wire. The source audit exposes one genuine omission rather than a hidden
upstream definition: A3 requires a separate action-introduced lineage, while
R2c reserves edges `27..28` for an R2d action-root dispatcher, but neither
record fixes edge 27's event domain, multiplicity, order, referent, judgment,
or owner rule. Moreover subsection 10.16 fixes the dependency direction

```text
action pass-A stream -> shared manifest -> shared support -> group-4 lineage.
```

Consequently a stored action-lineage vector cannot be traversed to generate
edge 27. That would reverse the dependency and let a quotient output select
its own support. This subsection makes the missing **fresh R2d
canonicalization choice**: action-introduction is a paired provenance overlay
on the already frozen root-6 descendant walk. The later lineage is a
projection of the same primary compiler events; it is never an input here.

#### 10.18.1 Combined seven-root stream and standalone-subject loci

For a retained base quotient context `B` and a complete nonempty flattened
action `A`, let the action schema frame use:

1. the action-rebuilt semantic values at roots `CanonicalPayload` through
   `Obligations`, computed once from `(B,A)` by the frozen A3
   `CanonicalActionResult` equations and the receipt-free QG1/QG2 compilers;
2. the byte-identical base `QPrimarySubjects` program stream at root
   `PrimarySubjects`, followed by the one standalone program of
   `CompileInstantiatedActionSubject(B,A)`; and
3. the complete same-tag `A` at root `ActionArguments`.

This is a compiler frame, not an eighth support root or an encoded copy of the
eight-group value. The direct stream order is exactly

```text
root 0, root 1, root 2, root 3, root 4,
root-5 base primary programs,
root-5 instantiated-action program,
root-6 primitives.
```

The first six segments reuse the subsection-10.13 walker and local incidence
order. The base primary programs remain byte-identical to `B.QPrimarySubjects`;
the action program is a suffix only in this compiler stream and is still
stored separately in group 8. For an identity action the suffix and every
root-6 incidence are absent, and the complete stream is the frozen candidate
stream. Appending identity to a nonempty action changes no segment.

The standalone program has a fresh node/path ABI and therefore cannot pretend
to inhabit the candidate four-way locus. Before the shared locus sum freezes,
use exactly these compiler-only cases:

```text
QuotientSupportInstantiatedActionLocusPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ActionDereferenced=0(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| ActionProject=1(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| ActionProjectGrammar=2(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>).
```

`ActionDereferenced(j,r)` selects the unique fresh source-path occurrence in
program value node `j`, resolves it against `BaseSubjectEnvironment(B)`, and
walks `r` in the result. A listed source-path node in subsection 10.17.5 has
exactly one such occurrence; zero or two abort. A `CorePayloadProjection`
literal emits `TheoremEndpoint=20` at `r=[]` and then walks descendants. A
source path nested in a formal component is walked but its enclosing
`FormalActionArgumentProjection` emits no incidence.

`ActionProject(j,r)` requires node `j` to be `Project` with a strict-earlier
source, reruns its exact one-of-nine semantic projection, and walks `r` in the
result. `ActionProjectGrammar` is admitted only for a
`DemandCompilerOutput` project, with the same empty grammar or exact operation-
record companion suffix as subsection 10.13.5. Binders, component references,
normalization nodes, reindex nodes, the clause, and the conjunction validate
their types and operands but add no direct row. The byte-identical syntax
prefix is checked once and never rescanned. These three loci must inject into
the next cut's shared locus sum without changing any candidate locus byte or
creating another coverage root.

The stream must already be one typed pass-A domain even though S1c3 has not yet
frozen the encoded shared path sum. Use this erased compiler-only coproduct:

```text
QuotientSupportActionPassALocusPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  FrozenLocus=0(QuotientSupportLocusPlanCandidateV1)
| InstantiatedAction=1(
    QuotientSupportInstantiatedActionLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   locus:QuotientSupportActionPassALocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionPassAPathBindingsPlanCandidateV1 =
  (dependency:QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1,
   grammar_companion:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1>,
   operation_record_companion:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1>).
```

Roots `0..4`, the root-5 base prefix, and root 6 inject their frozen locus with
`FrozenLocus`; only the standalone root-5 suffix uses `InstantiatedAction`.
`ActionProjectGrammar` remains a companion-only locus. Define

```text
TypeAtAction(root,FrozenLocus(l)) = TypeAt(root,l)

TypeAtAction(PrimarySubjects,InstantiatedAction(l)) =
  the unique result of the subsection-10.18.1 action-locus evaluator.
```

Every other root/locus combination aborts. These coproduct tags type the
nominal census only; they are not the final shared locus/path tags.

The action walk also extends subsection 10.13.3's erased binder and term-
provenance products. Endpoint binder identity and term-node origin are
different coordinates and must not be conflated. Freeze the compiler-only
types

```text
QuotientSupportActionBoundaryRolePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SubstitutionDelta=0
| SquareDelta=1
| SquareGamma=2

QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1 =
  (primitive:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1,
   boundary_role:QuotientSupportActionBoundaryRolePlanCandidateV1,
   field_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSupportActionPrimitiveSubstitutionRolePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  SubstitutionPrimitive=0
| SquareRho=1
| SquareSigma=2
| SquareThetaPrime=3

QuotientSupportActionFormalTermRootPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  BoundaryFieldType=0(
    QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1)
| PrimitiveImage=1(
    primitive:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1,
    substitution_role:
      QuotientSupportActionPrimitiveSubstitutionRolePlanCandidateV1,
    image_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSupportActionFormalTermNodeLocusPlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportActionFormalTermRootPlanCandidateV1,
   node_path:vector<GenerativeStructuralOccurrencePathStepV1>)

QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Boundary=0(QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1)
| TermBody=1(
    parent:QuotientSupportActionFormalTermNodeLocusPlanCandidateV1,
    body_edge:HistoricalBodyEdgeV1)

QuotientSupportActionBinderIdentityPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Inherited=0(QuotientSupportBinderPlanCandidateV1)
| Formal=1(QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionTermNodeOriginPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  InheritedOccurrence=0(OccurrenceId14V1)
| FormalPrimitiveNode=1(
    QuotientSupportActionFormalTermNodeLocusPlanCandidateV1)
| None=2

QuotientSupportActionTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1 =
  (term:Term,
   origin_at_node:
     total map from every valid JG2b2b0 term path of term
       to QuotientSupportActionTermNodeOriginPlanCandidateV1).

PrimitiveOfTermRoot(BoundaryFieldType(b)) = b.primitive
PrimitiveOfTermRoot(PrimitiveImage(p,_,_)) = p

OwnerPrimitive(Boundary(b)) = b.primitive
OwnerPrimitive(TermBody(parent=l,_)) = PrimitiveOfTermRoot(l.root).
```

The map has exactly the constructor tree of `term`. Every ordinal and path
conversion is checked. `SubstitutionDelta` and `SubstitutionPrimitive` are
admitted only for a substitution action. The square cases are admitted only
for a square action: `SquareDelta` names
`rho_i.delta=theta_(i+1).delta`, `SquareGamma` names
`sigma_i.delta=theta_(i+1).gamma`, and the three image roles select the exact
`rho`, `sigma`, or `theta_prime` field. A formal term-node path is interpreted
in its selected source term, never in a transported copy. Equal term or image
bytes and two binding constructors in one term therefore remain distinct.

The frozen candidate product injects exactly:

```text
EmbedCandidateTermProvenance(P).term = P.term

EmbedCandidateTermProvenance(P).origin_at_node[p] =
  InheritedOccurrence(q)
    if P.source_occurrence_at_node[p]=Some(q)
  None
    if P.source_occurrence_at_node[p]=None.
```

For each newly introduced boundary-field type or primitive image term root
`R`, `FormalTermProvenance(R,t)` assigns `FormalPrimitiveNode(R,p)` to every
valid node path `p` of `t`. A `Pi`, `Sigma`, or `Lambda` body extends its frame
by the exact rule

```text
BodyBinder(InheritedOccurrence(q),e) =
  Inherited(Historical(q,e))

BodyBinder(FormalPrimitiveNode(l),e) =
  Formal(TermBody(parent=l,body_edge=e))

BodyBinder(None,e) = abort,
```

where `(node kind,e)` must be exactly `(Pi,PiBody)`, `(Sigma,SigmaBody)`, or
`(Lambda,LambdaBody)`, and the inherited case reauthenticates `q`. The identity
is appended with the complete parameter or parameter-type term. A `Var` uses
the uniquely selected decorated frame. A concrete `Global` ignores binder
provenance and resolves only through retained history.

Boundary frames are fixed by semantic boundary position, not by the primitive
record currently copying their bytes. Let `EmbedFrame` inject every candidate
binder identity with `Inherited`. Let `FormalFrame(p,r,C)` be the oldest-first
frame whose field `j` has identity
`Formal(Boundary(p,r,checked_u32(j)))`, complete type `C[j]`, and the parallel
`BoundaryFieldType` term provenance above. For a substitution action

```text
theta_i : Gamma_(i+1) -> Gamma_i

F_Gamma_0 = the exact EmbedFrame of B's admitted action-base boundary
F_Gamma_(i+1) =
  FormalFrame(p_i,SubstitutionDelta,Gamma_(i+1))

F_(theta_i.gamma) = F_Gamma_i
F_(theta_i.delta) = F_Gamma_(i+1),
```

where `p_i` is the exact `CanonicalPrimitive(i)` locus. For a square action,
write `theta_i:Delta_i -> Gamma_i` and
`Q_i=(rho_i,sigma_i,theta_(i+1))`. Then

```text
F_Delta_0,F_Gamma_0 =
  the exact two EmbedFrame values from B's admitted square base

F_Delta_(i+1) = FormalFrame(p_i,SquareDelta,Delta_(i+1))
F_Gamma_(i+1) = FormalFrame(p_i,SquareGamma,Gamma_(i+1))

F_(rho_i.gamma)       = F_Delta_i
F_(rho_i.delta)       = F_Delta_(i+1)
F_(sigma_i.gamma)     = F_Gamma_i
F_(sigma_i.delta)     = F_Gamma_(i+1)
F_(theta_(i+1).delta) = F_Delta_(i+1)
F_(theta_(i+1).gamma) = F_Gamma_(i+1).
```

The complete-context equalities are rechecked together with these frame
equalities. Thus base boundaries are inherited, boundary `i+1` is introduced
by primitive `i`, and every adjacent copy retains one byte-identical frame.
The images of `theta_i`, and the `rho_i`, `sigma_i`, and `theta_(i+1)` images
in square `i`, are checked in their indicated freshly introduced delta frames,
so their free action-local `Var`s resolve to formal identities owned by `p_i`.

Reindexing, composition, normalization, and substitution transport these
decorations node-for-node and inject the complete decorated product of every
substituted image, exactly as subsection 10.13.3. Compiler-created nonbinding
nodes use `None`; a compiler-created binding constructor is forbidden.
Canonical contract values may merge only when their complete decorations
agree. Selecting a first/least primitive or reconstructing an origin from
value equality is invalid.

The action-only nominal directives are now finite and typed:

```text
QuotientSupportActionReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  FrozenRecipe=0(QuotientSupportReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1)
| FormalActionBinderFromValue=1(
    path:Dependency,
    binding_locus:QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1)
| FormalPrimitiveArgument=2(
    path:Dependency,
    primitive:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1 ::=
  FrozenRecipe=0(QuotientSupportOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1)
| FormalPrimitiveOwner=1(
    path:Dependency,
    primitive:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionPassANominalDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   bare_locus:QuotientSupportActionPassALocusPlanCandidateV1,
   path_bindings:QuotientSupportActionPassAPathBindingsPlanCandidateV1,
   nominal_result_schema,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1,
   nominal_expected_judgment,
   referent_recipe:QuotientSupportActionReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1,
   owner_resolution_recipe:
     QuotientSupportActionOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1).
```

`FormalActionBinderFromValue` requires the dependency to be `Term::Var` and its
selected decorated binder to equal `Formal(binding_locus)`. Its owner is
exactly `OwnerPrimitive(binding_locus)`; it emits the distinct nominal formal-
binder referent, not a primitive-wide binder alias. `FormalPrimitiveArgument` is
admitted only for edge 28 at exact `P_sub(i)` or `P_sq(i)` and emits the whole-
primitive formal-action referent. `FormalPrimitiveOwner` is admitted only for
that edge-28 argument or a term dependency whose selected terminal is a formal
`Var` with `OwnerPrimitive(selected binder)=primitive`; it emits the single
owner `CanonicalPrimitive(i)`. Boundary and term-
body loci distinguish binder referents but never mint owner subloci: a square
still has one formal owner, not three.

Consequently two distinct formal binder referents owned by the same primitive
and used under the same expected judgment may later share one formal support
key/class. Their paths, direct dependencies, and coverage occurrences remain
distinct; this is class quotienting, not binder identification.

All other directives inject the frozen recipe and resolve against the action
path bindings. In particular, edge 4, 5, or 21 over a formal `Var` retains its
frozen `RecordSlot` referent but uses `FormalPrimitiveOwner`; a `Global` uses
the frozen kernel-global history directive; an inherited `Var` uses the frozen
binder-history directive; and every other root term uses the frozen structural
directive. The action nominal row and these directives are erased pass-A state.
S1c3 must inject both locus arms, the inherited and formal binder loci, and all
three recipe cases into the encoded shared algebras. Thus
action-rebuilt roots, standalone project results, and primitive descendants
already have total binder/global resolution without freezing those final tags
or consulting an R2c subject path, representative ordinal, or value equality.

#### 10.18.2 Native primitive descendants and the edge-27 overlay

For a normalized term `t`, let `TR(t)` be the number of native incidences
emitted by the frozen term walker:

```text
TR(Var) = TR(Global) = 1
TR(Sort) = TR(UnitType) = TR(Unit) = 0

TR(Pi(x,y)) = TR(Sigma(x,y)) = TR(Lambda(x,y))
            = TR(Apply(x,y)) = 2 + TR(x) + TR(y)

TR(Pair(x,y,z)) = 3 + TR(x) + TR(y) + TR(z)
TR(First(x)) = TR(Second(x)) = 1 + TR(x).
```

For `s:NormalizedSubstitutionV1`, with `m=s.images.len`, define

```text
D_sub(s) = checked_add(
  checked_mul(2,m),
  sum_(0<=k<m) TR(s.images[k])).
```

The first `m` rows are `SubstitutionImage=5`; the section visit then emits the
`m` `SectionExpectedType=4` rows; image terms follow in vector order and exact
JG2b2b0 child order. Endpoint contexts are validated but not walked. The only
native descendant edge image is `{1,2,4,5,21}`, and every dependency locus has
nominal schema `Term`.

For an already completed native nominal row `r` below primitive `i`, define

```text
IntroCompanion(i,r) = r with edge=ActionIntroduced=27
```

and require every other field to be byte-identical:

```text
root, bare_locus, every path binding, nominal_result_schema,
nominal_expected_judgment, referent_recipe, owner_resolution_recipe.
```

This is a compiler-side pair, not a backreference stored by dependency
ordinal. At every visited descendant node, let `V` be its complete frozen
native local incidence vector. Emit `V`, then
`[IntroCompanion(i,r) | r in V]` in the same order, then recurse through the
children. Empty `V` emits no companion. Do not companion an edge-27 row, the
leading edge-28 row, a closure row, a wrapper, an endpoint context, a
standalone-subject row, or any root `0..5` row.

The edge-indexed admissibility image is exact:

| Paired native edge | Edge-27 referent | Edge-27 owner | Judgment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `LocalBinderUse=1` | the exact selected formal boundary/body binder | formal owner for `CanonicalPrimitive(i)` | byte-identical native `HasType` |
| `GlobalUse=2` | the exact retained concrete global | current or older owner | byte-identical native `HasType` |
| `SectionExpectedType=4` | the same record slot | byte-identical native owner: formal for a formal `Var`, current/older for a `Global`, structural otherwise | byte-identical native `HasType` |
| `SubstitutionImage=5` | the same record slot | the same three-way native-owner dispatch | byte-identical native `HasType` |
| `TermChild=21(e)` | the same record slot | byte-identical owner of the selected child: formal for a formal `Var`, current/older for a `Global`, structural otherwise | byte-identical native child judgment |

Only edge 28 has a whole-primitive `FormalAction` referent. Edge 27 does not
force a formal owner and never carries an operation-slot or occurrence
referent. It is provenance-only: after native resolution,

```text
Resolve(IntroCompanion(i,r)).referent = Resolve(r).referent
Resolve(IntroCompanion(i,r)).owner    = Resolve(r).owner
Key(IntroCompanion(i,r))              = Key(r).
```

The companion is resolved from its paired native row, not by passing edge 27
through S1b3's candidate-only edge guards. It adds no new support class,
distinct closure seed or closure occurrence, or history-footprint identity. If
the copied owner is current, it repeats the already earlier native seed and is
non-forcing. It does retain a distinct direct dependency, coverage occurrence,
and later lineage event. The native row precedes it, so first-occurrence and
closure-forcing order are unchanged.

For `A` with `n` primitives, define

```text
D_A =
  sum_(0<=i<n) D_sub(theta_i)
    for SubstitutionAction

D_A =
  sum_(0<=i<n)
    (D_sub(rho_i) + D_sub(sigma_i) + D_sub(theta_prime_i))
    for ContextSquareAction.
```

All additions, multiplications, lengths, and ordinal conversions are checked
before stream reservation. The exact root-6 counts are

```text
native descendant rows       = D_A
ActionIntroduced=27 rows     = D_A
ActionArgument=28 rows       = n
total root-6 direct rows     = n + 2*D_A.
```

Root-6 order is, for each primitive: its one leading edge 28, followed by the
paired descendant walk. A square has one edge 28 for the complete record and
then walks `rho`, `sigma`, and `theta_prime` in that order. Equal primitive,
image, term, path, referent, judgment, or key bytes never deduplicate rows.

The group-4 action-introduced lineage compiler in the later support cut must
rerun this same primary paired-event census and project the edge-27 events in
order. It may not inspect published group-6 dependencies, dependency/class
ordinals, coverage, support keys, or footprints. `CurrentOutput` is likewise
recomputed from reindexed output-lineage slots and the action-origin current
occurrences actually reached there; projecting every root-6 edge-27 owner
would incorrectly admit unused action material.

#### 10.18.3 Complete action universe and nineteen-node shared manifest

Let `ActionDirectImage` range over the complete typed vectors of
`QuotientSupportActionPassANominalDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1` above for
every valid nonempty action and all seven constructors. Define `U_action` and
`M_action` by the same path-census, `TypeAtAction`, nominal-structural-target,
`Canon`, and `sort_unique` equations as subsections 10.12.3 and 10.13.8.
Repetitions are preserved in the stream and deduplicated only in the manifest's
nominal-schema locator set.

The finite partition is now unconditional:

1. action-rebuilt roots `0..4` have the same nominal record schemas and reuse
   the total S1b2 walker, so their path and structural-target image is contained
   in `U_nf`;
2. the base primary root-5 prefix is the frozen candidate prefix;
3. subsection 10.17 and the exact action-subject loci above add only source and
   project schemas already in `U_nf`;
4. every native root-6 descendant and every edge-27 companion is at `Term`;
   and
5. edge 28 alone adds `T_sub` or `T_sq` project-wide.

Together with S1b2's immutable root-0--5 image, the combined project-wide
direct edge image is exactly `{1,...,21,27,28}`. Edge `0` and presentation-only
edges `22..23` have no direct image; edges `24..26` remain closure-only. A
selected action may have `D_A=0` and hence no edge 27, but the static compiler
image contains the case through any valid nonempty substitution image.

Conversely the root-0--5 action compiler image ranges over all seven
constructor schema families and contains the candidate schema image, while
valid nonempty substitution and square actions witness both primitive rows.
Therefore

```text
U_action = U_nf union {T_sub,T_sq}
U_action \ U_nf = {T_sub,T_sq}.

M_shared = sort_unique(M_nf union M_action)
         = M_action
         = sort_unique([Canon(T) | T in U_nf union {T_sub,T_sq}]).
```

The locators from subsection 10.16.4 are now proved rather than conditional.
The generated shared manifest has exactly nineteen nodes:

| Tag | Shared payload-free variant | Exact nominal schema |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| `0` | `U32` | `u32` |
| `1` | `NormalizedSubstitution` | `NormalizedSubstitutionV1` |
| `2` | `NormalizedGrammar` | `NormalizedGrammarValueV1` |
| `3` | `Term` | `Term` |
| `4` | `NormalizedGrammarOperation` | `NormalizedGrammarOperationV1` |
| `5` | `QuotientObligationPredicate` | `QuotientObligationPredicateCandidateV1` |
| `6` | `QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligation` | `QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligationCandidateV1` |
| `7` | `Occurrence` | `OccurrenceId14V1` |
| `8` | `OccurrenceVector` | `vector<OccurrenceId14V1>` |
| `9` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptor` | `QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptorCandidateV1` |
