| `10` | `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremise` | `QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremiseCandidateV1` |
| `11` | `QuotientNormalizedAction` | `QuotientNormalizedActionCandidateV1` |
| `12` | `QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitive` | `QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitiveCandidateV1` |
| `13` | `QuotientBindingRequirement` | `QuotientBindingRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `14` | `QuotientCheckedContext` | `QuotientCheckedContextCandidateV1` |
| `15` | `QuotientCarrierFormationRequirement` | `QuotientCarrierFormationRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `16` | `QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirement` | `QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirementCandidateV1` |
| `17` | `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparison` | `QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparisonCandidateV1` |
| `18` | `QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreement` | `QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreementCandidateV1` |

Accordingly freeze the compiler-plan wire

```text
QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanSharedV1 ::=
  U32=0
| NormalizedSubstitution=1
| NormalizedGrammar=2
| Term=3
| NormalizedGrammarOperation=4
| QuotientObligationPredicate=5
| QuotientSubjectWellFormedObligation=6
| Occurrence=7
| OccurrenceVector=8
| QuotientEnvelopeObjectDescriptor=9
| QuotientCandidateAdmissionPremise=10
| QuotientNormalizedAction=11
| QuotientNormalizedContextSquarePrimitive=12
| QuotientBindingRequirement=13
| QuotientCheckedContext=14
| QuotientCarrierFormationRequirement=15
| QuotientCarrierCompatibilityRequirement=16
| QuotientNormalizedTelescopeComparison=17
| QuotientNormalizedOutputAgreement=18.
```

`SchemaOf` is the table's right column. These are symbolic compiler-plan tags,
not `TypeIdV1` values. The candidate enum remains byte-for-byte immutable. Its
unique symbolic embedding is

```text
EmbedCandidateSymbolic(0)      = 0
EmbedCandidateSymbolic(t)      = t+1  for 1<=t<=10
EmbedCandidateSymbolic(t)      = t+2  for 11<=t<=16.
```

It is total, injective, strictly order-preserving, and schema-preserving:

```text
SchemaOfShared(EmbedCandidateSymbolic(t)) = SchemaOfCandidate(t).
```

Action pass B may now lower every nominal schema through the shared table.
The next cut must embed the candidate and action locus/path, expected-judgment,
binder, referent, owner, key, class, coverage, and projection algebras into
their shared wires; it may not regenerate or renumber this symbolic enum.

#### 10.18.4 Transaction, independence, and next cut

Construction aborts atomically on an action kind/base mismatch; an empty action
entering the nonempty branch; root, subject-suffix, program/syntax-copy, node,
source occurrence, square-field, primitive, image, or child-order drift; an
endpoint descent; missing, extra, duplicated, relabeled, or reordered native
row or edge-27 companion; a companion field other than `edge` changing; a
non-Term descendant locus; a companion owner differing from its paired native
owner; a formal owner for a concrete `Global` or any non-`Var` record-slot row;
a nonformal action-local `Var`; a shared boundary carrying two identities;
decoration loss, ambiguity, or value-equality selection; contract
deduplication with unequal decorations;
checked-arithmetic failure; a manifest/locator/tag/embedding mismatch; or
partial publication.

It also aborts if a stored lineage, support dependency, class, coverage row,
footprint, representative, R2c path/ordinal, QG2 path occurrence, syntax copy,
raw/source/owner ordinal, replay, receipt, construction transcript, R3 account,
semantic SR2 result, `nu`, `GCap`, `gamma`, or semantic-novelty datum selects
the stream, companion, manifest, or embedding.

Holding `(B,A)` fixed while changing only caller association, identity
postfixes, intermediate action images, representative traces, or any forbidden
material above leaves the complete action stream, counts, manifest, and
embedding unchanged. Candidate `M_nf`, its seventeen tags, S1b3, the
preservation kernel, S1c0, S1c1, all QG2 bytes, the exact `5*PR_c` census, and
the two admission values are unchanged.

This closes QG3-S1c2: the seven-root action stream, standalone-subject virtual
loci, action provenance product, complete primitive-descendant walk, fresh
paired edge-27 canonicalization, exact counts, action schema universe,
nineteen-node shared symbolic enum, and symbolic candidate embedding are now
fixed. The next lawful cut is QG3-S1c3's shared support algebra: freeze the
shared path/judgment/binder/referent/owner/key/projection wires and their
candidate/action embeddings, then instantiate the generic preservation
subjects against that concrete shared projection sort.

### 10.19 QG3-S1c3 shared support algebra and concrete subject typing -- CLOSED

This cut consumes, without regenerating, subsection 10.18.3's nineteen-node
shared symbolic manifest. It closes the smallest shared candidate/action
support algebra, the total candidate embedding, the action pass-B and pure
graph/projection compiler, and the concrete shared typing and denotation of
the three preservation-subject descriptors. It deliberately closes no
preservation theorem: the subjects remain proposition syntax without a stored
inhabitant or proof value.

The semantic audit at the end of this section matters. Once the formerly
abstract result sort is instantiated by the exact full shared projection, the
renaming equality is not generically a byte identity and the nonidentity-
action equality is not a byte identity. In fact,
the reindexing equality is uninhabited for every nonempty action because its
right endpoint necessarily contains formal-action keys which cannot occur on
its candidate left endpoint. This is not a wire inconsistency: A3 and R2c
freeze typed theorem subjects but explicitly mint no theorem authority. It is
an exact future theorem/corrigendum obligation and may not be reported as a
proved preservation law.

#### 10.19.1 Flat shared locus, path, and exact action lowering

The seven root tags from subsection 10.11.2 already form the complete shared
root domain and are reused unchanged. The historical `PlanCandidateV1` suffix
on that enum does not restrict its image: action support has the same seven
roots, and the standalone action subject is a suffix under
`PrimarySubjects`, not an eighth root.

The final locus is a flat append-only extension of the candidate locus:

```text
QuotientSupportLocusPlanSharedV1 ::=
  Direct=0(
    steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| Dereferenced=1(
    descriptor_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| PrimaryProject=2(
    project_node_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| PrimaryProjectGrammar=3(
    project_node_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| ActionDereferenced=4(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| ActionProject=5(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>)
| ActionProjectGrammar=6(
    value_node:u32,
    reached_steps:vector<TypedPathStepV1>).

QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   locus:QuotientSupportLocusPlanSharedV1,
   result_schema:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanSharedV1).
```

`ActionProjectGrammar` remains companion-only. The three action cases are
admitted only below the unique standalone root-5 program fixed in subsection
10.18.1. Roots 0--4, the byte-identical base root-5 prefix, and root 6 retain
the first four cases. Therefore flattening the erased pass-A coproduct does not
create a second path domain:

```text
EmbedCandidateLocus(Direct(s))
  = Direct(s)
EmbedCandidateLocus(Dereferenced(d,r))
  = Dereferenced(d,r)
EmbedCandidateLocus(PrimaryProject(p,r))
  = PrimaryProject(p,r)
EmbedCandidateLocus(PrimaryProjectGrammar(p,r))
  = PrimaryProjectGrammar(p,r)

EmbedActionLocus(FrozenLocus(l))
  = EmbedCandidateLocus(l)
EmbedActionLocus(
  InstantiatedAction(ActionDereferenced(j,r)))
  = ActionDereferenced(j,r)
EmbedActionLocus(
  InstantiatedAction(ActionProject(j,r)))
  = ActionProject(j,r)
EmbedActionLocus(
  InstantiatedAction(ActionProjectGrammar(j,r)))
  = ActionProjectGrammar(j,r).

EmbedCandidatePath(p) =
  (root=p.root,
   locus=EmbedCandidateLocus(p.locus),
   result_schema=EmbedCandidateSymbolic(p.result_schema)).
```

The outer candidate locus tags and payloads are byte-identical under this
embedding. The complete path is a fresh shared value because its symbolic
result type is the shared nineteen-way enum.

Action pass B is independent of every claimed tag. For a pass-A bare path
`p=(root,locus)` define:

```text
LowerActionPath(p):
  T = TypeAtAction(p.root,p.locus)
  a = Canon(T)
  require a in M_shared
  return
    (root=p.root,
     locus=EmbedActionLocus(p.locus),
     result_schema=Node_shared(a)).

QuotientSupportActionLoweredPathBindingsPlanSharedV1 =
  (dependency:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
   grammar_companion:Option<QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1>,
   operation_record_companion:
     Option<QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1>).

LowerActionBindings(B) =
  (dependency=LowerActionPath(B.dependency),
   grammar_companion=map(LowerActionPath,B.grammar_companion),
   operation_record_companion=
     map(LowerActionPath,B.operation_record_companion)).
```

Every present role is lowered independently and retains its physical
occurrence. Equal descriptors, equal reached values, repeated paths, and the
edge-27 companion's copied paths are never interned or deduplicated. Admission
requires the dependency binding's root and locus to equal the row's root and
lowered bare locus, and its result schema to equal the independently lowered
nominal row schema. A caller result tag cannot select `TypeAtAction`, `Canon`,
or traversal.

#### 10.19.2 Contiguous judgment wire and shared semantic identities

The stored expected-judgment sum now has all ten contiguous A3-C1 positions:

```text
QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1 ::=
  TypeFormation=0
| HasType=1(
    normalized_type:Term)
| DefinitionallyEqualAt=2(
    normalized_type:Term)
| ContextPrefix=3(
    normalized_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    prefix_ordinal:u32)
| CarrierSlot=4(
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1,
    field_ordinal:u32)
| OperationOrdinal=5(
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
    operation_ordinal:u32)
| StructuralOccurrence=6(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1)
| NormalizationSubject=7(
    rule:NormalizationRuleV1)
| StructuralRecord=8(
    symbolic_type:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanSharedV1)
| ConstructionRule=9(
    rule_path:DefinitionRulePathV1).
```

Tag 7 is a payloadful schema position, not a reserved empty variant. It is
uninhabited in every valid quotient support graph. Candidate and action
validation admit exactly tags `0..6,8,9`; any emitted tag 7 aborts.

Candidate expected judgments embed by preserving the outer tag and complete
payload, recursively mapping the two shared payload types:

```text
EmbedCandidateExpected(OperationOrdinal(p,k))
  = OperationOrdinal(EmbedCandidatePath(p),k)
EmbedCandidateExpected(StructuralRecord(t))
  = StructuralRecord(EmbedCandidateSymbolic(t))
EmbedCandidateExpected(other)
  = the same outer case and byte-identical payload.

LowerActionExpected(
  NominalOperationOrdinal(role,k),B)
  = OperationOrdinal(B[role],k)

LowerActionExpected(
  NominalStructuralRecord(T),B)
  = StructuralRecord(Node_shared(Canon(T)))

LowerActionExpected(other,B)
  = the same no-path case and complete payload.
```

The first action equation requires the named role to be present in the already
lowered bindings and to pass `TypeAtAction` again. The second requires
`Canon(T)` in the fixed nineteen-node manifest. No action case constructs
`NormalizationSubject`.

The representative-free primitive occurrence is promoted from its erased S1c1
locator into a fresh shared wire family:

```text
QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderLocusPlanSharedV1 ::=
  CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder=0

QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveOccurrencePlanSharedV1 ::=
  CanonicalPrimitive=0(primitive_ordinal:u32)

QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanSharedV1 =
  (binder:QuotientInstantiatedActionBinderLocusPlanSharedV1,
   primitive:
     QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveOccurrencePlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1 =
  newtype(
    primitive_locus:
      QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanSharedV1).

EmbedActionPrimitiveLocus(p) =
  (binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder,
   primitive=CanonicalPrimitive(p.primitive.primitive_ordinal)).

FormalId(p) =
  QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1(
    EmbedActionPrimitiveLocus(p)).
```

The source locus must have
`binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder` and
`primitive=CanonicalPrimitive(i)` for one checked primitive ordinal `i`.
`EmbedActionPrimitiveLocus` rechecks both tags and the complete checked
ordinal; no compiler-only wrapper or provenance product is encoded.
There is no formal-global variant, action-kind copy, subject path, binder
ordinal, square-field owner, value-node coordinate, syntax-copy coordinate, or
preservation-binder coordinate.

The boundary/body locator grammar becomes a final shared nominal family rather
than an encoded copy of the compiler-only provenance product:

```text
QuotientSupportActionBoundaryRolePlanSharedV1 ::=
  SubstitutionDelta=0
| SquareDelta=1
| SquareGamma=2

QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanSharedV1 =
  (primitive:QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1,
   boundary_role:QuotientSupportActionBoundaryRolePlanSharedV1,
   field_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSupportActionPrimitiveSubstitutionRolePlanSharedV1 ::=
  SubstitutionPrimitive=0
| SquareRho=1
| SquareSigma=2
| SquareThetaPrime=3

QuotientSupportActionFormalTermRootPlanSharedV1 ::=
  BoundaryFieldType=0(
    QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanSharedV1)
| PrimitiveImage=1(
    primitive:QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1,
    substitution_role:
      QuotientSupportActionPrimitiveSubstitutionRolePlanSharedV1,
    image_ordinal:u32)

QuotientSupportActionFormalTermNodeLocusPlanSharedV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportActionFormalTermRootPlanSharedV1,
   node_path:vector<GenerativeStructuralOccurrencePathStepV1>)

QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanSharedV1 ::=
  Boundary=0(
    QuotientSupportActionBoundaryBinderLocusPlanSharedV1)
| TermBody=1(
    parent:QuotientSupportActionFormalTermNodeLocusPlanSharedV1,
    body_edge:HistoricalBodyEdgeV1).
```

`EmbedActionFormalBinderLocus` maps every compiler-only role tag identically,
wraps every primitive locus with `FormalId`, preserves every checked field or
image ordinal and node path, and recurses through `TermBody`. It is total and
injective on subsection 10.18.1's exact image.

The final semantic sums are:

```text
QuotientSupportBinderPlanSharedV1 ::=
  Historical=0(
    parent:OccurrenceId14V1,
    body_edge:HistoricalBodyEdgeV1)
| Synthetic=1(
    profile_version:u16,
    constructor:ConstructorTagV1,
    raw_field_path:RawFieldPathV1)
| Formal=2(
    QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportReferentPlanSharedV1 ::=
  CurrentOccurrence=0(OccurrenceId14V1)
| KernelGlobal=1(GlobalId)
| Binder=2(QuotientSupportBinderPlanSharedV1)
| RecordSlot=3(QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1)
| OperationSlot=4(
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
    operation_ordinal:u32,
    parameter_or_result:ParameterOrResultV1,
    field_ordinal:u32)
| FormalAction=5(QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanSharedV1 ::=
  PublicField=0(EnvelopeFieldPathV1)
| AuxiliaryPath=1(QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportOwnerPlanSharedV1 ::=
  Current=0(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
    location:QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanSharedV1)
| Older=1(
    export_id:ExportIdV1,
    public_field_path:PublicFieldPathV1::DeclarationField)
| Structural=2(
    terminal_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
    terminal_referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanSharedV1)
| FormalAction=3(
    formal_id:QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportKeyPlanSharedV1 ::=
  Current=0(
    occurrence:OccurrenceId14V1,
    location:QuotientSupportCurrentLocationPlanSharedV1,
    expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1)
| Older=1(
    export_id:ExportIdV1,
    public_field_path:PublicFieldPathV1::DeclarationField,
    expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1)
| Structural=2(
    terminal_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
    terminal_referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanSharedV1,
    expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1)
| FormalAction=3(
    formal_id:QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1,
    expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1).
```

The quotient referent image has no `OlderExport`, `NormalizationNode`, or
`FormalGlobal`. Older history terminals inject directly into `Owner::Older`.
The shared formal binder preserves the exact boundary/body identity, whereas
the formal owner and key retain only the enclosing primitive occurrence. Thus
two distinct formal binder referents under one primitive and one judgment may
belong to one class without identifying their dependencies or paths.

Candidate embeddings are exhaustive:

```text
EmbedCandidateBinder(Historical(p,e)) = Historical(p,e)
EmbedCandidateBinder(Synthetic(v,c,r)) = Synthetic(v,c,r)

EmbedCandidateReferent(CurrentOccurrence(q)) = CurrentOccurrence(q)
EmbedCandidateReferent(KernelGlobal(g))       = KernelGlobal(g)
EmbedCandidateReferent(CandidateBinder(b))
  = Binder(EmbedCandidateBinder(b))
EmbedCandidateReferent(RecordSlot(p))
  = RecordSlot(EmbedCandidatePath(p))
EmbedCandidateReferent(OperationSlot(p,k,s,i))
  = OperationSlot(EmbedCandidatePath(p),k,s,i)

EmbedCandidateLocation(PublicField(f)) = PublicField(f)
EmbedCandidateLocation(AuxiliaryPath(p))
  = AuxiliaryPath(EmbedCandidatePath(p))

EmbedCandidateOwner(Current(q,l))
  = Current(q,EmbedCandidateLocation(l))
EmbedCandidateOwner(Older(e,p)) = Older(e,p)
EmbedCandidateOwner(Structural(p,r))
  = Structural(EmbedCandidatePath(p),EmbedCandidateReferent(r))

EmbedCandidateKey(Current(q,l,J))
  = Current(q,EmbedCandidateLocation(l),EmbedCandidateExpected(J))
EmbedCandidateKey(Older(e,p,J))
  = Older(e,p,EmbedCandidateExpected(J))
EmbedCandidateKey(Structural(p,r,J))
  = Structural(EmbedCandidatePath(p),
               EmbedCandidateReferent(r),
               EmbedCandidateExpected(J)).
```

All maps are total and injective. The encoded action
`Inherited(candidate_binder) | Formal(formal_locus)` identity is never stored:
it lowers respectively to `EmbedCandidateBinder(candidate_binder)` or
`Formal(EmbedActionFormalBinderLocus(formal_locus))`.

#### 10.19.3 Shared graph ABI, comparator, and candidate graph theorem

The final graph family is fresh even though its field order mirrors the
candidate ABI:

```text
QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalSharedV1 = newtype u32
QuotientSupportClassOrdinalSharedV1      = newtype u32

QuotientSupportDependencyPlanSharedV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalSharedV1,
   path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1,
   referent:QuotientSupportReferentPlanSharedV1,
   expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportOwnerMapEntryPlanSharedV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalSharedV1,
   owner:QuotientSupportOwnerPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportClassEntryPlanSharedV1 =
  (class_ordinal:QuotientSupportClassOrdinalSharedV1,
   key:QuotientSupportKeyPlanSharedV1)

QuotientSupportDependencyClassEntryPlanSharedV1 =
  (dependency_ordinal:QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalSharedV1,
   class_ordinal:QuotientSupportClassOrdinalSharedV1)

QuotientSupportRootCoveragePlanSharedV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   dependency_ordinals:
     vector<QuotientSupportDependencyOrdinalSharedV1>)

QuotientSupportCoveragePlanSharedV1 =
  (roots:vector<QuotientSupportRootCoveragePlanSharedV1>,
   dependency_to_class:
     vector<QuotientSupportDependencyClassEntryPlanSharedV1>)

QuotientSupportProjectionPlanSharedV1 =
  (keys:vector<QuotientSupportKeyPlanSharedV1>)

QuotientSupportGraphPlanSharedV1 =
  (dependencies:vector<QuotientSupportDependencyPlanSharedV1>,
   owner_map:vector<QuotientSupportOwnerMapEntryPlanSharedV1>,
   classes:vector<QuotientSupportClassEntryPlanSharedV1>,
   coverage:QuotientSupportCoveragePlanSharedV1,
   projection:QuotientSupportProjectionPlanSharedV1).
```

`KeyOfShared` maps owner variants tag-for-tag and appends the complete shared
judgment. In particular:

```text
KeyOfShared(FormalAction(f),J) = FormalAction(f,J).
```

Shared key order is exactly

```text
Current < Older < Structural < FormalAction,
```

then lexicographic full canonical payload bytes within the selected case. No
digest, term equivalence, owner erasure, recipe tag, source tag, or class
ordinal participates. The candidate embedding is strictly order-preserving:
all candidate outer tags keep their ranks; candidate locus and semantic-sum
tags keep their relative ranks; the symbolic mapping
`0->0,1..10->+1,11..16->+2` is strict; and every recursive product/vector map
preserves lexicographic order. Hence for candidate keys `x,y`:

```text
compare_candidate(x,y)
  = compare_shared(EmbedCandidateKey(x),EmbedCandidateKey(y)).
```

Fresh shared ordinals are checked reconstructions, never casts or copied R2c
coordinates. Define the fieldwise graph embedding by mapping those newtypes
numerically at the same checked positions, recursively embedding each path,
referent, owner, judgment, and key, and preserving every edge, vector position,
root row, dependency/class association, and ordinal. In particular:

```text
EmbedCandidateProjection(P).keys =
  map(EmbedCandidateKey,P.keys).

EmbedCandidateGraph(G).projection =
  EmbedCandidateProjection(G.projection).
```

The embedding performs no sort, deduplication, class recomputation, or
coverage scan. Strict order preservation implies that the embedded candidate
class vector is already the correctly sorted shared class vector on the
candidate image. Therefore every candidate class ordinal, dependency-to-class
entry, seven-root coverage vector, and projection position is retained.

The schema dependency graph is acyclic: path depends only on locus and the
already frozen symbolic enum; expected judgment depends on path; binder and
referent depend only on the closed formal locators and path; owner depends on
referent/path; key depends on owner components and judgment; and graph records
depend on those leaves. No graph, class, coverage, projection, preservation
subject, lineage, footprint, or eight-group value occurs below a key.

#### 10.19.4 Total action resolution and pure graph/projection compiler

Pass B returns this erased resolver input:

```text
QuotientSupportActionLoweredDirectIncidencePlanSharedV1 =
  (root:QuotientSupportRootTagPlanCandidateV1,
   locus:QuotientSupportLocusPlanSharedV1,
   path_bindings:
     QuotientSupportActionLoweredPathBindingsPlanSharedV1,
   result_schema:QuotientSupportSymbolicTypePlanSharedV1,
   edge:RawDependencyEdgeV1,
   expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1,
   referent_recipe:
     QuotientSupportActionReferentRecipePlanCandidateV1,
   owner_resolution_recipe:
     QuotientSupportActionOwnerRecipePlanCandidateV1).
```

`LowerActionPassA` uses `LowerActionPath`, `LowerActionBindings`, and
`LowerActionExpected`, independently lowers the nominal row result through
`M_shared`, and retains only the two recipes as compiler control. It rechecks
the row/path/result equalities before resolution. The recipes, action binder
identity, term provenance, paired-row relation, decorated environments, and
preflight vector are erased and have no wire image.

The erased resolver authority is closed by stream segment rather than by a
future lineage value:

```text
QuotientSupportActionResolverSegmentPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  RebuiltSemanticRoot=0
| BasePrimaryPrefix=1
| InstantiatedActionSuffix=2
| ActionArguments=3

SegmentOf(row) =
  RebuiltSemanticRoot
    if row.root in {CanonicalPayload,IndexedInputs,IndexedOutput,
                    PublicFields,Obligations}
| BasePrimaryPrefix
    if row.root=PrimarySubjects
       and row.bare_locus=FrozenLocus(_)
| InstantiatedActionSuffix
    if row.root=PrimarySubjects
       and row.bare_locus=InstantiatedAction(_)
| ActionArguments
    if row.root=ActionArguments.
```

The four guards are disjoint and exhaustive over the frozen S1c2 stream. The
action ambient and terminal sidecar are the closed erased algebras

```text
QuotientSupportActionTraversalAmbientPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  ContextAt(context:NormalizedContextV1)
| TelescopeAt(
    base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1)
| SubstitutionAt(substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1)
| SectionAt(
    base_substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    target_telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| ActivationSectionAt(
    base_substitution:NormalizedSubstitutionV1,
    target_telescope:NormalizedTelescopeV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| FamilyAt(
    family:NormalizedFamilyValueV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| ComparisonAt(comparison:NormalizedComparisonValueV1)
| GrammarAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| SchemeAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    carrier_index:CarrierIndexValueV1)
| ActivationAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    section:NormalizedSectionV1)
| LiveAt(
    gamma:NormalizedContextV1,
    grammar:NormalizedGrammarValueV1,
    grammar_path:QuotientSupportActionPassABarePathPlanCandidateV1,
    scheme:NormalizedSchemeValueV1,
    activation:NormalizedSectionV1,
    live:NormalizedLiveDemandValueV1)
| DischargeAt(
    index:CarrierIndexValueV1::DischargeIndex,
    discharge:NormalizedDischargeValueV1)
| ContractAt(
    base_context:NormalizedContextV1,
    contract:NormalizedContractValueV1)
| TermAt(
    context:NormalizedContextV1,
    expectation:TypeFormation | HasType(expected_type:Term))

QuotientSupportActionValueTerminalPlanCandidateV1 ::=
  Historical=0(QuotientSupportHistoryTerminalRecipePlanCandidateV1)
| FormalBinder=1(QuotientSupportActionFormalBinderLocusPlanCandidateV1)

QuotientSupportActionResolverRowStatePlanCandidateV1(B,A,S_A,L[i]) =
  (segment:QuotientSupportActionResolverSegmentPlanCandidateV1,
   source_nominal_row:
     QuotientSupportActionPassANominalDirectIncidencePlanCandidateV1,
   dependency_view:
     SupportNodeView(SchemaOfShared(L[i].result_schema)),
   traversal_ambient:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionTraversalAmbientPlanCandidateV1>,
   selected_binder_identity:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionBinderIdentityPlanCandidateV1>,
   term_provenance:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionTermProvenancePlanCandidateV1>,
   historical_lineage_origin:
     Option<QuotientSupportNonemptyLineageOriginPlanCandidateV1>,
   value_terminal:
     Option<QuotientSupportActionValueTerminalPlanCandidateV1>,
   resolved_history_terminal:Option<SourceRefV1>,
   paired_native_position:Option<u32>).
```

`EmbedCandidateTraversalAmbient` injects the frozen candidate ambient
fieldwise. In each of its four `grammar_path` positions it maps
`(root,locus)` to `(root,FrozenLocus(locus))`; all other payloads are
byte-identical. An action-suffix grammar path instead retains its exact
`InstantiatedAction` locus. Every context-valued payload carries the complete
S1c2 inherited/formal binder frame in the erased parallel decoration.

At one exact physical occurrence, an inherited terminal recipe `t` becomes
`Some(Historical(t))`. An exact `Term::Var` whose selected binder is
`Formal(l)` becomes `Some(FormalBinder(l))`; this case has
`resolved_history_terminal=None`. Every other node has `None`. These cases are
selected from `dependency_view` and the decorated binder state, never from
value equality or from the nominal recipe alone.

Each component is selected by this exact segment dispatcher:

1. `RebuiltSemanticRoot` evaluates the frozen A3
   `CanonicalActionResult(B,A)` root and reruns the S1b2 walker with S1c2's
   transported term decorations. Every `LineageSource(locator)` resolves by
   A3's retention equation to the byte-identical authenticated entry of
   `B.QLineage`; action-created term origins are carried only by the S1c2
   formal/history term product and the paired edge-27 event, never by a new
   historical-lineage lookup.
2. `BasePrimaryPrefix` requires the row to be the exact embedded candidate
   row and independently reruns subsection 10.14.2's `Preflight` from
   `(B.Q1,B.Q2,B.QLineage,B.QObligations)`. Its result is embedded fieldwise;
   no stored candidate preflight is copied.
3. `InstantiatedActionSuffix` evaluates the exact S1c1 program occurrence
   against `BaseSubjectEnvironment(B)` and its S1c2 source/component/term
   provenance. A source-backed history terminal uses the same Q1-bound history
   resolver; a formal component uses its exact formal provenance and never a
   lineage lookup.
4. `ActionArguments` evaluates the exact primitive and descendant occurrence
   in the S1c2 boundary/image frames. Native inherited binders and concrete
   globals use the base history resolver; formal binders use their exact
   boundary/body locus. For an edge-27 row at position `i`, let `j` be the
   exact earlier native position at the same local-vector index fixed by
   subsection 10.18.2. After checking every non-edge nominal field, construct
   the companion state with `segment=ActionArguments`,
   `source_nominal_row=I[i]`, and `paired_native_position=Some(j)`, and copy
   only `dependency_view`, `traversal_ambient`, `selected_binder_identity`,
   `term_provenance`, `historical_lineage_origin`, `value_terminal`, and
   `resolved_history_terminal` from state `j`. The companion never copies the
   native row's segment, source row, or pair pointer.

There is no fifth case and no exception table. A rebuilt-root lineage locator
which is not the exact retained base locator, a formal source presented as
historical lineage, or an action-created historical locator aborts.

Let the unique compiler-owned capability be selected by subsection 10.14.2's
complete Q1 identity. It is a dependent lookup of the already frozen
`QuotientSupportPrivateHistoryAuthorityPlan` type, not a field of `B` or an
encoded handle. The exact preflight is:

```text
QuotientSupportActionResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1(B) =
  (q1:QuotientBindingCandidateV1,
   private_history:QuotientSupportPrivateHistoryAuthorityPlan(q1),
   base_lineage:QuotientCandidateLineageBaseCandidateV1,
   base_obligations:QuotientObligationsCandidateV1)

require authority.q1 = B.Q1 by complete ordered bytes
require authority.base_lineage = B.QLineage by complete ordered bytes
require authority.base_obligations = B.QObligations by complete ordered bytes

QuotientSupportActionResolverPreflightPlanCandidateV1(B,A,S_A,L) =
  (authority:QuotientSupportActionResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1(B),
   rows:dependent ordered vector
     [QuotientSupportActionResolverRowStatePlanCandidateV1(
        B,A,S_A,L[i]) | checked i in [0,L.len)])

CompileActionResolverPreflight(B,A,S_A,I,L):
  require I=CompileActionPassA(B,A,S_A) by complete ordered bytes
  require L=map(LowerActionPassA,I) by complete ordered bytes
  authority = select the unique
    QuotientSupportActionResolverAuthorityPlanCandidateV1(B)
    satisfying the three complete-equality requirements above
  H_B = authority.private_history
  require H_B has dependent identity authority.q1 = B.Q1
  for checked i in [0,L.len):
    segment = SegmentOf(I[i])
    rerun exactly the corresponding dispatcher above
    require source_nominal_row=I[i]
    require LowerActionPassA(source_nominal_row)=L[i]
    require every path value/schema, ambient, binder identity, provenance,
            lineage origin, terminal, history result, and pair position equals
            the product emitted at this physical occurrence
  return (authority,rows).
```

The vector is indexed by row position, not row bytes. It may read the frozen
candidate `B.QLineage`; it reads no future action-result or action-introduced
lineage and no stored action footprint. Repeated equal rows retain distinct
states. Every component is recomputed from `(B,A,S_A)` and the unique
capability, then erased before graph publication.

The action resolver reruns subsection 10.18's decorated product and the
candidate S1b3 history resolvers under the unique Q1-bound private-history
authority. Referent and owner recipes remain separate authorities. Their
exhaustive action-only executions are:

```text
ExecActionReferentRecipe(
  FormalActionBinderFromValue(Dependency,l)):
  require the dependency value is Term::Var(i)
  require the exact selected decorated binder is Formal(l)
  return Binder(Formal(EmbedActionFormalBinderLocus(l)))

ExecActionReferentRecipe(
  FormalPrimitiveArgument(Dependency,p)):
  require edge=ActionArgument=28
  require the dependency is exact P_sub(i) or P_sq(i)
  require p is its exact CanonicalPrimitive(i) locus
  return FormalAction(FormalId(p))

ExecActionOwnerRecipe(
  FormalPrimitiveOwner(Dependency,p)):
  require either the exact edge-28 case above, or an exact term dependency
          whose authenticated terminal is Formal(l)
  require OwnerPrimitive(l)=p in the term case
  return FormalAction(FormalId(p)).
```

Before either value is emitted, joint coevaluation requires exactly one of
these recipe pairs:

```text
formal edge-1 Var:
  (FormalActionBinderFromValue(Dependency,l),
   FormalPrimitiveOwner(Dependency,OwnerPrimitive(l)))

edge-28 primitive:
  (FormalPrimitiveArgument(Dependency,p),
   FormalPrimitiveOwner(Dependency,p))

formal Var on edge 4, 5, or 21:
  (FrozenRecipe(the exact RecordSlot recipe),
   FormalPrimitiveOwner(Dependency,OwnerPrimitive(l)))

every remaining native row:
  (FrozenRecipe(the exact S1b2 referent recipe),
   FrozenRecipe(the exact S1b2 owner recipe)).
```

The pair is checked against the S1c2 nominal row and decorated terminal before
executing either component. A mismatched primitive, one formal recipe with a
frozen incompatible peer, or two independently selected owners aborts.

For `FrozenRecipe`, the generalized S1b3 executor consumes shared bound paths.
An inherited `Var` resolves to
`Binder(EmbedCandidateBinder(b))` and its current/older history owner. A
concrete `Global(g)` resolves only to `KernelGlobal(g)` and its authenticated
current/older history owner. `RecordSlot` and `OperationSlot` recursively use
shared paths. A formal `Var` on edge 1 uses the first displayed formal-binder
case; a formal `Var` on edge 4, 5, or 21 retains its frozen `RecordSlot`
referent but uses `FormalPrimitiveOwner`. Every remaining structural row uses
the exact shared structural terminal. There is no structural fallback after a
failed history or formal terminal, and no `FormalGlobal` construction. Edge-27
companions bypass this native edge partition only through the paired copy rule
below; their two recipes are still required to be byte-identical to the native
row's recipes.

The edge-27 pair is resolved only after its native row. The compiler copies and
rechecks its resolved path, referent, owner, and judgment, and changes only the
edge and fresh dependency ordinal:

```text
ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).path     = ResolveShared(r).path
ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).referent = ResolveShared(r).referent
ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).owner    = ResolveShared(r).owner
ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).J        = ResolveShared(r).J
KeyOfShared(
  ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).owner,
  ResolveShared(IntroCompanion(i,r)).J)
  = KeyOfShared(ResolveShared(r).owner,ResolveShared(r).J).
```

It is never passed through candidate-only edge guards. It remains a distinct
dependency and coverage occurrence. Because its native row occurs first, it
adds no new class and no first closure seed; it still receives a total mapping
to the native row's class. If its owner is current, that seed is an exact
repeat.

Define the pure graph compiler, which publishes no enclosing support record:

```text
CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A):
  require B is the complete admitted candidate context of subsection 10.15.1
  require A is the checked total flattened action of the constructor kind

  if A.primitives=[]:
    return EmbedCandidateGraph(B.G0)

  S_A    = CompileInstantiatedActionSubject(B,A)
  I_A    = CompileActionPassA(B,A,S_A)
  L_A    = map(LowerActionPassA,I_A)
  pre    = CompileActionResolverPreflight(B,A,S_A,I_A,L_A)
  H_B    = pre.authority.private_history

  dependencies = []
  owner_map    = []
  resolved     = []
  for checked i in [0,L_A.len):
    require checked_u32(i)
    row   = L_A[i]
    state = pre.rows[i]

    if row.edge=ActionIntroduced=27:
      j = state.paired_native_position
      require j is present and is the exact earlier paired native row
      require j<i
      require resolved[j] exists
      R = resolved[j].referent
      O = resolved[j].owner
      require row.path_bindings.dependency=resolved[j].path
      require row.expected_judgment=resolved[j].expected_judgment
    else:
      require state.paired_native_position=None
      R = ExecActionReferentRecipe(row,state)
      O = ExecActionOwnerRecipe(row,state,R)

    dependencies.push(
      (dependency_ordinal=checked_u32(i),
       path=row.path_bindings.dependency,
       edge=row.edge,
       referent=R,
       expected_judgment=row.expected_judgment))
    owner_map.push(
      (dependency_ordinal=checked_u32(i),owner=O))
    resolved.push(
      (path=row.path_bindings.dependency,
       referent=R,owner=O,
       expected_judgment=row.expected_judgment))

  require dependencies.len=owner_map.len=L_A.len
  return SharedGraphCore(H_B,dependencies,owner_map).
```

Thus direct materialization is a deterministic coevaluation of `(B,A)` and the
S1c2 provenance product. It may read the frozen candidate `B.QLineage`; it does
not read a later action-result/action-introduced lineage or any stored action
footprint. No later lineage value can select a row, origin, referent, or owner.

`SharedGraphCore` validates that the already constructed direct dependency and
owner-map ordinals are checked, contiguous, equal to their physical positions,
and in the frozen root/local order; it does not reassign them. It then assigns
the checked contiguous suffix ordinals and applies the exact subsection-
10.14.3 closure algorithm with shared types:

```text
C0 = {q | some direct owner is Current(q,_)}
C  = Cl_b(H_B,C0)
A0 = sort_unique_full_occurrence_bytes(C \ C0)
```

Only `Current` owners seed `Cl_b`. Each suffix row inherits the recursively
selected forcing path, uses the least exact witness under
`(first(q_force),edge_tag)`, and stores

```text
(path=P(q),
 edge in {ClosureAncestor=24,
          ClosureBinderSibling=25,
          ClosureSameBirth=26},
 referent=CurrentOccurrence(q),
 expected_judgment=StructuralOccurrence(q),
 owner=Current(q,AuxiliaryPath(P(q)))).
```

Formal and structural owners never seed closure. After the suffix, the core
constructs keys, classes, dependency-to-class, coverage, and projection by the
same checked equations:

```text
K_i = KeyOfShared(owner_map[i].owner,
                  dependencies[i].expected_judgment)

classes = enumerate_with_checked_class_ordinals(
  sort_unique([K_i | i in dependencies]))

dependency_to_class[i] =
  (dependency_ordinal=i,
   class_ordinal=the unique j with classes[j].key=K_i)

coverage.roots = [
  (root=t,
   dependency_ordinals=[i | dependencies[i].path.root=t])
  | t in [0,1,2,3,4,5,6] ]

projection.keys = map(.key,classes).
```

Every coverage vector is strictly ascending; the seven vectors are disjoint;
their union is `[0,dependencies.len)`; and dependency-to-class is total in
dependency order. All lengths, additions, multiplications, and ordinal
conversions are checked before allocation or publication.

For a nonempty action of length `n`, the root-6 direct prefix retains exactly
`n+2*D_A` rows. Put

```text
p_i:QuotientInstantiatedActionPrimitiveLocusPlanCandidateV1 =
  (binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder,
   primitive=CanonicalPrimitive(checked_u32(i))).
```

Each primitive contributes one edge-28 formal key

```text
FormalAction(
  FormalId(p_i),
  StructuralRecord(NormalizedSubstitution))
```

or the corresponding context-square structural-record judgment. Distinct
primitive ordinals make these `n` keys distinct. Edge-27 rows add no new
class, although other formal-Var judgments may add further formal classes.

The exact identity and append laws are:

```text
CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,[])
  = EmbedCandidateGraph(B.G0)

CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A ++ [])
  = CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A).
```

The first equality is literal fieldwise equality, not merely isomorphism or a
rerun with coincident output. Caller association, intermediate images, and
identity postfixes cannot alter the total flattened action or graph.

This core is sufficient for a pure action projection. It is not the final
group-6 action support: action-introduced lineage, three independently
recomputed footprint values and their agreement, preservation-subject
packaging, and the shared eight-group value remain outside this cut.

#### 10.19.5 Concrete preservation result type and endpoint denotations

Instantiate subsection 10.15.5 with

```text
K = QuotientSupportKeyPlanSharedV1
iota_candidate = EmbedCandidateKey

SharedSupportProjectionPlan<K>
  = QuotientSupportProjectionPlanSharedV1

SchemaOfSharedPreservation(SupportProjectionSort)
  = QuotientSupportProjectionPlanSharedV1.
```

This is symbolic schema closure, not a numerical `TypeIdV1` assignment. The
candidate projection embedding is exactly the fieldwise map in subsection
10.19.3 and never re-sorts or deduplicates.

The history-renaming binder denotes a universally quantified semantic
variable, not an encoded map. Close its dependent interpretation as follows.
For admitted candidate context `B`, `AdmissibleHistoryRenaming(B)` consists of
typed isomorphisms from the complete authenticated history/evaluation input
context underlying `B` to a target input context in the same history-evidence
class. It contains coherent bijections on the exact identifier sorts, including
`OccurrenceId14V1`, `GlobalId`, and `ExportIdV1`, and transports every
identifier-bearing component of `QuotientEvaluationIdentityV1` and its
commitments. It preserves chronology and birth/declaration association,
public/private and declaration-field status, binder-entry and source-ref
relations, authentication, typing/normalization, global-owner resolution, and
the three `ClStep_b` relations. De Bruijn indices, structural paths,
profile/constructor values, and raw field paths are structurally fixed; the
evaluation identity is transported rather than required to be byte-fixed.

The isomorphism carries the exact coherently transported QG1/QG2 input
coordinates and target evaluation binding, but it carries no QG3 compiler-
success flag or preselected target graph. It induces a typed identifier
transport on every relevant semantic payload sort, including identifiers
nested in normalized terms, contexts, carrier indices, lineage, footprints,
obligations, subjects, judgments, binders, referents, owners, source refs, and
output seeds, as well as on the private-history authority. This induced
transport is available to state a later equivariance theorem; it is not copied
as a QG3 result.

`Rename_Q(B,h)` is the unique target admitted quotient context `B^h` produced
from those explicit target coordinates by independently rerunning only the
quotient-local S1b2 and S1b3 candidate compilers and canonical sorting. It does
not reconstruct QG1/QG2 from absent representative or source inputs.

For every `h` satisfying the structural isomorphism conditions, the S1b2/S1b3
reruns must succeed uniquely, inhabit the admitted dependent candidate context,
and determine its target graph coordinates. Failure is an invariant abort and
may not remove `h` from the quantified domain. No source/target projection
equality, transported or literal, is assumed by this construction. An
incoherent, partial, nonbijective, or relation-breaking map is outside that
domain by the structural definition itself. Neither `h` nor a
witness/proof/map encoding is stored in the subject or quotient. Separately,
`DenoteShared(RenamedSeed,Seed_c;B,h)` transports the semantic interpretation
of the physically stored `Seed_c`, including its evaluation identity, into
`B^h`; no second renamed seed value is stored.

Let

```text
Pi0(B) = EmbedCandidateProjection(B.G0.projection)

PiAction(B,A) =
  CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A).projection.
```

For the exact semantic environment selected by each subject binder, the four
endpoint denotations are now total and share one concrete result schema:

```text
DenoteShared(ProjectSeed,Seed_c;B) = Pi0(B)

DenoteShared(NormalizedSeed,Seed_c;B)
  = Pi0(NF_Q_c(B))
  = Pi0(B)

DenoteShared(RenamedSeed(1),Seed_c;B,h)
  = Pi0(Rename_Q(B,h))

DenoteShared(ReindexedSeed(1),Seed_c;B,A)
  = PiAction(B,A).
```

The ordinal `1` in the last two modes must select the unique earlier
`HistoryRenaming` or `FormalAction(a_c,ArbitraryNormalized,SourceCandidate)`
binder. The action environment supplies the complete checked normalized total
action of the exact constructor kind; it may be identity. The preservation
binder is a quantified syntax position only and is never a formal-support
owner. A nonempty action first constructs the separate S1c1 canonical
naturality subject and its primitive occurrences, then the S1c2 stream and
this section's graph core. No dependency arrow runs from the preservation
subject into that ownership construction.

The final aggregate is:

```text
QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectPlanSharedV1 =
  instantiate
    QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectTemplatePlanCandidateV1
  with SchemaOfSharedPreservation(SupportProjectionSort)
    = QuotientSupportProjectionPlanSharedV1

QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectsPlanSharedV1 =
  (normalization:
     QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectPlanSharedV1,
   admissible_renaming:
     QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectPlanSharedV1,
   reindexing:
     QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectPlanSharedV1).
```

The three fields contain the exact subsection-10.15.4 templates in that order
and change no template byte. Each contains its own physical `Seed_c`. The
complete aggregate census is:

```text
subjects                         = 3
physical seeds                   = 3
preservation source paths        = 3*PR_c
binder declarations              = 1+2+2 = 5
endpoint modes                   = 6
PureSupportFunctionProjection    = 6
Equality nodes                   = 3
total proposition nodes          = 9
admission-premise values         = 0
proof/evidence values             = 0.
```

For constructor order `0..6`, `3*PR_c` is exactly

```text
[60,63,63,63,60,63,75+3*h_6].
```

QG2's separate census remains `5*PR_c`, and the enclosing quotient still has
exactly the two admission values proved in subsection 10.10.6. Every subject's
two projection literals remain distinct physical nodes at positions 0 and 1,
with `Equality(0,1)` at root 2. Concrete result typing makes each equality
homogeneous; it supplies no equality witness.

#### 10.19.6 Preservation-subject truth audit

The concrete denotations expose two facts which the parametric kernel could
not decide.

First, a nontrivial exact-identifier renaming generally changes complete
shared key bytes and their canonical sort order. The intended equivariance
comparison would apply the identifier action recursively to every key of
`Pi0(B)`, re-sort that image, and compare it with the independently rebuilt
projection of `B^h`; this cut proves no such equivariance theorem. The current
subject instead compares the untransported original projection directly with
the renamed projection. Its literal equality holds exactly on the setwise
projection stabilizer

```text
sort_unique(map(RenameKey_h,Pi0(B).keys)) = Pi0(B).keys,
```

assuming the still-unproved equivariance equation. Pointwise stabilization of
every projected identifier is an easy sufficient condition, not a necessary
one. Therefore the current subject is not a generic equivariance theorem.

Second, let `A` be nonempty with `n>0`. For every primitive ordinal `i`, the
edge-28 row has the complete primitive locus `p_i` defined in subsection
10.19.4, a formal owner, and one key

```text
F_i = FormalAction(
        FormalId(p_i),
        StructuralRecord(T_i)),
```

where `T_i` is the shared symbolic substitution or context-square primitive
schema. If `i!=j`, then `F_i!=F_j`. Candidate S1b3 keys have outer variants
only `Current`, `Older`, and `Structural`, so none equals any `F_i`. Hence

```text
{F_i | 0<=i<n}
  subset PiAction(B,A).keys

{F_i | 0<=i<n}
  intersect Pi0(B).keys = empty

PiAction(B,A) != Pi0(B).
```

This proof is independent of edge-27 class neutrality and of every descendant
resolution choice. The current full-projection reindexing `Equality` is
therefore uninhabited for every nonempty action. Identity action remains the
literal equality proved in subsection 10.19.4, and normalization remains the
definitional equality through `NF_Q_c`.

These results do not invalidate or reopen the frozen subject wire. A3 states
that `OrdinarySupportV1` retains but does not prove these proposition subjects;
R2c stores no proof; and A4 closes representation without minting universal
authority. This cut therefore closes concrete shared **typing and denotation**
of the descriptors, not semantic preservation, truth, inhabitance, or theorem
authority. No later compiler, classifier, selector, or generative-capacity
calculation may assume either equality from the presence of its syntax.

Before JG2b2b3 may prove or mint a preservation authority, a separately
versioned theory corrigendum must specify the intended law. At minimum it must
replace untransported renaming equality by an explicit equivariant transport,
and replace full action-projection equality by a precise transported,
restricted, extension, or support-delta relation which accounts for the
mandatory formal-action classes. This document makes no premature choice
among those relations.

#### 10.19.7 Transaction boundary, independence, and next cut

Construction is one transaction in this order:

1. validate the frozen shared manifest and all shared sum/tag tables;
2. lower every action pass-A path, schema, and judgment independently;
3. rerun the decorated action preflight and resolve every direct row;
4. resolve and cross-check each edge-27 companion from its exact earlier paired
   native row at the same local-vector index;
5. construct the current-owner-only closure suffix;
6. construct shared keys, classes, dependency-to-class, coverage, and
   projection;
7. instantiate the result sort and the three subject descriptors; and
8. typecheck both endpoint denotations and all three homogeneous equality
   nodes without asserting their truth.

Any tag, path, schema, recipe, binder, formal locus, owner, paired-row,
closure, comparator, ordinal, class, coverage, projection, route-count,
binder-mode, proposition-node, renaming-domain, or checked-arithmetic mismatch
aborts before publication. No partial graph, subject aggregate, receipt, proof,
or R3 account is returned.

The compiler reads no representative path/support/class ordinal, R2c raw/work/
source/owner coordinate, R2c `TypeIdV1`, QG2 stored route vector, source or
syntax copy, construction transcript, closure worklist, replay, receipt,
evidence target, action trace, intermediate action image, R3 account, semantic
SR2 result, `nu`, `GCap`, `gamma`, or semantic-novelty datum. Stored or later
lineage, footprints, support, or preservation subjects cannot select action
rows or owners. Holding `(B,A)` and the applicable formal semantic environment
fixed while changing only forbidden material leaves every shared algebra,
lowered row, resolver output, graph, projection, endpoint denotation, and
subject byte unchanged.

This closes QG3-S1c3 at the representation boundary: the final shared
nonsymbolic sums and graph ABI, total candidate graph embedding, total action
pass B and pure graph/projection compiler, concrete projection sort, three
typed subject descriptors, and their endpoint denotations are fixed. It does
not close or claim a preservation theorem.

The next lawful cut is QG3-S1c4. It must replay the common paired-event census
to derive candidate-empty/action-nonempty `ActionIntroduced` lineage without
reading support; independently recompute implementation, prior-support, and
current-output footprints; compare them only after the complete shared graph
exists; and package candidate and action group-6 support around the graph and
the three typed descriptors. It must retain the truth audit above as an
explicit future theorem/corrigendum gate and must not reinterpret subject
syntax as evidence.

## 10.20 QG3-S1c4: action-introduced lineage, independent footprints, and the candidate/action group-6 support package

This cut executes item 4 of the plan's required QG3 sequence. It closes, at
the compiler-plan boundary only, the group-4 action-introduced-lineage value
left open by subsections 10.11.5 and 10.18.2, the independent recomputation of
the implementation, prior-support, and current-output footprints over the
shared graph, and the packaging of the candidate/action group-6 support value
around the shared graph, its coverage/projection, the footprint agreement, and
the three typed preservation-subject descriptors.

It deliberately closes nothing else. The shared eight-group schema and the
final group-4/group-8 placement (ledger items L5.1–L5.5), the second
transitive no-R3/no-representative/no-forbidden-raw scan and its reproofs
(L6.1–L6.6), root `0xfd`, numerical `TypeIdV1` assignments, executable
JG2b2b2 authority, and every preservation theorem remain outside this cut.
The subsection-10.19.6 truth audit is retained unchanged as an explicit
future theorem/corrigendum gate: this section packages the three descriptors
but proves, assumes, classifies by, and derives nothing from their truth.

The immutable inputs, byte-for-byte and never regenerated or changed, are:
candidate `M_nf` with its seventeen tags; the S1b3 no-formal graph `G0` and
its footprint-agreement value; the section-10.15 preservation kernel; the
S1c0–S1c3 closures, including the frozen S1c2 action stream, paired edge-27
census, nineteen-node `M_shared`, symbolic candidate embedding, shared
locus/judgment/binder/referent/owner/key sums, shared graph ABI,
`CompileSharedActionGraphCore`, and the three subject descriptors; and all
QG2 bytes, including the exact `5*PR_c` route partition and the two admission
values.

The frozen dependency direction of subsections 10.16 and 10.18,

```text
action pass-A stream -> shared manifest -> shared support -> group-4 lineage,
```

is preserved. Lineage is a projection of the same primary compiler events; it
is never an input to the stream, the manifest, the resolver, the graph, or
the footprints, and no stored lineage value can select an action row, origin,
referent, owner, class, or output seed.

### 10.20.1 Candidate-side lineage: the exact empty group-4 action-introduced-lineage value

Subsection 10.11.5 fixes that the only additional top-level action-owned
positions of the eventual eight-group value are group 4's `action_introduced`
vector and group 8's optional instantiated action subject, and that the total
flattened identity action sets those positions to `[]` and `None`. Section
9.4 fixes that action-introduced lineage does not occur in a candidate. This
subsection closes the value those statements constrain.

The lineage row and container are fresh compiler-plan wire values over the
frozen shared sums:

```text
QuotientActionIntroducedLineageRowPlanSharedV1 =
  (lineage_ordinal:u32,
   primitive:QuotientSupportFormalActionIdPlanSharedV1,
   dependency_path:QuotientSupportPathPlanSharedV1,
   expected_judgment:QuotientSupportExpectedJudgmentPlanSharedV1)

QuotientActionIntroducedLineagePlanSharedV1 =
  (rows:vector<QuotientActionIntroducedLineageRowPlanSharedV1>).
```

These are symbolic compiler-plan schemas; no numerical `TypeIdV1` is assigned
and no `0xfd` byte layout is claimed. `lineage_ordinal` is a checked
contiguous `u32` equal to the row's physical position, following the exact
group-4 ordinal discipline of `FieldLineageV1.field_ordinal` and
`OutputLineageEntryV1.output_ordinal`. `primitive` is the exact enclosing
`CanonicalPrimitive(i)` identity of the projected event, wrapped through the
frozen `FormalId` newtype with
`binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder`; no other binder tag, action-kind
copy, subject path, square-field owner sublocus, or value-node coordinate is
admitted. The remaining two fields are exactly the census event's lowered
dependency path and expected judgment; the row is minimal, storing no field
whose value is a checked constant of every admissible edge-27 event.

The edge byte is deliberately not a row field. This is the fresh R2d
canonicalization of this cut: the container is by construction the projection
of exactly the `ActionIntroduced=27` events, the projector requires
`edge=ActionIntroduced=27` on every projected event before emitting a row,
and a stored per-row constant would duplicate that check without adding a
coordinate. Likewise deliberately excluded, because subsection 10.19.4 erases
them with no wire image: the referent recipe, the owner-resolution recipe,
the action binder identity, the term provenance, the decorated environments,
the preflight vector, and the paired-row relation
(`paired_native_position`). Also excluded, because they belong to support
resolution and their inclusion would be a support-to-lineage back-edge: the
resolved referent, resolved owner, support key, class ordinal, dependency
ordinal, coverage position, and every footprint. A lineage row records that
action introduced a dependency event; it does not record how support resolved
it.

Checked row admission requires, over the row and its independently projected
census event:

```text
event.path_bindings.dependency.root            = ActionArguments
event.path_bindings.dependency.result_schema   = Term
event.result_schema                            = Term
event.path_bindings.grammar_companion          = None
event.path_bindings.operation_record_companion = None
row.dependency_path      = event.path_bindings.dependency
row.expected_judgment    = event.expected_judgment
row.primitive            = FormalId of the unique enclosing
                           CanonicalPrimitive(i) of event's dependency path
row.lineage_ordinal      = the row's physical position
row                      = the complete projection of exactly that event;
                           no field may differ from the event's projected
                           value.
```

The `Term` requirements are exact: subsection 10.18.2 fixes that every
paired native descendant locus has nominal schema `Term`, and the companion
copies its paired row's `nominal_result_schema` byte-for-byte, which lowering
carries to the lowered `result_schema`. Under the frozen lowering law
`lowered.result_schema = lowered.path_bindings.dependency.result_schema`,
the two `Term` requirements coincide; both are stated for fail-closed
redundancy. The two
`None` requirements are exact: edge-27 companions arise only from paired
native edges `{1,2,4,5,21}`, whose events carry no grammar or
operation-record companion path under the frozen binding rule. Because
`result_schema` is the constant `Term` and already occurs inside the stored
dependency path, and both companion options are the constant `None`, none of
the three is a row field; each is a checked admission constraint on the
event, and a violating event is a fail-closed abort, never a row.

The candidate and the total identity action supply the exact empty value:

```text
ActionIntroducedLineage_candidate =
  QuotientActionIntroducedLineagePlanSharedV1(rows=[])

CompileActionIntroducedLineage(B,[])
  = QuotientActionIntroducedLineagePlanSharedV1(rows=[])

CompileActionIntroducedLineage(B,A ++ [])
  = CompileActionIntroducedLineage(B,A).
```

The proof obligation of this subsection is that the first two values are
byte-identical, not merely isomorphic: both are the same fresh shared row
type with a zero-length row vector, both canonically encode the zero length,
and neither carries an option tag, sum tag, count field, digest, or
candidate/action discriminator. This is the group-4 component of ledger item
L5.2's literal-equality positive control. The third equation is the group-4
image of the frozen `QAction` append law: appending an identity to an
existing nonempty action changes no S1c2 stream segment, hence no projected
event, hence no lineage row; it does not reset a nonempty lineage to `[]`.

Wire-level shape at this boundary: the value is a bare vector, not an
`Option`. Subsection 10.11.5 fixes `[]`, not `None`, as the identity/candidate
occupancy of the group-4 position; the group-8 subject option retains `None`
separately. An `Option`-wrapped lineage, a missing position, a
candidate-schema variant without the position, or a nonempty candidate value
is a fail-closed abort.

OPEN-AT-S1C4: the enclosing four-position group-4 wrapper — the exact byte
concatenation of principal, field, output, and action-introduced lineage
inside `group_4_lineage` of root `0xfd` — is not fixed here. Section 11 fixes
the group order and this section fixes the fourth position's value; the
wrapper encoding, its interaction with the nested-use rule, and its final
placement close only with ledger item 5 (the shared eight-group schema).

### 10.20.2 Action-side lineage: independent census rerun and exact edge-27 projection

For a retained base quotient context `B` and complete nonempty total
flattened action `A` of the checked constructor kind, the lineage compiler
independently reruns subsection 10.18.2's paired native/edge-27 event census
and projects exactly the edge-27 events, in order, into lineage rows:

```text
CompileActionIntroducedLineage(B,A):
  require B is the complete admitted candidate context of subsection 10.15.1
  require A is the checked total flattened action of the constructor kind

  if A.primitives=[]:
    return QuotientActionIntroducedLineagePlanSharedV1(rows=[])

  S_A = CompileInstantiatedActionSubject(B,A)
  I_A = CompileActionPassA(B,A,S_A)
  L_A = map(LowerActionPassA,I_A)

  rows = []
  for checked k in [0,L_A.len):
    if I_A[k].edge=ActionIntroduced=27:
      require I_A[k] is the exact IntroCompanion(i,r) of its earlier paired
        native row r at the same local-vector index, where i is the checked
        ordinal of the unique enclosing CanonicalPrimitive(i), with every
        non-edge field byte-identical to r
      require L_A[k].path_bindings.grammar_companion          = None
      require L_A[k].path_bindings.operation_record_companion = None
      require L_A[k].result_schema                            = Term
      require L_A[k].path_bindings.dependency.result_schema   = Term
      require the unique enclosing primitive of
        L_A[k].path_bindings.dependency = CanonicalPrimitive(i)
      rows.push(
        (lineage_ordinal=checked_u32(rows.len),
         primitive=FormalId(p_i),
         dependency_path=L_A[k].path_bindings.dependency,
         expected_judgment=L_A[k].expected_judgment))

  require rows.len = N_27 = D_A
  return QuotientActionIntroducedLineagePlanSharedV1(rows=rows).
```

The census rerun is primary, not a replay of a stored vector: the events are
re-emitted by the frozen pure S1c2 pass-A compiler from `(B,A,S_A)` and
lowered through the frozen nineteen-node `M_shared`. The frozen
`CompileSharedActionGraphCore` reruns the same pure compilers internally
from the same `(B,A,S_A)` and returns only the graph; no per-action census
is stored, exposed, or compared, and census agreement between lineage
projection and graph construction is exactly the functional determinism of
the frozen pure compilers on identical inputs. Refactoring the graph core to
accept or expose a census would reopen frozen S1c3 and is rejected. A stored
lineage, graph, or eight-group value is never traversed to generate an
event. `p_i` is the frozen locus of `CanonicalPrimitive(i)`, and
`FormalId(p_i)` applies the frozen `EmbedActionPrimitiveLocus`, which
rechecks `binder=CanonicalCompositeNaturalityBinder`,
`primitive=CanonicalPrimitive(i)`, and the complete checked primitive
ordinal; no compiler-only wrapper or provenance product is encoded in the
row.

The row order is the exact stream order. Root-6 order is, for each primitive:
its one leading `ActionArgument=28` row, which is never projected, followed
by the paired descendant walk in which every visited node emits its complete
native local incidence vector `V` and then `[IntroCompanion(i,r) | r in V]`
in the same order before recursing through children. The lineage vector is
the subsequence of edge-27 events of that stream in stream order;
`lineage_ordinal` renumbers contiguously within the subsequence and copies no
stream position, dependency ordinal, or class ordinal.

The exact counts are symbolic and inherited unchanged from subsection
10.18.2 and the plan's §13 S1c2 closed-item ledger, which names the
edge-27 count `N_27` (`N_27=D_A`): with `m=s.images.len`,

```text
TR(Var) = TR(Global) = 1
TR(Sort) = TR(UnitType) = TR(Unit) = 0
TR(Pi(x,y)) = TR(Sigma(x,y)) = TR(Lambda(x,y))
            = TR(Apply(x,y)) = 2 + TR(x) + TR(y)
TR(Pair(x,y,z)) = 3 + TR(x) + TR(y) + TR(z)
TR(First(x)) = TR(Second(x)) = 1 + TR(x)

D_sub(s) = checked_add(checked_mul(2,m),
                       sum_(0<=k<m) TR(s.images[k]))

D_A = sum_(0<=i<n) D_sub(theta_i)                    (substitution)
D_A = sum_(0<=i<n) (D_sub(rho_i)+D_sub(sigma_i)
                    +D_sub(theta_prime_i))           (context square)

rows.len = N_27 = D_A
total root-6 direct rows = n + 2*D_A.
```

Every arithmetic step is checked before vector reservation. The paired
native edge image is exactly `{1,2,4,5,21}`; a projected event paired with an
edge-27 row, an edge-28 row, a closure row (`24..26`), a wrapper, an endpoint
context, a standalone-subject row, or any root `0..5` row is a fail-closed
abort, because subsection 10.18.2 forbids such companions from existing.

The no-support-read constraint is absolute. The projector may not inspect
published group-6 dependencies, dependency or class ordinals, coverage rows,
support keys, resolved referents or owners, any footprint, the preservation
subjects, or a previously packaged support value. It reads the census events
and nothing downstream of them. Symmetrically, no later step of this section
reads the lineage value: the graph, closure, classes, coverage, projection,
and footprints of subsections 10.20.3–10.20.4 take no lineage input. This
retains the S1c2 noncircular boundary in both directions.

Fail-closed aborts of this subsection: a census or lowering byte drift from
the frozen S1c2/S1c3 values; a missing, extra, duplicated, reordered, or
relabeled edge-27 event; a companion whose non-edge fields differ from its
paired native row; a projected row whose fields differ from its event's
lowered fields; `rows.len != D_A`; a nonempty row vector for an identity
action; a noncontiguous or nonpositional `lineage_ordinal`; a support,
footprint, class, coverage, or stored-lineage read during projection; or
checked-arithmetic failure. No partial lineage value is published.

### 10.20.3 Current-output seeds and the three independently recomputed footprints

The three footprints are recomputed from the shared graph and the reindexed
output-lineage slots only, mirroring the candidate pattern of subsection
10.14.4 field-for-field. A stored footprint is never a selector; stored
footprints are final equality targets only; and footprint comparison happens
only after the complete shared graph exists.

Let `G_A` be the complete shared graph value

```text
G_A = the complete QuotientSupportGraphPlanSharedV1 produced by
      CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A), the subsection-10.19.4
      current-owner-only closure suffix, and the frozen shared
      key/class/coverage/projection equations
```

with the frozen identity laws

```text
CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,[])    = EmbedCandidateGraph(B.G0)
CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A++[]) = CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,A).
```

The current and older projections are recomputed from the complete owner map
of `G_A`, direct rows and closure suffix alike:

```text
current_projection = ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1(sort_unique([
  q | entry in G_A.owner_map and entry.owner=Current(q,_)]))

older_projection = ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1(sort_unique([
  OlderPublicFootprintEntryV1(e,p)
  | entry in G_A.owner_map and entry.owner=Older(e,p)]))
```

`Structural` owners enter neither value, exactly as in the candidate.
`FormalAction` owners enter neither value and seed no closure: subsection
10.16.3 freezes that formal owners seed no `Cl_b` suffix and enter none of
the implementation, current-output, or prior-support footprints. Erasing a
formal owner to smuggle its material into a history footprint, or filtering
the owner map through a stored footprint, is a fail-closed abort.

Current-output seeds are derived only from the reindexed output-lineage
slots and the action-origin current occurrences actually reached there:

```text
OutLin_A = the exact output-lineage vector of the action-rebuilt
  IndexedOutput realization, recomputed once from (B,A) by the frozen
  A3 CanonicalActionResult equations and the receipt-free QG1c lineage
  mirror; OutLin_[] = B.QLineage.output by complete ordered bytes

OutputSeeds_A =
  { entry.occurrence | entry in OutLin_A,
    after checking its output ordinal, field path, raw leaf path,
    and private-history-authenticated occurrence under Q1 }

output_projection = ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1(
  sort_unique(Cl_b(H_B,OutputSeeds_A))).
```

`H_B` is the unique Q1-bound private-history authority of subsection
10.14.2 as generalized by 10.19.4; it is a dependent capability, not a field
of `B` or an encoded handle. Each entry of `OutLin_A` has the frozen
`OutputLineageEntryV1` shape `(output_ordinal, field_path, raw_leaf_path,
occurrence)`; its occurrences are current-history occurrences authenticated
under `Q1`, including those of action origin that the rebuilt output slots
actually reach. Subsection 10.18.2's prohibition is enforced literally:
projecting every root-6 edge-27 owner into the output seeds would
incorrectly admit unused action material and is a fail-closed abort. The
output projection is not a filter of the `IndexedOutput` coverage root or of
whichever closure rows inherited that root; it is a separate traversal of
the recomputed output lineage through the same exact `Cl_b`.

The stored equality targets are read only after all three left sides exist.
For the candidate and total identity action the target is the frozen
`B.QFootprints`. For a nonempty action the target is the action-rebuilt
group-5 footprint value

```text
QFootprints_A = the footprint value rebuilt once from (B,A) by the frozen
  A3 CanonicalActionResult equations and the QG1c mirror
  (QFootprints_[] = B.QFootprints by complete ordered bytes),
```

computed independently of the graph and of this section's lineage value.
Only after `G_A`, `current_projection`, `older_projection`, and
`output_projection` all exist does the compiler compare, in this order:

```text
current_projection = QFootprints_A.implementation
older_projection   = QFootprints_A.prior_support
output_projection  = QFootprints_A.current_output.
```

Any earlier read of `QFootprints_A` — as a selector of a recipe, terminal,
closure seed or witness, class, coverage row, owner, or output seed — is an
invariant abort. A comparison mismatch is a fail-closed abort publishing no
partial graph, lineage, agreement, package, receipt, or R3 account.

The agreement value is a fresh shared-typed copy of the candidate agreement
shape, containing the three independently computed left sides:

```text
QuotientSupportFootprintAgreementPlanSharedV1 =
  (current_projection:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1,
   older_projection:ExactOlderPublicFootprintV1,
   output_projection:ExactOccurrenceFootprintV1).
```

For the total identity action, the frozen literal-equality law
`CompileSharedActionGraphCore(B,[]) = EmbedCandidateGraph(B.G0)` plus the
byte-identity of `OutLin_[]` with `B.QLineage.output` make all three left
sides byte-identical to the candidate's subsection-10.14.4 values, because
the fieldwise candidate graph embedding maps each owner variant to the same
variant, preserves every `Current` owner's occurrence component and every
`Older` owner payload byte-for-byte, and introduces no formal owner. A
`Current` owner's location component is embedded, not byte-copied — an
`AuxiliaryPath` location's symbolic `result_schema` tag shifts under the
strict `0->0,1..10->+1,11..16->+2` mapping — but neither footprint
comprehension reads it: the current projection reads only the occurrence
`q` of `Current(q,_)` and the older projection only the `(e,p)` payload of
`Older(e,p)`. The identity-action agreement value therefore equals the
stored S1b3 agreement value fieldwise. This is the group-5/group-6 component of the L5.2 positive
control.

### 10.20.4 The candidate/action group-6 support package

Subsection 10.11.2 fixes that group 6 stores only the semantic
graph/projection, its receipt-free footprint agreement, and — after S1b3 and
the preservation cut — the three separate preservation subjects. This
subsection closes that composition at the compiler-plan boundary:

```text
QuotientSemanticSupportPlanSharedV1 =
  (graph:QuotientSupportGraphPlanSharedV1,
   footprint_agreement:QuotientSupportFootprintAgreementPlanSharedV1,
   preservation_subjects:QuotientSupportPreservationSubjectsPlanSharedV1).
```

Coverage and projection travel inside `graph` as its frozen `coverage` and
`projection` fields; no second copy, digest, count certificate, or reordered
view is stored. `preservation_subjects` is the exact frozen S1c3 aggregate
with fields `normalization`, `admissible_renaming`, and `reindexing` in that
order, each containing its own physical `Seed_c`, and no byte of any
template changes. The packaging census is inherited unchanged from
subsection 10.19.5 and this cut adds zero to it:

```text
subjects                      = 3
physical seeds                = 3
preservation source paths     = 3*PR_c
binder declarations           = 5
endpoint modes                = 6
PureSupportFunctionProjection = 6
Equality nodes                = 3
total proposition nodes       = 9
admission-premise values      = 0
proof/evidence values         = 0,
```

with `3*PR_c = [60,63,63,63,60,63,75+3*h_6]` in constructor order `0..6`,
QG2's separate census remaining exactly `5*PR_c`, and the enclosing quotient
retaining exactly its two admission values.

The candidate/action packaging difference is structural, not schematic.
There is one package type with no outer candidate/action sum tag. The
candidate and total identity action supply

```text
package_candidate =
  (graph=EmbedCandidateGraph(B.G0),
   footprint_agreement=the identity agreement value of
     subsection 10.20.3, fieldwise equal to the stored S1b3 agreement,
   preservation_subjects=the frozen S1c3 aggregate),
```

and a nonempty action supplies the fully rebuilt

```text
package_action =
  (graph=G_A,
   footprint_agreement=the subsection-10.20.3 action agreement,
   preservation_subjects=the frozen S1c3 aggregate).
```

Group 6 is a common position of the eventual eight-group value: it gains no
top-level action-owned field. The only top-level action-owned positions
remain group 4's `action_introduced` vector (subsection 10.20.1) and group
8's optional instantiated action subject. For a nonempty action the common
group-6 bytes may nevertheless differ from the candidate's, and may contain
nested action-only variants — `ActionDereferenced/ActionProject/
ActionProjectGrammar` loci, `Formal` binders, `FormalAction` referents,
owners, and keys, and edge-27/28 rows — exactly as section 11 provides. A
rebuild difference not accounted for by a nested action-only variant is
ledger item L5.4's fail-closed abort, checked at item 5, not here.

The subsection-10.19.6 truth-audit gate is retained explicitly and
unchanged. The three packaged descriptors are typed proposition syntax and
store no inhabitant, proof/evidence value, or theorem authority.
Uninhabitedness is proved only for the nonempty full-action reindexing
equality: the normalization subject holds definitionally through the
quotient normal-form eliminator, the total-identity-action reindexing
subject holds literally, and a renaming that setwise stabilizes the
projection may satisfy the renaming subject — none of which mints, stores,
or licenses any proof value here. The renaming subject is
not a generic equivariance theorem; the current full-projection reindexing
equality `Full(ProjectSeed)=Full(ReindexedSeed)` is uninhabited for every
nonempty action, because every primitive contributes a formal-action key
absent from the candidate endpoint. Nothing in this section — and no later
compiler, classifier, selector, provenance calculation, footprint criterion,
class relation, `GCap` audit, or authority — may use either unproved
equality as a compiler fact. Packaging asserts concrete typing and
denotation only. A separately versioned theory corrigendum (equivariant
renaming; transported/restricted/extension/support-delta action
reconstruction) remains mandatory before JG2b2b3 may mint universal
preservation authority; this section makes no choice among those relations.

OPEN-AT-S1C4: the final encoded group-6 name — including whether the
eventual frozen record retires the S1a placeholder name
`QuotientSemanticSupportCandidateV1` in favor of this shared plan shape —
its field order and encoding, and its sixth-group placement under root
`0xfd` are determined by ledger item 5, which alone freezes names, fields,
and layout. R2e is limited to symbolic-schema lowering, numerical `TypeIdV1`
assignment, and representative-byte comparison; it may not choose a name,
add, remove, or reorder a field, or change layout. This subsection fixes the
exact three-field composition and its occupancy; it does not encode root
`0xfd`.

### 10.20.5 Forbidden inputs, back-edge audit, transaction boundary, and later cuts

Construction is one transaction in this order:

1. validate the immutable inputs byte-for-byte: candidate `M_nf`, `G0` and
   its agreement, the preservation kernel, S1c0–S1c3 (stream, census,
   `M_shared`, embeddings, shared sums, graph core, subject aggregate), and
   all QG2 bytes;
2. rerun the paired native/edge-27 event census (`I_A`, `L_A`) by the frozen
   pure S1c2 compilers from `(B,A,S_A)`; agreement with the census rerun
   inside step-3 graph construction is the functional determinism of the
   same pure compilers on identical inputs, never a comparison against
   stored or exposed graph-core state;
3. construct the complete shared graph `G_A` by the frozen subsection-10.19.4
   core, closure suffix, and key/class/coverage/projection equations;
4. project exactly the edge-27 events into the group-4
   action-introduced-lineage value (zero rows for the candidate/identity),
   preserving the frozen dependency direction `shared support -> group-4
